GURUVANIKAL · January 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0001 to GV2:P0014
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani

January 1991

What is the greatest duty vested in a householder? A householder should live a disciplined life and develop the experience and effort to raise their own children in the best way possible. Even if nothing else, they should at least develop an understanding to live together in harmony as father, mother, and children, and impart this to the children. Let them grow in that understanding if they can. For that, an appropriate model must be created. If they are to be capable of that, they need a self-developed culture.

Now people are growing up divided into various castes. Therefore, what else is there other than an eagerness to prove that one's own caste is superior? When people of each caste grow up in this manner, there is nothing else left to grow in the land (world)! By developing such a very despicable system and destroying themselves, it has become the cause of global destruction.

The era of the Ettuveetil Pillamars in Travancore lasted for more than a century. When they got such a long period of time together in their hands, if they had utilized it with the knowledge that it was a right obtained for the Shudra class, our land would not have suffered such a great blunder. They did nothing in accordance with the Yugadharma (duty of the age). Can anyone say that they have done anything in that regard? Therefore, those who think even a little cannot even accept their model. Some are rationalists, some are rationalists who speak Vedanta. You, who are adept at both, are being driven out by others. You will not be helpful in anything. The one who speaks of salvation does not obey even a single word of it. Other than speaking Vedanta, what connection does he have with the essence of Vedanta? Take the Gita Yajna itself. Other than reciting a few slokas from the Gita, what heritage of the wisdom of the Gita is there in that recitation? If one studies medicine, it only has meaning if one lives as a physician.

Vedanta - it is only good for unnecessarily troubling people. In rationalism, there is no argument about anything. What argument can one who knows nothing about this world have? For the one who came, it ended up being merely positivism (pratyakshavada). All these are blunders that occurred due to our lack of intelligence. If we are to change from this degraded state of action, the woman in the house must become good. Otherwise, no action can be accomplished. Isn't it a total difficulty if a girl grows up in the house? The thought is just to somehow get her off their heads. If there are two or three girls, the mother will fight with herself. If they argue with each other, she will beat her chest - 'Are these grown-up girls filling the house only mine?' Isn't it true? That was the pain of a mother speaking. But if they had been raised the way they should have been, if they had stood the way they should have, would there be any difficulty no matter where they are? We have not been able to impart this culture.

Due to the flaws in how we grew up, we did not get the knowledge to take care of our children from anywhere. If the father can lead a respectable life, the children will more or less follow it. If the mother cannot obey, manage, and make things run here, everything will be destroyed. It is not enough if only the father is good. Only if the mother imparts all the aspects of goodness will there be room for the children's complete knowledge and humility. Or, if a woman is good, the husband will come drunk and do all sorts of unwanted things. If he wants, he will make her drink too. To my knowledge itself, there are many such people. It is their children who need to be made good. This is the subject that householders should mainly pay attention to. There is no need for any Vedanta here. If Vedanta is served before an ignorant person, it will only be like a dog getting a whole coconut. Vedanta is fine. But

More important than that is life. What is meant by life, how should one live - we must know this.

We have made many mistakes with life. We must try to rectify this. It must be brought about through children. What we need first is the sense of dharma that leads to it. I have told many people that this is your disease, it came because of these reasons, and you should do this for it. I still say it. But no one has the ability to obey it. I am sad about that. What to do? I have tried to convince thousands of people. No one is able to take it. After two or four years of leaving without being able to obey, they will come back with the same disability. Then there is no remedy. Hasn't the time passed! This karma cannot be rectified through adults. It is only possible through small children. We can bring it about to some extent through them. Everything will be fine after three generations. That is the only way it can be done.

Is there any knowledge to impart to children? No. No matter how ignorant parents are, they will have some care for their children. But that's all there is to their care. What to do? Our contemporaneity has become like this. Understanding this, we must sorrowfully think about how we should be and what we need from now on. There must be a willingness to put that thought into action at any cost. This is what you have to do, and what you must do. There is no point in just listening and saying it is good. What is good? Whatever is appropriate, I will do it as my life experience dictates, must be decided and done by the whole household. If the women in the house say they won't do any of this, the house will be lost. It means the house itself will not exist. That is certain. The house is not going to improve with other people's answers.

Do I ever tell you any Vedanta? What is needed is knowledge worthy of being connected to life. The main component of a house is the woman. The woman, who is the main component, must have some greatness in her hands. Otherwise, when they reach ten or sixteen years of age, children - whether boys or girls - cannot be kept under control. They will go according to their wishes. If these guardians cannot keep the children under control until they become knowledgeable themselves, what is their use?

Do you want life or not? If you want life, it is not enough to live somehow. Even families who have tried very hard have gone down a lot. Because they can only apply the knowledge they deserve.

I have developed this idea by hearing a lot of abuse. Not only abuse, but there were even cases and quarrels. It hurt a lot. They stopped the abuse feeling themselves that it is not right to say more. Now they don't abuse much. That's the only change to speak of. There is a reason to suffer this much. If a Vedantic karma is given, people will not be able to do it. It is giving an introduction to a life dharma. A right has been obtained from God for that. By standing firm in that dharma, it is easy to earn merit and fortune from merit. This is the basis for the survival of life itself.

Among us, karmas like Thilahavanam, Mrityunjayam, Kshetrapindam etc. are performed, aren't they? Instead of that, Pithrunivarana karma is done here. Giving the deity what they want, the deity will also be liberated if they ask for it. Then the ancestors do not have to stand under the deity. You know there is a calculation of twelve zodiac signs and twenty-seven stars. Because the god and goddess in this stand and are born in their zodiac sign, all those evils will be transferred to us. This is the evil of the karma residing in us. This is what is called the result of birth. Explaining this to the people and comparing it with their experience

It will be said. And it will be completely correct. Once convinced that it is correct, it will be said that such and such a thing should be done. Those who want to will do it. This is the correction being given from here now. Those who believe here are able to understand this. But they lack the capability to fully accept and implement it. That is not their fault. The environment in which they grew up and the present circumstances are like that. The culture required for it is being taught. But the situation is such that it cannot be accepted and implemented. Even a small child is not free from the influence of this situation.

Guruvani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · February 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0015 to GV2:P0044
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

February 1991

Material things are essential for man. Without material things, one cannot understand or respect anything. Our body is karma; it is material. What can be achieved without the material body? To accept the dharma of the body, karma is needed. Only the knowledge gained through this will be of use to us. Position also belongs to that knowledge. How have we, men and women, big and small, imparted this knowledge through our lives?

There was a time when one could diagnose a disease by feeling the pulse. As it became more modern, it reached a point where it could not be done without touching the flesh, blood, bones, and marrow. No matter where or what is examined, it is impossible to know where the disease is lodged, and all undiagnosable diseases are grouped together and put into a language that is difficult to understand. We are thus enslaved to each language. There must be a change to this mistake. It is the householders who must correct this mistake that has come about through age. Only they can correct this mistake. One has to struggle a lot for that. One has to endure much more hardship for this than the hardship experienced in the fields of husband, wife, children, education, wealth, and lordship. Let this karma of setting things right begin with each person's own home first. This should be handled in a way that we and everyone can understand. Because from birth until one is able to walk, one can only grow in the environment of the home. A mother who grows up like this will have that tendency in her blood, marrow, and flesh. To develop this tendency in the mothers at home, men must first develop it themselves. The environment of the home should be developed as much as possible in that way. When that effort comes with the attainment of merit, the very method of learning the earth's knowledge will change. When there is no such knowledgeable purity of purpose, the karmic path of mankind itself goes wrong, the dharmic path goes wrong, they become children who sow destruction, and the goodness that the earth has as God's gift will also be lost. This must be done according to the changes of the times. It is because this is not done that thinkers, administrators, caregivers, householders of the world, and all those who work for the welfare of the people have to regret that the karma they do has fallen into a vicious circle. Even those who preach great traditions of teachers and spout moral advice and Vedantic hymns in public have realized that their own lives and the overall life of the society they lead have fallen into this vicious circle. They are those who do not think about the things that have come as God's mandate or evaluate the duties of the times. Therefore, the householders have been molded in such a way that the people are caught in the grip of devotion, liberation, logic, or atheism, and are ready to throw themselves into a great fire pit that is about to come.

All the methods that the Westerners once invented to save themselves, enduring shivering cold and ice, have now been turned back to destroy us today. Despite having a nature suitable for living in any way and a land with rich agricultural yields in our possession, we sat idle, doing nothing, making others work, playing the lord, and today we are experiencing the consequences of making others do the work of a slave. That very karma has returned. The situation has come to us where if we farm, we have to ask someone else to harvest it.

To harvest the entire crop, one must stand with folded hands before the reaper. Otherwise, half the crop will have to be left in the field. Thus we stand in the midst of all degradation. We have no water here, no justice, no fairness. What kind of freedom are we experiencing? We, who have no freedom, say we are free! Do we have anything to consume today other than polluted air? Is there any of the five elements that is not polluted? This situation is slightly better in our country. Because such large companies and factories are not operating in our country. Thus the world is being destroyed. Our feeling is that it is getting better. A few ignorant people who sing praises that technology and science are making things better; a few people who are not involved in any of this; a few ignorant people who sing yes to whatever is said! This cycle of karma, which does not know how to think or make others think about the constant destruction happening in the world, makes your life itself empty. I am once again reminding you of the things that have been announced to the world for some time now as the word of God. The technology of the West, which shows its greatness by writing, drawing, and conducting experiments to prove whether the greatest invention is ours or yours, is destroying the people not only through attacks from the earth and the sky but also through ideas, actions, and governance. I am reminding you once again that if the householders of the world do not think about this, they are voluntarily preparing to throw their own generations into the great fire pit that is soon to come. Because, no matter how they are created, is it possible not to send one's own children into that very society?

What is everyone doing in the name of politics? It is an action that perishes day by day, an action that must perish. If children who know nothing follow them, their life itself becomes that. It is when half their life is over that they think back. By then, it will be impossible to return. If they return, they won't be able to live, right! Leaders will admit when half their life is over that this is not right. If you ask any leader closely, they will admit that this was a mistake. If you think carefully, you will understand that this is a first-class adverse effect done to the younger generation. But we have never realized what a great mistake this is. If householders desire and act together, this evil will fly away from the earth itself. It is enough if you understand that this is a deception, it will go away on its own. As long as you do not understand it yourself, you will not be able to respect and believe it if this is told to you. No one needs to intentionally discard this action. It is enough if you continue doing your duty with knowledge.

What is that duty? It is the completion of the karma acquired in previous births. You cannot know and complete all previous karmas. You don't even need to know it. It is enough if you learn to believe. This is what Jesus said: your faith will save you. You must learn to believe, right. You are being given a place to believe. Standing firm in that source, you must act and complete each one. You, your wife, and children are caught in the same karma. Whatever you attain, they must also attain. The father must be a person who has attained love through complete faith, karma through love, merit through karma, and fortune through merit. A mother's womb worthy of this is also required. Have we raised a mother with such qualifications? Does the father, who should be the god of the children, at least reach the knowledge to show them God? Your search should be for this. No matter what else you search for and give, it will not be enough. This is karma, qualification. The father and mother who reach this qualification complete the offspring of the world. This is not something a sannyasi should attain, you householders must

What can be done if you walk away abandoning it? Caste, religion, and race are not applicable to this family life (Grihasthashrama). The dharma of the time should guide you as a light.

This is Kali Yuga. The dharma of Kali is the guidance given by a Guru who, beyond mantras and tantras, has completed the Shakta, Shaiva, and Vaishnava traditions, acquired merits through rebirths, attained liberation, and returned as one free from karma. Kali overflows in a way that proclaims even the ideas of Jesus and the Prophet are not enough. To fulfill the dharma of Kali, the wife and mother in our homes must be as good as the wife and mother of the Prophet. At first, the Prophet could not recognize the word that came from the light. He thought it might be his imagination. It was his wife who recognized and encouraged it. They were that good and qualified. You should be able to nurture a wife who says, "I will pray for you to attain goodness." Only by attaining such qualification and performing karma can you become the inheritors of the karma to redeem the era. Otherwise, no matter how great one is, one who is unqualified in karma will not get this. For this ever-changing interplanetary cycle to bestow goodness from their respective positions, God's mercy is essential. People should get this knowledge through the governments. The people and the governments must accept this appropriately. If one pretends not to have heard or seen any of this and remains immersed in mirage-like calls and soothing words, how can one think and understand the possible interplanetary destructions that may occur? If India is to be developed as the basis for the eradication of poverty among the children of the world and as the source of God's true word, we must maintain constant vigilance to accept this bodily dharma through our lives.

Guruvani

March 1991

There are seventy-one Chaturyugas in a Manvantara. Kali is the smallest yuga in a Chaturyuga. But when compared to Western calculations, this Kaliyuga is very large. Westerners only have a calculation of six thousand or seven thousand years in total. Kaliyuga alone has four lakh thirty-two thousand years. Thus, Indians have a calculation of countless Chaturyugas. Why did such destruction come upon Hindus who have such vast calculations? How many theories are there here? How many postgraduate fields of study and research departments are there?

The one who takes pride in being a theorist went his own way. The poor man was left nowhere. The result of the karma that came in this way must be experienced. There is no point in blaming God for that. If the fruits of karma are to be taken back, a method (path) that everyone can take together must be adopted. Great men like Jesus and the Prophet have brought before us and shown us the things they sacrificed by doing karma for humanity over different times. Those who are interested in reaping its benefits are trying to see their theories as many. This is the aftermath of a deception. Should we desire to escape from this deception or not? Which path have you adopted to escape? If any path is enough, then becoming a Christian or a Muslim is enough, isn't it -- isn't it the path of the Prophet? Regarding those theories, except for the fact that time has passed (Islam has ninety more years), they are the words of the Prophet.

Whoever is born will die after a certain period. Within this much life, having earned father, mother, wife, husband, education, wealth, and lordship, how much is virtue, how much is sin, how much is fortune, and how much is misfortune? How should children understand a father who drinks toddy and beats their mother? The logic that children must experience the fruits of their karma is a kind of evasion. This evasion is not what is needed. What is needed is the knowledge and humility to ask and understand something that cannot be understood from those who know. At least a good attitude to speak openly about things is required.

When you get an opportunity to know about the karma part that makes one think about what destiny one needs and what is not needed, do not have the attitude and language that "I am also smart." After you understand that destiny, the love, affection, and richness to share it with others should remain within you. That richness can only be achieved through sacrifice. Without this sacrifice, no matter how much gold you accumulate, you are not going to gain anything. Nature will surely destroy it.

If each person has a course of karma, that course of karma will remain in their life. If the father does it, in the father; if the mother does it, in the mother; if the children do it, in the children. If one does not deliberately try to change it, it will remain unchanged. There are two paths to change this -- the path of the Guru or the path of the Prophet. The other is the path of the Devas. The time of the Deva path is over. History proves that the Deva path was followed in the Treta and Dvapara yugas. Later, Krishna, Buddha, the Parayi Petta Panthirukulam, and the eighteen Siddhars said that the Deva path is not suitable for Kali. Without listening to or obeying any of this, one had to lie down getting beaten and scolded on the logic of "the lord's rice and the slave's labor." Because someone who came from somewhere gave food, education, and freedom and saved them, a section has attained courage in karma. If you are those who have attained the courage of karma, broad-minded children capable of showing what the courage of dharma is will be born in your home. That will only happen in householders; not in ascetics. How much have you tried with your life to raise such a lineage? Aren't you raising a lineage of offspring whose arrogance grows as wealth increases, doing all sorts of unwanted things? To prevent that from happening, with humility and qualification

You should think, feel, know, make known, and act with the richness that comes when karma-gati (the path of action) and dharma-gati (the path of righteousness) - through which both merit and fortune can be earned - come together. A sanyasi (ascetic) must be one who advises this path. The false coinage of royal sages who sat on treetops listening to stories without drinking water has been implemented here in the name of sanyasa. That is a poisonous system. It is not any of this; what is needed is dharma that accords with karma. It is that dharma which must be sought, found, and knocked open. That is, one must seek and find a visionary who is capable of saying that such and such will happen by such and such a time, and believe his word. Even if anyone says their word is incorrect, it is not incorrect. Their word is the will of God. The will of God must be respected. Grounded in that faith, one must inquire and know what the special rule of karma is that is capable of sustaining the Kali dharma.

By bringing the aspect of jnana (knowledge) into karma, in order to bring one from that karma to dharma, this visionary performs the action of seeing the weight of merits and sins, wiping away all sinful karma, and transforming the jivatma (individual soul) into a virtuous soul. We must believe in such a Guru as our everything. This Guru is capable of showing the entirety of nature to us, who are the owners of karma merged in nature. Through the glory of creation, and through that glory, this Guru will show himself as well. He will show that the Guru is present in all glory. But he is none of these. Regarding this, Narayana Guru says, 'If you inquire into all that says I, I, it is not many inside, but becomes one.' When one comes to believe that this Guru is everything, seeing its point of origin, one sees creation and sustenance. Then, 'neither this nor that, this seen object is not the meaning of what is seen, but the true meaning,' and becoming courageous, softly, - softly one will settle. If you are to be brought to this settling mentioned by Sree Narayana Guru, who would the Guru you have caught hold of be?

It is not easy to get such a Guru, and even if you do, it will not be firm. How should one knock to make it firm? Becoming courageous, realizing it is the true meaning, softly - softly settling down, one has seen up to jagrat (waking), swapna (dream), sushupti (deep sleep), and turiya (the fourth state). But these states exist in turiya and turiyatita (beyond turiya). What state must the Guru who shows who is standing in that state have reached? He must be someone who has crossed turiya and turiyatita. This is what is meant by saying that when one crossed the six steps and reached there, Shiva became visible. It became visible what Shiva was, what Shiva's karma was, what validity we have attained in that karma, and what we are going to become hereafter. It has only become visible. It is later that the value of Shiva must be discovered. Thus we have been brought here. There, one must see the Guru who is devoid of names and forms - ('The place is self-luminous.'* We believe in that 'self-luminous'). From there, one can see the extent of that Guru's validity. This is the principle of the Self (Atma-tattva). What can theoreticians, postgraduates, and psychologists say here?

The Guru is showing what the principle of the Self is. If you believe, you can find it. If you knock, it can be opened. By believing, finding, and knocking it open, one has seen all the deities of creation, sustenance, and destruction. It is later that the luminous goal, which is the primordial form of all this, is made understood.

'The intellect of discrimination will come like ten million suns rising.' That is what is said here. How can you take this? We have a difficulty in this. Because we have come to believe like this. Our ancestors also came and went like this. We have received birth as the remainder of the karma done by our ancestors. And also according to the fortune of our life. The fortune of this life must be enlarged. Liberation must be given to the ancestors. We must take up the right of our karma and, through dharma, show what merit is, and transform into the form of the Self. Thus, man has come to a position with karma worthy of becoming a deva, a rishi, a sanyasi, a jnani, and a liberated one. What fortune!

* When the sky blazes with sound-

Unayumathikulesha drishyajalam; Punaravide thripudika poorthinalkkum Svanavumadangumidam swayam prakasham! (Atmopadesha Shatakam, Sree Narayana Guru.)

Guru Vani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · April 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0045 to GV2:P0059
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

April 1991

How far is the possibility of experienceability?

All the actions of the past ages must be combined and given a new dimension. To be more precise, it is not that we bring it, but God makes us do it. Everything happens according to time. However, it is true that some things change due to our ignorance. The anomalies that have arisen because we are unable to do what is necessary at the right time are huge. The ignorance that takes place in places that are touted as civilized is terrifying. There are people in this civilized world who believe very much in witchcraft. Reports show that even in America, witchcraft is feared.

What Vedic and occult practices can be seen among those who are considered the greatest in culture. Even ugly practices like jumping and shouting 'let me show the test' are taking place. A man who is ashamed to say that he is a believer will not be allowed to deny it by his family. Or if allowed, the neighbors will not agree. Even if there are believers, it is like today's political epidemic. In a house, one is a devotee of Vishnu, one is a devotee of Shiva, one is a devotee of Subrahmanya, and one is a devotee of Devi. Everyone's belief is right and great for them. If you hear their logic in it, those who hear it will also think it is right. That argument will increase and become the feeling of 'I'.

From a grain of sand to whatever things are born and destroyed in this world, everything has its own absurdity. This world exists because of the support of those absurdities. Looking at it that way, it is clear that even absurdity cannot stand without supporting another absurdity. Humans are seeing greatness in everything like a frog in a well, supporting here and there without knowing the mutual support of absurdities. When living in this world understanding this, a 'one' is needed to lean on. The truth is that if you work leaning on that one, the work will be in everything. Otherwise, the work will become empty in such a way that it cannot be felt that anything has been done.

When the work that was stuck here and there in the frenzy of the theory of indivisibility strengthened over time, it became a big pile of anomalies. This effort of ours can reduce it a bit. It is also a nihilism to say that all these anomalies can be completely wiped out from this world. Therefore, all we can do is stand behind that divine determination, combine our intelligence and ability with that determination, and pray to give us the knowledge and ability to do this. If you think about it, we don't even have the position of an atom. If you look closely at this atmosphere, you can see how many atoms are flashing in a single shape. Just think that we are only one of those countless atoms.

It is not that there is nothing. Even if you think so, it will go to emptiness. All atoms have mutual access. It should also be understood that the nature of access reaches everywhere like gas.

Isn't the nature of the mind exactly like this? Whatever our desire is, the mind enters into all those desires like a gas. Whether good or bad, the mind enters in that manner. Some ascetics are an example of this. Through certain worship practices, they believe in gods and goddesses as God and worship them. It becomes worship according to their desires. Among many such people, some will feel a desire to show off their skills in it. Then the desire to use 'Siddhi' (mystic powers) will arise. This Siddhi will be to attract people or achieve selfish things. Whatever it is, the desire itself is the goal. One person's desire may simultaneously cause harm to many.

Since the time of creation, the world has existed with good and bad. Thus, we, who are a mixture of good and bad, and many like us, must think and understand what this world is before showing miracles and miracles among miracles. Otherwise, to change the destruction caused by these miracles, one will have to constantly show miracles and make efforts. Thus, the efforts of both these groups become zero. One created and another destroyed. No one could achieve anything. This is how the one called an ascetic strives and fails. None of them did this with the desire to become bad. They slipped in their actions. If we lack the inherent tendency to act with the will of God as the goal, keeping our mind in the presence of God, this flaw will unknowingly grip us. Such flaws are what come upon us who act in many things externally and internally.

As far as a devotee is concerned, there is an inherent special quality by birth. The Yogi has it through Yogavidya, the Karmi through Karma, and the Jnani has it spontaneously. Although the goal is the same for everyone, the devotee has the ease to do it with a sense of dedication, the Yogi has an attitude born of Yoga strength, the Karmi has the attitude of "see what karma I have done", and the Jnani has the ability to make it appear that whatever anyone does is right on their part and that he himself is that. Experience has proven that only the devotee and the Jnani can achieve it easily.

Since the devotee, the yogi, and the jnani have a sattvic nature, the realm of karma is self-experiential. But because there is also something called 'Sattvika Aham' (Sattvic ego) along with this, the feeling of 'experience of this' may come and cause one to distance oneself from the observance of principles. One will unknowingly take pride in the greatness of the things one has done. That sense of pride has affected everyone. The proof that this sense of pride is mixed in everyone is the nature of people who say, "Oh! It's somewhat okay," even when they see the best thing. They didn't say it was useless, did they? This flaw is most prevalent in the greatest intellectuals. There is only one way to prevent this flaw. Have a sense of dedication to the Guru. The devotee especially must have it. The Jnani, on the other hand, having the goodness of doing right in its respective place, will not boast in goodness but will always keep his pure karma only in goodness. And he will complete the karma as if it were ordinary.

To reclaim the goodness in karma, guidance appropriate to the age (Yuga) is essential. When it is in the Kali Yuga, guidance must be given by seeing its sum and substance. For that, there must be a clear understanding of what Kali is. Kali is present in people and others. The concept of Kali that it is a time when man's goodness is completely destroyed, unable to do good, is not correct. We ourselves say that we have arrived on this earth according to the results of our past actions. If so, a little result of what was done in Treta Yuga and Dwapara Yuga will also come in this Kali, won't it? If it comes, shouldn't the good done then also come? That is, evaluating the good and bad of the past times, the fourth...

When acting in time, assessing the balance of virtues and sins, the result of the action leads to a state of liberation devoid of good and evil, which is a state of dissolution. There is no time like the Kali Yuga where any goal can be achieved through hard work. That is why even our ancestral sages desired to be born in the Kali Yuga to attain liberation.

What is happening here is an effort to evaluate and understand everything that has occurred so far, rectify its flaws, organize it according to the times, give shape to the upcoming situation, and bring it forward. This is the knowledge given to us as Brahman. It will take a long time to put the knowledge we have received into practice.

When organized in the nature of an ashram, this ashram developed some differences from the traditional ashram natures that have existed so far. The customary practice of completely following the path shown by the predecessors is what is organized here. God has entrusted us to organize the worship systems of Vaishnavism, Shaivism, and Shaktism according to Hindu principles. This is an effort to bring a 'One' in place of all three. That is how far the experiential validity of this effort extends.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · May 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0060 to GV2:P0071
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

May 1991

Like everyone else, I too was someone who believed in Hinduism. It was like that until the age of fourteen. It was only later that I understood that the goal of faith can only be achieved through a Guru's path. What kind of Guru's path? The Sri Ramakrishna Mission seemed a little better. Even in that, there is no one with realization. Apart from one person having an experience, no one else has it. After that, I sought the lineage of Sree Narayana Guru Swami. I spent about seventeen years in Sree Narayana movements. But even there, I could not find anyone with personal experience. Jagadeeswarananda Swami was the person who lived in the room next to mine. He is the one who taught the Brahmasutra to Agamananda. When I told him about my experience, he said this: 'Child, you need to see an experienced person for this. I have studied some Tantra and Vedanta. I have passed Shastri from Kerala. I have studied and taught at Banaras University for about twenty-one years. In this life of mine, I have been able to meet only one person with experience. When I asked him, he avoided me saying, child, even though you are a good soul, this is not enough for spiritual realization.' This is what Jagadeeswarananda Swami said. Then my thought became, where can I find such a person.

Finally, I found a Pathani Swami. He was an experienced person. Even though he was a Pathani, he was not someone who denied anything. No matter what faith or what kind of person approached him, he was someone who would look at everything and say what kind of person they are. What did you understand by saying a person who looks at everything and speaks? He is a Pathani. A person belonging to the religion of Islam. He would call upon Islam and ask - what is that. Asking Islam means it is a realm belonging to his religion. But he would similarly call upon and ask a ghost, a deity, a sage, and a wise man belonging to Hinduism, and explain it to us.

This is the path here. I will tell you what happened after I had the experience, from the time I started following this path seen here today. It has been twenty-nine years since I started following this. During this time, both men and women here have had experiences. But none of them came with the knowledge and desire to attain self-realization, use it for themselves until the end, and utilize it for the land, the people, and the world. I am talking about the realm of experience. They will talk about family matters and local matters. They should talk. There is an obligation to talk, isn't there? So all that can be said. But

It was necessary to evaluate the experience here conceptually, in a meaningful way, before saying other things.

This is an idea taught by God. When saying idea, it is an idea about gods and goddesses, prophets, and gurus. It does not contain the idea of gods and goddesses. Beyond all that, the idea here is about the guidance of the lineage of sages. I don't know what kind of concept you have when you say sage. If it is a sage who worships gods and goddesses and stands under them, such sages will not get this right. That is, those who have not made the experiences of how many people have been born and died their own, do not have the right to this. Something like that is said about Krishna -- 'Yatrayogeshwarakrishna'. It is this superhuman who was born, nurtured, and maintained dharma through his actions who, at the beginning of Kali Yuga, pointed out the flaws in the actions of the Shudra and guided that this is not it, this is not how it should go. Take refuge in me alone - 'Mamekam sharanam vraja.' Those flaws will disappear.

After that, Parayi petta panthirukulam, eighteen siddhas and many others came. In Kerala itself, three gurus - Shankaracharya, Sree Narayana Guru, Chattampi Swami - came. We assume that their spiritual energy still exists. But we did not see their active life. Because they were sannyasis, we kept them at a distance. Having kept them at a distance, we take pride that they are great. But we did not get close to them. Then what is our relationship with them?

Those who worship Narayana Guru Swami should not ask or state their caste. But do they sit without asking or stating caste? Then one of his words -- do not tap, do not drink, do not drink -- they follow it with dignity! Another thing is that S. N. D. P Yogam was created by Narayana Guru himself. He included people of all castes in what he created. But when he saw that they made it the property of caste, he resigned from it in mind, word, and deed.

Because it was written like that, there was a case with the Dharma Sangham for ten years after the Swami's samadhi. Later, a verdict came in favor of the Dharma Sangham. It was said that they are the ones who behave without caste. Whatever it may be, we are not able to bear this, or love it, or achieve it through action, or understand the severity of sins and virtues. Society is not ready to accept it with its dignity.

Now the things here - most people come here for healing and similar things. Ninety-nine percent of the diseases are cured, some are not. It will be said in the beginning that the rest cannot be done. Even so, people will try to get it done. But when they think they can get it done, they have a lack of mental firmness or something like not being able to do it together at home comes up and it will not be possible. But for the majority of people, it is not like that. They work and get it done as if they have the karma to do it with interest until the end. You should understand this and each of you should come here and find out as much as the experience here. If you cannot find that what is here is guidance, there is no point in you coming here. If you do not find it like that, you will only get the result of going to temples and making offerings.

This is a place of experience. Experience is to see and know in the state that the sages have called Jagrat, Swapna, Sushupti, Turiya, and Turiyatita. As said earlier, calling upon the demons, gods and goddesses, acharyas, and other great souls of all religions

The person who was my guru was one who had attained the authority to stop you and say, "You are this, you have this flaw, you must do this." I am following the method taught by that person. What is here is not a path I created by studying. It cannot be said that this method exists in many places. The blessing of the Goddess, the blessing of the God, the blessing of the Supreme Power (Parashakthi) - thus everywhere there is nothing else to see but blessings and curses. And then they pronounce it adding 'Om'. What do they pronounce? 'Om Kali'. Or whatever it may be, adding Om to everything and pronouncing it is nothing but a Hindu fabrication. But you should not assume that because of all this, it has become a Veda, a Shastra, or an Upanishadic saying. You do not need such notions. What you need is the karma (action) that must be done properly.

Who should a householder (Grihasthashrami) be? A householder must be one who knows well what he has to do, what his capacity for action should be, and performs it with constant vigilance in actions that are useful to you, your children, the land, the people, and even the world. This capacity for action is left in you, not in me. Only if you have that understanding can you believe and accept this idea. This idea is not mine. It is enough to see that I am merely an instrument. But you must know how this idea was received. This idea has been received from crores of people like gods, sages, devotees, ascetics, and wise men. In this Kali Yuga, an attempt was made to give this idea to two thousand four hundred and forty-four gurus. But they themselves came, stood, and said, "This idea is such, we did not get it, Appa! Children, we could not take this up." These subtle beings said this with their hands on their chests, crying. They do not belong to one religion or one caste. These are the words of great souls born all over the world in all religions and all castes -- Children, do not lose this, this is for the children of the world. So its importance is this: this idea meant for the children of the world must be implemented through you. That is why you come here. You must show the world a discipline of customs, a life, a karma - grow it, and show through your life how to respect the greatness of life. A responsibility has been entrusted to you to reveal what karma is and how dharma should be maintained. Without having the capacity for this, you should not be people who just look at the greatness of the place you believe in, getting bewildered seeing some blessing and curse or some test. First, you should have this knowledge, pass it on to your children, and then to the children of the world. Pray to God to have this ability.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · June 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0072 to GV2:P0091
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

June 1991

There is no one who does not wish to be good. If one more is added to the nine poisons (Navapashanams), it becomes gold. If something else is added to gold, it becomes pure gold. Similarly, life should become pure gold. What is your role in this process? Each of you is enthusiastically rushing into various actions. Everyone desires to become good. But in which path should one become good? If I become good in that path, what will I become? What exactly am I? You jump into actions without thinking about any of this.

To accomplish anything, one needs the appropriate understanding, knowledge, experience, and practice. Our land, our home, and the actions of our guardians whom we follow, are all factors that should be useful in our actions. In that same path, our children and their children, who are to prepare for tomorrow, must also become enlightened. Every individual among you must ask yourselves - what have I done for my home, my country, and if needed, for the world? Even if they wish to do something, some will act against their home. Some will act against their country. Some will act against everything. However, there are a rare few who do good for everything. The action of such people is the action that aligns with all good actions.

First, let our home itself be fine. What is the obstacle to fixing it? No one wishes for their home to be bad. Does anyone wish for their wife to be bad or children to be bad? However, there might be at least one in a thousand who wishes so. 'If only my wife would die!' 'If only my husband would die!' There are also those who think, 'Even after I did all this, my son turned out like this, it would have been better if such a person did not exist.' At the same time, there are those who wish, 'I have nine children, if I could just give them one proper meal for at least one day before I die.' We are people who see everything creatively, aren't we? What is your creative perspective on these differing mindsets mentioned?

Today, there exists a situation where such perspectives cannot be corrected. What exactly is the thinking habit of the people? Some reach a state of downfall. Some reach the top layer like a high-level book and stand in a state of not knowing where to go. Is there any possibility for higher study evident in such a book? There is a possibility. The possibilities in this must be thought out. When our daughter is a child, why did she become my child? Why did I get such luck? Why did I get such a husband? Why am I acting out all these roles in this land? What is the purpose of all this? Why was I born? Why did I grow up? Did I come to show all this and cause harm? Did I come to accumulate wealth? Or did I come to teach mischief to all the people? We need a habit of asking ourselves each of these questions. That habit is our...

It should be passed on to the children. In that place, what is the action our ancestors have done to us and to the tradition that has grown like us?

This habit of asking oneself applies to everyone. This applies to everyone without distinction of educated or uneducated, scholar or layman. You are householders. If householders do not live as they truly desire, it is useless to think that its sinful or virtuous power will not come down through generations. The only difference is that the course of action each person has to take is different for each person.

No one is bound by death. We are in the action of intellect and education. We are in a hurry to seize power through a life based on the English model. This eagerness extends from lawyers to judges and beyond to the Supreme Court. The common man does not have such a great inclination. A group of people who work and make others work day by day for a meal, another group who exploits all these hardships, a minority who wants to do at least a little good; if there are people who do good, or if they want to do it, there are others who are ready to destroy them. What is the reason for this world to be so diverse?

This thought should start from the grass. Even an atom is capable of action, so everything is contained in it. If you study atomic matter and make an atom work, you know what all can be done. If we have this education, we do not need to seek the knowledge of others to know what the course of action is. Whose course of action should be redirected according to the nature of the thinking habit.

Disciple: Actually it should be from the parents.

Guru: What if the parents do not have such luck? Or the children do not have the luck to earn it. Even if they had, what is the condition to take it with correction?

Disciple: Good association.

Guru: What is a condition with good association in this manner? All the conditions available today are like a dense scene pulled together. Have we inquired what is the way to distinguish a condition from this, accept it, act on it, bring it closer to Dharma, and bring it from Dharma to the path of virtue? Our goal is good indeed. But it does not reach the action. What should we accept for this? What is the action each person has done so far? What is needed for tomorrow? What was the Dharma of the ancestors who passed away yesterday? Whether they portrayed it wrongly or rightly, should we or someone else have the stubbornness and vengeance on how to take it back?

What kind of characters are we? How will we understand such things? When scientists studied everything and grew up, how have the situations become? If you sit in Italy or Germany and press a button, the whole globe can be destroyed. They are trying and propagating to grow and develop science more and more. How should we take it? Or how many people can take it? In the action of our ordinary life, we stand in an action that we cannot overcome no matter how much we struggle. How should we change from that action? Without virtuous parts with Dharma, how will you survive with the action that each of you thinks out?

Disciple: Think and shape the right action. Stand firm in that action and move forward. It is impossible to say how it will come.

Guru: That action is beyond thought. How should we think about that action? It is not done using anyone's intellect. The knot of knowledge through the application of intellect has entered that action. It is difficult to untie that knot. We do not need any of this for that. No one wants to be bad on their own. Growing above everything that grows for our comfort, convenience, and for the good of the country,

Each of us must be the owners of actions that benefit the world and every living being. Parents should be the first to become like this. Children should also grow up knowing what their parents and their growth are, and whether they are capable of giving them what they need. A husband must know that his wife should be able to understand him, and a wife must know that her husband should be able to understand her.

Disciple:

It doesn't seem like such an easy thing.

Guru:

If you haven't understood this after living for so long, what will little children do? A little girl growing up in front of us is meant to become a wife, a mother, and a grandmother tomorrow. She is the one who has to manage a household in our land. We must know what to instill in her. This is not something she can acquire merely through association. It is only possible through life.

GURUVANIKAL · July 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0092 to GV2:P0101
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

GuruvaniJuly 1991

It is a fortune in itself to be able to be born in this Kali Yuga, which is the fourth quarter. It is generally believed that Kali Yuga is bad. But it is not so. If Kali Yuga is bad, how can the dharma of Kali take place? Shudra dharma can only happen in Kali. Who is a Shudra? The lowest in society. This dharma must be implemented in everyone from the Vellalachetti to the Chandala. They will only understand if the extensiveness of development is explained to them one by one. What is the use of beautifully presenting and making them hear the Gita verse 'Pratyakshavaham Dharmam' through presentations like Pathakam, Kathaprasangam, or descriptions of the Kali age?

When looking at a human being in terms of caste, there are Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra. No one has come as a Brahmin by birth. They have only come as a Shudra by birth. It is by action that one becomes a twice-born (Dvija). So, in which era can a Shudra become a Dvija? All the circumstances for becoming a Dvija have naturally reached the Shudra by the grace of God. It is just that we do not know about these circumstances. Ten or fifty years ago, on the side of neglect, there was even untouchability. We must observe and know the developments that have taken place within the last five thousand years, going back from the time untouchability existed. If we go without knowing, a difficulty will arise where this dharma cannot be practiced.

Therefore, it is this generation that needs to know the most about social conduct. This generation must become aware of the mistakes that have occurred to them and then think about the reasons for them. We must historically know what mistakes have occurred to each generation in the last five thousand years. The calculation of the Vedas, Puranas, and Epics is five thousand two hundred years. The calculation of the sages and the calculation according to the Yuga dharma are also the same. That is, from the birth of Sri Krishna, the birth of Sri Krishna has been placed at the boundary of the Yuga. We see Sri Krishna as the owner of a clear background. What did we do to work and reach that condition? Instead, keeping a Madan and saying 'Oh Lord'

Aren't we just calling out? As one's own karma, one places Bhadrakali and calls out to God. There are even people who count and place thirty-three crore gods like this. Is the course of karma the same for all these, or is it different?

Karma is the driving force for everything. It is by that driving force that Madan is made a god. If so, what all qualities must that Madan be filled with? What all such disguises are dancing before us. If religion is what dances like this, is it wrong to call it the opium that intoxicates man? That intoxicating opium, which stands as the driving force to become like whatever we depend on, is never reality. You argue that religion has a broad meaning that it is the way to reach the ultimate form. In the four yugas of Satya, Treta, Dwapara, and Kali, just because one argues that the dharma of Satyayuga should be practiced in Kali, it will not happen.

Man's thought that his own land, country, and the world itself should prosper along with his own home - that goodness, that is true dharma. When that is the case, how can one perform a karma that one cannot perform oneself, without being able to perform one's own karma up to the level of Sruti, Smriti, or Purana? We have made a very big mistake.

The dharma of Satyayuga will not be practiced in Tretayuga. From the beginning of Tretayuga, a different system will come. At the end of Tretayuga, another system will come. That will be for Dwapara. The systems that come at the end of Dwapara will be for Kali. The souls who said that molten lead should be poured into the ears if one hears a letter, have today reached a state where they think that if he learns a letter and gets four paise from it, he can live. What is that? That is what I said, Kali is the age of the Shudra. There are about one hundred and eighty castes in the Shudra class. Eighty-three percent of the Indian population is the Shudra class. The remaining minority always had dominance over this majority depressed section. Not just the Vedas, whatever you take, the Vedic authority belongs to him. But due to the special nature of today's times, it has come to the downtrodden at least a little. So it has become even clearer that this is the age of the Shudra.

But the intellectual power of the Westerner, who has a tradition of only four thousand or five thousand years, stands challenging even the Brahmin today. But even though the course of time is favorable, where does the Shudra in India stand today? The culture of the Shudra stands bathed in Shudrahood itself. The elders have a notion that a Shudra will become a Brahmin through association. If so, there must be a suitable way for that. Let me give an example. In the house of Madhavan Muthalali in Pettah, Trivandrum, there was a person who always walked around with a file under his arm. This person is a degree holder. His job is to prepare and post a plan showing how it would be if a road network was easily cut from Trivandrum to Kanyakumari. He does this every day. In the same house, a person is kept by digging a floor, cementing it well, and keeping it clean. This is done to wash away his feces and urine from time to time. He is not left uncared for, he is a person who is constantly suffering from epileptic seizures and has completely lost his mind. This is the house where Narayana Guru Swami used to stay. Not only that, the Sharada idol was cast in that house itself. They used to provide facilities and whatever was needed to all the sanyasis coming from everywhere, not just here. Among those who come, there will be good and bad. It is like that in every dharma. A special room and bed are kept for Narayana Guru Swami. If other guests come, there are special facilities for that too. Adambil Vasudevan's (Judge Vasudevan) house is also one like this. In that house too, there are separate facilities for Narayana Guru Swami to stay when he comes and for others who come with him to stay.

There is a similar house in Kayikkara (Vakkom). A great-uncle of this family was there. The present name of that place in Melkadakkavoor is Guruvihar. I was the one who later fixed that place. It was fixed based on a vision I had while talking about it. This is a vision from my own experience. So, how should this vision be seen?

I have visited many such houses. I have been to the houses of the former Dewan P. N. G. Unnithan, some of his relatives in Pattom and Fort (Kizhakkekotta), and many other houses. I have also visited the houses of Brahmins in Fort (Kizhakkekotta). Back then, I was fair and thin like a wick. Now I sit like a jackfruit, don't I? At that time, there were occasions when all of them had to invite me to their homes. If I start talking about that, I will have to say a lot of things. I will tell that on another occasion.
