GURUVANIKAL · September 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0102 to GV2:P0139
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani September 1991 Birthday Message

On birthdays, most people usually write something that people like and publish it in special editions. As far as I am concerned, I have felt that it is best to write and publish about the hardships and losses in my life - it is good to experience such hardships and losses in this Kali Yuga. The Puranas also say the same thing.

The word 'experience' has a very broad meaning. We have the knowledge of experience starting from calling Madan as 'Thampuran' (Lord). If one places Madan or any other such deities in the position of God and calls them 'Thampuran', the one who does so will never reach the position of God. Moreover, even if a person who dances like a demon and performs intense poojas and worships attains certain direct experiences and special powers that can baffle people and lead them to blindness, his soul will become more despicable than that of a non-believer who considers saying he has no faith as a matter of prestige. I have been able to see this as the greatest proof in my belief. Another kind

There are also devoted people. Those who offer vows, perform trance dances, buy and dedicate elephants, conduct Kathiredppu, Pathakam, Harikatha, and Kathaprasangam for the sake of wealth and achieving their goals. There is another group of devotees. Those who experience some minor visions due to intense devotion, and when it increases, a Yogeeswaran, Manthramoorthi, or Bhadrakali who died and remained in the family stands strong in them, and they do something to make people believe until their death. The family later turns him into a favorite Manthramoorthi or Yogeeswaran and makes him the family deity. Due to such practices, that family itself will wander and become bankrupt after one or two generations. Not only that, they will also become enslaved to great sins. There is a reason for writing and revealing this. Of the thousands of such families who come to me for the expiation of sins, I am only able to resolve the sinful power of ninety-nine percent. The sinful power of the remaining few cases that cannot be resolved is either the greatest among terrible diseases or madness in a never-ending Kiriyam nature. It is something that never ends and is hereditary in the family.

If we just say it like this, one might wonder if there is anything real. There is. There is a God who transcends the scope of what was mentioned earlier. A God who cannot be confined to a temple or an idol.

In some families, there are some ancestors like sacrificing great souls who have maintained honesty throughout their lives and have managed to reach at least the position of Brahmarakshas. In later times, some children will be born in that family in the zodiac sign where such ancestors stand. Even if that child does not become a self-realized soul, he will have acquired the maturity to liberate those ancestors by doing good deeds without keeping the aforementioned artificialities. If it is a woman, there will be a lot of goodness. If it is a woman, she just needs some help in the act of giving liberation. Ancestors who attain liberation in this way can show some paths through visions to a woman or a man and make them a good devotee or a wise person. Otherwise, they will at least make them a Yogabhrashta or a wealthy person.

The best way of all this is to find a wise Guru. If a Guru is found, one will be able to attain spiritual merit at least up to the fortune of his life. All these things mentioned may perhaps seem negative to those who are not self-realized. Therefore, I wish that this auspicious day may be useful for the people to get a clear idea for a good path. In this situation where our land, not only our land but the world itself, is filled with ignorance arising from a lack of love and some foolishness of enjoying making money through barbarism, I wish that reading the messages published on such auspicious days may lead to seeking a good path that will help to bring firmness in virtue and the fortune acquired in action.

Question: Guru! If you just summarize this topic like this, we who are ignorant do not know how to take it. And we have no idea how to take it through the words of a wise person. Guru: To take this topic through the words of a wise person, the very antiquity of life is required.

Question :

It has been felt in many ways and contexts that the antiquity of life is necessary. However, we have a strong desire to know about it. Therefore, please be kind enough to explain it in detail.

Guru :

What was said earlier is all about the antiquity of life itself. What can be done if doubts still arise? Now let us talk about what is meant by the antiquity of life. Biology itself is the principle of evolution that is consolidated and shown as the whole in the whole and the atom in the atom. The Hindu belief system is that it is an eternal theory. This is the very basis of the Puranas and epics. However, some scholars, whether experienced or not, have doubts about some aspects of this belief system that do not fit logic. Therefore, they claim that what is authoritative is what the sage Badarayana has written, what the four Vedas, the six Shastras, and the eighteen Puranas have praised wholeheartedly, and then the merit of the research study of the scholars themselves. They will use it in a logical language. Such scholars, who think only with their own logic, will not be able to see the truth in biology. Then they will seek refuge in Mayavada (the doctrine of illusion). But when Mayavada comes as logic, it says that there is no second other than the one. Later, holding fast to the truth that there is no second other than the one, they will bring in logic. This is their logical distinction of studying and teaching such logic for as long as they want. Using ordinary Puranas and logical words, they will also illustrate by stating the inference of mistaking a rope for a snake. If the one who illustrates this sees a snake (let's say an eight-foot cobra) in front of him, even if he dares to chase it away with courage and arrogance without fear, if cornered, the snake will turn around and bite. The truth is that one cannot escape from that snake bite without a mantra, a resolve, or knowledge. If one holds fast to Mayavada with the force of his scholarship and logic, will he not be affected by the poison? If it were a snake instead of a rope, wouldn't Mayavada become hollow if one adopts any method to move away without getting bitten or to destroy it? The one who has studied logic, the scholar, and the ignorant are equal in this matter. However, the sannyasi has some techniques (vidyas) by which poison does not affect the body, no matter which venomous snake bites, let alone an eight-foot cobra.

Question :

What is it that you call a technique (vidya)? Isn't the sannyasi's path one of knowledge (jnana)?

Guru :

Yes, the sannyasi's path is indeed knowledge. But that knowledge does not have the method of destroying or otherwise eliminating what is destined by God. The wise one (jnani) will know such things in advance and adopt the method of escaping from that incident.

Question : Guru :

There is history of huge snakes acting as umbrellas for sannyasis and of some sannyasis having them as companions, right? There is some truth in that too. The reason for this is that most sannyasis accept the conditions in the path of worship (upasana). For example, they are those who have attained perfection (siddhi) through actions in mind, word, and deed in the paths of worship like Shaiva, Shakta, and Vaishnava, and have attained the eightfold divine powers (ashtaiswarya siddhi) as a manifest dharma. Some others have adopted the path of Kalpaseva. This is a siddhi that involves consuming everything from leaves to the leaves of the nux vomica tree. Then there is another siddhi of cooking and consuming the nine poisons (navapashanas) in various ways. When performing such a seva, they will build a cave with nine faces and sit inside it. The medicine

While practicing this, the person will not be able to stand up or walk. Even urination and defecation may stop. There is a calculation that one should face a specific direction during a specific watch (yamam). The disciples accompanying them only change their facing direction to the specified one. The disciples would have been taught in advance about the care to be given at that time. By the time the eight siddhis (attainments) are achieved, the body will return to its original state. Once this care is systematically given to the body, old age and graying will not affect it. There is also a justification for this that death will only occur when they desire it. Serpents can recognize those who have attained two or four of the Ashtasiddhis. Serpents with this recognition will turn back without staying if they see them. Or else, snakes belonging to the bad category without this recognition might bite them. But the snake's venom will turn back and affect it, and only the snake will lose its life. All this is not achieved in one birth. These are paths taken according to the vasana (tendencies) accumulated over many births. Those who have attained siddhi in this way will perform many miraculous deeds. Such siddhas are often mistakenly considered as jnanis (wise ones). Haven't you heard people asking, 'Is he a siddha?' There are karma paths that enjoy telling various stories and details about sanyasis who come after attaining such siddhis. Especially in South Travancore, such stories can be heard. One such story goes like this.

In one place, there were two brothers, an elder and a younger one. One was educated and a scholar. The other was uneducated and a fool. Both of them would take turns going to guard the banana orchard. It was the uneducated one who went to guard one day. It is said that the elder brother went there after hearing hymns being sung here and there. He asked his younger brother how he learned this. He replied that someone gave him two bananas, and when he ate the bananas, the hymns emerged. The elder brother, who took the banana peels from the base of the banana tree and ate them, also became a poet. There are people who believe even this. I have heard people say that a swami seen in one place was seen in another place at the very same time. These are all tricks brought about by the use of gaunamani, swarnamani, and rasamani. Mistaking all this for jnana, the paths we have adopted are the flaws that occur in karma.

Haven't you seen some people playing with snakes around their necks? If he hasn't extracted the fangs or kept medicine in hand, he has definitely jumped into danger. There is an incident in Koorkenchery, Thrissur, where a swami known as Pambuswami walked around with a snake around his neck. None of this should be mistaken for jnana. A true jnani will not display special siddhis. Sri Ramakrishna Paramahamsa has said that siddhi should be discarded like dog feces.

Thus, through the habit of being born and dying over many periods, the vasana of that jiva (soul) will become that. Then, whatever karma path and dharma path the jiva residing in the body made of the five elements was made to practice, that jiva can return and fall into it, becoming the owner of a jiva that shows many antics. None of this is jnana.

Question: So, are you also talking about the theory of evolution?

Guru: Yes, this is evolution itself. We must remember that if the combination of the lights of the earth, the sun, and the moon does not happen, not only animals, reptiles, termites, and other living beings, but also the creative marvels ranging from the aforementioned navapashanas to the medicinal categories that cannot be distinguished by name or number, will not be able to exist or live in their respective forms, natures, and efficacies. Even electricity, which claims to stand at the peak of growth and progress, is significantly affected by the changes in nature. Therefore, anymore of such karma theories like siddhi, vidya, and mayavada

I only wish that one shouldn't become the owner of actions that desire to chase and threaten humans.

Question: By saying this much, how will people learn about our path and the actions to be performed; or will they not learn?

Guru: For people with faith, that is, those who pray believing in a Guru lineage, there is indeed a realization of results.

Question: The realization of results also happens for those who believe in other paths. Then why is our path specifically highlighted?

Guru: Our path is not one that has come by reading or following any specific path categorized as Yoga, Bhakti, Karma, or Jnana. This is my own experience. I have experienced with absolute certainty that everything that is named exists and nothing is non-existent.

Question: Others also say that they have experienced and realized it.

Guru: What we call our experience is what has been shown in the vision regarding the greatness and great souls in the Manvantaras, the origin of the Trimurti tradition, and its realization of results in the Treta Yuga and Dvapara. Treta Yuga and Dvapara are times when one can reach as far as possible by worshiping, praying, and practicing Yoga in the Deva tradition. But in Kali Yuga, that tradition will not bear fruit - not only that, those who follow that tradition have no salvation in Kali. That is why it is said that they have reached and are standing in that path. The path that Shiva advises Parvati in the Skanda Purana is the 'Guru Gita' which confirms this experiential truth.

In the Guru Gita, Parvati asks Shiva, even though you exist as the life of all living beings and are the greatest ascetic and the most eminent among the Trimurtis, whom do you meditate upon? The subject matter of the Guru Gita is Shiva's reply in the form of advice to this question of Parvati. Even the Trimurtis have no salvation. The Guru Gita discusses how the karma and knowledge of that Supreme Being are, how the disciple understands it, and how it helps in salvation.

All human souls have been caught up in the actions of the Trimurti tradition or the Manu lineage for many ages. It has been proven beyond doubt that no matter what action one is involved in, his action will not go beyond what he does to anything else. This is believed to belong to the original path of Manu.

The children of men have believed in one of these paths - the path of the Devas, the path of the Acharyas, or the path of the Prophets - until today. We have been shown all these paths, the actions that have come according to the order of gross, subtle, causal, and effect in everything, the sum of good and evil existing in each, the deviations that caused errors, and their corrections. Having seen in experience what is meant by chanting names in the Kali Yuga, and having taken and seen the matter which is its gross, subtle, and causal, whatever exists in the form of nouns - Deva, Acharya, Prophet, Sage, Sanyasi - and its action and what connects those actions

Our path is to correct the disciple in the fields of experience and action, having seen and understood the past karmas that are the gross and subtle causes.

The path that corrects the gross, subtle, causal, and effect to this extent is rare. That is our path. The method of the gurus and wise men who have come so far, out of their compassion and greatness, correcting the mistakes that come to us. Our path is the guru's path of correcting it again without error. Here, some may see and hear certain things in the states of waking, dreaming, and deep sleep. In the two states of turiya and turiyatita, when the waking-deep sleep states occur, the guru-disciple relationship here, or the greatness called guru's compassion, consists of distinguishing and making them understand what it is, and elevating the disciple to the height of a wise man.

Kali is the age when liberation is ordained equally for the Shudra, who does not understand the meaning of the distinction of the true varna-ashrama dharma but is eligible for complete self-knowledge in the Kali age, the Brahmin, the Kshatriya, and the Vaishya, who always wants to be only a karma-doer, a merchant, and a wealthy person. That is why even the sages have prayed, "O Lord, grant me a birth and allow me to see the Kali age at least once." And the birth must be in India itself. India is the fertile ground of knowledge of all kinds of sage lineages. The section on knowledge mentioned in texts like the Guru Gita is something that man must acquire through karma.

This greatness of the guru has come as evidence revealed through the words of the subtle-sighted sages that man can reach a much higher level than the knowledge that has come so far. That is why our path provides this through knowledge in thought, word, and deed, without any magical powers, in the form of a dialogue between guru and disciple.

Praying to that ocean of compassion who has shown and told this lineage the path to untie the knots of karma and dharma that have been entangled in the soul for lifetimes through the acquired karmas that are the gross, subtle, causal, and effect of not only this lineage but the entire human world, to guide them to liberation, and to complete the dharma of the next age by coming in this age, that it is indeed the right of the human world to be freed from births and deaths forever,

Your Swami

Guruvani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · October 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0140 to GV2:P0153
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

October 1991

A few good people have come to me from the institutions of Narayana Guru and the institutions under the Ramakrishna Mission. They have told me about their lives. They have tried to ask and understand the causes of the ups and downs in life. Among them was a man named Gopalan Mash. Gopalan Mash and a few others used to come regularly when I was living in a hut in Varkala. When they came there, they all had many different kinds of diseases. Gopalan Mash used to say that it always felt like some kind of evil was hanging over him. He was also looking for a solution to it. I said that if he did as I said, a solution could be found. He agreed.

When Sree Narayana Guru sat there and asked if there was anything left in that area, they said that it was stretching like a cave. The rest was leveled and lost. I told them to tie a good arecanut leaf base at the bottom, tie palm leaves on top of it, and place a lamp inside. They did so. When six months were completed, an incident occurred. When the milkman started milking after cleaning the vessel, he saw a light shining in the vessel. When he turned around, he saw a ball of light like a ball coming out of the cave. That ball of light then went up. They themselves said that when it went up, the dogs, goats, and cats in the area of a four-mile radius started crying and making a noise.

Later I went there and looked at this. What was tied with arecanut leaf base and palm leaves was made like a small ashram. Later, whenever I went there, a few people would come there. A few people turned against it and said that this person should be stopped from coming here. Not only that, they also came to beat me. When they came to beat me, this is the man who saved me from being beaten. Otherwise they would have beaten me. They waited again. But I didn't go there again.

All this cave area was fixed, and a statue of Narayana Guru was also placed. The people there cooperated enough in it. A vessel shopkeeper there - his name is Madhavan Pillai or Chellappan Pillai or something - first brought a lamp there. That then increased a lot. It was when people started coming and believing a lot that opposition came. When opposition came, I looked at what the reason for it was.** When I told Gopalan Mash the reason for the opposition that I got from the vision, he said it was true. It is like this.

In this mentioned place, there were two girls who were older and younger sisters. One of their uncles lived as a brahmachari like a sanyasi. Even before Narayana Guru Swami went there, he made an artificial cave there, made space for a sofa, a bed, a table, and two chairs, and was running it like that. If he saw Narayana Guru Swami anywhere, he would call him and make him stay there. At that time, Swami was known as Nanu Asan. Whenever this Nanu Asan stayed there, one of the girls would clean the uruli and prepare dried rice, chutney, and curd. Then the other girl would say, 'Hey, your man has come'. This girl was not favorable to Swami. There was a mirror there then. The people of this unfavorable girl took this mirror and smashed it on the floor. Then they chopped up this bed, sofa, and chairs and threw them in a deep pool that was there.

One day when Swami came, this was the situation. Then that uncle said. It won't be right for us here anymore. I have a relative in Varkala Mundayil, I will take you there, he said, and took Swami there. From there, Swami would go somewhere during the day. When you cross the Thoduve bridge, there is a place called Pullanjimoodu. When you walk there, there is a jackfruit tree and a spring pit on one side. People come there and bathe and all. A little further away from that, there is another jackfruit tree. If you go and sit there, you can hear the roar of the water. It seemed good to meditate sitting where you can hear this roar. That is the reason for choosing Aruvappuram. It is the sound of water crashing down there. What will you feel when you reach the final stage of this sound?

You can see the water falling down in several stages, finally producing the sound 'Om'. That place where the Swami used to sit normally also had such a greatness. He used to go and sit there because of that greatness. But there were four thugs there. Thugs ruling a place is everywhere, right. These thugs were special people. Narayana Guru Swami is someone who says do not make toddy, do not give it, do not drink it. But these special thugs belonged to the group that said toddy should be made, given, and drunk. They saw the Swami there - at Nadaya. They would call the Swami and say, Swami has a sense of difference, we do not have a sense of difference. Swami would agree to whatever they say. He would also go behind them. Otherwise they would beat him. They would not let the Swami go until they made him agree that they do not have a sense of difference. One day they called the Swami like this. There was a toddy shop there that day. They entered there and bought toddy for the four of them and then for the Swami. This is their lack of sense of difference.

After this incident, he went and sat under a jackfruit tree at a place called Kattuvilakam. When he sat there, a mother came and said, I will give Swami a place to sit. That place was a hill. Like an inverted palm jaggery. When he sat there and looked around, everywhere was like this. He sat there. That is how Sivagiri was formed.

Even before Sivagiri was formed, Aruvippuram, Muttakkad, and Aluva were formed. When I described this place like this, Gopalan Mash said. It is true Swami. I told them a way to do karma. I also told them that if they do it like that for twelve years, they can achieve it. They started doing it and it was after about a year that they came to beat me. Then I did not go there. That place is today's Guruvihar.

It was after that I came here. I took this Gopalan Mash with me to find a place to tie four palm leaves. In the meantime, an announcement had been received that one should sit in such a way that no one sees for fifteen days. Therefore, I went and hid in a house in the said place. Thinking that fifteen days were over, without remembering, I went to the railway station to go to a place. When the train came and I was about to catch it, the son of a sister of the mother of the house where I was hiding pulled my towel and hit me.

The formation of Guruvihar is not in history. I made it. Did you see the experience I had when I tried to fix it? Did Narayana Guru Swami do anything wrong that the sofa, bed, and table were chopped up and he had to submit to the thugs?

From there I had a thought that I should come straight here and make a place. But there was no way to come from there. Many patients were lying there. Varkala has a terrible kind of stone. These patients are lying on that stone. Therefore, I cannot go anywhere. The family members of these patients were coming there. Then a hut-like thing was made. So it passed there. So after passing, in the twelfth year, when I went there and tried to fix it all, this beating happened. Then I did not go there. After that I came here.

Rithavani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · November 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0154 to GV2:P0167
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

November 1991

Every action here takes place according to the words received as a divine voice. It is not something figured out by my intellect or anyone else's. Receiving a divine voice means becoming qualified to be a guide to the world. For that, one must know what the karma that has been worshipped until then is. It took twenty-four years to understand all these karmas.

Seven years out of the last twelve years in Varkala, twelve years here, and another five years, making it twenty-four years in total. Even then, it was said that it was yet to be completed. Many times, the karma was performed saying it was completion. For that, Brahmacharis are needed. But there are no Brahmacharis either.

When I came here, there was a large line of Muslims. The rest were Hindus. Thus, many people started coming. They came for nothing else. Their various diseases were the reason. Even when these ignorant people came, an oracle announced 'completion' once. These people asked me what completion is. I also don't know what this completion is. But I understood that apart from what was investigated and known until then, there are other karmic paths to be found intellectually and otherwise. This is what is meant by 'do it with intellect, logic, and the power of yoga'. It should be done with the power of yoga. But with the power of which yoga should it be done?

Nine months after coming here, a woman jumped into the well and died. A child who came for treatment for a disease. Here there was a fairly well-off rich man. He brought his lorry and car and dropped people here. At that time, there was only half an acre of land enclosing this**. People broke the fence enclosing this place and entered inside. They stood ready in an aggressive mood with stones, sticks, and logs. A terrible commotion. Krodhrobam also came. Then the local police and all of them conducted a day and night search for three days. All that was here was a small hut. A number of letters were strung on a large wire so that they touched the floor. The only thing the inspectors had to take from here were these letters. They understood something from those letters. The people who wrote those letters initially addressed me as 'you'. Then it became 'Your Highness' and finally 'Guru'. There were letters from many such people. In addition, eight or ten baskets full of new clothes were stacked. Seeing that, they asked how this happened. I said that each person brought and gave it. Thus, on the night of the fourth day after all the inspections, they told the rest of the people to go, except for the ten or twelve people they had detained from here. Then they asked me what to do. I told them to do as the investigation proved. They would ask me from time to time if the Swami had anything to say. I told them to do as necessary.

Initially, these policemen and officials would not even drink water from here. They would only drink if they went outside. Later, they themselves started drawing water and cooking and eating. There was a mute person here. On the fourth day, he started speaking. They saw that this mute person had not spoken and started speaking on the fourth day. When they saw this incident and these letters, they themselves became convinced of this. After that, they stopped all their inspections. Its chief came, bowed, and apologized to me. They had unknowingly done many things. They had stepped into the room where I sit and lie down after applying ash. You are someone who knows how to forgive. Please forgive. Then you know how to purify all this. They said that I should stay here only after doing so. They said that I would have to come to his office in Thiruvananthapuram one day and then left.

That was the day before a birthday. In those days, there was no pandal or celebration like this for a birthday. It was just arranged somehow in the space available here. Some

People kept coming and going here. The police came that day too. To tell me that I had to go to their office. So, I had to go there as well.

Why am I saying all this? Is there a need to say all this? Yes, there is. You are people who believe and say that if you do good, you will get good. Believing so is not correct. You might get good. But that is in a different state.

The one who pulled the girl who jumped into the well and died to the shore snatched what was on her neck and ears. After all this commotion, I was asked what should be done with him. I told them to do as they see fit. I have my duty to do. Therefore, the officer left saying that I should come to my office. You know they have a special room. There is a system with a device like Doordarshan that receives messages from all four sides. They wanted to see me through it. They saw me. Gave me coffee and rice. Asked me to go. I came back. Again, many completions took place.

I am talking about trying to repair the houses of people who were victims of curses. This completion should be done by eleven Brahmacharis. One of them is a girl who was studying for B.Sc. at that time. According to Vedic law, there is a rule that older women should not enter, stand, or come. And that this is the history of the Maharshi. But there are other things in the history of the Maharshi. No one sees that. Let's take Suchindram itself. How did that happen? Suchindram means the place where Indra got purity. It was here that Indra got purity and recovered from the curse he caught.

Today we also have contacts. There is education, there are karma dharma cultural organizations. There is everything. It is among people who have all this that the karma that even Narayana Guru Swami had to face, as I said earlier, exists. I am telling the story of going to finish the rest of that karma. Out of eleven people, ten entered, and now only that girl who was studying for B.Sc. is left. When that girl started to enter, her grandmother stepped on her clothes. It means do not enter there. Because, what if this girl cannot be married later? That is a very difficult thing for them. The girl did not enter. Therefore, instead of her, another girl with vision was called and made to do this. This was completed by making this many people do it, and eight of them had a vision together. The symbol received in the group that had such a vision is the seal placed here today. 'Lotus and Omkara'.

So, when we tried to finish the rest of these curses, what all do we have to face. The girl who hesitated to enter for this karma - she had a vision earlier - got a disembodied voice. 'Will teach you'. It happened eight times like that. There was a drizzle that day. This girl sat still in that rain out of fear. She is a girl who is about to complete B.Sc. It was that girl who could not participate in this completion. Thus, completion has taken place twenty-seven times.

What is completion? As I said earlier, we are all owners of dharma who have received karma that can be contained by working with complete dedication. We have received such karma today. You don't even need to doubt it. My experience itself is the biggest for me. The proof for that is the experience this girl had following that. The next year at the same time after this, this girl got a disease. In her house itself. If any disease comes, they usually bring it here. The girl's foot

The nerves up to the head were twitching and beating. It wouldn't stop even if held. He was immediately taken to the hospital. Dr. Velayudhan is the son of this child's aunt. I told them to take the child there and show him. The doctor said he couldn't do anything. Later, seven surgeons took turns examining him. He was lying unconscious. He would shudder occasionally and lose consciousness again. There was no hope. They said if he regains consciousness, let him. They asked us to pray. After praying, on the twenty-first day, we were told that he would survive but there would be no use. Thus, his life was saved. Two boxes of ice had to be changed daily. That much heat. After ninety days like this, it reduced to one box. He got his life back. But he became useless for anything. He hasn't recovered yet.

What I was trying to say is this: this is the experience I had when I tried to complete a task that was cursed by Narayana Guru Swami. That place is today's Guru Vihar.

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · December 1991 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0168 to GV2:P0186
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani December 1991

I only know how to say what I see. I don't know anything else. I will do what I am told. Even though the people who were with me here at that time were ignorant, they would come and pray at the time they were told, just as I told them. Large stones are lying loose on all four sides of this. We sleep on top of those stones. We pray while walking over them. Thus, walking, lying down, and suffering, all this was completed. After that, everything started to happen one by one.

It was shown how the Trimurti system came into existence and what caused it to be cursed. This Trimurti system will not change except in a Kali. That change is for humans. Or for the world. Even in India today, whose story is celebrated and told? Krishna is considered the greatest among the avatars in Hinduism, isn't he? Why did the Dwarka of Krishna, who did so much good when he was alive, sink into the sea? Did that great man do deeds that deserved to sink like that? Moreover, our mothers also pray by addressing Krishna, who saved sixteen thousand and eight families, in obscene language, 'You who stole the clothes.........come running.......Krishna......' This is the nature of the devotion of our Hindus.

After Krishna, a lineage of countless gurus came. Buddha, Vardhamana Mahavira, Shankaracharya, Parayi Petta Panthirukulam, Guru Nanak - thus India is a great sanctuary of sages. Not only in Kali but even in the time of the Devas, the sages had the power. We know the story of Gautama who cursed Indra of the Devas. It was Anasuya, the wife of a sage, who freed him from that curse. How great is the power of that woman.

If we have a girl in our house, what we should teach her is not the story of Krishna running away with clothes. In reality, we do not know how to take the knowledge we got in this era. Did we take what so many great souls came and practiced, did actions worthy of an acharya, and taught us to take merit from that action and fortune from that merit? Did we take what Chattampi Swami came and said that Shudras have the right to Vedas, what Narayana Guru Swami came and said not to ask, say, or think about caste? Then how will we take the benefit of association with great souls?

None of us want to do an action that causes harm to ourselves. Then how did we get so much harm? I have already told you many experiences of how the very actions I have done for each person have been stolen by their own people. I have never said anything that is not in my experience. The people standing with me here are not even my family or locals. They are children of various castes and religions. Why do I make them do all this? I used to do all this myself at first. One day the plate I was worshiping with slipped from my hand. That day I was told, 'You should not worship, you must not worship, you do not know who you are or what you are. The power you worship will suffer harm.' Instead, one should develop others in that position and guide them as a Guru, rather than doing the action oneself.

At that time, one family used to give me food. They would also give food to those with me. There was a day when they gave food to forty-two people. When I went and asked, that mother said that they were not doing anything special for the Swami. We haven't put a handful of extra rice for the Swami. We haven't bought coffee or tea.

I did not know any of this. How should I understand this? I came to know about this only after seven years. It seems that two or three families like this set aside food for me and then eat. Even if they have to feed a small child in their house, they first set aside a portion for me before giving it.

While living like that, I got a message not to sit where two people could see me. On the twenty-first day, due to a lapse in memory, the incident I mentioned earlier happened. The incident at the railway station where I was grabbed by the towel, shaken, and beaten.

It was after that, without me saying anything, I made these children say it. After that, I haven't even picked up and placed a single flower. Then, all I do is give a garland or something for a wedding, that's all. Learn the lesson through me that if you do good, even if it is delayed, you will surely receive good.

I now remember something I said twelve years ago. That this place where I sit will become a densely populated town with shops and markets, surpassing other nearby places. That happened. Today, Pothencode surpasses places like Sreekariyam, Kanyakulangara, Venjaramoodu, and Mangalapuram. I have said that after three hundred and fifty years, this will become a world pilgrimage center. It is written down here.

The time of gods and goddesses is the Dwapara Yuga and Treta Yuga. Kali is not the time of gods. We have not been able to change the practice of worshipping gods and goddesses to this day. When Vedic rituals failed to yield results, they did it in the form of 'Tantra'. They have taught us to call the great priests 'Tantri'. So, they admitted that they are not doing Pooja, but Tantra. Doesn't that mean what they do is Tantra and then Mantra, not the truth? When even the one doing this says it and we cannot understand, when will we be able to properly carry forward the Dharma of Kali?

Until now, we have acted and shown results not through the quality of association or knowledge, but through the aspect of science. Because of that, we have been unable to achieve anything. We cannot even explain how the changes from our childhood to the present day have come about. Such huge changes have occurred. This is the quality of Kali. We should be able to admit at least that much. But our ancestors have said that Kali is not good. We have also assumed the same. If so, who will take the goodness of Kali? We have assumed that the same practitioners who call themselves Tantriks are performing the duties we need, keeping God at the forefront. But what have those Vedic priests, whom we thought were doing Dharma for us, become today? Learning a little English, they are half-starving people wandering in search of some business or job - even a peon's job is enough - for a living. But wherever they are, they have their Tantra. Whether in an office, a factory, or anywhere else, no matter how many people are there, only he is able to win everyone's love even today. Why doesn't this intelligence come to the rest? It is the remnant of the karma done by their ancestors as staunch believers without letting go of rituals. Our ancestors do not have such a karma practiced and done in that way for us to follow. Therefore, whatever you say or show to this lineage, that tendency will remain deep within them without leaving. I am speaking from my experience, which is the cause of the hardships I had to face in my life from such people.

When you go to such places, it should be to acquire merit from good company. It should not be with the expectation of gaining wealth, power, or any other similar positions and honors. Whatever God gives you, let that be enough for you. You will get exactly what you deserve according to your karma. I am never a hindrance to what you receive in that way.

I am one who has seen and experienced karma in the five states of waking, dreaming, deep sleep, turiya, and turiyatita. I am one who has realized what the four yugas are, what mistakes have occurred in these yugas, and how we, having reached the fourth quarter, Kali, following these dharmas of time, should realize that dharma and what good it will do for us. From a state where a Shudra was not allowed to hear a letter, we have become worthy of the karma to enter anywhere. But if we firmly resolve to remain suppressed within the old fortress of varnashrama dharma, how can we achieve anything? How can true karma and dharma be brought about? Varnashrama dharma has been brought about in such a believable manner that it seems like a reality. But we must reclaim its true nature. To reclaim it, a lineage of acharyas or a lineage of prophets, or at least a perspective of that level is necessary. But we are born into a lineage that lacks the karmic qualification to work for it or make others work for it. This is the subject we need to understand. Based on which place will you take this? It can be any place you believe in. I am not specifying a place. You must be told this. You must know if there is truth in it.

It is said that liberation is the goal of karma. How can one attain liberation without severing past karma? I have told you only a billionth of my experience. You must be convinced of it through experience. Otherwise, you can only proceed by believing what someone else has experienced and told you. We ourselves must think about how to become clear in this karma, which is progressing with the fat of knowledge from scriptures and Puranic epics. Foreigners like the British and Arabs brought their karma and propagated it among us, but what could we give them in return? It is said that India is the seat of dharma. But we prefer theirs. But he says this is what is good. Because he, who doesn't even know how to wear a dhoti, manages to wrap it somehow and walks barefoot here. He is one who has made money, bought cars, played, and enjoyed life. He did not come here to beg. He comes after depositing the necessary money in the bank. But he walks around here like a beggar.

Our people do not even know how to wander around like beggars. They do not even know why they wander around like beggars. He has understood everything here. Some people come here too. They come and tell me their experiences** here. Otherwise, I have not gone anywhere or known anything. I have not even studied a single letter of this. My only wealth is my experience.

Having known the course of dharma that has come through the three times, we have received a pure time to become an authority in a way that sustains merit by reclaiming that dharma. What do you do for that? That is what Sri Krishna said, 'Mamekam sharanam vraja'. Believe in me. Abandon everything else - what does that mean? It is said that arrows pierced the body of Bhishma lying on the bed of arrows, let the blood drain, let the sins I committed end like that, he said. Why don't you understand all this? Is it because it doesn't align with the scriptures? Unfamiliar things never

Do not say that. We must go along with science. Otherwise, we will have to say that they were created for nothing. That is not so. A lot of myths have crept into science. They must be discarded. There are plenty of truthful things. We should be able to accept them as truth. We must be able to realize that mistakes have occurred in such and such ways, find the light from those mistakes, see that light in children, and reclaim it.

GURUVANIKAL · January 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0187 to GV2:P0209
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani January 1992

When devotion arises, a special joy is attained. There is a specific way to increase that joy. At that time, if those without devotion come near, not only will we be unable to increase the devotion growing within us, but it will also fade away. Their tendencies will also enter us. To carry forward the growing devotion without interruption, many sacrifices have to be endured. People who have endured such sacrifices are generally rare in our lineage. Without the inherited nature of devotion increased through sacrifice, it is impossible to achieve material gains. Thus, we have become lacking in both devotion and material gains.

When true devotion comes, there will be no hesitation to do any work. Action is a special quality that stands alongside devotion. Whatever is done, there will be no laziness. There is a special joy in working at that time. This is because one sweats through the work. That is good. But along with it, some kind of emotion or agitation might arise. Agitation should not occur there. Great humility must also be shown. Only through that will our karma cycle be broken. A great love for everything will also be felt.

When true devotion develops, one rises slowly. One behaves only gently. Along with that, many unusual intellects will blossom. It is an intellect capable of doing everything necessary. The karma created in devotion, if not exhausted by performing it, cannot be untied even after many births. It will also follow like a divine curse.

When devotion arises, one should pray to God, surrendering, 'You gave me this much devotion, I could forget everything and get closer to you. But the next moment you also give me vile natures capable of destroying all that; what should I do about it?'. Along with praying like this, if one sincerely desires the defects to change, there will be a lot of crying. If one cries like this ten times, we will have very big changes, and along with it, a defect.

Once there was a sannyasi. A sannyasi whose logic and intellect blossomed through devotion. He had a defect. A prostitute lived next door. Even though she was a prostitute, she had a virtue. She wouldn't speak ill of anyone. She only thought that she was worse than everyone else. She is one who does wrong. The sannyasi had a thought, I will drive her away from here. He would speak ill of this prostitute to everyone. But because of this hatred of the sannyasi, he was destroyed.

To know the reason why he was destroyed like this, this sannyasi approached a guru. The guru said, 'Hatred is black magic. Your power of penance was lost because of black magic. But that prostitute is not like that. Except that such a karma happened to her in some course of karma, she is one who thinks that the mind should always be kept good. God will have mercy on those who harbor a thought of repentance about it if a mistake is committed.'

When devotion comes, even the voice will acquire a pleasant soft rhythm. Avoiding noises, it will become calm and slow. When devotion comes, many imaginations will awaken and rise in us without our knowledge. In the initial stages, one can only cry and speak. After passing many such stages, all emotional agitations will die and a state of knowing nothing will arrive. These changes in state must be recognized with great purity. Otherwise, it will lead to the destruction of devotion itself.

Girls should always take special care to keep the other members in the house satisfied. Moderation should be maintained in everything - 'Mitamavyaya'. Along with the increase in devotion, the passion for action will also increase. Even so, it should be done moderately without making a fuss. If moderation is maintained, it can be done forever. Understanding this principle, always keep doing the good that we can do. It was mentioned earlier that many defects will also rise along with devotion. This is an opportunity to work while praying meltingly, 'Please remove these defects that occur to me.' If you want to make someone obey, you should speak while doing the action. Only if you do, you can make them do. If a fellow being makes a mistake, do not speak without tact. Approach them gently and speak with humility adding greatness, that is the best.

That is how I used to do it. Perhaps if someone did not obey, I would do their work along with my work. If there is any love in them, I must grab it. That is why I did this. Then there would be a love and along with it a grudge from them. Because of that grudge, I got plenty of opportunities to cry. That is my success.

I have always done everything with a smile to everyone. You can smile at boys and girls. Then they will only speak politely. I have experienced that smiling at boys of the same age is not so good. Because I treated everyone equally, those older than me disliked me. I have suffered many disadvantages from that.

As far as boys are concerned, they have no limitations to act. You don't need to show off in front of someone who is experienced in acting, he will see everything.

When devotion comes, a certain state without any feelings of hatred will also come. That is when bliss occurs. If you get to experience that bliss, it will never leave you. If you experience that ecstasy for at least two seconds, it is a great thing, it is worth that much.

Devotion is a special merit. Not everyone gets it. Devotion that arises out of sorrow for a wife or husband is not devotion. Devotion that brings ecstasy - that is a blessing. If that devotion is attained for three and three-quarters of an hour, it means a Samadhi has been attained. If a sensible person gets this, he will try to make it experienced. Devotion is a special thing that should be experienced. There are three states of experience of devotion: waking, dreaming, and deep sleep. Everyone has it. In this state of mind, the past, present, and future will be experienced intertwined. When experienced in this way, the feeling of having seen before, the feeling that is currently existing and working, and the feeling of merging into something will occur at the same time. After waking up, a habit of all this being imprinted in the mind will also come. Later, a state will come where words are obtained from it by making it manifest. Then it will start to be obtained through the respective lights. With that, the movements of life will be shown.

The waking-deep sleep state will lead to a state beyond consciousness. That state can be called Turiya. The state commonly referred to as Sanchara Samadhi is Turiya. Along with seeing the movements of life, Sanchara Samadhi in the Turiya state can also be awakened. But for that, a Guru is needed. The Guru must be an authorized person according to Brahmanishchaya to awaken Sanchara Samadhi. This is the realization of life mentioned above.

Where is the seat of our Prana? Prana resides in the Annamaya Kosha. The body is made of Annamaya Kosha. That is, Prana resides in the body. There is a space inside the heart. It is spread in the layers of the brain. That is where Prana is located. You who see and experience where Prana is located and what its course of action is, do not pay attention to the movements of Prana. Whatever your thought is engaged in, you do not think by giving it its complete form. Even the meat trade has its own form. Even if it is a tailor, after sewing, if there is any defect in it, he will understand what it is.

It was mentioned that a great person will get ecstatic bliss for three and three-quarters of an hour. The children here** get it like that for days. Yet they do not keep it. Because they did not fast for this. The children of the boss have a habit of not wanting whatever is given to them. But try giving it to a poor man's child. He will receive it with both hands extended. That is not the situation here. There is a desire to use this. But they do not know how much benefit there is.

This should be used by giving importance to one's vasanas (inherent tendencies). One must think and understand the defects of each person's karmic path. What all were experienced in previous births, how one lived, what all actions were performed - all these must be seen, thought about, and understood. The knowledge you get is to remove these karmic paths.

Light, its different colors, the frequency of each, how it merges - to show each of these. Along with that, you can also know about your karmic path.

Didn't I mention earlier that when devotion comes, various emotions will arise. If he is a renunciate, no matter what he has to face, he will stick to the subject of devotion. If not a renunciate, sometimes it might be lost. To prevent such loss, the Guru must be present. Once this state is reached, a medium is no longer necessary.

If you look at the sky, it is empty. What all exist there? Let the mind be as vast as the sky. Let it be as deep as the ocean. Whatever comes into the mind, it cannot maintain a permanent thought process on one thing. Wherever we sit, past mischiefs tend to come. Future mischiefs also come. But in this, the one you like the most will remain permanent. But through the medium you have accepted, even that can be overcome. It can only be overcome through that. Therefore, a medium is essential.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · February 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0210 to GV2:P0246
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

February 1992

Along with devotion, some negative aspects also arise. What are those negative aspects? What all can we change in it? What all need to be completely discarded?

The experiences of those with and without mental knowledge will be different. Mental knowledge can be experiential and Vedantic. Whether one has mental knowledge or not, the first negative emotion that arises along with devotion is related to the five senses. The five senses represent the five qualities of the body, don't they? These qualities quickly rush into the mind. At that time, the mind must be protected without putting these qualities into practice.

Due to the lack of vasana, no matter how highly one can think, emotion will also be present along with it. One could say it is out of love. But there is no devotion. That is not good. Devotion must be separated from this. If you want unadulterated devotion, you must work daily and steadily to eliminate that adulteration.

There are many reasons for this in many religions. In Islam, women are not allowed to enter the mosque. But they can be buried there. There are mosques where wise women are buried. For example, Thuckalay Mosque and Beemapally.

Why is this so? It is the spiritual knowledge contained in the respective religion. This knowledge (Vedanta) is not in Christianity.

In the initial stage of devotion, our topic is how to control the five senses that rush into the mind. Suppose a certain emotion has taken hold without changing. Then at times, devotional thoughts will also be regained. We should try to increase the time we get like this. If we try, we can maintain this time for up to a day. If kept like this, it can be achieved in six months.

For this, there must be a dedication somewhere. Somewhere does not mean to a devil. It should be dedicated in a form you like with purity of heart. It is best if it is to a Guru who is God. If you see a bird, it is very beautiful. But if you check its nest, it will be a mess of stench. Man is much more stinking than this. Why is there an attraction for men and women to this stinking body, the seat of the eight passions? Out of love, and more so out of beauty. The eye only sees, it is the mind that brings closer. If this cause of attraction is not changed, it will destroy. Therefore, whenever these tendencies rush into the mind, they must be changed through action and dedication.

Is the paddy in the granary? How can one dedicate to the paddy that is yet to come into view inside the granary? In that place, one should dedicate to the Guru. That place should not be assigned to a deity. Only a Guru can be a witness to any action and dedication. A deity cannot be that. Any bad thing can be dedicated to the Guru. When dedicated to the Guru, it will be destroyed. How is it destroyed when dedicated? The course of action can be seen in the light. Then the Guru can be seen in the place of the course of action. With the Guru coming to that place, the bad aspect will catch fire and be destroyed, and good will come.

The state of Guru is equal to God. Guru and God are not different. A favorite deity or a Guru's gift is accepted as a medium to see God. There is a difference between these two. The favorite deity (Goddess) will keep the worshipper under it. Guru is not like that. The Guru state is an indescribable lightness. Because the Guru's light is filled in the five elements. The five senses are its transformation, the Guru's light filled in the five elements then becomes knowledge through the five senses. That knowledge then spreads to the Annamaya Kosha, Pranamaya Kosha, Vijnanamaya Kosha, Jnanamaya Kosha (Manomaya Kosha), and Anandamaya Kosha.

It was mentioned earlier that everything in the world is created by the Annamaya Kosha. (In the absence of Prana, nothing grows.) Once the knowledge as light reaches the Annamaya Kosha and Pranamaya Kosha, all this can be recognized in the part called the mind's knowledge. That recognition becomes a special recognition in the Vijnanamaya Kosha and is completed in the Anandamaya Kosha. Then there is only bliss, nothing else remains.

That bliss is the bliss of life. When bliss comes with special recognition, the Guru's life will shine in all living beings and exist as the Paramatma. This is the attainment of the ultimate goal. The experience that starts at a small level becomes dense and acquired by the passage of time, the advice of other wise men, and self-knowledge. This ultimate attainment must be experienced and known. Asking and knowing is not good. To experience and know, there will be no more curiosity. Once the experience begins, you can sit near the Guru who advised and ask and know.

Only then will it become clear. In any other way, it may lead to doubt and doubt may eventually lead to a decrease.

What should be the disciple's dedication? Whatever one does, it is all for the Guru. With this attitude of dedication, one should see all the attributes of God as the Guru. Then it will be understood that the Guru is one who has seen, experienced, and realized everything from merging with the five elements to the knowledge perfected in the Anandamaya Kosha. Then the Guru, God, Brahman, and Parabrahman all become one.

Growing with knowledge through birds, animals, and all living beings, rising to the highest levels, when one becomes capable of knowing what God is, one realizes through what all this life has worked for the knowledge that is touched by the five elements and perfected in the Anandamaya Kosha. The state of Guru is a personality that has gained freedom from all those conditions and flowed with self-realized knowledge.

To the true disciple who approaches that Guru, when a small experience is received testifying to this Guru, his inner self will whisper that this is the path to the ultimate goal. Here he does not need to ask. The fulfillment that cannot be attained no matter how much sacrifice one makes is obtained by the grace of the Guru that comes through dedication. It is enough to act with the firm belief that it will be obtained.

Thus, by operating, developing, and destroying the blissful experience that comes through love, sacrifice, and compassion in the five sheaths, one can know how it worked in nature in the beginning, middle, and end until destruction. Then one can know what the disciple is, what the Guru is, and why the Guru became a condition. Thus, it will be clear that the things to be asked and known should be by sitting near, and if sitting near, there is no need to ask. The problem is not whether near or far, the mind is the problem. Closeness is for minds. No matter how far away, one can hear them talking sitting near. Because the body has been taken away, there are some things to ask and know. Once known with cause and effect, questions and answers gradually decrease and eventually the need for them disappears. Now, what should be the purpose of the Guru?

Elevate the disciple to be like oneself and a little more. The Guru, the disciple, and nature become one. Nothing remains that does not fit into it. It was said to elevate the disciple a little more than the Guru. The best example of this is Sri Ramakrishna-Vivekananda. The Guru has no literacy or worldly knowledge. But the disciple has both. When self-knowledge was imparted to such a disciple, it became much broader.

One should try to change the mind day by day and elevate it through action. Patience should be gradually developed. The love for what is believed should shine through humility. The disciple's love and humility will be according to his logic, ability, and luck. If there is a slight deficiency in the mind, it should be immediately changed through dedication. One should not pray desiring anything. His resolve should be that the quality that is useful to me should be useful to everyone through my action.

When devotion increases, there will be coolness in what is loved. The humility that I have done very little will come. That devotion is what lasts, what must be maintained. Only by seeing the Guru as Parabrahman and loving him to that extent can one achieve. For that, everything must be dissolved. One must acquire intellect for intellect, love for love, and humility for humility. If one has the good deeds of past lives, one will see the Guru

You will experience it immediately. You will also understand that it is the complete love of the Guru. What is needed first is love and humility towards what is seen. It should happen spontaneously without one's own knowledge. Such people can endure any sacrifice for the sake of attaining God. They will be sweet-spoken. If these qualities are imparted to children, that is enough to improve the world. God is the same everywhere.

Guruvani

March 1992

If you do this carefully, countless people will come and go here. Through you, not only you or your home, but the world will benefit. The mind should be enslaved only to God. If you firmly think about what you perceive today in such a mental state, within six months you will be able to discern the course of the world. Within three years, you will develop a nature capable of clearly understanding and predicting the past, present, and future of any individual or country.

If you grant yourself dignity, great things will happen through you. People will say that the good things happening in the world are happening through you. Any fatal disease will be cured in a week.

The Guru should not be provoked to anger towards the disciples. It will cause great losses. It is enough if one of you develops the habit of thinking. Everyone will become alright. You should think about what we should transform these things given by God into. No one can come here and commit any wrong. He will be destroyed. I might not do anything for them to be destroyed. Because they are people who frequently come and fall at my feet. But nature will destroy them. Then I will not be able to do anything.

Our goal is to provide peace and tranquility to the world. This action is like pulling a mountain tied with a comb thread. If it comes, it is a big mountain that comes. If it goes, it is just a comb thread. There must be such a strong loving nature. Only after we experience its sacrifice and its love will we have goodness.

Suppose one of your colleagues has a small flaw. Four people should not get together and talk about it to each other and make it bigger. If you do that, another harm will come from it. But if it is handled secretly in the form of advice, that flaw will change. All that is our experience here, isn't it.

No matter how much noise they come with, you see them calm when they come here. The reason for this is the glory that has come to this atmosphere through our prayer. Previously, those who came here came for healing. Now knowledgeable people and those curious to know about spiritual matters are coming. That too is the result of our prayer. God will give us double the result of what we do. No matter how educated a person comes, they bow to us. What did the monk who came from Bangalore say? He said he couldn't express the glory of the air he breathed from the moment he entered this gate. He said he hadn't experienced such a pure atmosphere in any other ashram.

Because this atmosphere is pure, no one can enter with unwanted behavior. It is an environment here where such people cannot stay. You only need to think about what to do with the time you get and how to use it. You will get results for what you do and what you think. Cash income may be less, but prosperity is more.

The noisy demons seen in the world today must destroy themselves. Only then will we be satisfied; that is our goal. It is enough if two or four of you go out and work -- all these noises will be destroyed, and material and spiritual prosperity will be strengthened in the land. That is where the survival of our idea given by God lies. Whatever work you do, your intention should be this -- this idea should survive with the world, and through it permanent peace and tranquility to the world. When working, the feeling that 'I did all this, I brought it about' should not come.

That will do harm. What if someone sees? What if they don't see? Doing it for something, not to show anyone. You must think with your intellect and act very carefully. When that happens, good will come into action without our knowing. This atmosphere will always be calm and glorious. The thinking habit that takes shape from it will be strengthened through action, shaken, troubled, and finally become full of goodness. Whoever among you does whatever work sitting anywhere, everyone should together intend to come forward in that work being done. Those working inside and outside the ashram should know each other and share love. That is true love, merit.

Many obstacles will arise between the time the capacity for action moves from one state and reaches another strong state. There, dedicated action completely devoid of ego will reach the goal. The intention that 'God, make the big and small actions I do fruitful, whatever I take and give, let it be fruitful' should always be there. There will sometimes be moments when devotion grows, the mind fills, and intentions arise. If done strongly at that time, all harms will be cut off.

Let's go. Three or four intelligent people should get together and think about how to implement this in the world. Everyone should pray that those who work and those who have vision come forward. Only that will help the upliftment of this lineage. Through the lineage, the country, and then the world. This is the nature of the spread of this idea.

How can the love of God be instilled in these blind people? Before that, if one worships God, if one develops love for God, one must think about what the consequences will be. We have the notion that worshipping God will bring complete happiness. But what I have to say is about an experience completely opposite to this. This body is the cause of sorrow. Those who worship God will experience sorrows from time to time. Because the vasanas (tendencies) that have been residing in us for ages through countless births are being untied one by one. Occasionally, happiness will also come. We must give importance to that happiness. It is an imperishable happiness. But there are those who live according to what they see without paying attention to any of this, considering themselves and being considered by others as knowledgeable. We can see them going to London and Paris for their own happiness and to bring happiness to others. They only feel happy if they go there. At the same time, if their own mother is lying nearby unwell, they don't even have the time to go and check on her. What is all this? It is a lack of love. If the parents have money, they will find the time to inquire about them. But what should be our perspective regarding happiness?

It is not like going to the market, the hospital, a political speech, or a temple; there is a love here that is above all that. Everyone participating in the Akhanda Namajapam here experiences a calmness, knowingly or unknowingly. Those who come here somehow blend in with the ways here. A calmness from this atmosphere and from certain individuals, we must preserve that. Even if there is nothing in action, a calmness is felt. The reason for that is that God makes the actions we do fruitful. Now there is more to change and come from this atmosphere. This has been accumulated here a long time ago. It is the foundation needed for this lineage.

What is the nature of this era? It is corrupted by spreading demonic qualities. In my childhood, there was a time when there was a notion that producing an unnecessary sound or word was a mistake. Today it is exactly the opposite. A time ruled by the demonic. God has a plan to change this. The pain of this is first felt by the sannyasi. A situation where he is not given any kind of help or convenience. Everything around him will be completely unfavorable to him. No one will obey him, and the opposite side of it will be very strong. If he is spiritually inclined, all the demonic forces will attack him and make him suffer inexpressibly. However, where a chicken is slaughtered and an oracle dances, there is no difficulty. Those with the nature of an oracle will not be able to stand here if they come. But there are some sannyasis who seem to have reached the perfection of sannyasa. Those in whom devotion and humility are strong. Such people will unknowingly stay here.

The reason for all this is that there is not even a trace of the demonic here. To completely remove the demonic nature, we established a karma here a long time ago. God has informed us about it as completion. Two thousand four hundred and forty-four gurus and their entire lineage have been shown to us. Among them, the first seven gurus have ten thousand crore disciples each. The rest have four thousand crore each. The demonic nature in all these guru lineages has been eliminated.

The gurus were brought into the divine light. It took four and a half years to purify these guru lineages that stood in a corrupted atmosphere. Even after that, many completions took place. Finally, in 1973, God announced, 'What I had desired for ages has been fulfilled.' With this, the disciple lineage of these gurus has been taking birth all over the world since '73. Children higher than them will be born in this lineage. Our prayers, resolves, and actions are for that.
