GURUVANIKAL · April 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0247 to GV2:P0263
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

April 1992

In an ashram without brahmacharis, sacrifice will be less. An ashram can reach its true nature only if it achieves at least ninety percent of complete sacrifice. Those living in such an ashram must first be careful about the weight of good and evil in their own minds.

We have love for our father, mother, and others. But if we observe carefully, we will know that this love is not that great. It can be understood only if observed very carefully. That is why at times we feel love to a greatly increased extent. The tendency to seek to increase one's own happiness through love for others is innate in everyone. Any method will be adopted for that.

As man goes along with the flow of emotions, love is lost. Eventually, a state of uncertainty will arise. What should we do about it? We must place our witness in 'one'. That witness must also be appropriately present before us.

Why do we see flaws in a Guru? Because the general concept of a Guru, or the chosen deity, has different states. A perfect Guru is beyond states. If one does not have the qualification to voluntarily obtain such a Guru, or to find one by searching, or to see perfection in the Guru once found, they will leave at a certain stage. Whatever was done until then will also be ruined. That is what was mentioned earlier -- love will decrease and one will end up in a state of uncertainty. Therefore, whatever the life may be, we must have a certainty in that life. Even a butcher needs that certainty. A householder leading the most worthy life can attain more merit than an ascetic. Whether a householder or an ascetic, the Guru's love will be according to each person's capacity for action. One who is firm in the Guru-disciple relationship will not doubt the Guru. He will not hesitate to perform his duties.

The life of a householder has five parts. Five duties - household duty, duty to gods, duty to ancestors, duty of charity, and duty to the self. Of these, the household duty is exhausted in action. None of the others can be done by knowing them oneself. The fulfillment of the past, present, and future life fulfillments is to be accomplished through the performance of these duties. Only a Guru can do this. The gods have an authority in the five duties. Because two periods - Treta and Dvapara - are the periods of the gods. In the four yugas, one yuga belongs to man. Another is completely for man to change his demonic nature. Gurus who teach these five duties with discrimination are rare.

A Guru's love is not human. Therefore, it is not so easy to understand a Guru's love. The disciple must learn how a Guru is. A Guru will also stand with a thief -- he is his Guru too. Even a thief does his work praying, "Oh God, let no one see this."

Everyone's devotion is of a different kind. There are those who pray, "Give me time to pray after all this work is done." There are those who pray, "Let my husband go blind." After the age of twenty, girls develop a kind of devotion. There is no shortage of those who pray crying for the fulfillment of their needs. That is for the realization of their desires. They will do any service to get things done. If the desire is fulfilled, they will kick it away. Some, however, maintain devotion without desiring anything. One can understand all this only if one knows what dharma really is.

A Guru is like a thief. He will stand with every thief. He has to beat and shake his karma like an arecanut flower. How should we respect such a Guru? If we cannot respect him knowingly, we must believe blindly. But that blindness should not be of an ignorant kind. If it is an all-knowing Guru, one must surrender oneself.

A disciple will be one who cannot tolerate hearing anyone say a fault about the Guru. The disciple's feeling will be, "It is my need that my father should be well." This is perfect faith and love. Anyone can accept a Guru. But how many can become like Vivekananda? When the Guru fell ill, all the disciples inquired. However, only Vivekananda drank the pus kept in the vessel. Similarly, Jesus also had twelve disciples. But there was a flaw in love on each side.

Even among children of the same father, love is expressed in different ways. That is the only way it can be in this world.

Some people may not be able to fully trust the ashram. The reason for this is their inherent tendencies. For this to change even slightly, three elements are required. Three times in each stage, thus a total of nine times, the change will come and become firm. This is how the karmas of the three periods—past, present, and future—are combined to bring the disciple to a steadfast faith that makes him worthy of liberation. To attain this state, the guru's radiance must spread across galaxies.

The Guru will stand by everyone, in order to crush their karma. If opposed, it will not be possible. That is what Jesus did. Jesus knew about the karmic path of Mary Magdalene. Buddha knew about the karmic path of the Kapalika. But Shankaracharya made a mistake. The disciple may not have the sincerity of the Guru. The Guru cannot abandon anyone, can he?

The devotee must be vigilant about how to guide the mind. Purity of action is essential. Suppose one could only plant a mango sapling and water it. But even that much must be pure.

The knowledge to accept a Guru must initially be obtained from the mother. No matter what past-life flaws one has, half of them will be resolved by the mother's goodness. The mother must understand all the habits of the child. Such a mother herself should raise the child. A look of love and seriousness must both be full in the mother. Because of love, the child will not be able to abandon the mother. Seeing the mother's seriousness, the child should develop fear and devotion. Raising such a mother is the most important thing. It is also the most difficult karma. If a mother who hides things from the father raises the child, the child will not turn out well.

We will receive all kinds of blows. At that time, we must have the love and courage not to abandon and leave. That is the removal of our sinful power. Humility can also be acquired in this way. These kinds of things cannot be obtained from anywhere else other than from a Guru or a lineage of Gurus.

This is the first step of ashram dharma. The disciple must carry it and impart it to others in an exemplary manner. Then he should travel extensively and acquire knowledge. Finally, he must return to the ashram itself and lead a peaceful life.

Guruvani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · May 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0264 to GV2:P0282
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

May 1992

Since the time people started living in the world, there has been a way of distinguishing between good and bad.

What do we mean by good?

When some people sing, it will be sweet. That is missing in the singing of others. Street children will sing more sweetly than some musicians. If there is sweetness of voice, no one will feel offended even if they get angry. The Tamil language is sweet. Listening to Tamil songs increases devotion.

Since ancient times, attempts have been made to distinguish between good and bad. Compared to the time when this earth and its creatures emerged, human life has only been a very short period. If we examine the course of time, we can see that a much higher culture existed in many periods than what exists now. Culture may change with the change of time. Environment can also be an indicator of culture. A man's qualities change according to the circumstances he finds himself in. But there is a culture that does not change according to time, place, and circumstances. That is the real culture. That culture is capable of transforming the mind in present life. Mental transformation can come with money. It can also come through knowledge and habit.

You should think about why you come and go to the ashram. Sometimes there may be fifty ashrams among fifty lakh people. The people who come and go to these ashrams are only a very small percentage. That too, keeping in mind their needs according to their tendencies. Some come to be leaders, some to steal. Others may come to be fools.

We should think about the reason why each tendency and the matching according to the tendency come together. Only those who are very careful will understand this. Each place has a different type of culture. The whole country desires good. The government also desires good. But good does not arrive sufficiently. However, it can be made good by rubbing and rubbing. Like smoothing a rough stone by rubbing it.

Human life is difficult in every way. Living according to ashram dharma is even more difficult. The five dharmas were mentioned earlier. From where should it become right for ashram life? There are two or four types of ashram life. Those who come to the ashram suffering without health, without even a means for a meal. Those who come to the ashram with food but desiring something else and not being successful. There are those who grow a beard, sit around, and do many times more than what a sanyasi does. But others do not see any of this. They don't see it either.

But we who see and understand all this should have a thought. Due to circumstances, even very great people go wrong. We should be able to overcome the circumstances that lead to such mistakes and accept only what is suitable for ashram life. None of these are blessings that can be achieved so quickly. If there is a perfect sense of purpose in ashram life, if there is a determination to act according to the goal no matter what happens, even if there is nothing to do, one will not act and become empty. It takes very little time to beat something out. But that time is not enough to nurture it.

If hunger is destroyed by detachment, it will become a devil. We must understand where this culture of detachment was passed on from. There is suffering other than all this. The life of lying on the street, suffering, embracing all sorrows, and accepting the earth as one's ancestral home. That is sacrifice. The sacrificer

He considers a life of hardship as comfortable. In ashram life, what one should desire and embrace is sacrifice.

What should be the nature of sacrifice? There are people who endure sacrifice to buy a cow and those who endure sacrifice to buy a bicycle. If so, what a child endures by planting a banana tree and watering it is also a sacrifice. Now another aspect. Haven't you seen slaughtering by saying 'Bismillah'? Haven't you seen the 'Aham Brahmasmi's? Everyone else is a devil! Thus incomplete, arrogant and blind actions. It's all knowledge.

Contrary to all this, a collective effort is required to achieve the perfection of knowledge. A caste, religious or national perspective is not enough for that. A collective effort, life itself, accumulating all ideas, is needed. We can stay in the ashram only if we have the knowledge to think according to our ability. When working sincerely, if four people criticize, there are those who drop it and leave. That is where purity of action should come. Our actions should not cause pain to others. I, who lived for the movement for seventeen years, was thrown out without even being given a meal of rice gruel. But I could not say a word of blame about them. Either my action lacked strength. Or if it has strength, the assurance that it will come.

'Jvaramatri vishrathikondumoo- nnaritam velakal cheythumarthiyo?' as asked in Anuthapadasakam, I had this assurance. I tested whether ash could cure illness. It worked. I realized it at the age of thirty-two. All physical ailments were cured by the ash here.

How to achieve peace with this life? There can be peace for all our emotions. It is then that one becomes ready to serve anyone and has a dedicated attitude. The form of such a person can be seen in everything. The ashram will grow and grow and become only him. This is how it should be targeted. One should come to the ashram with such high thinking. To do what is needed in the ashram, to ensure that no one makes a mistake, many such blessings will come. If one thinks that I am staying in the ashram to practice and make others practice dharma, one will not be able to make a mistake. Even if it is made with difficulty, there should be a vessel to keep it in the heart.

What we have got now is the result of some good deed done over lifetimes. The knowledge to preserve this good deed is what must be acquired. Only by doing something and as a result of some goodness can one reach here. This place can also tear away goodness in a moment. Remembering that the next moment that action is not for us, we must constantly perform actions. No matter how hard it is, if there is an interest in doing good, this place can destroy all people. Loving humans should be with ability.

There should always be a thought about what one can do as far as one understands. If you are attentive, there will always be a dignity in the action you do. There will be a humility in dealing with others. If jealousy cannot be completely abandoned, there will at least be an obstinacy for it. No one can stay here with love for their home and relatives. No matter how many days you stay here, it should be with sincerity and true love. It is wrong to pretend to be sincere and to maintain a cunning love. If there is love for something, it must be innocent. Innocent love is equal to supreme love.

Regarding those with vision, special care is needed in this matter. Even if a slight misfortune affects those with vision, it will affect the entire ashram.

Small children and mothers come here, don't they? It is difficult to reform mothers. One should only get close to mothers as much as necessary. If one gets closer than necessary, bad influences might spread. This is an institution. This is not just a place to accept sannyasa. There is a shortcoming among us that we are unable to sufficiently respect and understand even the spiritual experiences here. What is the tradition of this ignorance?

Shubhavani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · June 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0283 to GV2:P0297
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

June 1992

We have received a new method. Satyayuga, Tretayuga, Dvapara, Kali - there are four eras like this and each era has its own nature. The dharmas in the four eras function in four ways. In Satyayuga, both men and women will act without error. Satyayuga has a duration equal to the other three yugas combined. The change of time and the dharma of time is God's will. It is in the mind. We humans are the children of those who belonged to the lineage of Manu and acted desiring the presence of God.

Hinduism has a very long history and calculation. Compared to Hinduism, Christianity's is a very small calculation. Hinduism is more than just a religion, it is a culture. The invading Brahmins settled on the banks of the five rivers and studied our history. They were people who saw God with their intellect. What they saw here was a group of honest people without deceit. They were people who would sleep anywhere.

They came and settled at a time when the era changed. Because the era changed, it became impossible to see God directly. Therefore, they went to the forests and seashores, looked at the horizon, and did penance in many ways. They saw various kinds of lights and forms. What they saw, they carved on trees and stones. When they thought about it keeping it in their minds, they got words from the light. They got mantras. The great sages discovered each of these things in this way. Many things were carved on stones and given form and color. Westerners called it the mind. The consciousness called mind. The light called consciousness. That is the truth. We must learn to know that it is the truth. We are people who know the truth with our eyes, not with our intellect. The immigrant Brahmins came here and started practicing each thing. They started ruling the country intelligently. They made the bitter rulers kings. They made them gods. They made people believe so. Tretayuga and Dvapara yuga were thus ruled by 'visible gods'. In Dvapara, the real began to be manifested. Finally, Krishna came. With that, it was proved that all this exists and that God can show what exists. This is history.

The Aryans who studied history created caste. Historically speaking, Kali belongs to the Shudra. That is, he is the authority to handle governance. He is entitled to liberation. Then, intelligently, they put a price even on letters. They took the Vedic into their hands. Then they made the Shudra beat the Vaishya. This diverted the course of the dharma of time. Instead, for at least two or three generations, the Vaishya beat the Shudra. Thus, the Shudra did slave labor and became the owner of the synonym 'ignorant'. The Shudra class, which was subjected to untouchability and ostracized for more than ten generations, did slave labor, lacked food, became dark and exhausted. After that, he became arrogant, a great sinner, and a victim of God's wrath.

It is God's decree that there should be a king at the end of Kali. Otherwise, the king should have ruled all this time. Thus, the one who chased and the one who ran all fell into the pit together. Paternity was lost. The one who lost paternity died, decayed, and that 'pitru' wandered, changed signs, and begot fatherless children. In the meantime, many great souls came and said, "You must see this irony of fate." Then these fatherless children called this great man "fatherless" and chased him away beating him. Thus, they made the Muslims rule there.

In Kerala, the Brahmin was accepted as the priest. Because of his tricks, we were unable to know anything. The English who came here were not respectable people. Those who were unfit for any department there became Viceroys here. They came here and created everything - dress, language, education, law, and government.

Today, an era of hybridity has arrived. Hybridity for humans and even for a blade of grass. This is a very serious matter. We must keep the word of God so that this does not spread to our children. God is in your favor. Jesus and the Prophet have made it clear - that messengers will come and mislead. At first, I had doubts about it. Even when an avadhuta repeated this at Varkala Santhigiri, I doubted it. But the scoldings I had to hear to reform the girls were not minor. Finally, when I received the message, I was convinced.

We need an assurance here. This is not for a caste or a community. If Thiruvalluvar and Naranath Bhranthan did this, can't we do it? Think that if my clan (lineage) will improve, I might die for it. If those who come to the institution are worthless, they must be reformed by enduring any sacrifice. We can certainly carry this forward. Because through the Prophet, through Jesus, through Sree Narayana, through Chattampi Swami, God has given us the experience and qualification suited for the times. Even if cut into pieces inch by inch, we can say boldly -- wasn't Jesus crucified...... We will build up the Guru's dignity. Jesus was ready to be crucified. Sankaracharya offered his head. But the disciples opposed.

A sacrificer will instill goodness in another by enduring anything. The disciple must be extremely attentive. Devotion without attention is useless. It is attentive devotion. If there is such a thing, one will know whatever happens anywhere. Anything must be done for those who come to be cured of illness. Even if one has to be cut into pieces inch by inch, it must be done. There are those who speak Vedanta only for the good of their own children. That is not the case for those who come for service. Whatever they need, they will definitely attain from here. A position must be assigned for such people. One must be able to get done what is needed at the right time. Do not wait for a good time to come later. That might not be obtained sometimes. There is a sacrifice of its own for this. If that is understood through action, there is nothing more to be achieved beyond that. If there is sacrifice, one will not have to beg anywhere. I lived in such a way that in humility, all qualifications will be subservient to this, more than you. You do not need a model greater than this. This model can be carried around with any amount of pride. No siddhi or vidya is needed for this. Only the heart's experience you have received is enough. You must pray to get that heart's experience until the end.

Do not oppose those who do good things. Do not disrespect them. It is difficult for the greatness of ashram life to come to you. There are two reasons for that. One, the South Indian kings were Shudras in Dravidian culture. Two, the North Indian culture was not received by Nairs and those below them. Therefore, their character will not come to you. In addition to all this, you saw and learned the rhythmic worship practices of Tamil Nadu. Because importance was given to Shaktheyam, you became ignorant. A section of such ignorant people became Christians. Instead of learning good things, they copied European culture here. Thus, Hindu culture disappeared here.

India is a natural land. Our culture has an unimaginable depth. It is because life was not organized in accordance with it that our knowledge was lost. We know nothing of Upanishads, Vedas, and Puranas. Renewing this situation

Cannot be raised. Understanding this, there should be a change. The little children playing odd and even here are much more knowledgeable than today's parents. They are capable of knowing any events happening in the world sitting here. But the thought in them should be developed. Adults should live understanding this. To love truth, there is no greater achievement than this.

Attention and devotion are the most important. Then, as the author of the Gita said, everything should be done with intellect and the power of yoga. This is a great fortune. This fortune is for those who take care of it. Detachment is essential. The detachment to endure and forgive anything and to dedicate for something with humility. In this hybrid era, that is the only way to rise from our miserable state. We must strive until we ourselves become the reality. Any trivial matter should be viewed seriously. Physical and mental cleanliness is paramount. Our life should not be like that of the capitalists.

We must have a perfect awareness of the example we should set for the world. One should have a clear knowledge about Sandhyavandanam. Three, six, nine, twelve -- of these, six and twelve are important. Pakkanar's life of detachment is an example for that. Everyone should be loved. Behave truthfully. Whoever comes for whatever reason, there should be an eagerness to know it with enthusiasm. That is humility. There should be punctuality. Help each other. Abandoning the detachment caused by mutual enmity, one should maintain the detachment of mutual love, help, and cooperation. Goodness has disappeared from the Shudra culture. One should seek a lifestyle that helps goodness to come fully. Through that, the karma to build Kalidharma should be brought about. That is our way.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · July 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0298 to GV2:P0312
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

July 1992

What is the general understanding of an ashram? In the past and present general understanding, ashrams are of four types -- Yoga, Bhakti, Karma, and Jnana. These four form the basis of the earth's culture itself. There might have been many other related characteristics to this.

The course of action of householders should be viewed with great importance. Because they are the ones who should be helpful in the Karma Kanda. Brahmacharis and devotees are very few. They should only be that much. They are guides. Therefore, it is enough if their number is small. But householders are not like that. Sannyasins and devotees all have to emerge from householders. Therefore, it is the householders who need to become good.

A center is essential to become good. Religious principles are said to be uniform. But customs and rituals are different. Nothing should be denied. Only by respecting everything can the essence of each be understood. The essence of various religious principles is the same. This is what I have learned without a doubt through my experienced life.

Some people deny the temple system. They can deny it. We also do that, don't we? Blind denial will do more harm than good. Denial should be with cause-and-effect discrimination; it should be by compiling and summarizing the scientific awareness and essences in religious principles. Sage Charvaka rejected many things taught by the Vedas with logic. It is not enough to say that observing and experimenting through self-experience are the methods suitable for scientific examination. The essences known through examination must be compiled, summarized, and established through action.

The Hindu religious code or culture is very vast. Christians and Muslims in India, and almost all religious groups, separated from Hinduism. They separated due to the excess of hardship they had to suffer from it. That means Hinduism went wrong somewhere. But the mistake that happened to Hinduism is God's will. Very few people know this. But even those who know do not act with determination. We know about the Varna Ashrama Dharma. Since the beginning of the world, we have been undergoing changes according to the times. The people of the Satya Yuga were like gods who could see and pray to God in person. When the Satya Yuga passed, the times also changed. How many Chatur Yugas and Manvantaras have passed! One Manvantara is seventy-one Chatur Yugas. Looking at this, a human's time is so insignificant. Within this insignificant period, we can acquire only a very small amount of knowledge.

Although a human's time is very short, we should at least be able to know the harm caused by the Varna Ashrama Dharma during that time. A Shudra has no place in the Varna Ashrama Dharma. But there is a Shudra in every home. There is no point in denying it. He worked innocently for everyone. What reward has God kept for his innocence?

The Brahmin knew. His was the right of Kali, the ruling authority. That is why he even gave the position of army commander. Because of the kings' punishment, the Shudra could not escape from the bottom. By beating, the entire power of sin was placed on his head. Even the Shudra becoming a king is according to the Brahmin's rules. Even the king who is passed under the belly of a golden cow, tied with a thread, and raised to the status of a Brahmin is not prescribed the Vedas. Only Murajapam could be heard. Can the subjects of such a king hear the Vedas? In the Varna Ashrama Dharma, the Shudra was caught and made a king. But he has no knowledge. This ignorant king is the visible god! Thus the people became ignorant and bound. Buddha came to change this. Before that, at the beginning of Kali, Krishna came. Opposed everything that existed. Said Sambhavami Yuge Yuge.

Now how difficult is it to make the images of Kali in Kali Yuga into brilliant people who are Yuga Karma Yogis. The situation is that there are only dark-skinned white men molded by the British in India. These poor people completely imitated the British. Not only dress and language, but even governance is their imitation. Ours is a tradition where there were kings who only obeyed what the Viceroys said. We have neither our culture nor our religion. Even for one who has studied philosophy, our culture is just stories. And the Westerner's is history. Thus we are slaves in every way.

In Kali, there is no place for God. In Kali, the place is for the Guru. How many great souls came and told this truth! First Moses came. When he said that he was speaking the word received from God, the world mocked him. Two thousand years later Jesus came. When he was crucified, he showed his form to the one who believed. Even though they were stubborn and had to suffer severe punishments, they worked with determination. Those who worked multiplied doubly. The Jews were exhausted in front of their sacrifice. Christians have that tradition even today. They were able to establish the world's government. Among them, they came to India and did a favor. If Madan becomes Mathai, he can walk on the main road. We swallowed his good and bad alike. We imitate them in every way. When he came here and grew his hair without getting peace, every beedi roller and boatman here started growing their hair.

Our country has come to such a bad state because our mothers became ignorant. Our Pitru has no purity. Children were born in the family according to the zodiac sign in which the Pitru stands. Children with intelligence and logic will be born in a family that has purified the Pitru. In that field, Brahmins are always at the forefront. The entire Shudra lineage became that of drunkards. Even if he is knowledgeable, he will be perverse. But the Brahmin did another extreme thing. Three are Sambandham. One is Veli. The one in the Brahmin's house is Veli. All others are Sambandham. To complete the karma he does, he needs the woman he married through Veli. Or if not, he will show Yonimudra and do Tantra. We are spit in his Mantra and Tantra, right. What knowledge can be imparted in a lineage that has degraded to this level?

No Acharya has come who has done Pitru Shuddhi in its completion of Karma. Shankaracharya did Vedic. Even Buddha said that if you just believe, everything will change. We should know that there are many Pitrus who have reached the position of Buddha. Pitrus are filled in all subtle realms. These inner dwellers are the ones who killed Jesus. Pitru domination has been established in that position. Pitrus rule the position of all the Acharyas who have risen.

The light that Buddha saw, the ray of light mentioned in the Quran, the tongue of fire in the language of Christians - whatever religions have described that consciousness as, all of it is here. The children here see that conscious energy in all these ways. Ancestral purification (Pithrushudhi), which no one has done with complete purity so far, is being done here. The ritual performed here involves catching all the ancestors and bringing them into the Guru's light. If you do ancestral purification here, then you will have no ancestors. There will be no ancestral curse either. Then what you need to look into is how to grow the lineage. The result of what you do today will be useful after twenty-five years.

We do not deny anything. Each is right up to its own extent. But what is desirable for us today? Good children should be born in our families. Our children should have the knowledge to self-fulfill whatever we are running after. There should be good Karma Yogis, Jnanis, and Sanyasis in your home. You, who are the cause of such an excellent creation, should have a clear understanding of the ashram.

Guruvani

Birthday Message

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · September 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0313 to GV2:P0403
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

September 1992

Disciple: Aurobindo Ghosh has said that Lord Sri Krishna is not the supramental light. It is not clearly understood what that is. There should be some light on this from the Guru's mouth.

Guru: When talking about that, we have to go far back and see. Manvantara -- or Chatur Yuga, how many Chatur Yugas make a Manvantara, we have reached a miserable state of not knowing all this clearly, and much time has passed. It is a mistake that happened through another right.

Disciple: Two words, right and wrong, have come from the Guru's mouth. I have a desire to know what that right is.

Guru: When talking about the origin of our India or the world, it takes so much time for the change of Yugas and floods to come. After that, when entering another change of Yuga, the Manvantara comes. It is said that the world changes and floods come with fourteen Manvantaras. But many events happen in between.

Disciple: Although the events that happen in between are recorded in history, it was not understood that they are related to this.

Guru: It was not understood that it was related to this because there was a flaw on the part of a Jnani's knowledge.

That is, in the third Chatur Yuga of Vaivasvata Manu, when the sage Satyavrata was ruling as the lord of the world, he looked back. He should be seen as a person who was born and brought up in the intervals of Yugas and floods. Only then will we understand.

But no matter in which intervals one is born, if any word or deed happens forgetting the original source, it is difficult to correct it later. Especially when looking at the causes and effects of the origin of the Sanatana religion. Causes -- it is talking about the causes of the seven Saptarshis. What the concept of those Saptarshis is, should be revealed by a Jnani. It was when it became an opportunity where it was covered with stories, sung, told, and reached a karma made into a Kathakali, that Krishna's birth became necessary.

Disciple: Is the Guru suggesting that trouble arose in the Sanatana religion due to Satyavrata's flaw and Krishna had to come to resolve it? If so

It has not been revealed whether Sree Krishna, who came according to the divine will, was able to complete his mission perfectly.

Guru:

Even before Krishna was born, the Yadava clan had a situation where there were kings for each clan at different times - for each Varna. So the Shudra varna had started before the beginning of this Kali. Kamsa belonged to this Shudra varna. Since Kamsa was a wise man, a devotee, and a scholar, he had the fortune to become a king. But the birth of Kamsa and Krishna happened before the end of Dwapara. Since he was knowledgeable after giving birth to Kamsa, it was thought that it could be brought through him step by step.

Disciple:

Should it be considered that Kamsa came as a condition of the divine will?

Guru:

That divine will brought that royal power to the Shudra class in many times in a way that gave them royal power. In the part of Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, Shudra, we are all Brahmins alike in Satya Yuga. But when it changes from there, it changes according to Yuga Dharma.

Yuga Dharma means Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, Dwapara, Kali. In Satya Yuga, all people are Brahmins. Everything happens truthfully. They only do truth. Beyond that, they do not think or make trouble by thinking about things that do not matter. Human nature was like that in Satya Yuga.

Disciple:

Human nature was like that. Humans have changed from that now. The reason for such a change should be revealed.

Guru:

That was said earlier. None of us were forgetful in Satya Yuga. After that, the Brahmins - those who are meant by Brahmins are wise men. The karma revealed by Satya Yuga is that the time of such wise men is over. There is another reason for that: Treta Yuga is the time when Kali and Dwapara join together. Satya Yuga is the time when Kali Yuga, Dwapara and Treta Yuga join together. In such an era, man - the early man of Treta Yuga - lived up to one hundred and fifty years. A man less than forty feet or thirty feet is said to be rare. But as Treta Yuga as a whole comes across as Yuga Dharma, an idea does not stand beyond two thousand, three thousand, four thousand, five thousand, ten thousand years at that time. Due to antiquity, it will have many falls. Great men will be born here and there. It will go on like that under those great men. Thus, there was a cover from the beginning of Treta Yuga. It happened as if human intelligence was completely covered. For example, after this truth disappeared, some intelligent, knowledgeable and wise people meditated and found some lights through that meditation and found out what exists in that light and acted as a witness, and that is why India's philosophy lies as an atomic science. Today is the time to talk about the atomic science of the atoms in a fly's leg. The administration was carried out according to the guidance of spiritual men who found out how many crores of precision more than that.

Disciple:

When the truth was covered and people were groping in the dark in Treta Yuga, although some lights were available here and there, the people were generally in darkness. Although there were ways according to the divine will to escape from it, the people generally could not take it. Was it because of their ignorance? What was the reason for it?

Guru:

Even in that era, the working capacity of life is great. So we can see many intelligences rising from a man. Similarly, if we examine each person in this world, the working capacity created by the combination of their intelligence and life has been seen here in Karma. To carefully understand what that karma is and to take it together

Knowledge does not happen to all people. It happens to a few people and is imparted to them through the rulers according to the Yuga Dharma. This is taught by the wise.

Disciple: We can understand that the knowledge of the wise was not widely available to the people because of a misfortune that it was more on one side and could not be spread.

Guru: The words that came from the mouths of the women who are called Veda Vahinis were kept as Smritis and Shrutis. That Karma is what is meant when it is said that Vyasa came and divided it into four. Dividing into four means the Dharma of Satya Yuga, the Dharma of Treta Yuga, the Dharma of Dwapara Yuga, and the Dharma of Kali Yuga. Many wise men could not understand what the Dharma of this Kali Yuga is.

Disciple: Why is it that even the wise cannot understand it?

Guru: As mentioned earlier, wise men will come as authorities for two thousand, three thousand, five thousand, or ten thousand years. If we look at our great country of India itself, it is known that more than six hundred princely states were ruling. Then this rule was controlled by a sage or royal envoys. The one who comes for two thousand will preach for two thousand and go. Or if he is the one who comes for ten thousand, he will make a vision for ten thousand and go. They are called visionaries. The ordinary man of that time did not have the knowledge to think about the karma in their sub-divisions. Why?

Disciple: Is it because ordinary people cannot think about all this?

Guru: One who lives truthfully has no karma to think about anything. He is living truthfully. But that does not mean he will leave love, respect, and humility. But he does not have a high thinking habit. He does not look at its theory of evolution. He has no thought about the past and the future. He evaluates the contemporary action, makes it work, and gives it energy, words, and amount. A mistake happened from this.

Disciple: I want to know why such a mistake happened.

Guru: There have been times when South Indian culture and North Indian culture were going on very differently. In fact, Satya Yuga is a period when North Indian culture and South Indian culture went together. Many events happened due to the change of that Yuga.

This Yuga has the duration of the other three Yugas. In this Satya Yuga, an Acharya or what is said first about it is not what is said later and comes here. Because the end of truth must come. The people who come at the end will say something and go. It will go into darkness as people cannot take it. The increase of people will also be like that. Ordinary people will be plenty. Then it will go into that darkness and be like that for a long time.

Again ascetics or visionaries will come. An example of that is the penance of the Saptarshis. Their concept is what is called the seven lands. That karma is there. Then the special Tantra that formulates the nine and the special Navagraha, along with that, the calculations starting with Rasi, Porutham, Nakshatra, Nazhika, Vinazhika, etc., were linked together. Then a guru left these calculations divided like this. The verse in Guru Gita is proof that those who imparted those calculations had come at different times.

'Anekajanmasamprapta karmabandha vidahine'

That is, the Gita says that those who came at different times starting with Krishna Dwaipayana divided the four Vedas from the words of this Vyasa. These Vedas have been divided over many periods. It is not possible to divide it at one time. It is said how many Vyasas there are. So they came through the history of many Vyasas. Thus in all those times

came as a guru, a king, and an emperor. Thus, there were many minor gurus of different eras and many major gurus of different eras.

Disciple: Gurus who guide for a specific period and gurus for an even longer period appear. The reason for this should be clarified.

Guru: The reason is that every soul has accumulated karma. The Puranas say that someone did penance for a thousand years. Another person did penance for twenty-five thousand years. Yet another did penance for a Manvantara. This can go into the stories of the seers who say this. There are people among them ranging from a thousand to five thousand. There are also those who speak for five hundred years and leave. Moses, Jesus, and the Prophet are examples of those who came for two thousand years. They came as prophets. They did not come to the path mentioned above. They came as self-prophesying ones. They created something else through their own creation. Because, over time, due to the widespread layout of the country, making it impossible to bring any of this straight, they performed their duties in each country according to the language and dress they got at that time, changing and quoting the language, and it became diverse. Thus, in the early days of the Treta Yuga, many gurus had come. By the end of the period, many gurus had come. They spoke in one place for a thousand years, in another place for two thousand years, and in another place for ten thousand years. People lined up under the gurus who went like this, and kings created royal assemblies to show that my guru is greater, my guru is greater. There was a time when decisions were made in it. If we look from one end of a ten thousand acre land, the other end will seem to touch the sky. The one who ruled the land within this limit became the king called Vanavaramban. Inside such kings, there was a royal assembly that praised and glorified their king and their guru. When it came to be so, because these scholars spoke in many ways, it became impossible for anyone to guess what the position of this scholarship was. There was a time when, in the same royal assembly in some periods, due to the honesty of some women, they could sit with their eyes closed and tell the truth. It was also there in men. Such times kept coming as truth itself. But the story told for ten thousand years is not the one told for two thousand years. The story of the one told for ten thousand years is not the story of the one told for twenty-five thousand years. Then they - not only scholars but also kings - became obstinate among themselves. Some of those kings ruled by considering what was said with a gap of two thousand years, some ruled by considering what was said for five thousand years. Some others ruled by considering ten thousand years. Then the defect of not being able to think and know what is right and what is wrong happened here by itself.

Disciple: What would be the path given by God to overcome these limitations and go to the ultimate truth?

Guru: I told you, it is Manvantara. All six Manvantaras went well. It went exactly the same way, there was no difference. But the sage named Satyavratan who came in the third Chaturyuga of the seventh Manvantara tried this, backwards. What have they done in each period? Which lineage is that?

When looking at the matter of that lineage, everything is contained in the Manvantara. From the Saptarshis onwards to the time of Manu, they saw what a Manvantara is. Each one merged into that Manvantara and due to the inevitability of that self-illumination, began to think towards that vital point.

Disciple: I pray to clarify what that vital point intended by the Guru is.

Guru: The sage named Satyavratan came and looked back. What is human effort, how is it. This is what is called the vital point. No matter which way you look, everything has operated under this Manu. Everything goes there and joins Manu. The Guru was conceived as Brahman.

How does the Guru become Brahma? That has not been looked into. The Guru is Brahma, that is the truth, it is the action of the ages - when it came to this, the authors of the Puranas who came in each era wrote each of the Puranas by linking them together. It became unknown to anyone which Purana to hold on to to get its truth. It went into the action of this Aham Brahmasmi. Each of us is Brahma - that is also good in the path of philosophy.

But the rulers and royal preceptors of that time did not act with the knowledge to think about what would not happen here if each of us ignorant people became Brahma. That's where the mistake started.

Disciple: Can you advise a way to overcome this mistake and escape?

Guru: No matter what era the mistake occurs, it is corrected in the Kali Yuga. The Brahma resolution waited for ages for such a Kali Yuga. So, when the end of each era comes, the dharma of the next era must be implemented by taking the last knot of the era. For that, a preceptor who blends the dharma of the past era and the coming era must come. Only five such preceptors will be born in an era. Others will say ten thousand, five thousand, five hundred, and so on. But here and there, a little bit of truth will be seen. Everyone will think that this is a matter for the whole world. Then the rulers and statesmen of that time together wrote and filled the Puranas, epics, Aranyakas, and Upanishads, saying this, and left in their respective times.

Disciple: The Guru said that there are preceptors who come with ideas for short periods of time. But the people who cannot distinguish between the preceptor who comes to correct what has come so far and implement the dharma for the future and those who come for this short period of time fall into error. How can we escape from this and understand the preceptor who truly predicts the dharma of the time?

Guru: For this, one has had to be troubled for two or three reasons. The periods called the scientific age seen today existed even then. For example, who is said to have invented aeronautics - Bharadwaja. When Bharadwaja invented it, the Guru said - 'This is not to be implemented now, it is to be implemented in the coming time. If implemented now, it will do harm.' Because the disciple did not understand that - because he could not see as far as the size of what he had invented - he went straight to the kingdom of Kubera.

He went to the kingdom of Kubera, finished building it there, and returned during the time of Rama. He returned and took Sita away. It was Mandodari herself who kept it very beautifully, without losing honesty and without losing the merit of women's dharma. The Puranas say that she acquired the merit of a mature woman, nursed this Sita or did what she needed, and kept her without any difficulty. At the same time, it also became the cause of the destruction of India and the loss of prosperity of the kingdom of Kubera. Later, visionaries and prophets have come at various times.

Disciple: Although we have heard about one Prophet and one Christ, the knowledge is that many preceptors have come at different times.

Guru: Yes. Many preceptors have come. But the names are many. How many Yogeshwara Krishnas, how many Buddhas, is proof of that. Coming like this, people called Gurus of the ages or visionaries come to do the things needed for the end of one era and the beginning of another era.

But there are two or three things to say about this. The words of the visionaries are to stop in a few words without losing the scope of meaning in philosophy. But if contemporaries are not capable of understanding it, the intelligence and ability to completely see the Gurus or prophets coming for a thousand years, two thousand years, ten thousand years, and a Manvantara in many countries at the same time, and the action part of the knowledge of the vision, become unrecognized.

At the same time, every scholar and ruler of every country would call upon the existing elite scholars and, as a preamble to poetic imagination, impart a kind of foresight to this king if needed. We often forget the many instances that prove the existence of sage traditions or prophetic traditions that had acquired the title of Rajarshi, sleight of hand, and supernatural powers. Not only us, but the rulers of those times and the gurus who advised them would look back at the past, impart foresight in that manner, adorn the royal administration, and the most cunning and intelligent rulers would try to handle it in such a way that their greatness remained intact. If we examine history, we can see that an arrangement of preserving and causing to preserve an administrative machinery molded in the crucible of intellect, hiding the truth over time, is still going on among us today. It is still referred to by both scholars and commoners. Its meaning is Chanakya's strategy. It means hiding the truth with intellect.

In this situation, many instances have passed where statecraft, sleight of hand, supernatural powers, and the tradition of servitude have been misunderstood as wisdom. We can see this even today if we look. Not only in this country, but in many countries, we can see some actions where a certain amount of intellectual prowess, sleight of hand, and the possibilities of using medicinal substances have created wonders, and as a result, the aforementioned intellect, increased by scholars and commoners, has been used to belittle the will of God, making people forget the firmness of God's will. For example, we can see the intellectual strategy of avatars operating by adding the title of Hamsa, calling themselves the final prophet, the final Christ, and recently among Hindus, sage and wise man.

We can see everyone who believes in the aspects of the countless gurus who lived and worked from Sri Shankara to Sri Narayana in the past, praising and glorifying them. That includes Moses, Jesus, Buddha, the Parayi Petta Panthirukulam, and the eighteen Siddhars, from the time of Sri Krishna onwards. However, without respecting and understanding the sorrows they experienced in their times and the life duties they practiced, some tricks have been made to establish that my guru is great, my path is right, my caste is great, and my religion is great. It must have been seeing this that Kumaran Asan was led to say, 'First building a fort with praises'. It might be to change the current administration, or the administration that existed then, that Asan was led to say in an authoritative tone, 'Change the rules yourselves, or else they will change you.'

All the wise men who have come so far have struggled, embracing their lives, because they had no way to remove the obstacle that all their contemporaries in life were leading astray into their hearts. The truth that neither we nor our ancestors could see the truth revealed by the great souls by giving it its due greatness is evident from the life of every great soul.

The Prophet's flight is one of these. Thus climbing hills and mountains, and when that was decided, hiding in a cave, doing many things not just for food or clothing but for survival, and then coming forward and trying to bring them into a monotheistic belief, his pain in that action is proclaimed, 'Alas, the Jinn rule the world.' Another example of this is Jesus' question, 'Father, have you forsaken me?' Similarly, the lives of Shankaracharya, Sri Rama, Ramatirtha, Vivekananda, and three great souls born recently in Kerala itself. If you look closely, if you tell the truth, there is no one to take it. The proof of this is what caused Jesus to lament, 'I have much to say to you, but you will not understand.' Through sacrifice and suffering, their power of penance - in Asan's words, 'Attained blessedness and performed self-penance'.

If we look at it without exception, today's science proves that the time has passed to think and know where the blunder that has befallen us today started and where it should be stopped. In this transition of science, if one struggles and wanders, a book

Just like getting ten new words when reading, we might get one word from it. But there are no children to use it.

The experiences we see here might be the thirst for God that saw this. It has been said and received in that very way. Explaining this, each of us are the children who should acquire it through karma and reside in the merit. Instead of this, each of us today is finding and following the scientific study of foreign and worldly karma. There seems to be no mistake in seeing Asan's words 'Change the rules' as a guidance. Let's look a little higher and see what Sree Narayana Guru said at the end of Atmopadesa Satakam.

Alumithumalla sadarthamallaham sa- chidamruthamennu thelinju dheeranayi sadasadithi prathipathiyattu satho- mithi mruduvay mruduvayamarnnidenam

A special time has come for us to achieve that realization.

Disciple: Haven't we only been able to know that Lord Sree Krishna, Jesus, and Prophet Nabi came?

Guru: No. In between, the Parayi Petta Panthirukulam, the eighteen Siddhars, and from Sree Rama to Rama Thirtha, the depth and weight of karma given by the poet's imagination as Jala Samadhi can be understood if we look at it. The tradition of the ascetics called (Lingayats) saying Sree Sankara's path, going to a place called Kailasam in North India, and jumping into a deep gorge still exists today.

Disciple: Could you please tell me why this mission could not be implemented even after so many great souls came?

Guru: As mentioned earlier, we have pointed out the mistakes we made. It is known that the sage Sathyathranan took up to ten Chaturyugas of the four yugas of this Vaivasvata Manu to know what the causal karma is. Then he looked at all the seven Chaturyugas of the last phase of the previous Manu, that is, the sixth Manu, and the three Chaturyugas of the Vaivasvata Manu. Then it was the karma path of Manu itself that was gross, subtle, cause, and effect. Having found this much, he found that the 'A', 'U', and 'M' of the primordial sound came through this Manu. One reason for this is that he could not find out from what and how the creative willpower, which is the dharma and karma to ask and know the honesty to distinguish the time from time to time. Secondly, for some reason, the honesty acquired by the great men of those times through the power of penance was lost. For that, the universal 'Aham Brahmasmi' and the four sentences of the Hindus came out, formulated the Vedas, Puranas, and epics, attained Advaita in a single soul, and it became practical. But what all trades have come and gone through these three letters. Without looking at what that trade karma stands for, after going like this for some time, hanging on the sweetness of the sound of that letter Om helped the scholars to go deep into the depth of philosophical initiation. The great souls who read, knew, and taught it established each karma dharma and ritual order under each guru. Through it came the Vedic tantra. It strengthened and fell into a deep karma lapse where it was impossible to know where and how the aforementioned philosophical initiation came from. We forgot the small discriminative wisdom needed to remove this cause and take the effect. Because there is only one truth. To tell the truth witnessing anything, we today always have standing as evidence in the path of action in front of us

'Karmam kayamirukkeyavarjjyam cheyvathu guruvenorthu cheythu karmafalodaya vasana pinne

'Victory is delayed and desire will fade away'

People have fallen into the unitary prayer that says this,

Like the rhythm of a chenda player's beat, when Hum.. Hum.. Hum.. Hum.. Hum Hum Hum Thatharikida came, the aforementioned Hum disappeared. Without distinguishing between this Tharikida and Thatharikida, the beat cannot be recognized. Thus, it reached the point where all these eighteen instruments are considered inferior to the chenda.

Since this emerged from the succession of the mind, it comes out as an undeniable truth. By giving this very alphabet a sound structure that transforms it into Shaktheya, Shaiva, and Vaishnava forms, art was created. Following that, the Tantra called Vedic was introduced, and getting entangled at the base of Tantra, they gave a unitary sound to everything like Tantra, Mantra, and Yantra, gave it an Advaita nature, and forgot God.

Then, by using each knowledge system and linking everything from medical science to Tantric science, the value of Dharma was forgotten. Not only that, it brought in the Mayavada (doctrine of illusion) of nothingness within nothingness. Everything was confined to the 'Ya' of Maya. This Mayavada succeeded in bringing us to a point where we cannot recognize how, where, and what the crucial origin is like. Following that, by mixing and using the intertwined rationalism and liberationism, the truth was forgotten.

Along with that, Yoga and Marma sciences spread and flourished. Leaving aside the Dharmic part of gross, subtle, effect, and cause, which is the core of Vedanta that stands miniaturized like a banyan tree turned upside down, it brought the Mayavada, which is the Karma part, to the forefront. The degraded Dharmic part of 'Aham Brahmasmi' was left unpreserved. It was consolidated, brought back again, included in Mayavada, and destroyed.

For all of us, this became like a piece of iron that fell into the gymnasium or framework of a clever person who schemed without the special Dharmic consciousness of the changing times. In that way, to put it in the language of today's scholars, a decline of Dharma (Dharmachyuthi) happened to the entire human world. But it is unknown where it is. Believe that this is a truth that has happened without any distinction of religion or caste. Finally, the intellect, which was amazed by the spread of the rootless creeper, is spinning from there.

Disciple: Common people are those who spin in the intellect of these scholars. So, in a situation where even scholars are spinning like this, the whole country will be caught in this spin!

Guru: To escape from that, the foolishness of not being able to take what has been given to us in various ways and at various times from the Brahma Shakti over the ages has made us as if we have been carried away.

Disciple: What is the way forward now?

Guru: Now we have only one condition. What that means is, what is said day by day applies to yesterday, today, and tomorrow. 'Great sages praise what they see day by day.' So who is it that says what is seen day by day? It is the great sage, the visionary. That visionary is not someone who comes for the aforementioned five thousand or ten thousand years. A Guru is a great spiritual power that stands within the discipline of a Guru of changing times. That means, just like for one solar system, the Guru is the authority of seven solar systems. When we say one solar system, it is an interstellar karma involving nine planets, twelve zodiac signs, and twenty-seven stars. Thus, seeing the vision of seven solar systems, it is filled with gross, subtle, cause, and effect of the five elements, five senses, five sheaths, and then the Jivatma and Paramatma. The one who sees where all the activities of life take place in these five elements is the one who sees minutely. Then what is the karma in every tree, stone, living, and non-living thing? That is what is seen minutely. Seeing what the unity of Jivatma and Paramatma is by miniaturizing it like this is the discrimination between Atma and Anatma mentioned in the Gita. As long as one does not understand what the karma is of making self-offering with the soul and offering what is discriminated, there is no point in Mayavada or rationalism prevailing.

Disciple: You used a phrase earlier saying that we are fortunate to be able to see, understand, and resolve all of this. I did not understand what that means.

Guru: I mentioned earlier that Kali is the era that corrects any mistake. The minimum duration of Kali, which is characterized by the dharma of Kali giving prominence to it by discerning and acting upon the past three eras, is 4,32,000 years. Within this period, to correct mistakes, accept the good, and bring about the authoritative nature of Satya Yuga, the human beings born and raised in Kali must realize truth, dharma, kindness, and compassion without letting go of them. By foreseeing what, when, and where everything will happen, a visionary must explain and convince others about the necessity of Kali. Standing on the essence of the philosophical initiation that condenses the meaning, which is the helpful nature of all times, into syllables and preserves it as the expanded Brahman confined to Brahma, Vishnu, and Shiva, if an eagle's far-sightedness falls upon the earth, it will only see what it needs. But if it is considered as an elevation reflecting charity by seeing all the qualities of the earth, both the one who ran and the one who made him run will end up falling on their knees.

Disciple: So, is everything we completely understood as religion and spirituality incomplete?

Guru: Not exactly. It has become clear to us that the suitable path to set out towards the door of Satya Yuga is to see how the five elements are contained by controlling the organs of knowledge using the organs of action, and then to figure out how to achieve the unity consciousness of the individual soul (Jivatma) and the supreme soul (Paramatma) - and how to destroy the karmas that have come down through Manvantaras.

Disciple: Even though the path has become clear, our dharma or Indian curiosity has gone astray, bringing many great souls to various places, and through their calculations, bringing our external and internal selves to our knees, putting us in a dangerous journey of having to follow them. Given this evident situation in the contemporary world, even though Indian curiosity has suggested a way to return to that path of truth, people are unable to adopt it on a practical level.

Guru: I already said that we have found such a path. We have been discussing a few brief parts of what was found. As a small part of this, a beam of light is striking the earth in one or two places in Kerala. However, similar light is striking in many places. Because there is no one to receive it there, natural disasters like those in Russia have happened in China and other major countries, and the beam of light striking Kerala is illuminating here in a pattern of four, forty, and four hundred, guiding the thinkers and religious scholars here on the right path.

Disciple: What do you mean by four, forty, and four hundred?

Guru: In four years, scientists will confirm that this light exists. In forty years, they will confirm that this is true and decide how to utilize it. These growing children will be reborn every twelve years. In four hundred years, the value of its theory will spread into this society. Just as the Portuguese and Mughals came here and made everything ours, from universities onwards, their own and destroyed everything else, and just as we rule today using their language and their universities, the whole world will have to practice the language of Kerala and the dharma of India.

Disciple: What does this beam of light do?

Guru: To carefully grasp things like this, this place is light, it is truth. If they are struck by this beam of light and found to be unworthy, they will be cursed. When this light strikes, living beings will be lost. That will happen on the side of good and on the side of evil. If they choose good, they will receive good. Otherwise, they will be destroyed. This can happen in any country like this. It was given in Russia but they didn't take it, did they... Didn't you see it in Armenia itself? Forty thousand people went down. Today, there is a rush saying that what currently exists is not enough. The people who discover this light will announce what this insufficient event is. Along with that

From here, a capacity for action will be reflected in a way that the world can understand what our mission is. This courage of action—to humbly take with devotion, love, and respect, and to give with sacrifice—is a special intelligence that not only our movement, not only those who love our movement, but all those who love this world must possess. Understanding this and acting upon it, people described as writers or scholars, literary figures, and monks and celibates who are meant to make people understand the glories of the visionaries, must come forward. All of them have a right to complete fulfillment. Because the third generation of householders—men and women, wherever they are in the world—who value this completeness of knowledge by paying the full price, making it their way of life, goal, and mission, will be those who handle true skill in action. They will be the perfect prophets of this mission, which is the will of God. I truly say that every child who acts bravely for this will be praised as holy figures who provide the purity of action essential for the eternal salvation of the world.

(Distribute copies of this for every Chothi star and do it in whatever way it needs to be done.)

GURUVANIKAL · October 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0404 to GV2:P0422
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani October 1992

I am doing and making others do this work here not from my own ability. I have no education, no wealth. But the knowledge here is one step ahead of all the knowledge in the world. Let any rich or worthy person come. They have much to learn from here. No one in the world can ever say this is bad, but it will only grow through you. I live here humbler than you. I don't have to struggle this much just to survive. It is enough to wear a loincloth and sit quietly as a Swami. Everyone together will take care of all my needs.

In the morning from Sivagiri, lying that I was going to Muttakkadu, I used to go to the beach. I spent ten years lying on the beach. Then the Guru said, 'You go to the sanctum, you have some things to get.' I went to Kalladimastan, bought him tea. Ate the leftovers, and came back. Then the Guru said, 'He gave you everything he had to give you. And a little more. He won't let you near him anymore.' How great the Guru is! And what the Guru gave. After that, I went to see Puthuraswami. What I saw was him making a fire in the sun and performing a homam. Everyone who came brought firewood. I told them not to trouble this poor man like this. Then one of them came to hit me. I said Puthuraswami would leave here within four months. It happened exactly like that.

What will you get from such actions of monks? What can you learn? People follow each one without thinking about any of this. You are the ones who should seek a permanent culture. You should follow everything keeping that in mind.

He gave us an idea that no one has ever received until today. Your own children are doing this looking at the light. They are telling it in front of you and making you understand. Therefore, you must be vigilant to grow this lineage. This is a way for us to know this traditionally and to be saved. For that, utmost honesty, humility, and mutual love must be maintained.

Whatever you do, you should do it praying for this ego to be destroyed. If you give food with such a thought, the blood, marrow, and flesh will change. This way, you can change all qualities. The sister, daughter, or wife is living honestly. The thought will come that I cannot live without her.

Wear whatever clothes you want on the body. Do not clothe the mind. You must learn to live with the thought that my mind and body should be useful to the home and the country. We have to struggle harder for our goal than the fishermen did for Jesus.

How and why is the Pournami fast observed? The Pournami fast is a single meal. On the day before Pournami, you should start the fast without using meat and fish. The next day, eat one meal after five o'clock in the evening. Those who take a single meal the day before can eat lunch the next day.

Pournami is a glorious day in every way. Between the dark fortnight and the bright fortnight, Pournami is more important. The dark moon (Karuthavavu) gained importance because of the ancestral offerings (Pithrubali). Brahmins celebrate the full moon (Veluthavavu). They made us do the opposite. Even that was made to be done in reverse.

Observing the fast on Pournami is mainly for the purification of ancestors. The offering on the dark moon day will not be effective for an ancestor who passed away as a devotee from among us. If there is a Guru, there is no need for any of these offerings.

In our lineage, more than two thousand families have performed ancestral purification. All of them should be considered as ashram families. All their other duties have been exempted with this - duties to ancestors, duties to gods, and the duty of charity. Now, what is meant by the duty of charity? No matter how good things you give to children sleeping on the porch by keeping them in the house, it is of no use. Just by giving charity, his karma will not be corrected. That will not be the duty of charity.

'Ottar' only likes to walk along the way. If we help such people, the result will be taking on their sinful power. Then, giving a share of what we cook is not charity. Some old families call it doing charity. It is not right to say that there is poverty today. But the fact is that there is no poverty today. It is hard to think that there is even a single house today where gruel is not made.

As mentioned earlier, more than two thousand families have performed ancestral purification. Out of that, at least five hundred families were unable to make gruel. But that is not the situation today. A lot of changes have taken place. Despite all this, our previous experience - worship in the Trimurti system - has not changed. The drumbeating of the Varnasrama Dharma has not changed. The caste thought that followed it has not changed. Now caste thought is retreating inward. Caste has now become political. What is the reason for this caste to come?

I was born in the fortress of caste. I have experienced that difficulty to some extent since my young age. Why did the Vaikom Satyagraha take place? It was for the freedom to walk on the road outside the temple. This caste is that harsh. Even today in North India, Harijans are burned to death. Fearing this, people became Christians and Muslims. Someone who could not bear to see this came to power. C.P. Ramaswami Iyer.

The Congress was formed around that time. Because of that, there was a slight benefit. The Vaikom Satyagraha gained strength. That is the achievement of Gandhiji's Congress. C.P. did a trick to create communal solidarity. Not out of love for the poor, but out of fear that the Hindu community would perish. Here, the NSS and SNDP were strengthened. Shankar and Mannam were called and advised. Some Ezhavas were told to make Shankar their leader. All this was done to break the Congress. C.P. was certain that when the community became strong, the Congress would collapse. By making speeches at a place near the church in Thalayolaparambu (through K.R. Narayanan), Nairs and Ezhavas were turned back. Although I was very young, it was not difficult to understand all this. None of this was done to improve the community.

How much is the Congress today? Everything is in the name of caste. If you look closely at all this, it is not difficult to see that caste has not changed, but has turned in a different way. Every party is a fortress of caste. As a result, the culture has degraded. This is why politicians oppose spirituality. Mahatmaji had both. But his successors lacked that culture. Independence was achieved through Gandhiji's sacrifice. What did these successors achieve after independence? Leaders without self-culture brought anarchy to the culture. Politicians will not allow self-culture to develop. Because once self-culture comes, politics cannot survive. The revolutionary faction in politics are those who foster anarchy.

What is our path? There are good people in every section. In Hindus, Christians, Muslims, in politics, everywhere. Bring all of them together and lead them to this culture. That is the wealth of the country and the world. We can understand where we have failed historically and traditionally only when we reach this richness. Everyone who comes here should strive for that. When you give food to your children, when you send them to school, they should be convinced of this. The shortcomings that have come to us traditionally can only be resolved through generations. Cultural knowledge should be ready to be received in whatever way it is imparted. That knowledge should not be used for any personal gain. There are great intentions behind this. What is happening here is something none of you have seen or heard. It can be fully brought about through your children. This is something no acharya has ever said or done before. The foundation of that is the Guru Pooja conducted here. Do you know what is actually being done here in the name of Guru Pooja?

November 1992

What happened yesterday? Along with that, instead of copying wealth, ability, education, and moral state, what is each of us doing today as a duty to increase tomorrow's repetitive capacity, and what should we do - I remind you of the path I have found to be useful to you for such thinking habits and the actions we repeatedly continue today and tomorrow for our life's difficulties.

Have you ever thought about yourself? Running in the journey of life, how many years will you be in this world? Sixty or seventy, at most a hundred years. Just like a government employee retiring after their term ends. Then surely, doesn't this human birth you have received have a meaning?

If so, from whom will you get the self-awareness to know that meaning? Will you get it from a book-learned person? From a scholar? From a politician? Or is there nothing beyond this world? If so, any absolute villain could just do whatever they want and leave this world.

When you simply say that there is a power ruling this world, are you saying it after knowing anything about that power? Aren't your darling children playing and growing in front of you destined to become wives, mothers, husbands, fathers, grandfathers, and grandmothers tomorrow? Have you done anything to impart the necessary knowledge to them, who are to turn the wheel of governance and the wheel of dharma in tomorrow's world? Is it enough to just give them some material education? As parents who took up the karma of creating children who sow destruction to the world

Have you ever thought about your pitiful condition of having to pass away from this world?

Aren't we humans one? Aren't Hinduism, Christianity, and Islam created by us? Did the Supreme Lord Sree Krishna, Lord Buddha, Prophet Muhammad, Jesus Christ, Sree Narayana Guru, or Chattampi Swami ever speak of caste or religion to anyone? Aren't people with vested interests the ones who created caste and religion in the name of these masters and fenced them inside?

Aren't all these masters the common wealth of the world? Aren't Hindus, Muslims, and Christians the children of India? Why do the children who should work together for their home, country, and the world in the unity of love, embracing the religion of sacrifice, divide themselves in the name of caste and religion? Is it right to make people fight each other in the name of caste and religion using the names of these masters? Even if we do not accept them, can we trample their ideas down? Let the children who are capable of accepting their ideas accept them when they come.

Don't you need a thought that is beyond caste, religion, class, and color differences? Do you have a place to impart a sense of righteousness for your children who should give light to tomorrow's world? Seek it. Surely it will reach you without much delay. It is God's will. Only a visionary who knows the past, present, and future can save you. How will you recognize that visionary? He will be beyond the differences of caste, religion, color, and class. Jesus Christ, Aurobindo Ghosh, and Nostradamus give the world the proclamation of the arrival of such a superhuman radiance. If you are human, let this thought follow you. Santhigiri continues its message.
