GURUVANIKAL · December 1992 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0432 to GV2:P0447
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

December 1992

What is done in Santhigiri under the name of Gurupooja is a karma that removes all ancestral doshas and merges them with God. Until today, no one has performed such a Gurupooja. Muslims perform this karma under the name of Ratheeb and Christians under the name of Anatha Chollikkuka. What Hindus do in this regard is Pithrubali. What Buddha did for this is not clearly recorded. What Sankaracharya and Narayana Guru did is a karma in the Hindu model. But no one has been able to perform ancestral purification in a way that catches the devil beyond heaven.

Will you believe if I say there is a devil beyond heaven? This is a very big subject. There is a section known as Yogabhrashtanmaar. Those who have attained power through penance and stand apart. These are the ones who harm the world. No one has been able to see them through any medium until today. Their position is between the devil and God. They have practiced Hamsayoga, strengthened the air in the body, and mastered the nature of flying. Therefore, it is impossible for anyone to cross them and reach Brahmaloka.

These Yogabhrashtanmaar are those who have conquered even Brahma. They will harm anyone who tries to enter Brahmaloka.

Gandharvan is such a Yogabhrashtan. Sree Rama could not conquer him. It is a historical truth that Sree Rama had to retreat saying that a Guru coming in Kaliyuga will destroy you. It was he who gave Jesus to death. The Prophet, looking at him, cried out and said, Alas! Jinn rule the world. Even if he comes in a body, no one has been able to recognize him so far. But we know the history of this Gandharvan clearly here.

All the karmas happening here are beyond intellect and thought. That is, whatever the divine will is, it happens according to the will of that great power. Everything happening here was received here as a disembodied voice years ago. This can only be called the glory of that great power. Everything that happens in this world is in that concept. Anything will happen only according to that will.

When Moses said that words would be received from God, the people opposed. Giving the same idea, God gave Jesus, who had no qualification or dignity to speak of among the people, to the world as a guide, born two thousand years later. God caused Buddha and the Prophet to be born in the same way. But the world did not accept them. Even after that, the world did not accept anyone who came according to God's will. The result? Giving place to the Vedic, caste and class differences came and everything was destroyed. This is the cause of the greatest tragedy of the world's human society.

This great dosha has been following humanity for twenty-five Chaturyugas. To free humanity from this dosha in this Kaliyuga itself, two thousand four hundred and forty-four

gave birth to gurus. All these gurus had a special rule based on experience. What Christians call 'tongue of fire' and Muslims call 'Noorhan' is this special rule. Hindus call it a voice from heaven, vision, light, etc. This is generally the special rule we have, but ours has another special feature added to it. Whatever we ask, we will get an answer from God.

You are the ones who know, see and hear this. If you have a determination to live thinking at least a little about this movement (idea), you can remove the devil that torments the world. You can put an end to the killing and murder happening in the world.

There is one thing that the family members who perform Guru Pooja must fully know. Along with purifying the ancestors, the fallen yogis between the devil and God are also being caught and removed. Therefore, the grandfather will not mislead your lineage. It is the harm done to you from your zodiac signs that is being removed with this. After twenty-five years, the result of this will begin to be seen. Can anyone destroy the coming generation like this?

The souls of the liberated ancestors will be born in your lineage for good. Thus, the children born after purifying the ancestors are those born embracing this change. By the time the children born embracing this change after nineteen seventy-three put this idea into practice, the torment of the devil mentioned earlier will disappear from the world itself. Those who pay attention can see that every child born after purifying the ancestors has special characteristics in character from birth.

Once women come here, they will stop bad practices. Men too. Its goodness will spread to the neighboring house as well. When that goodness begins to come, there will be opposition in every way. I have imparted to you as knowledge a determination to endure it. The harm of not imparting such knowledge has happened in the world along with other torments.

It has been mentioned many times that our ashram has some special features from other ashrams. It is not just sannyasa that happens here. But as far as sannyasa is concerned, its subtle nature is here. Anyone can achieve it. It does not require any special birth qualification. If there is the determination, dispassion, goodness and along with it sacrifice of a sannyasi, anyone can achieve it. A householder needs not only that, but something more. Is it available from the hidden cover you have traditionally embraced? If we evaluate Hindu customs themselves, aren't there diversities? What should be done to bring from diversities to unity, or even if thought about, nothing has happened.

Now let me say something about myself. I am not a person who desired this much. Everything came without my knowing. Although I have tried to make you understand this truth I have known in many ways, you have still not understood it. But it cannot be said that there is no slight difference in those who believe here. Isn't it comforting that thoughts of religion, class and caste have decreased a little? Along with that, a desire to be united has also come. Before this, Shankaracharya, Chattampi Swami and Narayana Guru were born in Kerala itself and worked for this. (Because of the scandal about paternity and the lack of reins of Brahmin hegemony, Shankaracharya had to work in North India.) But unity could not be established. Because followers

Instead of making a fuss over it, they did not bother to accept the idea, nor did they make any sacrifices. In that place, they gave importance to miracles and tricks.

We have started the effort to take up the sacrifice in the idea here. God gave us a way for that. That way should be useful. Anyway, the world definitely needs a correction. Even if born for ages, one may not have the fortune to see and know this. But this is a fortune for you. The fortune that gives the acquired karma that is useful to remove the devil that harms the world. Even if education, wealth, and lordship are lost, this fortune will always be with you without ever fading. If three generations do not make a mistake, this will become the total idea of the children of the world. If you make a mistake in this, God's will will have to be implemented through another birth in the fourth generation. This is what I have to inform you.

Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0448 to GV2:P0458

January 1993

It has been seventeen years since we got this path of ours as an idea. It was unclear for nine years of that. Although I have suffered a little, I have not done anything to get this much. What we have got today is a state that no one can even think of. God has made this done in such a way that it is equally useful to a householder, a yogi, a worker, and a wise man. It has been taught in such a way that every house gets it. This is a new model. There is no model available now. It was there in the sages before. Either because of the greatness of time or because of our mistake, it slipped away today. That is why the previously mentioned government drives us.

Sanatana is not a religion, it is dharma; it is culture. It is efficient. Great men have advised how to carry it forward. But we could not take it. From Nair downwards could not get close to this. Because it is the Varnasrama dharma itself. All these dharmas are there in our house itself. The Brahmin who does the Vedic work, the Kshatriya who rules, and the Shudra who stands without touching anywhere, this situation has been going on for a long time.

In Kali, it is Manu's decision that the rule reaches the Shudra. But they showed a trick to keep it in hand and rule. That is the Varnasrama dharma. The order of worship went down. It was decreed that the sound 'Om' should not be pronounced. The Vaishya was beaten down. Then caste came. Man became like wild grass. Reached a situation where there is no knowledge at all. The people became a mad class knowing only from 'Om'. Along with this, the rule was also given in the hands of the Shudra. Although the rule was given to the Shudra, the Brahmins kept it without imparting spiritual knowledge until his thirst was quenched. Thus the position obtained by the Shudra became useless. If the father dies and there is no right to offer bali, then what is the story? The old Nair families led a very degraded life. The Brahmin kept a relationship. But there is no relationship with them. Could earn some money. Because of that, the mother's name came into existence without the father's name. If a father without paternity is accepted, the ancestor will not get liberation. The children or nephews of this father should take up the paternity and work for the paternity and thus become pure. Otherwise, Madan, Marutha, Mantramurthi, Amman, and Yakshi - this ancestor will stand behind each of these kept inside and wander. Later, if born from this, they will become mad without knowledge. Justice and fairness will not be there in them. If children are born to such people, they will become those who do not hesitate for anything with desire only for money and women, and Kali will increase and lead the world itself to the Kali of Kali.

The earth we inhabit is encompassed by nine planets and twelve zodiac signs. Only through this can a human be born. Children born with pure paternity must exist in this Kali Yuga. For that, offering sacrifices by standing under a god or goddess is not right. In today's condition, a Guru who has surpassed the position of a god, found the presence of God, and provided guidance from that position (state) - a Guru who has acquired knowledge from that position, accepted the Brahmopadesham, understood how to apply it from time to time, and advises - is what is needed. That Guru is the one who must correct the evils of the present time.

What is called Hindutva today? All the ways suitable for degradation are present in it today. How should the degrading elements in this be torn apart? It is a lineage that tore apart our rights, kept us within a fence, and made us uncultured by not allowing us to be educated. If this continues, what is the situation? Whom should we, and who should rule us? Does this not mean that we had no right to think about this? Children born when the zodiac signs changed without paternity became arrogant, beat and destroyed each other, and became people with a history that is disgusting to hear. Tired of seeing this, Buddha advised the Pancha Shila. He advised living like siblings born from the same womb. He said this two thousand five hundred years ago. You don't have to look at your predecessors, Buddham Sharanam Gachchami. We have nothing to give to God. God only has to give to us. Buddha learned what the government was while sitting at home. When he remained silent, he was married off as a condition for it. Unable to fight, he hid and did penance. Then he received a word from God (in the light). Because he was a Rajarshi, kings and subjects obeyed the word he received. When it progressed, people who understood nothing did vile and despicable acts in Buddhist monasteries. Brahmins waited for it as a convenience. The unjust were driven out of India itself. Some fled and hid in Ceylon. Everyone left in the Buddhist monasteries was beaten to death. What created the Purappadu instead of worshipping God is the remainder of that. The panegyrists who were there before that, Buddhist monasteries should be made into Devi temples.

Brahmins cleverly brought back the Vedic system. Meanwhile, Shankaracharya came and advised Advaita. He said that the evil that has happened is because science and the Vedas are not known. For that, he established four Mutts and placed Acharyas there. There too, the Vedic person came, did not give entry to anyone else, and monopolized Sanyasa for Brahmins only. Thus we became poor people with nothing.

Due to the glory of time, today we are independent. Muslims and Christians came for trade, saw the hooliganism, stabbing, and cutting here, told stories, got close, won over with money, and seized the rule. They even became emperors. Through their rule, they imparted education and some culture.

Later we cried out for freedom. We gained freedom, but what did we gain? What is today's freedom? Did we gain anything? No. We gained nothing. Is what we have accepted today Western culture, Indian culture, or a hybrid of both? Everything shines here. The Christian here and the Muslim here are all Hindus. They did not come from Europe or Arabia. You can't go to Europe or Arabia and take paternity, right? When there was no other way, they changed their religion. That's all. Everyone is a sibling. What does a Hindu have today other than the sinful power of arrogance and boasting that my uncle has killed ten people? In this state, the merit was destroyed, and the wandering children had no way to live here. If they can live a little, the pride that they cannot talk to the neighbors will grip them. What is needed now to change this culture?

This is your past history. You must think about this aloud. Many great men have tried to change this. Nothing has been accomplished. We need a decision on what we will be tomorrow. How you can accept this idea, and thus how the efforts of great men can be made fruitful - that is what is going to be said next.

February 1993

God has provided us this path to fulfill the efforts of great men. This is experienced even among householders and children. Your own siblings can be shown as proof of this experience. This cannot be taken in hand and shown. What was taught word by word for seventeen years has been noted down here. It cannot be taken out now. If there is anyone after my time, they will do it.

The ancestors of your** family must be purified. That is what the Gurupooja here is for. Your fatherhood was lost and the offspring went astray. The children have intelligence and ability. But because of their ugliness and meanness, the parents are in a situation where they can neither swallow nor spit. It is difficult to change them from this state. We can advise them. We can pray for them. That is all that can be done. This flaw will not be seen in children born hereafter.

This system of removing the flaws of ancestors and merging them in the light is not just in India, but nowhere in the world. By seeing what gods and goddesses are, catch hold of the deity of worship, and catch that light. This is an action that no practitioner inside or outside India can do. To grasp this, one needs God's grace.

There is something called Yogagni. In that place, the flaws of your house are burnt in the divine light. The subsequent light is dedicated to God and instead, the Guru is given for worship. When you start accumulating merit by worshipping God in the name of the Guru, you might even be turned upside down. Because you are a fallen offspring. When accumulating merit, the body and mind will ache and there will be many difficulties. Blood, marrow, and flesh will heat up. Humility and truth

How can we correct the children who have degraded without merit, knowledge, and freedom of worship? Whatever we do, they will reject and destroy it. Children born from an uninterrupted birth cycle after Guru Pooja will develop a special character by the time they reach sixteen years of age. At the age of twenty-five, they will rewrite everything. They will find what is needed. This is an attempt for that. Children born after this obstacle is removed will think and act intelligently. Thus, they will reclaim the country. We only need to give them some knowledge for that.

Peace and harmony must be fostered in the country. These children will be the ones who work stubbornly for that, regardless of caste and religion. These children should be raised with the determination of being an Indian, without the difference of Hindu, Christian, or Muslim. For this, education centers and employment centers are essential. Through these centers, wealth, enlightenment, governance of the country, and everything else can be acquired. This is a matter of such foresight.

For this, God has given us a visionary nature. First, gods and goddesses and sage ascetics came. Many things were done through the gods and goddesses. They obeyed what was said. It worked like green leaves and scissors. Those who knew nothing called me a sorcerer. No one bothered to investigate what this was. After about three years like this, some contemptuous behavior started to appear. Some things worked out, some did not. Usually, chronic diseases were cured ten days before the predicted day. Only holy ash and water were given. ◌^ But later it stopped curing.

According to the word I received that children should be made to do this, children are doing this. I was told that this is for the children of the world. I am not doing any fraud. I am not doing anything of my own. I have no beard, hair, or matted locks, no mantras or tantras, and no other trickery. It is done with the holy ash given by my God, with the tongue in my mouth. You can accept this if you want. Now it is you who have to implement this.

There is a way for both men and women to become good here. Within that is Guru Pooja. Purifying the corrupted ancestors, teaching to call upon God in the place of worshiping demons, giving new blood, marrow, and flesh with the help of that God, and making the children born from it the basis for ruling the world. What is needed for asceticism is here. Asceticism is the light of God. There are householders here who have received it. They are householders who look after all household matters just like you. There are hundreds of people to introduce here who receive light and form among them. I am not showing this. This is not mine. It belongs to God. Then I have suffered a lot with my body. I wish to see the will of God working in your children. I also have a desire to sustain this. It is for these desires that this language is spoken.

There are people of all castes and religions here. None of them came to become ascetics. I didn't place any of this. This is the work God does to show that God has glory among you. The experience happened to them on their own after coming here and believing. Both those with knowledge and those without any knowledge have this experience.

Because children remove the evils by seeing my form, my body has pain and suffering. I endure that. The goodness residing in the children is being copied to the world. Therefore, those who do Guru Pooja should not accept other modes of worship. Because the demon will block everything again. Your old disease will return. This is an inexhaustible wealth received from God. How you can sustain it and bring it before the world

All that must be done. This is the aspect that should control the administration of the world. Or if you violate this, it will be stopped for the third generation. For example, as a result of the violation during the time of Moses, Jesus had to come. A qualified Moses was given but not accepted. Therefore, after two thousand years, Jesus, who had no significant paternity to claim, was given. When the king Buddha was given and not accepted, Shankara, who had no paternity to claim, was given. Even after that, Gurus were born. All this is God's work, to see if you will accept it. Now, you can only raise elephants and howdahs of what has passed. You cannot cultivate the idea. All this is God's will.

If you reject this idea, there will be danger to the world. If a natural disaster occurs, where will you end up? All this is trivial for nature. You can see its signs here and there. If you do not respect God's will, danger lurks in front and behind. If arrogance comes when you get some money from abroad, God will teach you. This is a land where everything grows. But there is starvation here. Arabia is a land where not even grass grows. But there is prosperity there that attracts you to do slave labor. Because no one lives there without praying five times a day. You have no discipline here. That is why this danger has come upon you.

Guruvani March 1993

God gives guidance. You implement it for your children, for your home, your land, and your country. If you do not see this in its proper measure and weight, you will have difficulties. Guru Pooja is done here as a foundation stone for that.

Most of the people who have come here are communists and Naxalites. But the experience here is that all isms and all arguments collapse on the basis of faith. No one should think that this is just asceticism. There is a way of life here. Not only that, except for nonsense, everything else is here. You must have the determination to impart what is here to the children of the world, no matter how hard it is. We do no other mischief here except prayer. Your goodness and fortune must come through your actions. If an ascetic acts, he will deviate from the custom. But if karma is not carefully maintained, everything will be lost. You must pray to impart this prayer, which you have been told to do eight times, to each child and to the children of the world. That is the primal duty. If fortune is to be helpful, first to God

You must pray. Then comes the work for our survival. After that, the intellect for the survival of life. We must develop it and reach a determination for a personality to repeatedly fulfill the actions of a hundred thousand people. Through that, the discipline and order to benefit the world. Only if we act in this way will it be useful. There should be a permanent existence in faith. Do not just go by what someone says, but go to the Guru and know it. Along with that, there should be the consciousness to surrender everything to God. What we are doing today is for how many chaturyugas or manvantaras. God gave us a medium for that. We need a special quality for that. Forgetting male, female, big, small, caste, and religion, we must spread the love of siblings born of the same womb. If you see a flaw in anyone here, you should privately feel the pain and tell them. Similarly, in your siblings' home, if they are unable to get a girl married, or if a family is on the verge of being lost due to the bad habits of a boy, you must be ready to resolve all that.

Never blame a sannyasi. Even if he has nothing, he has endured a lot of sacrifice. Because of the sannyasi's sacrifice, there is a complete benefit for any matter. If you have an understanding of this, luck and prosperity will come to your children. That is, if the sacrifice of a sannyasi is respected, its benefit goes to the householders. The situation where one can go to the land where kerosene is produced and earn money came about because of the religious consciousness of a section of the people.

I heard a lot of abuse for this, endured it, suffered pain - and God also broke a flaw. Although the method of an ashram is adopted here, it is different from the general concept of an ashram. What is the existing concept of an ashram? There are four types of understanding for this - yoga, karma, bhakti, and jnana. The existence and system of this earth are mixed with many of the various aspects of these four. When we say householder, his course of action is what fills this earth. Because householders and their various actions are more in number. Brahmacharis and devotees are very few. And there should only be that many of them. They are guides. The sannyasi and the devotee all originate from the householders. Therefore, it is the householders who need to improve. They are the main ones. Our ashram system is designed with an emphasis on this idea.

All religious principles are said to be harmonious. However, customs and attire are different, aren't they? The essence is the same. But it can only be understood if there is the tolerance to respect everything without rejecting anything. Some people reject the temple system. But that should be with complete knowledge. Rejecting it otherwise is intolerance. The Christians and Muslims here separated from the Hindus here. They happened to separate due to the excess of the hardships they suffered. But the ancestral basis of all these sections is the same. Therefore, everyone is brothers. Hindu religious principles or culture is very great and broad. But everyone has the obligation to think that a mistake happened somewhere. It may be due to the passage of time, it may be due to a mistake in the practice of the dharma of the time, it can be many things like that - we must know that it happened like this according to God's will. Very few people have this knowledge. At least those who know should understand this and act. People in a single house are unable to live without jealousy. Then what about in society? In the country? You must have at least a rough understanding of the varnashrama dharma. We must change according to the times. In the Satya Yuga, man

They lived like gods. In those days, one could pray to God by seeing Him directly. It should be understood that when the era changed, everything changed. The period of a Manu is seventy-one Chaturyugas. Looking at it that way, a human being has only the lifespan of a worm. Within this short period, we can only acquire a little knowledge. And that knowledge itself is experienced from the evils of the Varnasrama Dharma.

There is no place for a Shudra in Varnasrama Dharma. But in which house is there no Shudra? The Pulayan is in all our houses. There will also be a qualified woman. Can we ignore all this together as a family? A Shudra is a Shudra. He worked for everyone. The Brahmin knows this. He also knew that Kali is rightfully the Shudra's. Whatever is built and brought up is the concept of God and the purity in the words of the sages. When we say sage, there is a sage according to each time. According to that time - it is the dharma of the yuga. The dharma of that yuga stands as a part of humanity. The mistake lies in handling it by keeping it as varnas and the mistake that occurred in not doing so. That is why leadership was given to him. The Vaishya, on the other hand, was kept outside. Even the Nair army was created. The rule of the kingdom was divided and scattered. This is due to the duality of the kings. The entire power of sin struck by this duality and others fell on the Shudra's head. Without knowing that duality, without respecting them, those who recited the Vedas at that time did not know the dharma of the time. Knowing it, the kings should be advised by a guru who knows the dharma of the time by calculating the time in those respective times. Although there is plenty of evidence that this happened before, we all know how to say, 'Isn't Kali the dharma....isn't it the destruction of Kali'. When the great sages who did not fully know what Kali dharma is described Kali, they forgot to talk about the dharma of the Kaliyuga. Perhaps if there is any mistake in the time of the sage who knew it then, or if the guru who stated the dharma of the time is denied, this is likely to happen. We see some evidence for this here and there. First, those who made mistakes in the unknown royal dharma or sage dharma made it written that Kali is bad. It must be assumed that there were many sages who saw that the dharma of Kaliyuga was right. Vishwamitra is an example of this, as stated in the description of Kaliyuga that one can see rocks crumbling and flying due to the beating of the sheaves. And the act of Devendra who sent Menaka to disrupt Vishwamitra's penance. It is said in the same story that Vishwamitra created Trishanku Swarga itself with his power of penance. Then my life is an example. I will try to tell it as best as I can. You should try to take it as you can. By looking at it by including karma and dharma with life, those who determine your Kali or determine the time, Kali is the time we have been allowed to finish it properly. Here the dharma is the same for the Brahmin and the Chandala. The same goes for the Kshatriya. Sree Krishna is the great soul who showed through his life that this dharma is right. Similarly, Kali dharma is a subject that is equally possible for a householder, a sannyasi, and a sage. When we hear or know about Janaka's assembly, the decisive imagination that could be said completely about truth and untruth was going on through two women, Gargi and Maitreyi. Even today, at one end of the almanac, the figure of a blindfolded woman holding a weighing scale is given. We still respect them as the words of women showing what the truth and dharmas in Janaka's assembly are. But let there be no doubt that this is a land that has been taken by saying, seeing, doing, and making others do all this. However, because the sannyasis or sages who could show such a true situation slipped into error knowingly or unknowingly, they could not tell how to handle the dharma of the time. Because of this, it has become clear that each of us is the owner of the fortune of a true householder like Janaka, who maintains vision through advice, truth through vision, and dharma through truth.

Guruvani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · April 1993 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0483 to GV2:P0494
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

April 1993

Our duty is to transform the Kali of Kaliyuga into a human being. That is very difficult. Seeing the attire of today's doctors, the patient would die instantly. The wisdom of our ancestors is such that they praised the foreigners when the Zamorin was defeated. Westerners came here and spread their attire and culture. They ruled us and taught us how to rule. The poor can only imitate, right? The king had to obey what the Viceroy said. The people became slaves too. Not only our culture, religion, and customs, but even our heritage became alien to us. To the one who studied philosophy, history is theirs, and our culture is merely a story.

There is one thing we need to understand clearly. In Kali, there is no place for God. In Kali, the place is for the Guru. Many great souls came and said this in this fourth quarter. But we did not accept it. When Moses said he would receive the word from God, his contemporaries mocked him.

When Jesus came and spoke after two thousand years, he was crucified. But Jesus appeared to the one who believed. Even if they were poor fishermen, they worked passionately. They even had to endure severe crusades for twenty-one years. But they multiplied many times over. They even overthrew the government. Among them, they also did a favor by coming to India. If one adopted the name Madan or Mathai, a situation arose where they could walk along the road.

In the Varnasrama Dharma, the Brahmin had supreme comfort. The Kshatriya had governance and the Vaishya had trade. The Shudra was excluded from everything and kept outside. He has no knowledge or sense of dharma. He only knows how to imitate. Despite being comfortable in every way, without finding peace, when Westerners came here and grew their hair, the beedi roller and the boatman here started doing the same. The reason for this culture to degrade like this is the mother's lack of knowledge. The Pitru (ancestor) is not pure. A child will be born in our house only according to the zodiac sign the Pitru stands in. An intelligent and rational child will only be born in a family that has performed Pitru Shuddhi (ancestral purification). The Brahmin is ahead in this matter. Because of Pitru Shuddhi, only intelligent ones will be born in his family. But everyone born in a Shudra's family will become a drunkard. Even if they have knowledge and intelligence, this drunkard will only walk astray.

The Brahmin has done another big trick. Out of four marriages, three are Sambandham and one is Veli. Three women from lower castes for Sambandham and a Veli from a Brahmin Illam. If there is no woman from the Brahmin Illam, their karma is incomplete. Or if there is no woman, they will build a Yoni-Kandam. That will also be shown as a mudra in Tantra. You are satisfied if you see Mantra and Tantra, aren't you? In this degraded state, what knowledge have you been able to impart to your children?

Not a single Acharya has performed Pitru Shuddhi. Shankaracharya did Vedic rituals. Buddha said, just believe in me, everyone. There are plenty of Pitrus who have reached the position of Buddha. There are elevated Pitrus standing everywhere and in all positions. Not just Buddha's, there are Pitrus in the positions of all those who have risen.

Buddha saw the light. In the Quran, it is called rays of light. Christians have called it tongues of fire. We have here all the things said by gurus of all religious sects. The children here can see all this. I said that no one has done Pitru Shuddhi. We are doing that karma here with complete purity. We catch all these Pitrus and merge them into the Guru's light. After that, you have no Pitru. That is, you will not have to suffer the curse of the Pitru anymore. After Pitru Shuddhi, you should look to grow the lineage. The result of the karma you do today will be produced after twenty-five years. We should not insult anyone. Everyone is right as far as their matters are concerned. But we need the knowledge to ask and know about our matters. At least our home should be united. That is why you come here. Not to become a sanyasi. If you impart this culture, sanyasis will be born in your home. So, what and how should you, who are the basis for creating such a culture, accept?

The culture in this world itself is such that any great person bows down before their own children. The karma done here is capable of recreating this culture. That is the Pitru Shuddhi here. If you have only two children, let both be united. If there are ten, all ten. There is no ability or knowledge of mine in this. But it is one step ahead of all abilities and all knowledge. No matter which rich or worthy person comes, there is something to learn, know, follow, and be a model here. No one can ever say that this is not worthy.

There is no point in throwing it away in the smoke just because it is good. It will grow only through you. If it was just for me to survive, there is no need to struggle this much. I could just wear a loincloth and sit quietly as a Swami. Everyone would take care of my needs. But I prefer to live as one among you. During my time at Sivagiri, I could go to the Guru by lying that I was going to Muttakkadu. I spent ten years lying in Kadappathu. Then the Guru said, 'You go to the Sannidhanam, you have something to get there.' He meant for me to go to Kalladi Masthan. So I went one day. Met him. Bought him tea. When I returned after seeing him, the Guru said, 'He has given you everything he had to give. He will not let you near him anymore.' How great is the Guru and what the Guru gave. One day I went to see Puthura Swami. When I went, he was performing a homam in the scorching heat. Everyone who came was bringing firewood. I said, 'You should not trouble this poor man like this.' When I said that, someone came to hit me. I said that Puthura Swami would leave that place within four months. It happened exactly like that. Seeing that a bundle of old clothes Kalladi Masthan gave someone to keep would cause harm in the future, I threw it from the Poovampara bridge. Soon after, he said he saw a flood in a vision. After a month, the Poovampara bridge was washed away.

What can you learn from all this? You cannot learn anything by following such people without understanding anything. You will not get any culture. You are the ones who have to impart a lasting culture. For that, God has given us an idea that has never been given before. It is your daughter herself who performs this karma (Pitrushuddhi) looking into the light and speaks in front of you.

You must be vigilant to grow this lineage. You must make this a traditional knowledge. For that, you must be the most honest, detached, humble, and mutually loving. When you cook food, serve it, or do anything, you should pray that this ego be destroyed. Then the blood, flesh, and marrow will change. Thus you can change everything. Those living with you are living honestly. The thought will come that I cannot live a life without them. It is enough not to dress up the mind anymore. Wear whatever clothes you want on the body. Only if we adopt a determination and struggle harder than the determination the fishermen adopted for Jesus's ideology, will this be useful to the home, the land, and the country to that extent.

Guru Vani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · May 1993 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0495 to GV2:P0511
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

May 1993

Love for one must be pure. Cunning love and love pretending to be sincere are wrong. Because you are experienced in the spiritual side. Experience must be respected. If those with vision develop insincere and impure behavior, it will spread widely.

It has been said many times that ours is a new method. This age is Kali. Kaliyuga has its own nature; just like Satyayuga, Tretayuga, and Dvaparayuga. The change of ages is God's will. There are references to it in Manusmriti. Hinduism has a very long history. There is a calculation. Compared to it, the history and calculation of Christianity is very small. Hinduism is a culture rather than a religion. We belong to a lineage that worked desiring the presence of God in Satyayuga.

At the end of Satyayuga, some Aryans (Brahmins) migrated and settled on the river banks (four). They studied our history and culture. They understood in their minds that we are without deceit, honest, seeing God directly, and good people. The people here had no hesitation in sleeping anywhere. Moreover, they were of the thought that whatever is God's will, that alone is enough for them.

Time changed. With the change of time, God was no longer seen directly. In distress, they went to the forest and did penance in many ways. Gradually, they began to see light and form. What they saw in penance was drawn on a tree with a green leaf. When they thought about it in their minds, they got words from the light. They got a mantra. Great men started discovering such things one by one. What was discovered was carved on stones. They were given form and color. Westerners call this the mind. How did this mind come? That is consciousness. Consciousness, light. We must learn to say that is the truth.

It was during this transition period that the Aryans migrated and settled. They started practicing one by one. The practice grew in many ways and reached up to the rule of the country. They ruled intelligently. They made others kings. They made people believe that the king was God. Thus, the king ruled Tretayuga and Dvaparayuga. In Dvapara, they started manifesting. It came to be that all this is true and if needed, God can show all this.

Studying this history, the Aryans created caste. He knows that Kali belongs to the Shudra. That is, the right to rule. Intelligently, even a price was put on letters. The Vaishya was beaten by the Shudra. Time changed this. Later, the Vaishya beat the Shudra for about three generations. The Shudra was made to do slave labor. Doing slave labor, the Shudra became a Shudra. By making them do slave labor for ten generations, they were made lower castes. They worked hard without food and in distress and got tired. When doing slave labor, there was no arrogance. After that, arrogance increased. With arrogance, the whole great sin fell on his head. Because of ego, God hated him. It was said that there would be no king at the end of Kali. The whole of Kali was to be ruled by the king. Thus, the one who chased and the one who ran fell into the pit. Paternity was also lost. When the father died, there was no way even to offer bali. Paternity was destroyed, ancestors wandered, and when children were born with changed signs, the clan itself was destroyed. Meanwhile, great souls came and said that you should become one. But they were tortured, chased away, and killed in many ways. Finally, Muslims were brought and made to rule.

In Bengal, a Brahmin eats fish. Not in Kerala. In Kerala, the Brahmin came only for Vedic rituals. Because of the Vedic's lies, the Shudra became someone who knew nothing. Now, for some time, there has been a light again. Although the British came as a precursor to it, they are not decent people. A good-for-nothing there came here and became the Viceroy. The British came here and created dress, language, education, administration, and law. Before that, although there was no law here, one could live thinking of God.

Today, the era of hybridization has arrived. There is hybridization in all living beings. Do not take this lightly. You must heed the word of God. God is favorable to us today. Jesus and the Prophet have said, 'Messengers of God will come and lead you astray'. I had doubts about those words. Even when the Avadhuta came and told me, I was convinced only when I received the revelation.

This is not meant for just one caste, religion, or society. If it didn't work when Thiruvalluvar and Naranath Bhranthan did it, wouldn't it work if we do it today? One should think that if my lineage will improve, I might as well die for it. We should try to accommodate everyone who comes to the institution by any means. If they are good for nothing, keeping them here and making them sacrifice will bring good. This is the time of this lineage. Therefore, even if you are cut into pieces, you must stand firm in God. You do not have to experience the sacrifice of Jesus being crucified or the persecutions suffered by the disciples. Because God has given you the freedom and social status to resist and glorify the Guru. Although Shankaracharya offered his head, the tradition of opposing the Kapalika is before you as a lesson.

But you have shortcomings. None of you came with the desire to go and stay in an ashram for at least one day. There is no inclination for that. The whole land lacks that familiarity. Nor have you learned to obey at least one person at home. Ashram life in this manner will bring danger. If karma goes wrong, one will have to take another birth just to suffer it. Even after suffering for this throughout one's life, the pain has only increased; nevertheless, there is no limit to endurance. Those who cannot do it should tell themselves that they cannot and withdraw.

Once you decide to stand firm in a belief, you must obey what the Guru says. Ashram life is about untying every knot of past lives. When you wander, seeing and hearing things, a habit of thinking will develop. One must respect the sense of duty. Householders and visitors must respect those staying in the ashram. For householders to be able to respect and love like that, those staying here must have good discipline. If one experiences and understands hardships from the movement, what can that personality not become! Even if you come to the ashram, ask for some service, and do it, you should consider it a fortune.

If a human birth is obtained from a bull, the nature of the bull will be mixed in it. If a human birth is obtained from a sloth bear, deceit and treachery will be more. If it is from a monkey, fickleness will be more. The softest are the deer and the rabbit. Most women have a heart like a rabbit. If you look closely, you can see about all births.

My effort is to destroy the past inclinations of human birth and nurture the present inclinations. I will only ask if you can just do this today. Because the sannyasi knows that due to the inclinations of past lives, one cannot obey directly. Only the lowest and the highest will be made to obey. The lowest will be beaten to death. The highest will be told to stop.

Only if you think about what the course of karma in me is and who I am, can you go along with this. Since all inclinations are intertwined, it is difficult to dismantle and rebuild karmic capacity. You can only be saved if you obey and keep your word.

Some women smile with jealousy. No one will understand that. She herself can also act in reverse. If a woman lies, we cannot prove it. A Brahmin woman does not have the knowledge of a Nair woman. The Brahmin who says that a woman should not be included in anything, [includes] her in Vedic

Why was it taught? Giving women freedom for worshiping God and for education is very good. In Hinduism, freedom is given to women who progress by worshiping God well. Rising above all this, they are now reaching towards wisdom. Position must indeed be given to environment and qualification. That is the special rule of the time.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · June 1993 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0512 to GV2:P0522
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

June 1993

Those who believe here will understand the secret and reality of human life, karma, and the reality of the form of karma. If one keeps their word without failing, the course of karma will become pure. This society does not have the nature of inquiring into and respecting anything. Generations to change this nature

You will have to. You must do at least a part of the purpose here. That is sacrifice. That sacrifice must come in every action. Only if there is a greatness will it be respecting this divine decree. The Guru's desire is to improve everyone. With a sense of duty, what the Guru commands, the disciple must obey. The dialogue between Krishna and Arjuna can be taken as an example.

You must have a realization that there are only perversions in the world, and we will change that. Those who are here are not knowledgeable about Hindu culture. Their assumption is that they have general knowledge. But that knowledge cannot be applied anywhere. That is why they are unable to accept and respect anything. They came from a land covered with snow blocks and faded glory and ruled us. The generations that came in between with the ignorant people who were here have gone completely wrong. A few devotees and monks who thought and suffered about the future and generations of this land - a few yogis who saw all this and cried out and laughed - who among these can we accept? This is also the reason why no one understands the things happening in this ashram.

When I received a message from God to do the prayer happening here, I did not understand then that there was such a big purpose behind it. It was only after asking and understanding the visions of fifty people that I began to understand its total sum. Each person's vision in each way according to the broadness of their minds, when evaluated together, I understood that God is enjoying watching a group of machine-like people with no knowledge operate to fully implement world justice. It will cause danger if you take such a big thing without giving it this kind of dignity. If not kept in that state, there is nothing more dangerous than this. These girls who got the opportunity to live here must protect this even by sacrificing their lives. Otherwise, its destruction will happen through them.

The existing perversion must be completely destroyed. You must have the ability to trample and muddy this perversion and dig a trench for it to flow away. Each resolution made here goes to the respective zodiac signs and is changing the snow accumulated there. But what has happened in the hearts of the people cannot be changed. What is in the subtle can be changed by the power of resolution. This process is happening in nine planets, twelve zodiac signs, and twenty-seven stars. All these changed things are being vomited on earth - like the dust particles of the moon falling on earth. If it continues as it is happening in this atmosphere today, you cannot even think about the danger that is going to happen to the earth in the future. Only the one with eyes can see. You must do this karma without failing in punctuality. Otherwise, the one who comes next will destroy it.

You have no knowledge about the Guru lineage. It is out of fear of the Siddhas that some contemporaries have done certain things. But it is not like that for you. You have got the opportunity to do it with a full heart. Yet you are unable to do it. Whatever changes are made in the atmosphere, you must act for that change to be useful to the people. The karma you do must be held in the atmosphere and come back as the nature of nature.

Until recently, there were poor people who worked for the lord, got the result, and stood submissively saying this is your rice. Even now there are such people in some places.

But there is another side to this today. The incidents of cutting down these lords also arise from nature. Every action has a reaction, doesn't it? Every action is merging into nature. The lotus grows in the mud. Nothing else will come out of something pure. It will only merge away. It cannot be taken and applied. This is the nature of creation since the beginning of the world.

Eras are changing. People do not have the knowledge to change according to the change of eras. When that happens, there will be some kind of natural disaster. Seeing this much, you could not see this. Then how will this idea be brought forward? One group says that everything will change if the bonds of caste are untied. Another group says that the trinity system is good. Yet another group says that everything exists within their religion. We have a consensus through experiences. Our chanting will fly and through the imagination in it, all baseness will catch fire and be destroyed from the subtle power in the universe. God's insistence is that this should be done by a group of poor people who cannot do this with value and knowledge. To cut the severity of the spiritual landlord, the trinity system, a revolutionary force in line with the idea of the Naxalite Guru lineage must awaken. You should not apply your own intelligence and cunning here and spoil this. This is a field where Buddha and Sri Shankara worked and lost. We should only think about how to act in that merger of whatever we see. The great souls are those who bathed at four o'clock when the snow was drinking and flowing in the river Ganges, and did penance by chanting in pain. Then it changed to mantras and tantras. The capacity of an ordinary person should work in the place fixed by doing penance and come down from there. Only if it comes like that, it will be right.

The word of the discoverer is logic. Someone should do that word with sincerity. Because it was ruined by doing action. It must be purified by doing action itself. The scoundrel has made mistakes through the scoundrel, the yogi through the yogi, and the sannyasi through the sannyasi. It is not easy to correct it. If the action is not through calmness, it will go astray. This is not a subject that any scientist will yield to.

Our asset is experience. The beginning will be difficult. According to the degree of sincerity, the difficulty will change. When praying, the first thing that happens is difficulty. You may not even feel like praying. Again interest, again fading - that's how it is. Just as one does not like to eat the same food every day, prayer also has changes. The reason is the deformities in one's own past lives. Other religions do not say this. Because it only has an elevation up to the status of a deity. The Quran says a lot about liberation. It is not complete. Because none of these religions have a history and antiquity of the length of eras. The Bible has a history of at most six thousand years. This is not enough to reach the secret of human life and the reality of the form of action. The field of complete knowledge is very, very far from all this. There are no artificialities there. There is no severity, purdah, and other forms.

August 1993

Life towards sacrifice

Action towards virtue

How should we view the past?

How should we view the present?

Virtue through life.

The beautiful sight of what life is, crossing the scars of past lives. When experiencing that beautiful, divine nectar beautifully, you will be fortunate to see what the action is that fills our solar system, rejecting the scholar and the ignorant, knowledge and ignorance. Take this with value and see and know what the path of dharma is, which highlights the calculation of the manvantara that this world has contained for human society to live as one.

Gaining a Guru will be the opening of a door to recognize what the dharma and adharma you do are. Like taking what someone else has accumulated without knowing and squandering it without pain, some dangers lie hidden in you, and in that stream of light you can see the action that makes you yourself judge virtue and sin. When that door opens without knowing any work, struggle, or hardship, you will think about what you have unknowingly become the owner of. After that, when it becomes your ego, do not waste the time God has given us to recognize and take each of these—the presence of Ishwara, the presence of God, the presence of Brahman, the presence of Parabrahman—which are contained in this unbrokenness obtained in this Guru-gain. Tie up the mind that runs towards multiple gains, forgetting the Guru-gain, to close down such unbrokenness. Do not lose the opportunity to see and know what the causes and effects are, called the holy feet, the Guru-gain given by God, so as never to forget the truth of life that becomes the scholar and the ignorant who praise to the skies the aspects of action that fall into the ego, which is the cause of the three gunas in the aforementioned language, that the causes and effects known by the three gunas in front of the Guru are strong matters. The opportunity has come to tell you this now; as an experienced person, without ego and with humility, I repeatedly request you.

Keep this in your memory daily so that God may have mercy on the ignorant who take leadership roles by saying the above sentences without wasting the opportunity to say in a refined manner at the same time as holy feet and social reformer. He who becomes a great scholar by cleverly calling the tools to be tied up as the bond of karma should also know this.

Lying in the sea, on the beach, in the sand, on the roadside, and in the river sand, dressing up as a Vedic scholar at the same time, bowing to the pure form made capable of the avadhuta lifestyle, without blocking the stream of light on the roadside, closing it in the presence of the heart, taking what is needed at the needed time, become worthy of the love and respect of good people.

Your Swami

Guruvani

September 1993

Various kinds of ups and downs have occurred in our lives. Therefore, old tendencies will stir up in our actions. The best thoughts and enthusiasm will rise up. But your capacity for action will not allow it to be executed. If you think of living in the ashram for a full two days, it won't be possible. You will leave before that. After doing a little bit, one will speak authoritatively. We must understand that this is unnecessary for us. It is fundamentally due to a lack of knowledge that one speaks so authoritatively.

Once devotion sets in, do not act impulsively with initial enthusiasm. If we do not reflect on who we were before, these flaws will affect it. Actions will be tainted with flaws. If a good action is mixed with bad, the goodness will fade. If you observe carefully, you can see that you have acted in various ways on the same day. There is a single word used by ascetics for this behavior - 'past tendencies'. That single word contains the account of births and eras. You can see color variations in many places on the wings. Those are the places where the soil has washed away.

Human life and tendencies are very, very old. Come to think of it, there are no other creatures in the world as filthy as humans. If a man or a woman goes astray, it will cause immense harm. Especially when it comes to women. It is a demonic form in which all the flaws in the world have melted together. But it must be transformed. I see myself giving priority to that. But one should not provide more facilities than necessary. Experience shows that if you do, they will climb on your head. These mischiefs of theirs were earned by the ancestors in the past. Now, it is the imitation of the Western world. It is the duty of men to make efforts to remove the flaws of womankind. Decide that I will dedicate my life to removing the shortcomings of womankind. But no one bothers to do that. Even if they do, it is of little use. But God has provided us with all the ways to do this karma. The reason is that God has compassion for them.

Compared to other ashrams, mostly young people come here. That is God's will. Men and women who come here go back with many good desires. The children born from those desires will turn out well. We have done many rituals here to remove the flaws in women. Along with that, you should also have an aching prayer that this flaw may be removed from us. Perhaps the result of the karma we are doing will come after a long time. That should not matter. Remember when the result of Jesus' sacrifice came into effect.

Today, you are in a situation where you cannot forgive a single word from each other. This hardship of ours is not for our life. It is for the future generations. All this has happened because of the flaws of the ancestors who have passed away. The destiny of ancestry will stand up to the measure of merits and sins. When born in ego, deformity occurred. Everyone has a path where ancestry was lost. We must always remember these truths.

Who was the Prophet? A person born without any education in a family that made and sold idols. How great is the history of that Prophet. His words became the Veda. It is because he moved forward acting only on the words received from God. But we do not know how to think like that. You were able to come here and work without knowing anything. That is a great fortune.

This is a time of mixture. It is clearly stated in the Gita. We can listen to the bad words of a thousand people. We can tolerate whatever anyone does. But it is enough to have the determination that we will complete this. Even sages have prayed to be born in this fourth era. Those who prayed like that have become gurus in this era. At least from now on, your prayers should be offered to God. This is the best time for a Shudra to become a Brahmin through his actions.

The sense of duty should not become distorted. If it does, the consequence is that one cannot even slightly comprehend the boundless greed for misdeeds that may accumulate. This current practical situation might be suitable for these times. It is a mistake only if it doesn't happen so. Because, in this earth that has been a fertile ground for goodness for a long time, if the evil of the desire for misdeeds does not occur, the virtues arising from the evil elements cannot be transformed. The words of the great souls might have been with this in mind. It has been described using 'Kali' itself - 'Kalikala Varnana' (Description of the Kali Yuga). The foresight, named 'Jnanadrishti' (vision of knowledge) or 'Darshana Drishti' (vision of philosophy), cannot be wrong. You should always have the sorrowful vision that an uncivilized guise, lacking the vigilance of how institutions sanctified by the words and deeds of great souls and the people operating them should be, might consume the world itself.

There are many reasons for such a sorrowful vision. The greed of the rulers, the dereliction of duty by householders, the hate-filled distortions of organizations, and the unfulfilled desire for sacrifice of the successors of ideal men are the main ones among them. That desire for sacrifice became a weightless measure. It became a posture where only language and attire have importance. A predatory nature is prevalent in all this. Through that predatory nature, seizing the governance of the country, sucking the blood of the nation, and manifesting religious conversion, denial, and destruction in a way that erases what is real, India was hijacked in the same way it was endangered for over two centuries, unable to distinguish between darkness and light. Occasionally, there are some lightning flashes. But they are also temptations in a shining guise. Succumbing to the enchanting beauty of this shining guise, the great nation of India and its people were crushed and became exhausted. Although the few survivors, who were crushed under the guises synonymous with deceit that flashed like lightning and were left with only their lives, realized the freedom they desired, distortions are overflowing to the extent that it seems it was a mistake.

Let me point out some of such distortions. Perhaps because the life left the body of that good man, Raja Ram Mohan Roy, those overcome by emotion severed the eyes of his statue. Another is that a statue of a holy son of Kerala was attacked, and the movement was treated negatively. If the children of Kerala themselves felt so, it means they did not understand that the love they desired has materialized a hundredfold in the form of this statue. The reason it is not understood is that Indian culture has been buried. Instead, a complete statue of Aryan culture has been raised. It is filled with malicious pride, capable of cutting and eating the true nature of man with its intellect. The previously mentioned flashing lightning is the result of a government that has lost its virtue, acting forgetting statecraft and human strategy. In the heat of this lightning of Aryan culture and governance strategy, Indian culture turned into ashes; along with some human remnants that were symbols of Indian culture. However, we also have some great men who rose from those ash heaps, combined the material and the spiritual, and created movements without borrowing ideas from the outside world - Buddha, Shankaracharya, Aurobindo, Sree Narayana Guru, Mahatma Gandhi. At the same time, some rulers and organizations, who have been corrupted by foreign cultures, dissolving their blood, marrow, and flesh in them, and refusing to leave them, perhaps unable even if they wanted to, have already corrupted those movements. Let me state one thing clearly. Such human remnants can only be rediscovered through philanthropic visionaries. That must be known through pain. Nothing that is not known through pain will have love.

A romantic obsessed with sinful love, who destroys the virtuous love of many, cannot discover this either. Danger lurks in romantic feelings that lack the discernment of good and evil. If there is a lack of knowledge about duty, as a result of a culture that reaches a state of being crippled by danger, animalistic cravings full of corruption and delusion will rise up, overturning all the good intentions of human society. That will lead to total destruction. I pray with the good desire that rulers and idealists with patience, foresight, and patriotism will use their intellect, effort, and lives to discover the previously mentioned visionary—an idea that cannot be corrupted by the time and actions of that visionary—and utilize it for world peace and harmony.

October 1993

Some people here have a habit of turning a blind eye to certain things while doing some good things. I have worked hard and done this much so that you can manage. Do not destroy it. It is easy to ruin. It is difficult to build. All this is the result of the efforts of many people. Those who treat everything lightly are not fit to stay here. Even if you can only stay here for one day, your sweat must fall here. To that extent, your shortcomings will fade away. The attitude that 'only what I do is right' is not good for anyone.

Those with devotion will know whatever happens anywhere. Devotion without attention is useless. It must be done with attentive devotion and the strength of yoga. One must strive hard (even those who are ill) and do what is necessary, inch by inch. Some can do agriculture as a service; some can plant and nurture saplings. One can also serve by giving a portion of their own harvest. Those who work from here should be given a position. At the same time, they must do what is needed at the right time. That good opportunity which comes in handy may not occur again. If you feel the sacrifice involved in what you do, there is nothing more to gain. Sacrifice does not need to bow down anywhere. All merit belongs to sacrifice. Even without any special qualification or attainment, the words of a person of sacrifice will create a heartfelt experience. You should pray that this blessedness serves you until the end. Mocking fellow beings, opposing those who do good deeds - these must be avoided. All this is contrary to ashram life.

Kerala mainly has two shortcomings. Kerala will not have the character of North India. This is because South Indian kings fell into Dravidian culture and became Shudras. Tamil Nadu, on the other hand, accepted lower-level worship. Giving importance to Shakteyam, even the prominent people there were ruined. Many among them became Christians. Christians imparted English speech here instead of good things. Thus, a culture unconnected to Indian culture grew in Tamil Nadu and Kerala. The kings here were degraded in every way. We, being the subjects of degraded kings, naturally became degraded as well.

We do not think about the depth of our culture. Humility is the hallmark of our culture. India is blessed by nature. We have everything here. The cornerstone of our cultural heritage is the Upanishads, Vedas, and Puranas. But because we stood apart pretending to have knowledge, we do not know its meaning. Now it is difficult to elevate the old state. Understanding this, a plan itself must be created and brought forward.

We have children here who can sit here and tell about any event in the world. There is no point in saying that they got that ability as a divine gift. If this knowledge is to be brought forward, one must understand and live within them. God has not given us any flaw that requires us to bend our backs before anyone. If one loves truth, there is no greater achievement to come. The fortune brought by attention, devotion, and the strength of yoga must be carefully preserved.

Detachment, the strength to forgive and endure anything, and the humility to dedicate all detachments for one purpose should be adornments. With this skill, we can become good. We must strive until the Kali age becomes a reality. For that, attention is absolutely essential. We should not live here the way capitalists and their children live. Special attention is needed for that. Attention must be cultivated through exemplary discipline.

As far as detachment is concerned, the story of Pakkanar is a model. God has given us permission to have love for everyone and thus behave truthfully. We must help each other. Mutual enmity is evil. That must change and another kind of detachment must come. Then evil will leave you - and goodness will come instead.

Although the trinity nature is mentioned as Brahma, Vishnu, and Maheshwara, Shaivism is prominent in practice. It is the same all over the world. Based on this, Sree Narayana Guru gave priority to Shiva. The Vaishnavite cleverly gained dominance. Its origin happened right here in India. The origin of human history itself is here.

There is a history of Parvati worshipping Shiva. In the nature of linga worship, this cannot be mistaken. Because Parvati is a creation. Having had the vision from birth, she could see the connection with Shiva across births. That is the basis of the origin of karma. Linga worship came into existence due to the scent of desire that spread from the origin of man. The history of Christ and the history of Islam begin from Adam. Parvati also adopted this very nature (Adam's peak is the Garden of Eden in Arabia. Shiva's footprint is in Ceylon.) The Indian concept is that Shiva is not born of a womb. The concept is that he was born by will, that is, by the will of God. 'Sankalpamatrena Parameshwara:' has two meanings. One is that he originated from the will of God. 'Aaseedagrelasabhadevada' means he originated from light. The Vedas also say the same. Sree Narayana Guru also thought in the same way. But there are very few who have thought about its true nature.

Good, evil - only if you know both can you know the sum of good. Evil has the upper hand. Because the origin is from evil. The origin is from the Annamaya Kosha. There is no bad without good, and no good without bad. Like the left hand and the right hand. Why give respect to the right hand? The fruit of karma is individual. Nothing can be called right or wrong. One must know what the qualification for it is. Luck must also be considered. One must know how karma is purified. One must discriminate and understand who I am, what my action is, what I was before, and what I should become. From human discrimination, one can say what is wrong. As far as we are concerned, it has become very easy in that matter. Think about the culture of the time when people did not know how to say paddy. That time and today's time cannot be reconciled at all. The difference evolved from the habit of human desire. At a certain time, this will not suit the body. The atmosphere will be polluted. Then what will we become? This is a greatness of creation.

Our willpower is a physical inquiry. Scientific progress is not accepting what is not suitable for human destruction. There is also a way to find the truth through science. When that is found, science is over. There will be a doubt whether what was discovered is wrong. There will be fear of the world. How many times has the world progressed and struck back like this!

The presence of God can be seen in the seventh state. Until then, it is demonic. Although it is said that the demonic nature was mistaken by doing right and wrong for God's purpose, it is not demonic. They are two states, like Asura and Deva. Disobeying God's word is demonic. That demonic nature is what made Adam go wrong.

What is shown in the vision cannot be said exactly as it is. It does not become evidence for the people. What to do about it? Even those who have seen it here cannot say it. At the age of twenty-five, the second layer of the brain will begin to clear. Because it is shown in the state of the three times. They have had many births with willpower mixed with right and wrong. In between this, there may be a silver lining of compassion. It may be the compassion that came from a poor man when he was given a meal. Even when trying to hit with a pestle, if a grain of rice flies off that pestle and falls into this poor man's pot, even that is a virtue. He will take it and offer it to God as an offering. It is because of the fruit of that poor man's prayer that he gets to see God's service. There are many who pray, seeing that service, wishing they had an opportunity. Because it is not given knowingly, what is received cannot be respected knowingly. A consciousness must then arise to think why I came here. No one has the pleasure to think like that. It does not join through luck.

Adam had someone to walk with him as a helper and tell him things. The Prophet did not get that either. He held on tightly to whatever he received and took from it.

Those who do not know at least one language do not get the opportunity to learn anything else. One thing will be given - vision. Through it, everything will be known. The knowledge of any action will be obtained from it. All the lowly karmic paths lying in this world will end through this karma. It will correct the wrong, make it a tool, and create another value. It is not enough if it is only in the subtle; the karmic path must also reach the people. From the subtle to the waking state, dream, deep sleep, Turiya, and Turiyatita - thus, up to the presence of God, crossing the position of Devendra and the state of Ganapathi, the lord of the Ganas, it has the right up to creation, preservation, and destruction.
