Guruvani November 1993

Today, ages have changed. Inner light, outer light, Atmajyothi Paratpara, Jyothirjyothi, Swayamjyothi. Atmajyothi itself is of many kinds. Each person carried each of these through each birth. Those who carried it like that have taken birth as Gurus. A Madan, Yakshi, or Rishi can become a Guru. There are those who are born as Gurus to Gurus. Those who come as Gurus like this will lead the dimensions of vision from the waking state, dream, deep sleep, Turiya, and Turiyatita and lift them up to the presence of God. The position of Devendra and Ganapathi's

An individual soul can be guided beyond its status, even up to the state of showing creation, preservation, and destruction.

Therefore, how careful should you be? Those who wish to become a Brahmachari or Sanyasi have the opportunity here to seek and attain what they need. No matter how many times and where you fail, you must seek again and again. Those who come and stand here must have such intense determination and dispassion. They must know every small and big thing that happens here on a daily basis. A Brahmachari must be at the forefront of everything. What is the characteristic of a renunciate? No matter what he has to endure, he will always be eager to instill goodness in the hearts of others.

You know what today's rulers are thinking: how to copy the justice and laws of foreigners. They are the ones who seek ways to bring science forward based on small experiences and thus destroy their own culture. Today, it is possible to take photos of subtle forms in the atmosphere. Even if that is possible, it is only a physical manifestation. However, spirituality is misunderstood today. Although Vedanta expresses the idea that by talking and writing extensively, one will eventually reach the truth, it does not seem that anyone has reached the real truth to date. Truth is merely the light of the soul that penetrates and stirs the course of karma, which is subtle in its inner nature. We have that method here. This is also a method that gives importance to those who have Brahmanhood. Our understanding is that there is sinful power even in the practice of the Vedas and Shastras. The basis of this sinful power is a competitive spirit between the Dravidian and the Aryan. Even today, there is a feeling that being an Aryan means being great. But we must remember one thing. The Dravidian lived with truth at the forefront.

The Aryan, on the other hand, came from cold countries. Seeing the forests, hills, and plains here, he stayed. He took control of the education system and the administrative system through intellect. The circumstances here were favorable to him. Imparting knowledge, culture, and love, he studied the original texts and understood even creation, preservation, and destruction. He even wrote the Amarakosha and made everything his own. Brahmanhood became his monopoly. But the Dravidian always lived only with truth at the forefront. He always lived by worshiping in the forest. Even today, there are Malavedans and Mannans who live like that. The forest will act as they say. It cannot be said that Dravidians are not great people.

When a new culture was imparted, truth was abandoned for deception. There was a time when even an elephant or a tiger would move away on its own. The deity who was grasped and manifested in the Treta and Dvapara Yugas is still worshiped today in the mental culture of that forest tradition.

Times have changed today. Everything must change with the times. Westerners have had a history since the time of Abraham. Similarly, many Acharyas came here, reformed many things, and created history. No one respected it here. However, even today, even wild animals respect the words of the Malavedans. What is the reason for this? Even though they are forest tribes, their minds are not distorted. Their women are truthful. The Aryans, on the other hand, studied our culture, made calculations for themselves, and lived as great people. But through the changes of time, they failed to maintain the truth. They wrote many praises. They built a fortress so that the Dravidians could not even peek in. But in Kali, they failed to repeat and evolve. They only wished to live as pleasure-seekers forever.

Even today, there are women among them who walk around holding palm-leaf umbrellas. There is an aura of purity in all their ways. Even today, their only desire is that ours should spread. If you go to North India, you can understand this very clearly. Some among them tried to change this. But it didn't happen. Those who tried could only speak for a transformation from the old. They couldn't give anything new.

The downtrodden seen today were once high-ranking. But they were not allowed to become high-ranking traditionally. If you go to North Malabar, such people have more beauty. They also have a good culture. Their women are as honest as the Vedic authorities. A culture existed there that Arabs and the English could not penetrate. But it became very old.

Although many Acharyas have been brought in this Kali Yuga, they also became exhausted believing that if they win in tricks and logic by adding some old things, they can ascend the Sarvajna Peetham. Therefore, they had to return to the old itself; just like taking the medicine from here, changing its appearance, and bringing it back here.

Dayananda Saraswati has revealed Manu's calculations in 'Veda Paryatanam'. Even those who have studied this will only say the old things. Because if they say that, their position will decrease. Nabi and Jesus were able to establish some boundaries and set up some children. That is why they were able to bring about a global transformation. Although Buddha came here, is there anything left of it today? Hinduism does not have a single person to say that.

Who has newly transformed Hinduism? Although Shankaracharya, Narayana Guru, and Madhvan did some things, a mistake was made by adopting the Trimurti system. We see the history of Vivekananda and Ramatirtha going to foreign countries. But apart from showing the old things themselves, nothing else is seen in it. This now stands in our contemporary history. However, in the field called the Trimurti system, there is a historical truth written back as the son of Brahma until the birth of Manu. The calculation of this Manvantara and Kalpa is said in different ways in different places. Because all the above-mentioned people performed some Siddhi or sleight of hand or Parakaya Pravesha, or completed the action that makes Hatha Yoga naked by calling it Raja Yoga, Hinduism has made many such mistakes and still lives in that old culture.

How can this be changed? We are experiencing all the guilt of what ruled us and made us rule today. Only an Acharya can change this. This can only be changed through time and humans growing with time. But you don't have the mental strength to instill this truth in you!

The humble nature of the old should be taken and absorbed and made into a tool to bring it into action. Mind, word, and deed must become one. Along with that, the emotional distortions that get mixed up should be removed. It is difficult to let go of old habits. It can only be changed over time by establishing something new. Determination is absolutely necessary for that. What helps to maintain determination should be brought in as strength and what does not should be discarded. It is difficult to bring it without letting go of the habit. Human effort is needed to change the path of a condition. And God's grace according to its attainment. What are the things called attainment? Luck or Yoga - that is the main thing. Which one we

One must think that one is caught in Yoga. Whatever shortcomings (vasanas) we have must be overcome through a familiar Guru. One should not have any doubts about that Guru.

Now, how can a true Guru be found? What comes in the waking, dreaming, and deep sleep states (vision) can only be correctly perceived without the intellect. In this world, truth alone does not exist. It means that what is not true will also come in waking, dreaming, and deep sleep states. Discerning what is right is possible only through consciousness. Consciousness contains the Guru. One must surrender to that Guru who encompasses consciousness. Visions will come to ordinary people as well. A vision is a mixture of the past, present, and future. Who we were in Treta and Dwapara yugas, what the present should be made into, what we should become in the future, and how to discard the past - visions are given to understand all this. Like a local drama, both good and bad will appear in the vision. There must be a place to ask and know about it. Only what is received verbally from the Guru should be accepted. You must discard the implied meanings that suit your intellect. Once the latent tendencies of past lives, which exist in a subtle form, are shown, they must be removed. It should be known not through the intellect, but through consciousness. If you are convinced that what connects you is Yoga, you should develop a sense of dedication by clearing it through consciousness without looking at worldly knowledge. What if consciousness is tied to the intellect? The intellect will spread and give importance to knowing through worldly knowledge. That is, if a vision is thought of through the intellect, mistakes will happen. If it grows in the intellect, it will not become clear. Even husbands and wives should not discuss visions among themselves. Because they do not have such knowledge. It should only be communicated to the Guru. The Guru alone is the authority to listen to, correct, and elevate the vision.

December 1993

Through what did the existence of the world come about? The truth will be shown only after the complete form of untruth has been shown. One can ask the subtle Guru or the functional Guru through word, deed, and intellect. The Guru can change the residual karma only by making the disciple perform actions repeatedly. A vision is beyond thought and intellect. It should not be doubted. In this world, whether it is great men or other living beings, no two are alike. That is the glory of God. Experience (realization) is glory. Acting from a place of experience must be done carefully. Acting where there is no experience will not yield meaning. There is no question of stirring up baseness there. But where there is experience, the baseness within us will be stirred up without our knowledge. If one is experienced, one will quarrel with the Guru. If one is inexperienced, one should not get confused.

It will only go away. There are so many examples of this here. If this is the case, one might feel that it is better not to act. Even if it is out of madness, bringing devotion and advertising it should not be seen as a flaw. It should come like that. Life is the remainder of having taken a body many times. Not everyone will get this fortune. Even the things experienced by the greatest sages are obtained by children here. If devotion is carried forward with discipline for three months, then it is enough to carry it in the mind always. It is enough if children pray for a short time. Otherwise, the inclination will be lost. One cannot eat good things in large quantities, right? Can one eat ghee like tapioca?

Some people only see the silk dress on the outside. How should love be carried forward? There is a secret in spirituality that we do not know. If you want to take it, it will hurt. Foresight is something that has thousands of spiritual visions and spiritual paths. The path of devotion, the path of yoga, the path of karma, the path of dharma - this is the misfortune of this path that it cannot be recognized even in the Yugadharma Siddhanti through a god or a goddess to explain and understand this.

It is like what happened during the time of Rama or Ravana, and after that, what was the direction of the vision that came and went many ages ago, it is of no use to say slowly that the multitude of people who do not even have the knowledge to correct it will not understand anything.

But there is an easy way. We need to take the great ones as examples to know and cross the steps of a great Yugadharma, which is not known how long karma and dharma have been flowing astray, by looking at the course of karma from Svayambhuva Manu, who got the foresight for Manutaramkalpas, to Vaivasvata Manu, and by looking at what a god, a sage, an ascetic, a wise man, and a devotee are.

Let us take the shortest time. The time we mean is Satyayuga, Tretayuga, Dwapara, and Kali. How many thousands of years is the foresight of a wise man - called wise men or karmis - who turns a chatur yuga into a karma creation with these four times! How many such wise men make up the length of an age! Who corrects the dharma of that age? Then how great will be the pain experienced when entering the inner chamber of spirituality? Here, the big pillars of the fateful degradation caused by past karmas are being untied. In that place, the good is being practiced and made into our lives. It must be done without any mistakes. Then one will wander with many difficulties. If you pray with the house, you will wander with the house.

Devotion has the nature of getting agitated quickly. If one gets very used to sattvic, then one will act knowingly. One who has not got used to it like that will get agitated like a brute. Such agitation will lead to ruin. The character that should come over a long period of time comes all at once. Whatever good thing is done, there will be a difficulty at first.

Pray for five minutes while working for punctuality. Then always fix it in the mind. When this happens, we will be played with bad things. Disease will also come. Similarly, sorrow will come along with the vision. Whoever we see, the sorrow they experienced will come to us too. The vision should not be told for everyone to hear. If you do good, an opposition to that good will arise. When doing good, foolishness will come. When doing something to prevent foolishness, one will also get confused. Any trick might be used here. Thinking that this is the result of serving, an opportunity should be given to correct it. When unbearable pain comes, one should not back away from there but stand firm with diplomacy. If you back away, everything is gone.

For example, how did R. Shankar become among the community? How did Mannam become? Mannam stood firm until the end. What is the reason for being able to stand firm? He has a diplomacy among the common people. Shankar is not like that. He only thinks about big things. He doesn't get things done either. He doesn't say things lovingly. Humility is very less. Administrative policy

Because it was not there, Shankar applied statecraft. Statecraft alone cannot correct the one at the bottom. The mind must be recognized directly. Ezhavas and Nairs have the same structure. These poor people think that God is tied to the string of fear.

With a face made of silver, burnt rice flour and charcoal, Bhadrakali's dress, headgear and sword. One gets scared just by seeing this. We have none of that here. January 1994

It is difficult to determine when Hinduism originated. Hinduism has imparted all rituals to us. The ritualist, the idea of the ritualist; the yogi, the idea of the yogi; the jnani, the idea of the jnani. Thus, there are no ideas that have not been imparted to us. But all these ideas were not handled properly with the required knowledge and experience. Which of these to accept, which to reject - you (householders) do not know this. Vedanta became everything to you. Vedic rituals gained the most respectable position. With the entry of Aryans, their women could perform Vedic rituals. Dravidians were kept away from it. After that, Brahmins introduced an addition - the sacred thread (Poonool). This sacred thread system came about five thousand years ago. Thus, a situation arose where it was not known what is right and what needs to be corrected.

Now, the word sannyasi evokes contempt. If something that was good for a long time becomes corrupt, it happens like that. Recently, many sannyasis came to Kerala - Narayana Guru, Chattampi Swami. They were despised by their contemporaries. But when they showed their spiritual powers, people had great

Significantly. Some will say it is an illusion. It is not an illusion. At times, the power will manifest itself. I have such an experience.

The time when we were preparing to build an ashram in Kallutti. Bharathammavan was sent there a month and a half ago. When there were fifteen days left, I also went there. When I went there, no work had been done there. There is no water. There is nothing in that area, water. Can water be brought from the river to the top of that hill? I felt a little sad. How will this be done. I just took a bucket and put it in the well. There is no water. I put the bucket and came back. After fifteen minutes, a child came running and said that water had come into the well. Sathyan came after that. After that Bharathammavan came and fell at my feet. In an hour, the well was full of water.

Now another incident. Bharathan and four others from Vakkom came to Varkala to see me. I am not there. The neighbors said that I had not been there for two days. When they were sitting on the rock feeling sad, they saw me coming up after taking a bath with soap and a towel.

This is not a power. It happens automatically when a stage is over. Sree Narayana Guru coming out of the anthill is a power. Now its time has passed a lot. It is not that ghosts, yakshas, gandharvas, and kinnaras do not exist. If these are properly practiced systematically for twelve years, power can be shown. But the life of the one who shows this power will stand under it. Near the old kings, sanyasis did some medicines and showed power. Therefore, the kings controlled their food. Gradually eating leaves, then reducing that too, it will reach a stage of eating even nux vomica seeds. After that, there is a gulakalpaseva with navapashanas. After consuming this, one will reach a stage where one can sit in any snow without water and food. Their body will glow at night. It is believed that this is divinity. If this is called divinity, what is the story. But, because of them, poor sanyasis have no salvation. But there is one thing, Narayana Guru and Chattampi Swami all died of illness. There is a Neelambaswami, a disciple of a Manikandaswami near Nilamel. He also shows some tricks. Pambuswami also shows power like this. None of this is divinity, it is a skill.

Yoga is a much higher system than all these. There are many skills called gaunamani, stambhanamani, etc. There are also swamis who appear by getting on the back of many people. None of this is sanyasa. What we need is whatever is needed for peace of mind. There are some things that God can give us. We will also have the same age as God. The child born yesterday will be older than us. You can see all this yourself. You can see in which animals this life has been born before. That is why Hindus believe in reincarnation.

The character of past lives has been described in the history of human beings. Women who believe here, who have no inclination, can see their past lives. It is not because they worked for it, but because of God's mercy. It has also been convinced how to be in the remaining life. That is part of God's mercy. Therefore, it is a blessing to be able to participate in the sankalpas and prayers going on here for your peace of mind and to be able to spend anything for it.

For some time now, the things told by God have been done at various times. In nineteen seventy-three (1973), the thing I desired for ages was fulfilled. It was informed from the light that I am happy from today. Following that, everything informed is being done. You know the happiness and sorrow experienced all this time. You also know that the announcements and the actions according to them are literally correct. There were many happinesses and many sorrows in it. But whenever there was any sorrow, even when there were big cases in its name, God protected without any dangers affecting this.

In Kallutti, a lotus with two thousand four hundred and forty-four (2444) petals was shown in a vision. All these petals fell off. All these petals represent the disciples. All these

It was informed that they are the ones who are meant to arrive. The meaning of this was understood through a message received after several years. These two thousand four hundred and forty-four (2444) people are gurus of the past. All these gurus will be born in this lineage. One of the many karmas we were made to do was for this purpose. God is granting us immense rewards by making us perform very small karmas. Along with these gurus, their millions of disciples are also yet to be born. All of them must be born before the end of this Kali Yuga. Because of what we have done for their birth, we hold the position of those gurus. And then, liberation. Another special provision is that the entire lineage (gotra) of those who create the tradition directly carrying this ideal will be purified. How many births would it take for you to complete this!

Only rarely in the solar system is a Guru born with the qualification to witness such karmas. That Guru will not have any unnatural reactions. However lowly a person who approaches him may be, he is with them; if an exalted person comes, he is with them; he will not even hate a scoundrel. Since multiplicity arose from oneness, nothing is forbidden to such a Guru.

God stands with compassion to impart this glory to the householders. You must be prepared to do this with capability and deep sincerity. Whatever we do, it must be in a way that causes no pain to anyone. One must do anything to protect one's own life - if you realize you cannot escape, you must kill. Krishna himself has done this. If someone attempts to kill us, we must carefully evade them. But if they are adamant about killing, we must have the inner strength to strike back.

Everything should be in moderation. Your interactions with anyone should be moderate. You must always maintain the awareness and vigilance that you are performing these glorious karmas. There must be a firm resolve to behave at home only with a sense of unity.

Guruvani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · February 1994 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0614 to GV2:P0640
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

February 1994

Among us, people have a certain perception about the ashram. That it is meant for acquiring occult powers, or that it is a place of fake godmen, or for the accomplishment of an individual's selfish motives, or established to enjoy life by engaging in smuggling or business

Or a place, and many such concepts. Whatever it is, an ashram means one has to follow a certain discipline. As far as a Hindu is concerned, there are three traditions: Shaivism, Shaktism, and Vaishnavism. But they cannot be distinguished from each other. Vaishnavites believe that they emanated from Brahman. Each sect believes the same way.

Through the establishment of the Chaturvarnya system, a lot of ignorance has spread. If we study the history of the last three thousand years, we will understand what kind of difficulties we have overcome to reach here. Caste is the most formidable barrier. In every home, there are people of four castes - Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra. Those who hold power are the Brahmin and the Kshatriya. The Shudra and the Vaishya are oppressed. As times changed, the Shudra also had to be given power. But the Brahmin and the Kshatriya refused to hand over power. With that, the situation became 'might is right'. There were many such kings. Those who claim to be scholars are still trying to do the same today.

Today's most important thought should be how to change the next generation by removing the flaws in the worship system. We must think about the problems in today's politics. Today's rulers are those who have not understood the essence of Indian culture. We must firmly believe that we cannot survive unless our culture and heritage grow and become stronger. When Westerners dressed according to their physical appearance, we blindly imitated them. Even great souls said this. But you refused to accept it. Our anxiety is to accept whatever Westerners show and say. Where were we born, where did we grow up, what is the glory of our nature? We are not ready to think about any of this. We are only investigating the easiest way to make wealth. What will become of the children of those who learn to make wealth like this? Is there any place for culture here? For you who live like this, what will you understand if someone says 'ashram'?

There are many reasons for the corruption of culture. The main one is worship. Towards the north, the deity is Chamundeshwari. What can we impart by worshipping Madan, Marutha, etc.? What is to be achieved by gods and goddesses who have no place in the Kali Yuga? If a Shudra can study the Vedas, why should there be a place for Chaturvarnya? If an Avarna can have the worship rituals of a Savarna, how can a marriage from a Shudra's house be a 'Sambandham' and a marriage from a Brahmin's house be a 'Veli'?

You all must have heard about Ayyappan. Who has Ayyappan been portrayed as! From where is the story told that Ayyappan split and fell? This Ayyappan was a person who lived like a sannyasi. He also had a little vision. He was a perfect 'Sadhu'. What should we understand if it is said that such a man went for tiger's milk for a woman who was immersed in worldly affairs and had a headache? He did penance with the concept of Dharmashastha. Thus Ayyappan's status became great. Today Ayyappan is called 'Hariharasuthanayyanayyappan'. This is the poet's deceit. If asked where Ayyappan came from, what all nonsense is he saying? You who have learned this do not know what an ashram is. What will you understand if someone says a temple or a bhajan mutt? The place where we worship each of our things is a bhajan mutt. The place where they are worshipped is a temple. There are many types of ashrams and Guru traditions. A Guru is not someone who shows miracles. What you need is to be brought through humility and led to fortune. This should grow through children. This is what an ashram is for.

We have a culture that led a very low standard of life. Because knowledge is very small, we have an arrogance that knowledge is power. The arrogance of 'I' is in all human beings. There are those who think, 'Oh, they criticized me'. They don't have the knowledge to think that it was good to be criticized. Ignorance is more in girls. There are shortcomings in the lowest and the highest, regardless of age. Where was I born, why was I born, grew up

These girls mostly grow up without any thought about me. They have a tendency to just drift along without doing anything or getting anything done. Everyone is very far from their duties. Every home has a tradition of living in ignorance for a long time. A tradition of eating and living without ever working. We are the children of this wretchedness. Caste-based struggles can be seen everywhere. No one knows how to think about how to raise girls other than to engage in such struggles.

If this habit is to change, a suitable government must come. For that to happen, one must pray with an aching heart that the ignorance that has come to reside amidst this human society be removed. Do this. It is very painful to think about you. Pray that our emotions do not flare up and ruin this place. Pray that these kinds of nonsense burn and perish, that all this perishes with this world. The first thing you should do is to beg God to give us a way to remove these shortcomings from our offspring and to give us the mind and action for it. You will change.

You have a lack of administrative experience. There are those who have perished by committing fraud in administration. All that can only be changed by the grace of God. If you keep a strict routine for prayer, there will be great results. You are people who cannot love each other by saying good words. No matter who they are or how good they are, you can see a stain deep inside them. You must pray that fraudulent thinking disappears from the world. What great men say does not easily slip away. Hearing Jesus' words, they waited for one thousand five hundred years for the kingdom of heaven to come. Work must be done swiftly. Otherwise, they will be confused in their actions. When doing something, you should think good thoughts and do it. Those who have done so will be seen with ten hands when they are born again. But there will be no such arrogance. Those with vision should imagine that a good way will be formed in everyone. If you imagine with a stain, the imagination will become bad. You must pray that if there are any flaws in what we and our lineage have done, please resolve them and join us to you.

In nineteen seventy-three (1973), there was an announcement that the thing I had desired for ages was completed today. The nature of that desire has been happening here for some time. An opportunity like the one Moses got was also received in India in that same proportion. But a somewhat subordinate worshipful nature that could not take that opportunity existed here earlier. According to Hindu scriptures, there are four yugas (Chaturyuga). There were acharyas as authorities for each Chaturyuga. Krishna came before the beginning of Kali Yuga. He removed the customs that existed until then. But it got buried in myths. Krishna was tied among the gods and goddesses. That should never have happened. Krishna is an avatar. Therefore, he said to discard what existed until then and worship him. Moses was not that pure a soul. When Moses was born, the subjects were slaves of the Pharaoh king. The Pharaoh knew that Moses would be born among these fallen people. Therefore, all the male children born were being killed. The background of Krishna's birth was also similar. The king killed all the male children. Moses' father and mother somehow hid and raised him. They prayed fervently. Then they set him adrift in the water. The Pharaoh king's daughter and her maids saw him, realized he was a slave child, had him picked up, and gave him back to his mother. She raised him until he was four years old and then gave him back to the palace. Moses was not given wisdom on any of these occasions. When he came of age, while hiding in a shepherd's house, he married an honest girl there according to the word he received. It was there that the baptism of wisdom took place. Krishna was a wise man from birth. Even after Krishna's time, although there were many gurus among us to correct things, you could not take it. India is the birthplace of many great men. Therefore, we do not need the Varna system and the Trinity system here. After nineteen seventy-three, we started getting everything completed here.

The Prophet had also received the message. But it was also said that it would be told only when requested. This prayer being conducted here is something that has been revealed.

It is the girls who have got the fortune to do this. Through this, we should be able to remove the evil that caused the downfall of women. What the Prophet said fifteen hundred years ago became true today because we were able to believe and act upon it. Similarly, what we say today might not be understood by today's generation. But God has a purpose in making us pray today. Everything that was revealed here has been preserved here. It cannot be revealed now. It can be revealed after this generation.

Guruvani March 1994 The foundational nature of all matters of our ashram has come through prayer. A state will come where the world will understand what our prayer is for. A ritual called 'Poortheekaranam' (Completion) was conducted twenty-seven times. Of the twenty-seven stars

Those completions were in character. In nineteen seventy-three, twenty-seven were completed. It was on the day the twenty-seventh was completed that the message 'what I desired has been completed' was received.

In this atmosphere, the atoms of perfect emotions are full. Therefore, men and women should handle things carefully here. Every word, every look, and every expression of yours will spread and linger in this atmosphere. It will never leave your life. This is a field where Dayananda Saraswati, Rishikesh Sivanandan, Sankaracharya, and everyone else failed. Then there is no need to talk about your case. If you can use your skills and abilities for this, it is a great fortune.

I love your work, not money. You need the determination and pain to realize that this is the result of a great sacrifice and to respect it by always sacrificing. You must maintain humility, and along with humility, care in words, and a pleading prayer, 'Father! I am doing something, making something happen, always make me do it according to your will.' This will only work if there is a dedicated attitude to dedicate life for this. If those who work today, even a child, carry this out without making a mistake, there is no doubt that their names will be recorded in the pages of history. It may take one or two generations for that.

This is a fortune your ancestors did not get. You have received that fortune. This is not for being enslaved to anything or anyone. The great sinful power of killing the Prophet's children and seizing power is the reason why looting and murder happen wherever Muslims take power. Therefore, your life and the mischief in your life must be untied and resolved here.

If you live a disciplined life, you will be able to understand that good and bad come intermingled. The instinctual bad lying at the bottom of life should come out, untie, and good should come in its place. Those who do more good things will get as much bad in delusion. If one is a seeker or a sattvic, they can stop it themselves.

Our experience is like getting a new earth. Before getting it, we don't know what it is. The word earth refers to the body. Many things are stored in the godown of the heart. It should be kept from spoiling. No matter how hard it is, you should be able to finish it. There should be a state where we do not need both merit and sin. If you earn merit, then you have to be born again to experience its result. If you encourage your mind, no one can defeat you. The men and women standing here are knowingly or unknowingly doing some good things. You cry when pain comes. Even through that crying, you should pray, 'May I be able to do your will.' We do not need siddhi. Siddhi is a specialty of time. It is enough if siddhi acts as a specialty of the respective time. Siddhi is not needed for this time. Krishna showed and stopped the full nature of siddhi. It is not allowed anymore. God is leading us to the Satya Yuga. For that, we need to do it there and take it here. Otherwise, you don't have to do anything for God. You have to do it with a broken heart and take the result of that pain. Instead of working and letting the mind go into mischief again and again. You should never go back even an inch after doing it. Everyone's good qualities should be modeled. There is something to learn even from a small creature. Learn from everything and maintain the determination that at least one person will improve through my work.

The greatest guru of a dull lineage will get ten male and ten female disciples. That will depend on the guru's luck. Of these, three will be excellent, seven will be medium, and the rest will only reach the light of God. They will not merge in the light of Brahman. No matter how many others there are in that dull lineage, they will only attain salvation in the next birth as members or servants of this guru lineage.

Then there is another group. Those who gain insight, rise up to some extent, and then degenerate by not doing anything. Another group consists of householders. They take everything in moderation, lead a family life, and ultimately attain liberation. Or they merge with the Guru. Both these groups should not harbor any doubts about the Guru. If ever such a doubt arises, they can attain liberation by confessing to the Guru that such a doubt occurred in the past and seeking a remedy. Even householders, if they do not err, can reach up to the state of the Trimurti. If they do err, depending on the magnitude of the mistake, they will be born for countless lifetimes, and at some point, by accepting the discipleship of an exalted Guru in this Guru lineage, they can attain liberation through that. For those who come into the fold of this Guru or Guru lineage and then reject it after some time, it is difficult to even get a human birth in subsequent lifetimes. Even if they do get one, they will have absolutely no wisdom. Later, by praying for knowledge, they will experience many lifetimes through some other lineage and eventually merge into the state of the Trimurti. Now, there is yet another group. Those who rise on their own to get close to the Guru and the lineage, reach a level where generally no one makes mistakes, but then fall into error due to jealousy or some other bad actions. The physical condition given to such people will be such that they cannot work in any way towards attaining God. Then, in one lifetime, they will have complete devotion and humility. From there, after being given many lifetimes, meeting many gurus and grieving with a weary mind, in one lifetime God will show mercy, grant a healthy birth again, and give liberation through that. That will be obtained only through severe sacrifice, after becoming exhausted from searching and reaching a helpless state. There is still another group. They, having been born across many lifetimes and ages, struggling and worshipping in the Shakta, Shaiva, and Vaishnava traditions, will in their final birth come under gurus who appear every two thousand or three thousand years, and thus attain elevation according to the arrival of those gurus. This is merely a brief description of a very detailed philosophical vision.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · April 1994 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0641 to GV2:P0657
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

April 1994

The result of the Pournami prayer is supremely excellent and cannot be replaced by anything else. Knowing the purity of intention across many births, it is on Pournami that one can act upon and fulfill it. The Pournami vow yields the benefits of all other vows combined. By observing all these vow benefits, one can become a Guru. It is also on Pournami that the Guru attains liberation. This is spiritual. For worldly matters too, Pournami is something that helps attain perfection in the same way. The specialty of Pournami is that darkness does not spread even a bit. On one side, there is joy and coolness. On the other side, the quality of being able to capture anything. Seeds acquire more quality in the coolness. The quality is capable of generating and sustaining it.

If one attains Samadhi after accumulating the essence of merit through countless births, that Samadhi has perfection. The atmosphere is calm on Pournami. There is no distance between sunrise and sunset. When one rises, the other sets. When one sets, the other rises. Wherever the rays of the sun and the moon fall, all the above-mentioned qualities enter. The Pournami vow helps the Guru to reach perfection as God and Brahman. Whether one thinks about the benefits of the Pournami prayer or not, one should imagine that all our sins are destroyed, good and evil are separated, and our prayer will yield benefits in a way that contributes to world peace, so that all moving and non-moving beings receive the benefit. The planets will achieve balance, and through that, a special power that contributes to the welfare of the world will emerge. Pournami is the most suitable day for creation and dissolution. The creation, moreover, will be beneficial to the whole world. Just as this state is envisioned in a noble person, it acts in humans as well. Although the spiritual and the material are mutually complementary, whatever we give priority to will grow. A mother who gives birth to a child on Pournami must be conscious. She must know that this is for the world and that it has been ordained by God in this way.

We have proceeded by respecting what already exists. When coming through the path of the ancestors, some obstacles arose. After that, a deviation was made. One might ask how this was obtained despite respecting what exists. It is difficult to answer that. We do not think about how those who came before were. But we adopted such a path. How this happened is a subject to be thought about. Sivananda called Hatha Yoga as Raja Yoga. But Raja Yoga is none of this. True Raja Yoga is falling into it unknowingly due to the good deeds of previous births. For example, Krishna. He did not reject anything. He solidified what existed until then and showed that he is none of this. Krishna acted with the desire that actions suitable for the times should come. What has been done so far has value. But he is one who stands stepping on it. Krishna is a perfect sage. It was as a result of reading the Gita that Karl Marx said to behead. But the essence of the soul was lost. The intellect of an ordinary person should not interfere in the things done by visionaries.

I have learned one thing from the great souls who have come and gone. Most of them have rejected the past paths. If we want to be able to see what the past is, we must come through the past itself. As far as we are concerned, we do not need the past. Because we got a new path. We got this through an incorporeal voice. It will only tell us to do this at that time. No one has been able to know everything from the beginning. It is this breath that redirects us at times. It redirects us through notifications. Mistakes are corrected in Kali Yuga. Then it will let us into Satya Yuga. What order can we give to this correction? It could only be done on a small scale. More is to be done from now on. I have said a little bit. Even so, I have been seen as a denier. A character for this has arisen from that imagination itself. From the words that the thing I had desired for ages has been fulfilled today, it will be understood that this is an imagination from earlier.

Now the question is how to take this forward. That is, the system of worship. Those who created each system of worship did it after asking and knowing how it should be. We also do the same. We have another special feature. This was given to us without us asking for it. I cannot do it myself. Those who ask for this, those who do it, and those who make others do it have a responsibility. That is why it is said that householders cannot do this. But householders who live like sages can. That householder must be a person who is determined to take it to a certain stage and then change.

How and who should implement this idea? I am not the one to implement this. Even if one person asks, many people know, and some people think about it, it can only be implemented according to the working capacity of the person who is going to implement it. Is it because they did not believe in Narayana Guru that the lineage became like this? Then how should the belief be?

This cannot be implemented by mere belief alone. What should be included in what is called belief? There is a need for an awareness about belief. We utter a word because it arises in the mind. Just saying it is not enough. It must be put into practice. Belief will end up in the eight egoisms. The feeling of 'I' will always stand out. Only the one who turns these eight egoisms into eight prosperities can put this into practice. We must imbibe the love and affection of one who is pure like this. You can work by standing together with his working capacity. Alami yaluma sadarthamallaham sa- chchidamythamennu thelinju dheeranayi sadasadithi prathipathiyattasantho- mithimyduvayi myduvayamarnnidenam Narayana Guru has said. Only one who acquires these eight prosperities, turns around, thinks deeply about what is needed, asks, and acts can do this. Anyway, the course of action for this must be pure.

What if we think we can't do it? The mind should not be tired. Predecessors have achieved many things like this. So humans can do this. For that, the mind is needed. There is a story for this. Two people are working for liberation. One person does all the actions he gets with a firm mind. He laughs and plays. But he does not think about the fruits of his actions. One person is a severe ascetic. He has no thought about anything else. When asked a monk about their eligibility for liberation, the ascetic still has one more birth. Knowing this, he beat his head and cried. When the other person came to know that he has as many births as there are leaves on a tamarind tree, no matter how many births pass

"I too have liberation," he said, clapping his hands in joy and laughing. It is said that he attained liberation right then. That is the firmness of the mind.

You have not yet attained the courage, righteousness, and compassion to achieve this. Those who have the vision will not enslave their own merit to another. They will not do anything devoid of humility. They will not let firmness bring about arrogance. No matter who they see, their mind will not wander towards them. No matter who comes, they will not be subservient to them. They will maintain their merit in all fields. Even if one acquires the eight siddhis, one must be able to maintain honesty. For that, determination, dispassion, richness of humility, courage, and happiness are all needed. One should not feel hesitant or inferior in front of anyone. One must have the firm conviction that "I will achieve this." Disregarding honor and dishonor, one must stand firm as a seeker of truth.

For those with vision, their qualification is nothing else; they will only have the position of a watchman of a public property. An owner-like caretaker! Only if we have the firm conviction of why we have come here, can we do all this. Whatever you do, there must be a resolution to it. Pray for a few days that the discipline, order, and method for this should be instructed from the Divine itself. If mere trickery has this much discipline, then surely it will be instructed from this which has come as ordained by destiny. Once received, at least ten people should attain the completion of action with strict observance. Everyone should implement this with perfection.

Guruvani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · May 1994 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0658 to GV2:P0674
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

May 1994

Jesus did not implement his own doctrine. Even though they were silent, what sacrifices the followers made to implement Jesus' doctrine! Their sacrifice became a model for generations. In that regard, what have you been able to do? Respect each person only according to their humility and merit, and then let it go. Maintaining honesty in humility, gaining courage, and without becoming anyone's slave, only then should each of you live here. Without this firmness, do not handle a single thing here. If you can get the word, ask and learn, and complete the task, that is the greatest thing. The habit of confessing in one's own words and closing off must be discarded. It is bad. It brings far-reaching harm. It only has the effect of rubbing a burning coal in Kashi. What Jesus and the Prophet achieved was through the sacrifice of their followers. Shankaracharya also had such a situation. But the followers could not step down from their position and work freely. A mistake happened there.

Madhva's circle is large. Madhva worked by stepping down more than Shankaracharya. Shankaracharya handled things with scholarship and lost. This should be a lesson for your practice. Buddha or other great men like him did not come knowing who they were themselves. It happened from God's will. Buddha thought about the three things he saw. Seeing that Buddha would go into an ascetic nature, his father tried to avoid the circumstances. Since it was God's will, that attempt did not succeed.

It is in India itself that the beginning of all spiritual subjects lies. Along with this truth, you should also think about the special features of the times. We must act according to the times. Even if we are only ten people, there is a lot to do in this country where there is a law to pamper the thief. There must be action to overcome that law. How should that action be absorbed? When the part of pain for that comes, what will you do? When distinguishing right and wrong, there must be enough sacrifice to correct the wrongdoer without killing him. This is only possible if it hurts.

How does it hurt? The way of life must be disciplined. There should be no sense of failure. One must believe and obey blindly. Give blood and sweat, and if necessary, even life, to grow the movement. Who is there who does not call upon God when a natural disaster strikes? Is that what faith is? Then what is faith?

Unless you hold from the outside, it won't be clear inside, right? For that, patience is needed. Patience is the first part of faith. We ourselves must experience all our hardships. If you believe in the Guru, it must be through the greatness of the Guru and the disciple in the relationship between the Guru and the disciple. The example of Sri Ramakrishna and Vivekananda can be mentioned here. It was Vivekananda who brought Sri Ramakrishna to the outside world.

We have come from a new system. The lives of householders who believe in this should be a model for others. You and I are not together for belief - action must be based on this decision. If you are not in belief, I am not with you. If I am not in belief, you are not with me either; even if it is husband and wife. There is no need to look at whether the wife or the husband leaves through belief. My belief is what is great to me.

The prayerful dedication of the disciples - only through that can it be achieved, and it must be achieved. It must become the eternal possibility of human culture. If I do not err, the Guru will not err. If the Guru does not err, God's will will be done. The Guru and the disciple are the firm bond of this settlement. Essential things must be requested and obtained from God by the Guru and the disciple together. The sacrifice needed is to bring anyone available closer to the movement and make them work. God can do anything. If a word can be given from the sky, what can God not show.

Siddhi comes by itself. Siddhi has happened to me by itself three or four times. It happened like this in Varkala, Kayikkara, and Kallara. But that is not what we need. We should not stop there. We should have everything through God's mercy itself. You should pray that whatever is needed for this lineage to survive until the end, should happen through Your complete mercy. We ourselves should discard past mistakes. May God provide the way to discard them. We can do it that way.

The kind of independent thinking that exists in the nation today came about because of British rule. We unknowingly got caught up in the artistic flair of the Muslims. It is proof that we did not even have anything on our side to impart a unique artistic flair. That flair was not imparted in us. The flair of our country will evolve only through our lineage. It will not happen suddenly. Those in this lineage today are higher than the positions of their parents. You must have the determination to grow to heights. The previous generations could only see and hear what we have developed today. They could not develop timely functional capacity from it. They could not think about past values. But we can do it today. Science says that the great must stand near the great. If the great does not stand near the great, the great will perish. Our heritage itself must grow for our home and our country. Not the heritage of the West. There must always be a Mahatma in a country.

We must create good flairs for ourselves. For that, we ourselves must make an effort. If a person who has no place to stay raises his children within ten cents, it has its own merit. One must understand about that merit through experience. What good is there for the people, what good is there for the land, what good is there for the father and mother, what harm is there - one must be able to discern all this. The unique flair and heritage mentioned earlier - these parts must be known and developed from the Guruvadams. God has given us the opportunity to reclaim those lost parts. If it is not done properly, it will be lost again. It must be developed as a support and shade for God's will. If it is not developed like that, or if an attempt is made to develop it by holding on to worldly justice, God will not give it again. Murders and arson happen in the Islamic religion all the time because a culture suited to the times is not developed. The children of householders who believe here must be guided in this flair. In this generation, there is a difference like light and dark between those who are connected to this and those who are not.

Among Harijan children, those with high culture and those without are not the same. People from Nairs downwards have not been able to advance here. They are like moss on the surface of the water. Because they were born in homes without flair and grew up without action, they could not improve our culture. That is the reason for the universal disease in them. They are not willing to let go of the flair, and they cannot disagree with the truth either. That is why there is pain. Because of this kind of conflict

What a struggle it is to uplift a heated and ruined society! Only one who can love can be held within this. Don't this pain and the tendencies that generate pain all converge into one? If all these crash and shatter against a great power, everyone's actions will attain excellence. If one does not rise above base instincts, they will fall into the pit of despair.

Every man and woman here is the seed of a lineage. That determination should be present in every stage of their growth. If one can live solely aiming for the growth of the movement according to the Guru's will, it is God's will that is fulfilled through them. How blessed is that! You do not have to struggle at all to develop good tendencies suitable for that. You only need to obey the Guru's word. When you obey the word, one of the tendencies will untie. In its place, a new one will form. Along with this, there should be another determination: how much value and weight does the work I do have? I will not enslave it to anyone else. Our strong dignity blossoms in this way.

Humility and competence constitute dignity. A Brahmachari should be the symbol of this dignity. What do you do if a burning coal falls from the stove? You immediately pick it up and throw it back. Similarly, you must respond immediately to what you see. There should never be a lack of courage in that matter. If diplomats come near such people, it will be like coming near a block of ice. What if they fall among these hypocrites? They will have no way out.

The gain from the life here is very great. To achieve that gain, one must live forgetting caste and religion. Even if your beauty is lost, wealth is lost, or you have a disease, life will not be destroyed. It is enough to have courage. If there is courage, there is a blessed life. The gain achieved in this way is very great.

If we look back from the time of the Ettuveetil Pillamar a hundred years ago to the British rule, we can see that there are many things left unachieved by us. We must achieve all of that. It is a matter involving that much work. Our field of action is meant to accomplish that. One person must do the work of a thousand people; the work we have to do is that immense. If you work like that for one year, you will get the result of working for a thousand years. Within fifty years, the actions and thoughts of you poor people will become subjects of research in universities. For that, your humility and competence must rise to that level.

GURUVANIKAL · June 1994 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0675 to GV2:P0696
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

June 1994

When I started this** here, I got a message; you should think about your life before starting this. I didn't understand what it meant then. I asked my Guru, Padaniswami. He said; I have only received two things. You will get the rest. One, it is not enough to seek only the spiritual. Two, you will have to change your ascetic garb.

Here, under the name of completion, a prayer and ritual were performed twenty-seven times. The twenty-seventh time, on the seventh day after the prayer started, I was told to place a Kumbham (pot). The first intimation about this was received two years prior to that. I also received a message that its efficacy would increase as the years go by. For up to four years, there was no ability to do this. The next time, it was done on a small scale. This has shown us a nature that works and flows for humanity, from the five elements, through the five senses, and through the five sheaths. It is being done with a systematic change from what has been happening to date. This is not something everyone gets.

Every religious nature has had something like a vision or prophecy. Siddhi is not knowledge. This is a path where Siddhi matters cannot be handled at all. Those with me have insisted that I show some things. If I ask Brahman whether to do it, it will say to do it. But after a while, it will say it is not good. Thus, it has to be stopped there.

Detailed information about placing the Kumbham was received recently. It should be done with the determination that our lineage must have the strong ability to carry this. Among Hindus, the Trinity worship system is strongly prevalent. There was a time when this was indispensable. When it was hidden under the Varnasrama Dharma, it failed to take root in the soil of Kerala. It was with the advent of British rule that Varnasrama Dharma suffered a setback and waned. This has not completely changed even today. Political scientists have kept this from being known outside. Although independence was achieved through that, good things have not been touched. That will now only be passed on through children with aptitude. It should be understood thoroughly and taught to the land and the children. The impurity of the land's ways is hidden in the people.

Each worship showed the past Acharyas and their deeds. From the early days of Kali, Gurus were born for this purpose. The twenty-seven resolves mentioned earlier were performed to give birth to children free from baseness at a certain stage of Kali. After twenty-seven rounds, it reached inside. If I were to understand it directly myself, arrogance would arise. Therefore, I was not informed.

Gross, subtle, effect, cause - nothing happens without these. Those who should become the most qualified are householders. They have become bad today. Whether truth exists or not is becoming evident in householders. Those here are able to understand this completely. Those who have sight also get the word. It is because they lack aptitude that they cannot do this with determination. Determination will only come to Brahmacharins.

Although it was said years ago that Karma should be placed, it was not done due to lack of circumstances. This can be done as an offering for your incurable diseases, and those who are mentally broken can also do this. This can also be done to change bad character. In addition, this can be done for the fulfillment of desires. We have received that whatever we think, it will have an effect.

Something like this is something that happens occasionally in the world across epochs. If there is ever a mistake in this, you will definitely get a chance to correct it. It will provide you with a virtue that will never end in any era and an earning that will never be destroyed in any era. You should bravely take each one, saying let whatever comes come, and let whatever has to go go. We have seen, experienced, and believed each of these. All this is for you. You are the ones who have to grow and maintain this in an organizational nature.

Question:

What is the reason for a repetitive nature to occur for the four Yugas?

Guru:

The very reason for this question is because the tradition in Hinduism has become old in countless ways. Sanatana Dharma means the culture of the great souls that existed in those times. It is explained in history that the culture that came and went as Shaktheya, Shaiva, and Vaishnava tried to improve man to a certain extent. Meanwhile, the way some Acharyas have come following the lineage of Manu is also seen. But those who have come like that are not in the lineage of the first Manu. In the lineage of Vaivasvata Manu, the first Manu of the current Manvantara - to be precise, because some flaws that happened to the Acharya named Sathyathranan in the third Chaturyuga went into selfishness, the rule according to God's will did not happen. Sathyathranan's was like a theory of evolution. Therefore, in order to implement the Brahma's will, the Trimurti system was brought

in the seventh Chaturyuga.

Question:

Was there no Trimurti system before that?

Guru:

No. The Manus are the ones who fulfill the ideas according to the Brahma's will about the growth and decline of the world for a period of time. The origin, existence, and dissolution of this world have been created with such fourteen Manvantaras. The nature of the soul according to the Brahma's will has come like that.

Question:

What is the reason why most people do not know that there is such a lineage of Manus?

Guru:

As said before, the feeling of 'I' (the feeling of Aham Brahmasmi) came to the lineage of Manus. In that ego, they happened to see in a state of knowledge that all the knowledge activities that have existed in this world till then have come through them. Even if you take and look at the Vedanta books, you can see this feeling of 'I' standing out. Even if you talk to any ordinary person, you can see this independent feeling of 'I' (my party, my family) standing out. When it comes to the term Brahmachari, such feelings became through the lineage of Gurus. In the lineage of Devas, the Deva I caught or the Devi I caught has more ability (glory). As a result of this coming in everyone, Chamundi, Yakshi, Pei, Marutha, Madan, and Mantramurthi all became lords due to their own selfishness.

Question:

Why did this happen like this?

Guru:

As said earlier, when the Brahma's will did not happen due to the ups and downs in the lineage of Manus, the Trimurti system was created according to the command of that Brahma power. In this Trimurti system, what Vedanta is became non-existent. Its nature became pooja, prayer, and manifestation. When that nature also changed and came to the pride of having Marutha inside and whether to show a test, mankind became unable to fully see the glory of God or to carefully know or study its measure or centers of knowledge.

Question:

If you say like that, won't it mean that the things that have happened so far are not right?

Guru: Yes, that it is not right is what has been said so far.

Question: You yourself said that the Trinity system came into being to implement the Brahmic resolve because the Manu lineage made a mistake!

Guru: The Varnasrama was introduced in such a way that the people who worshipped in the Trinity system could not change according to the time and eligibility, and in that caste and sub-caste were introduced. Prior to that, myths were created through the study of Sruti and Smriti and made into authorities. Before that, empirical arguments came and some sannyasins themselves handled the Siddhi systems. When it reached this extent, the Manu system faded even from memories. This present degradation is the result of not changing the systems that needed to be changed according to the change of time and the futile idea that the Brahma position should be in the hands of the Deva Brahmins at any time.
