GURUVANIKAL · July 1994 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0697 to GV2:P0750
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

July 1994

There may be some shortcomings in those who come and go daily and those who stay permanently in the ashram. The reason for that is - when you get close, some subtle aspects may not be understood. People of many natures will come to show off by showing and doing many things. What if the people here stand like an oracle with a drawn sword in front of them? Those who handle more devotion and those who worship without knowledge will make such mistakes. Some ignorant people traditionally worship with the attitude that I must achieve this, otherwise I will throw you away. It is not his devotion, but his ignorance that is the fault. You cannot say that his (Ezhava's) devotion is not right. As far as Nairs are concerned, even if they make fun of worshipping God, they will not say it bluntly. Because they are accustomed to being humble.

Why do we come to the ashram? What should be done as householders? Even if those standing here are ignorant, those who come should respect them. Because isn't he working from your center of faith? If you give respect, he will do it a little more straight. Gradually a goodness will arise through that mutual love.

This should not be seen as a normal ashram. Because there is a special experience here. The good and the bad all see this with perfect love. The great man will talk about its greatness. The bad man will at least say that there is no discrimination there. You should behave with the middle class in a way that is neither. The people here should behave in a way that does not offend these three types of people.

Since it is a place of experience, priority should be given to experience. Even if they have failed and suffered losses through their actions, they can go somewhere and succeed. If strictly implemented, it will only become like a normal school. It means less sacrifice. When you have to catch and tie a madman, you should do so. Since the common man is struggling for money, special attention should be paid to him. A familiarity that allows working closely is what needs to be developed.

Why is it said that words should be guarded? Jesus only implemented ten words, right? Only if the word is guarded in the same way can it be implemented. If there is a mistake in action, it can be corrected by action.

If a word is spoken wrong, it cannot be corrected. Many mistakes have occurred here because words were taken lightly and handled without thought. Through words, much prosperity and fortune can be earned. You have a lack of experience in this. If a mistake is made in a small matter, do not use big words.

When devotion arises, what takes hold first is demonic nature. How would it be if an ignorant person loves? They will give up to the ocean. What if they oppose? They will pluck the ocean out. One who loves moderately will only stand moderately even if they see a severe mistake. Women possessed by Yakshiyamma will speak with clenched teeth. Yakshiyamma's nature will blend into her and she will become like a demon. When she dies, Yakshiyamma will become a demon. Then she will never rise from that. How would it be if gods and goddesses are worshipped? If gods and goddesses feel love, even the words spoken will come true. A demon does not have that much deceit. Those who worship gods and goddesses will make a mistake. Whatever they think will be accomplished. Immediately, he will attain arrogance. Rising a little higher, when he starts to climb to that position, he will also go wrong. Either he will go mad, or he will become a fool. If you hold onto a god, you should not worship a guru. There is a church that sacrifices roosters. There are many such things in temples too.

How will a guru behave? They will not let you near any of these unwanted things. Whatever you do, there is a benefit. But the guru will not even show it. To one who comes like this, coming very strictly, they will trouble in every way. If you want to improve, improve, or else go - that is the meaning of it. Although they show love, they will also show this. Some gurus will destroy your pride. Even then, you should stand firm. Later, they will reveal the flaws in that pride which is an insult. This experience of the guru should be useful to you.

The goddess has a quality. She will pull you close and create emotion. If his karma does not yield results in the way the goddess intends, she will trouble him. Finally, she will turn him into an emotional creature. If there is also a vision, such a situation will definitely arise. This will continue until the status of the god and goddess.

Those who come to the ashram should pay special attention to one thing. Do not tell those standing here about the things seen on the way. Because, while doing big things, getting caught up in small things will destroy the good. The lack of experience without attaining humility is the reason for all this. There are some intelligent people. They will also insert their intelligence along with what they believe. We should take this forward without getting caught in any of this. By embracing this idea through sacrifice, we can move forward through virtue. You cannot move forward if you hang onto the ends of small words. You should discard the knowledge that ties up knowledge and take the humbled knowledge.

With what thought do you go to a house, temple, or ashram? When going to a temple three or four times, some people will get the thought of going on a pilgrimage. When going many times like that, the thought will come to go to an ashram and stay there. When it becomes many times like that, an ashram experience itself might come. When it becomes like that, when some experiences come, some trivialities will arise. If you live in an ashram for twenty-five years, you should not only study for twenty-five years, but also give what you have studied. If the receiver is not ready for it, then how will you give it?

When you stay here, you should pay attention to those who come and go. Some will come as rogues. Some will come with antics. How will you teach them? Those lower than us, those higher, the sattvic, the rajasic, everyone will come. Some will do what they have to do with the time they get. Some will stand like a potato. What if you study and do this as a guru lineage? You will be able to keep your own model.

A sannyasi who had lived an ashram life for twenty-five years and reached maturity came here. He is a person standing in the Trimurti tradition. Having reached three steps (status), he also knows how to elaborate on what is up to there. Those who come like this without a guru can see more than those who have attained through a guru. Because the nature is a mixture of rajasic and tamasic, humility was lost. What can we learn from that person?

In the Trimurti system, Rama stands supreme. At the beginning of Kali Yuga, Krishna came. Even though Krishna is far ahead, in North India today, Rama and Durga are worshipped. What should be known through temples and what should be known through ashrams cannot be known today. A person who has gone to a temple for many days and done pilgrimage will find it difficult to leave the ashram. A person who comes to the ashram without this familiarity can only find faults.

The story has been taught that monks should eat only green leaves and water. But if it is to be useful to man, that is not enough. Vasistha, Vyasa, and Bhoga are the ones who have contributed something to us. They all lived only for society.

Before going to the ashram, one must think about why one is going. Otherwise, there is no use in going. The movement is formed to make this understood. There are no humans without love for God or some other kind of love. Whatever kind of love it is, isn't it sattvic? Devotion comes when the feeling of love increases and reaches its peak. When love for God comes, other loves will leave. Everyone has love in different measures. When one maintains a love in the guru parampara, one can study each and everyone. Even one who has completed a pilgrimage as mentioned earlier will be convinced about this only if he gets many ashram experiences. What is gained in a hundred lives can be gained in a single life with ashram experience. One can be convinced in ten years whether what one thought was right or wrong. Thus, a lot of knowledge can be gained through ashram life. One can think deeply and know about past stories. Then, will one enjoy hearing the story of Ghatotkacha?

Familiarity with the ashram over time is necessary. No matter which community one is born in or which field one works in, this can only be achieved through the age of the soul. There should be a thought about what an ashram is from the beginning. If there is such a thought, no one can just stand idle in the ashram. The ashram has everything needed for continuous work. One should increase familiarity by doing it at home, in the country, and in the ashram.

That . . . 'effort' (shramam) is the ashram. We do not know that what we do many times stays in our soul. The subject being done must be completed. One will not feel respect even for a monk who has not completed it in that way. Rajas and tamas will be prominent in him. Even if one feels respect for such people at first, one will gradually realize that there is a mistake. How many such people have come and gone from here. Not a single monk who is respected from beginning to end has come and stayed here. It takes time to study a monk. The same goes for a brahmachari. No matter how much penance or effort is made, if one is great by oneself, no one will feel disrespect towards him. Such people are very few. If the father and mother are not capable of experiencing, the children will not be good. The ashram is a combination of all these aspects.

If a strong person speaks, any scholar will have to yield. We have seen everything we need. In the ashram system, not only the perfection of sannyasa, but everything can be seen.

Birthday Message

September 1994

I have spoken as a message on many birthdays. If you take them one by one and look at them together, the goal of birth should be to obey the will of God, who is the owner of all the people who are born and die in the course of ages, not just the virtuous souls for a long time. It is a good thing to know the right and wrong that has come and gone in the world so far, whether physical or spiritual, and to know the reason for that right and wrong. We all know that if there is a right, there will be a wrong along with it. Very intelligent and highly venerable, and whatever human ways have been passed down in between, all of them are born entitled to a way of action. Our doctrine is written as a doctrine that does not hurt grass, worms, and ants. Moreover, gurus of some lineages walk around with their mouths tied in such a way that the air going in and out should be controlled while speaking. In principle, all this is true. How will the karma that runs around with human life be completed by these methods? Or how will men and women, young and old, realize the field that is believed as a guru lineage? We have reached a meaningless action today in pretending to be a scholar or an ignoramus by talking too much about the greatness or smallness of a guru or guidance in the action that is spoken in pairs, the intellect that speaks without losing dignity, and the intellect that does not distinguish between honor and insult.

The doctrine that shows compassion to life or living beings is the Hindu or Sanatana aspect. A philosophy that has been controlled for many crores of years is contained in religious doctrines. Does it mean that it is complete if a great scholar writes a sloka by bringing it to the nature of vowels and consonants through literal interpretation and extending it to infinity where it is impossible to distinguish between what exists and what does not? Like that, we have an ignorance that has formed doctrines by studying, teaching, knowing, and not knowing for a long time. Although we can go like that if we remember the meaning of any religious doctrine, do we think that all the meanings that have been brought down to us from our ancestors who imagined and acted upon a devil or each form and name that has come and gone so far have been completed by these fragments of Vedanta? Or, many historians have come and written for ages, great scholars and teachers, not only that, Siddhantakaumudi, Laghusiddhantakaumudi, not only that, if it is said that only the good should be taken and the wrong should be discarded in the action where the right and wrong copied for many lakhs of years are mixed, what will be used to discard it? What will be used to take it? Such a habit of thinking has become necessary and essential for all of us. Today's subject before us is many historical truths lost in search of the ultimate truth of many doctrines considered to be the meaning of this world. We look at that historical truth to the visionary who says everything is karma and the visionary who says there is no karma at all. From which side are they speaking? Both have spoken truly in two ways. In what way did this karma come into being, in what way did it cease to exist?

We all know that if there is karma, there will be a cause and an effect in it. Many people - many great men - have tried their whole lives to find the glory of the time we were born, which stands intertwined without establishing whether it exists or not. Should we understand that all investigations are complete if we read or write up to the encyclopedia to establish that everything in the world exists, as these philosophers have strived for us for many ages?

We know a truth. I, mine, my father, mother, children, wife, and husband - thus, all the things we mentioned earlier are about measuring the good and bad of everyone who is mine. Should we believe that if we all applaud and pass the best word among those noted, and take the goodness of that word and go around babbling about it, we have transformed the knowledge and familiar karma and dharma of dharma, karma, knowledge, and wisdom, and returned its pros and cons?

But experience has proved to this day that it is not like this at all. The goal of everything of the truth that starts by saying I am telling the truth, I saw the truth - of each person - is their own truth or their own life. It is the possession of a great broad mind, the goal of a long time. What have we been able to learn and know as good and bad that we have studied so far? For all those who think, it is the truth. Thus, the yoga, karma, knowledge, and devotion formed by the truth told with or without detailed knowledge about anything, in many ways, as long as man exists, all that is truth. If we speak words of purity and melting heart about that truth, whether there is anything more to come of truth and untruth or not, how can we reach it by giving karma and dharma for ages?

Can we complete the truth of experience that originated and was experienced from such thoughts by voicing it with words like this? What have we been able to do so far other than establishing it on the side of saying good and bad? This has emerged before us as a big subject. Without any difference in religious doctrine, emerging as good and bad, ruling and making the world rule, from man to god. What should we understand that we have completed with a life that bargains with the dharma and karma of the special rule seen by respecting and making others respect the course of karma that has come through the concept of god and goddess, or the prophetic tradition and guru tradition of the respective religion, writing stories, playing and making others play, and using words that can reach the path of salvation if necessary, and putting it into practice?

Our goal is to complete by passing on the qualifications of the respective times of the fourteen Manus known as the Manu lineage. That is, evaluating the karma and dharma told about Manu and karma in the path of karma called Swayambhu, each Kali Yuga has been brought as the nature of the end of each Chaturyuga (Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, Dwapara Yuga, Kali Yuga). The reason for bringing Kali to an end like that is to carefully know what Kali dharma is, having reached the light of time as a human being, grass, flower, plant, and medicinal plants. If we think about how we have passed through the Manu lineage, the Trimurti tradition, the Vedic religion that emerged from it, and the Varnasrama dharma, we have respected and worshipped the truth passed on by the Vedanta religion by carefully studying the Vedas and epics to this day. It is impossible to count and say this. Therefore, Buddha reached the attainment of the world of Nirvana with the three refuges, thought for many ages, and helplessly showed the three refuges. Thus, Kali Yuga is ordained for us to reach at some point in a truth that we have been searching for for many ages. The truth that says 'Idam Shareeram Paropakarartham' (This body is for helping others) is the one where karma, dharma, and truth work together by taking and seeing the right and wrong of the other three times. Our doctrinal religion is the one that takes and sees such a nature of time and works it out.

According to the Manvantara calculation, it is known that a Manvantara consists of seventy-one Chaturyugas. Out of such fourteen Manvantaras, the twenty-eighth Chaturyuga of the seventh Manu (Vaivasvata Manu) is currently underway. The Trimurti system and the Sanatana principle system exist to identify where it went wrong and to act from there. The goal of Kali is to take everything that has been acquired and complete it. Instead, our Vedanta religion has only one God, only one Brahman, only one prophet, and only one Guru. This is where we have made a mistake. To correct this, for a long time, that determination of Brahman itself has acted as our support and shade. The reason for our destruction to this day is our inability to evaluate the respective times and merge into the action of completing it.

Anyone with some knowledge knows that it is the Kali Yuga that completes any state. The Guru Puja in Santhigiri is an action of its own. The six Manvantaras shown by God or Brahman and the first three Chaturyugas of the seventh Manvantara are true. Standing in this true action, working and maintaining the path of Dharma, the Manvantara is an imagined Dharma that has crossed over as Manvantaras up to the measure of the soul's merit and fortune. When followers of such a Manu come, they are called visionaries. In the same way, a visionary who continuously completes the Manvantara in the paths of the Guru has many followers in Guru-disciple relationships. In it, each Guru or each prophet completes the right and wrong in the Kali Yuga itself, counts each state in the special condition of God, purifies it, and attains the truth.

There is a visionary who has completed many Chaturyugas in this way. There is a visionary who has been divided into five Gurus for one Yuga. The fifth among them is a person who works here and there and moves from one to another. This fifth person is the one who transfers that Yuga to the next Yuga. The Guru who completes the Chaturyuga in this way is also a visionary. A Manvantara is a combination of many such Gurus. It is written that the completion of fourteen such Manvantaras is a Brahma Kalpita called a Kalpa. If the calculation is that a Guru who has found this much completes one day of Brahma, how many days and nights will the lifespan of this Brahma be?

In fact, a human being does not even need to think about such things. Because what can he think about this with the measure of his life? Or what can be thought about this with one life? But it is not like that, a human being should live, and there should be an action in life to study what he did until the end of his life. We do not know the secret that we all live to know the blessedness of the life experiences of visionaries, Gurus, and many thousands of Gurus in many ways, who have seen and said in the gross and subtle, in cause and effect, that many thousands of crores of human beings have lived and performed actions, and a theoretical action exists here as good and bad. Therefore, even if we cannot explain and know any of this, if we do not know the truth of life that attains Dharma through an action sacrificed for us by someone who knows, we are a nothing that is not a nothing.

The number 'one' has value only when we start counting 'one'. Otherwise, 'one' will become nothing in the action called number. Similarly, the meaning of each of our lives is the counting of many lives. Even after saying this in this way, only one word has been said in many places.

Even when someone starts writing the life story of a person who has lived up to a hundred years, it becomes a system mixed with right and wrong. Then one might ask why this is being written. To understand a truth through this. Not only the many crores of souls that you and I have lived as, but the wealth of many crores of human beings, is the evolution of life. In that way

When it comes to whether God exists or not, or if God does not exist, then what is the point of us holding on to such a concept of God? Wearing the veil of knowledge and having reached the level of a world encyclopedia, if we are shown a picture of a large forest or a large mountain where truth, righteousness, and justice cannot be distinguished, can we understand the path of action that discovered everything in that forest—from the banyan tree to the hardwood trees in all parts, to the plant species, to the medicinal plants—and how and where to use them? We are moving forward in a way where we do not know how the guidelines were formed to identify them and recognize their qualities. For example, if we look at people born and raised in the homes of Ayurvedic physicians, they also know some form of medicine. The physician also knows how to pluck the root of a plant or something similar. We know nothing about the long period of time and the struggles involved in discovering this. Or we have not been able to respect and understand anything regarding that truth of life. Even someone who administers a single herb (ottamooli) or a raw herbal medicine becomes an owner of medical knowledge. That person has no realization about the pros and cons arising from this; the disease might be cured. However, it is not to say that we must walk backward to learn and realize about every object that has existed up to today. But when we study the concept of a theory of karma that has grown as mentioned above, in the context of saying 'building a fortress with memories beforehand so that time does not pass forward,' Narayana Guru has written it as a prayer fragment for children to read: 'Like the eye that remains still when one counts one by one and the counting ends with the ultimate truth' (like the eye of your mind). When we praise and understand the billions of theories of the guides whose thoughts have evolved in that manner, how can we know the mistakes that might occur?

How can we recognize the truth that has come and gone in the midst of stating a truth about a beginning and an end? How can we explain the essence of the word 'truth' to each of us who has grown up in front of a reality where we do not know where the end of the truth that begins with "This is the truth, the truth I have seen" lies, or where the destruction of that truth lies? There is only one 'word of truth' that completes it. Thus, everything is one. Taking the summary that the meaning of everything is one, when we try to recognize the truth or untruth that has spread across the world in the billions of knowledge such as the realization of showing a photograph of the forest mentioned earlier, if we talk about the facts, stories, and theories of science by discovering atoms and molecules through the advancement of various studies we have today to know the secret of the universe called truth or untruth, does it mean that we have completely understood the position of the knowledge that has spread and risen to this day? Even there, the truth of 'you and I' remains.

When saying this, we all know that our knowledge about the truth is limited only to the extent of each of our individual knowledge and the things we have counted and experienced. But aren't each of us standing within the calculations of the horizon, discovered and stated according to the precision of the eye that measures the sky, the sea, the backwaters, and the forest that is the world where numerous living beings have grown to this day? Similarly, for us who calculate the truth, there remains a truth where we do not know to whom, what, and how to explain and convince. That too is a truth itself.

Let us look at a theory of evolution that perceives such truths and untruths in this way. Oh! His dream or the dream I saw, or the waking state I experienced - or the path of discovery of the deep sleep state of 'I', the subtle, the cause, and the effect is what has been spoken about from then until the absolute (Akhandam). Thus, each of us is a vast repository of thousands of absolute states. Each of us has a nature that knows the gross, the subtle, and the absolute through the thought of God, by dividing it into gross, subtle, cause, and effect, which sees the absoluteness of each person in that manner, or sees the truth of life. He nothing

"I don't know, poor thing" - we only know a particular state of truth mixed with truth and untruth, completed by saying such words. We do not reach the action of the visionaries who say 'when the meaning ends after counting one by one'. Thus, we cannot complete anything in the place of a visionary. We cannot even complete ourselves. The completion of such a thing is our subject. When we reach that subject, each of our subjects wanders away from the imagined action like a sky-flower found by counting and touching each one. Previously, it was said that an atom could not be split. Now they have found that an atom can be split - atom, paramanu or the ultimate atom. Because we have reached such a study, without the realization of the vision of karma and the path of karma that has reached up to the calculation of Manvantaras by stating the measure of our lifetime, we fall into the group of saying 'I know, I have heard, I have understood' as if we have acquired the fullness of the measure of truth of everything, just like completing the measure of devilishness by talking about the unrespectable outcasts and barbarism. Or else,

All that is uttered as ego (Aham), If inquired within, the many become one.

Each of us will stand at the point where this is summarized. Thus, we continue as the essence of the ego, as if this ego called 'I' has completed the ego of many things. Having studied such an ego, how will you and I recognize the path of karma that has been completed as something else? Again, we say that the truth of the scholar, shaped by letters and recognizing the form of sound through the dictionary, and the truth of a layman, and the completion of each knowledge in between, have been achieved. Does it mean that this person who speaks of the completion of 'Aham Brahmasmi' (I am Brahman), and thus everyone who has become 'Aham Brahmam', has completely understood the meaning of the scholar, the layman, the forest dwellers, and the karma, dharma, virtue, sin, and the secret of the Upanishadic light of the forest dwellers in between? Does it mean that we have mastered all the truths and dharmas measured by intellect, measured by dharma, measured by karma, and measured by virtue? We know one thing, a knowledge as great as us. The knowledge of many crores of us. But when we know or try to know that the evolutionary knowledge of our life through the passage of time is quite incomplete, we can only see in our measure the sum of good and evil that harmonizes with the truth, dharma, and adharma that may come and go. In this truth, there are those who start studying, those who have reached the end of their studies, those who have received class promotions, and those who express the love and respect of each class today. Rather than enjoying telling the stories of the English, or ancestors, or doctorate holders, it is when we come to know whether we have achieved anything that there should be at least a slight weighing of what the part of knowledge is. Or knowing the secret of each thing that stands with its meaning exhausted by counting one by one, and the evolutionary memory of life. Or knowing how the knowledge divided into the five elements (Panchabhootas), five sheaths (Panchakoshas), and five senses (Panchendriyas), and the mixed life, are connected with the Supreme Soul.

This is the theoretical argument of life. Everyone should not end up going to the search for conditions where they enjoy saying 'I' (Aham) while standing in the action of blabbering their scholarship, having studied and become a scholar. There is a secret of logic stated by Narayana Guru for that too. 'The essence of Aham also continues.' When we study and reach this point, like the differences in states or like the class system, everyone is great when standing in the sum of their class, or knowledge, or luck. But the greatness of our knowledge is not here. This is where the truth spoken by our ancestors arises. Continuing like this is the secret of life.

A visionary or a state is spoken about a life that has completed such a secret through long births and deaths. Of such states

The evolutionary secret of each life is a combination of many thousands. Each theorist is a combination of many thousands of evolutionary secrets. Thus, a great visionary who has seen many truths is the one who should teach us the truth in an exemplary manner. The visionary of the Yugadharma is the one who explains the meaning of many such truths.

This opportunity we have received to be born is in the Kali Yuga, which is the result of many such Yugadharmas. The fulfillment of such a Kali Yuga is the complete secret that leads each of us forward. Thus, the visionary of the Yugadharma is the one who separates truth and falsehood through the path of vision that reveals the ultimate secret of life. That is what each Acharya is. Yugantharadharma is the combination of many such Acharyas.

As they continued to write, recognizing the unbroken and long dharma of Yugantharadharma, the knowledge of Jagrat (waking state), Swapna (dream state), and Sushupti (deep sleep state), and the brilliance of the stream of light that constantly knows the truth became each fulfillment. That is the essence of the Upanishads. The meaning of such an abbreviation is the life shown by the forest dwellers. It is not possible for us to distinguish and understand this. But we have an easy way. This much had to be said to explain that. We can also say it in a single word.

This is known as Jagrat, Swapna, Sushupti, and Turiya. Without dismissing it as a dream, or dismissing it as an illusion of the waking state, it is a limitation of the knowledge that recognizes the karma called Jagrat, Sushupti, and Turiya. This is the theoretical secret of the ego that says 'Aham Brahmasmi' (I am Brahman). Without destroying ourselves by stating theories in that way, and without completing it by explaining the meaning of the theory, one step is said about the 7444 visions that are completed in the stream of light of the knowledge of Jagrat and Sushupti mentioned earlier.

Regarding such philosophical arguments - there is no need to complete them by saying they exist or do not exist. Knowing this much in the place of knowledge, the truth of the truth that elevates each of us by standing in the supreme position called Ishwara Sannidhanam (presence of God) and Daiva Sannidhanam (presence of the Divine) with 7444 visions is Ishwara Sannidhanam. Following that is Daiva Sannidhanam. Thus, experiencing and expressing the presence of Brahman of the other and the Supreme is the meaning of life.

The truth of the Guru is to explain and complete or to know and complete each aspect of knowledge. Such truth is the greatness of a life where realization and familiarity come together. The path of dharma of such a glorious stream of light is fulfillment. Thinking about such fulfillment, whether big or small, according to one's own intellect and becoming exhausted is the ignorance or knowledge that goes to see the end of the Vedas. Retrieving the changes that are truth and falsehood mixed with right and wrong passed through the six Manvantaras and twenty-eight Chaturyugas of this kind of knowledge, the attainment of Manvantara or the measure of Chaturyuga is the passing of knowledge and the fulfillment on the part of knowledge many times.

This is the word of the Guru that we must understand and grasp. When that word becomes truth, it becomes the Guru and God, and the life dharma with the greatness of the Supreme. This is 'Word is truth, truth is Guru, Guru is God.' The tradition of vision mentioned by the ancestors is for seeing and knowing this. The secret of knowledge called Grihasthashrama, Brahmacharya, Vanaprastha, and Sanyasa, which plays a major role in this, can be completed by all of us only if it is completed through the Grihasthashrami. Such fulfillment is the continuous right of life.

This is what we have theorized as the Guru. This is the difference between people called scholars or knowledgeable people creating the meaning of a word using the meaning of vowels called Swara and Vyanjana and becoming a Guru, and an experienced Guru. The truth of Guru Pooja in Santhigiri is to see and complete this.

Your Swami

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · October 1994 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0751 to GV2:P0771
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

October 1994

A person preparing to take Sannyasa will have to face many difficulties. Those who set out as Avadhutas should practice in the ashram and go forward with some karma in mind. If so, what is intended can be achieved. Only if one obeys as a Brahmachari for at least twelve years will everything become right. A single moment is enough, the Guru-disciple relationship might happen. The Guru will not have to talk to them like rubbish. That is how the rightful availability of the Guru and the rightful disciple happen.

Since childhood, I could see a form of light. It was Krishna's. And I didn't know how to express it. In the third birth, died at the age of twenty-five. Initiation was received at the age of twenty-five. On that day, Krishna was made visible. This is the reason for having that face. The face of Rishi Saibaba was shown in the position of the Guru. That too with proof.

'He who worships at the feet of the Guru...' Ezhuthachan has said. There is no need to look at the right or wrong of the Guru. The gods have committed many wrongs. Even those wrongs are praised. The Guru is above even the gods. The Guru can be seen above the state attained by the disciple. It is God who makes the Guru a Guru. The disciple sees what is contained in God's will. Therefore, it should be seen in its own nature. It is God who directs each soul. Even Gurus are of a thousand kinds. It is never possible to study the Guru. God is giving him intelligence every moment. Therefore, the Guru's word must be strictly obeyed. Did you see Parasurama obeying his father, who was his Guru? He killed his mother.

The Putrakameshti ritual was performed and yielded results. That is mantravada (sorcery). Religious gurus are like sorcerers. Only one with a tongue and life can say that you should do this, your life's purpose is this. I did not get this by desiring anything in my life. Since childhood, I knew nothing but the love of God.

My own life is proof that the tendencies of past lives will take hold of us. It is a fortune that the Guru is worthy. If a worthy Guru is found, a soul that has endured sacrifice will get another birth, and everything that was accomplished over time will be revealed.

The Gurus of the age might have done many things. But not all Gurus might be able to do so. If one tries to deceive externally, someone will deceive the deceiver. Only the truth should be revealed openly. Do not confuse things. Past lives should not be hidden. If so

It will be like writing the biography of Sree Narayana Guru. No sacrifice has been revealed. There are many flaws in writing the history of the Prophet as well. Disciple: How is it possible to record all the things that have been received? Guru: One should only say what one has received. It should not be written without a name. The one who has the karma to write will come and write. It is enough if you do the karmas you have to do here carefully. If Rama came as Krishna, then why worship Rama? Shouldn't someone be able to carry and fly those divine parts? Jesus and the Prophet had people to do what they said. Similarly, isn't it enough if you also have determination?

What do we need to change the animality that is full of vasanas? It will only be right if you offer it saying that there is nothing called mine. Depending on the context and situation, the Guru will say different things. If you obey that, it will flow together. Intelligent people, if not careful, will make mistakes. One who accepts the Guru will not look at the Guru's words a second time. Even if it is wrong, they will only try to implement it with difficulty. What a wicked person does will come to light. But a disciple will not repeat the Guru's words. The Guru's word is the glory of time.

What is the glory of time? What is the secret of keeping Tipu's samadhi decorated in this way? He died of a cut. But it is called samadhi. Tipu is a Muslim. But respecting his courage, the British themselves built the samadhi. Why is that? He had an honesty. I have spent a couple of hours there. India is always the cornerstone of the model. The people and the land of India are glorious. Being able to be born in such a land is a great fortune. Even though this land had so much glory, we are not able to unite. Even though there have been some damages due to an unexemplary lifestyle, no one can come here and do anything. Such people will only destroy themselves.

Communism has a nature of spreading everywhere like having no root. Where is its root? In Germany. Where did the capitalist nature in this come from? From the colonial system established by the British. The British were somewhat just at first. But when what was only in the hands of the upper class was transferred to the ignorant poor, this capitalist nature came up. The origin of this nature was in India itself.

What came to India through Krishna a thousand years ago, the British took away, studied and implemented here. If you study about arts, you will understand that. If you see the art of Christians, you will not feel any love. How did a nature of not knowing all this carefully come into us? A demonic nature has spread in us and is ruling the roost. The physical remains of all great people have been preserved in this condition itself. This is to show that it is a decision of God. It may come to be that it is somewhat destroyed according to some changes that occur in the yugas. A reason before creating history. Such things are being said here for you to be convinced about the nature of the yuga and to confirm it.

There is a place (Bermuda Triangle) in the middle of the sea where no science works. A devil with God's blessing has done this.

What was the mistake Tipu made? He thought that there is only Islam in this world. Whether an aggressor or a good person, if they came here and worked before us, India has a nature of seeing a glory in the resting of that physical body. All the mischiefs in the world should come and rest here. Do you think that if you understand this and sit idle without doing anything, your life will be spared? Those who handle art and culture should be especially careful about this.

You should pray that the nature of today's education system should not come to our education center. Education is to utilize this present time, to know about the future. It should be able to be imparted happily.

You must understand that the mistake that has been going on for twenty-five Chaturyugas has been passed on to us. For so long, this mistake has remained in the atmosphere and spread. It is as a result of this that the world has plunged into such a disaster. How should we respect the power that foretold this? Even if we can only act for a moment, we must do it right. If we do so, the result of that action will remain here. No one can take it away. Shouldn't we think and act on how to carry forward that foundational power of life? Who will you become if you work even a little bit for this.

How many Acharyas have come here recently? Didn't everyone starting from Buddha arrive? If the British, who were born in a nature where it is impossible to survive, could achieve this much, why can't we.

All kinds of nonsense will come here, we are catching it and leading it on a pure path. Our ability might be less. It is enough to display the ability we have without getting tired, we can achieve anything.

You are the ones who have come with the right to salvation like Uddhava or the Gopis. It is much easier for you than that. After Krishna, a few Acharyas came and imparted some virtues to the people. By combining these virtues, we have brought a new virtue. God is showing us things beyond the Yugas and making us do them. It is a very responsible matter for us. We must be able to work and consolidate all artistic and cultural values ourselves.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · November 1994 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0772 to GV2:P0785
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

November 1994

It is not enough to live in the ashram like living in a normal place. This can only be done by suffering and feeling pain for many days. Many people have a notion that if they have a direct experience, everything is done. With the thought that everything can be achieved through the use of Siddhi, the realization of what Siddhi is and what knowledge is has been lost. All that used to happen at one time. But this is not the time of the Deva. Nothing is going to be achieved by manifesting the Deva. What is achieved in many births can be achieved in one birth in Kali. Understand what the basis of our action is and do it keeping that in mind. The easy way of eloquence that does not know what pain is, is wrong. This action that changes the course of human action is difficult. The course of human action and along with it the course of action that has been wrong for ages must be changed. Have you thought about why, in this place with so much wealth of experience, no matter how great the action is, one has to experience so much difficulty. All suffering and all pests must be dissolved and ended here. Therefore, this will only end by causing suffering.

There is no ashram discipline at all in South India. Not at all in Kerala. Why is that? Kerala was ruled by Nairs. The administration fell under the upper-caste hegemony. Therefore, it was not possible to impart knowledge to those below the Nairs. Among the rulers of that time, only in Kochi was there a Kshatriya. In other places, the ruler disguised himself as a Brahmin. Under the rule in Travancore, the people grew like wild grass. Humans got a liberation because the Christians arrived here. But that also did some harm. Today they are experiencing the result of the harm they brought to India. The British were the ones who ruled everywhere. Today even that name is not out there.

Now what do we need? As we travel along the path we have been told, whatever we do, we must plead and beg, 'We have no knowledge for this; how should we do this, what should we pray for. . . we don't know, father. . . . please give us everything we need'

You must pray. I will mention caste here. You shouldn't speak like that. Mistakes will happen. They will understand what I say. Chandalas and Brahmins are all members here. Everyone has the freedom to work equally here.

Because the kings who ruled Kerala were not Kshatriyas, they did not know how to respect a sanyasi. The king ruled as an obedient servant to the Vaidika. If the king is like this, how will the subjects be? What all should we be careful about when we work? A Brahmin knows rituals and rules. But they behave trivially in front of others. However, they do what is necessary in front of the Acharya. If you don't have this awareness, you will return to the old culture itself.

You have no sacrifice at all. It is said that Westerners have no sacrifice. But their hardworking nature is not small. In China, women go to work on the seventh day after giving birth. But here, how long is postpartum care? Don't you need four people to take care? A pond from Ochira to the north is a furlong long. What magnificent stone steps their women build at the bathing place in a place without even a pinch of sand! Seeing this, the wealthy Nairs also did it. The children of the same mother were divided into carpenter, brass-smith, goldsmith, and blacksmith, given a thread over the left shoulder, named Vishwakarma, and started worshiping. Women stayed at home and became bad. They are the ones who became the worst among women. Therefore, not only did they completely lose their beauty, but they also fell into severe poverty. Hybridization and lack of food - in this state, that entire class has degraded.

We must see this with pain. I am not talking about today's Brahmins. They are poor. Their ancestors did all this. If they do not understand this, aggressive behavior will occur. The Ezhava's ignorance is his arrogance. Tying a knot on the head, drinking toddy, and showing off that 'I am also someone' is all even the most educated can do. Some converted and became Christians and Muslims. What the arrogant person says to God is also the same: If what I think doesn't happen, I will throw you away. The forest dwellers come to the king with honey in a bamboo tube, turn their backs, and give it. What does he call the king? The Ezhava's nature is also the same - arrogance. The Namboothiris of that time fixed all the Ezhavas with laws. Some of those who got some culture studied medicine. The physician came and kept everyone else away. Ponnamban, Thandan, Chovan, Channar, Panikkar - these are all names bought with money. Sometime back, when I went to Varanappally, I saw Ezhava women themselves calling those belonging to it 'Kochamma'. This arrogance is also there in worshiping God. Worshiping God without humility, they became arrogant not only in education but in everything they touched. They don't even know how to show humility. All these traditions must be broken. This is what was said to be our hardship.

The Ezhava will stand with pride wherever he goes to show that his customs are great. They didn't even take what Sree Narayana Guru did and showed. First, he taught them to cook and eat. Then he taught them to offer Bali. He built a house for Vavubali. Finally, he sat himself for Guru Pooja. He took a handful of Guru Pooja rice and gave it as Prasad. But the community didn't understand it. Some will say Swami didn't tell for Pooja. Understanding all this, we should work with the knowledge that we have such a thing. Humility is the greatest. Obedience is even greater. And the qualification to match it.

I had a youth spent plucking flowers in Sivagiri and living in some Oottupura. Growing up in this nature, I have observed and learned everything. I have cried in pain on many days. How can those without love and obedience be reformed? If you have come here to do all this after staying here, you must do it even without food and sleep. When lifting up what has fallen, there will only be those who scold. You must suppress and destroy your mischief and do this with difficulty. Otherwise, it will be like Durvasa doing penance. Durvasa only had time to curse and do penance. People of all communities will come here.

Remember that this is the central place for all of them. You should not point out everyone's flaws like I do. It will hurt them. Mischief will also come. I say this to remove ego and bring humility.

We are small in number. Even so, people of all kinds are here. That is not because of our greatness. It is by the grace of God. You should remember that the service you do is service to the Guru. Service is service to the Guru; a word is a word spoken to the Guru. It is the love that cannot be found anywhere else that attracts people of all communities here. There is a uniqueness to the growth of all aspects of ours. It is growing without our knowledge, just as the country grows.

Those with vision will understand one thing if they observe carefully. The light seen here has a uniqueness greater than the light of gods and goddesses. Different types of lights. Those who see it must have the wisdom to recognize it. If not, they should ask the Guru and know. Only the Guru can recognize it. After creation, a long time passed without any evolution, not even knowing what the earth was. Even God's will had to be shaped by time. If man has a certain honesty, the world of gods and the world of Brahma each have an honesty, and that belongs to God.

GURUVANIKAL · December 1994 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0786 to GV2:P0800
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani December 1994

I have not failed to see what the Acharyas have earned for the world. But some of them, at the time of finally leaving the body, told stories of wonder, and introduced practices like jumping into the river and merging in the act of abandoning the body, or wrapping the body tightly in a thousand cubits of cloth and floating it in the river Ganges. Among another caste of sannyasins, there is a practice of committing suicide by jumping into a gorge. Thus, our tradition has operated with many deceptions of wonders hidden in karma. You should think about what can be done to rectify a lack of distinction that has flowed and perished in this lack of story. I am telling you one of my experiences. It took many years to realize that the aforementioned systems are today. What is this that is seen, what is said, where will it reach if it goes on like this - in a state of helplessness. But I did not stop my search. At one point, it was even said that I was beaten and chased away from Varkala. There was also a group to say that I went with two girls and was never seen again. So there was a time when they made me happy by saying such things. I left Varkala according to the notice that I should not sit where two people could see me.

From there I went to a house in Manavoor. The people of that house would always come and call me. Two people I knew also came with me. Do not sit here, it is dangerous if you sit here

It changed when I heard the disembodied voice. The people who came with me dropped me off at Mannamboor. There, someone came with a car to pick me up. I went to Paravur with him.

When I reached there, I saw the celebrations and activities of a death anniversary. A ghost came there. After seeing its activities, I returned to Mannamboor the next day. When I went back, I got a good reception. This house is at the entrance of a fish market. The shops and everything else there belong to this family. The people in those shops were waiting with sticks, logs, and weapons. The head of the house, perhaps feeling something then, told me: 'Swami, don't stay here now. You should leave now. I will explain things later.' He had brought me forward. And I left. At that time, they were standing there, raising their sticks and logs, shouting slogans. Due to God's love, nothing happened there. I didn't know why all these preparations were made. I left without knowing. I understood the matter only later.

Because the mother in that house was cured of her illness and their daughter's danger was averted, they used to come to me often. I wrote them a letter and sent it. 'Child, you are staying in a house where you live respectably. Don't forget that... Whatever the relationship between you two is, let it be, I will talk and set it right.' Seeing this, the mother and father thought that I was interfering in a way that would spoil their child. I had sent this letter four years before I went to that house. It was a letter sent after doing a ritual for them. It was this letter that they received when I went there four years later. That's when they felt love for me - as mentioned earlier, they felt like dealing with me properly.

My experience is always like this. Wherever I have gone, I have had to live as an object of ridicule. Even after six or seven years of coming here to Pothencode, I had such an experience. Many patients came here, were treated (only with holy ash, holy water, and prayer), and were getting cured. Among them, a woman was brought here by her husband for the cure of mental illness, and after being cured, she was taken home. After a few days, that woman came to the ashram with a relative of hers, wishing to stay for two days. The next day, I looked and didn't see her. She hadn't gone anywhere else. She was inside the well.

Thus, incidents have emerged in life and danced a destructive dance. But all this was not something a human being could bear. That it passed is only by the grace of God. That in every state - by state I mean the place a sannyasi or a brahmachari should reach and attain - is being attained state by state. How that is, is that according to the calculations of the scientists who are contained in this world, where atomic science reaches - whether they call it energy, or power, or the glory of nature, or whatever name they call it - the nature of life and soul evolving through the covering of the five elements that we are experiencing daily today, by the knowledge of the five senses, by the recognition of the five sheaths. No other science is adequate for this except the help that has worked for many past lives to put all this together. I am informing you of this because it is a truth I have experienced. There will be an opportunity to know what a large part of the experienced truth is. Otherwise, I cannot say that these are the negative aspects resulting from my following some sannyasi, siddha, or god.

We have a tradition where we have been able to see up to the Trinity system as a visible dharma. You can believe that it exists. Because we cannot just throw away the actions done and caused to be done by our ancestors, can we? That must come. And it must be grasped along with it. That is how we must proceed. But when you grasp it, you must have an understanding.

What is that knowledge? When a god, goddess, sage, ascetic, wise person, devotee, or a person of action comes, if there is no lineage of gurus to teach and make them experience eternal peace, you should not go that way. If you go, you will be destroyed. I have the evidence of thousands of experiences for this. Since that evidence is mine alone, I don't care what anyone says. I am not someone who has accepted the traditions I grew up in. I walked through all of that at first. Then I understood as much as its knowledge, and by God's grace, I got that knowledge. Again, it took years to understand its various aspects. Only after that did I want to say something to the people. It's only been ten or twelve years since I started talking.

How should you maintain this tradition? Whatever devil, god, goddess, sage, ascetic, or wise person has entered your path of action so far, all of them will be given to you in the glory called Guru, in the animate and inanimate, in form and formless, in light. Or you will be made to experience it in the Guru's action. Otherwise, you have received the tradition of a theory that is taught in an orderly manner. When you merge into this truth of experience, you must be careful about one thing. All you have to suffer, love, and respect is sacrifice. Otherwise, you have not been given wealth and other abilities to be happy. May God himself give you wealth and ability. Let the lucky ones experience it. Or let the lucky lineage of children experience it. Wealth and ability should not be lost in ignorance.

If you catch a guru, that guru should be able to tell you from the devil to the city of vampires (Narayana Guru's vision stops here) and beyond that, what is the nature of the trinity, in which sky they stand, which realm is their place. Otherwise, you will fall into trouble by getting caught up in the uproar of miracles and thousands of tricks, and in the action and inaction of royal people. Living as subjects of all this, you do not know that you are being pushed into the deep abyss of action to capture a poor section of people who are the owners of some knowledge and make them fight each other. This is my knowledge experienced. You who believe here have that evidence. Whatever devil, god, or goddess comes before you, if you tell them to get out, they will go. Or if you say 'you will burn' two or three times, it will burn. That is your Guru's power. It will burn in the name of the Guru. With that strength, you will be able to catch up. Then, through the action of this path of dharma, earning merit, a complete richness that you must take together is ready before you. You must view it broadly and pass it on to the children who have reached the action. That should be the ultimate goal of life.

We know thousands of sacrificial methods. Thinking that the Ashwamedha Yaga can be completed by capturing its karmic flaws is ignorance and the path of destruction. It is a path that sows destruction and reaps destruction. When accepting a Guru's path, you must fully believe in it. Do not catch it without substance and put it in your mind. You must be able to change from that way, no matter how hard it is. Sitting in front of the god strengthened by caste and religion, chanting mantras and performing satyagraha, nothing is going to happen. And it hasn't happened either. Those who live according to this must also make others live according to it. This is the path God has given you to get out forever from the abyss of ignorance in which your father and mother were caught. I hope you will be able to capture a great theory by traveling only on that path. For that, it is most appropriate to worship it as Pournami.

Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0801 to GV2:P0819

January 1995

We only have an acquaintance without the actual knowledge of what Pournami Vrutham is. The sun, moon, and earth - these three spheres are associated with Pournami. The earth is meant for exhausting karma; one enters this field of action (karmabhoomika) through the dynamics of life that has evolved due to the power of the sun. You should be guided by a knowledge that tells you what to do and what not to do in this field of action. You should not let yourself be led into destructive actions by being impressed with mere fragments of Vedanta. I have a request that you should not alter these words of truth which are spoken for your understanding.

Such a prayer is being conducted here according to the instructions. While performing this prayer, you must remember one thing: during this prayer time, which clears the mistakes of many ages, if even a small scratch of ignorance enters within, it will spread. It will spread in many ways. We are praying to rectify the defects that have occurred in all the actions of householders, as well as in all the actions of sannyasins and brahmacharis. What we are doing is not something that matches the capacity of our minds. Therefore, some mistakes and ignorance might occur. There is nothing else to say other than it is God's will that makes us do something so profoundly special that no one can even describe. You should always pray that may God grant this Guru and the Guru lineage, given to us by God, the opportunity to remove the shortcomings not only of myself and my ancestors accumulated over time, but of the entire global population, and impart goodness of double that measure.

The children you raise and nurture, and the children who have taken sannyasa and stand here, are equally participants in this noble karma. You must also think about what should be done so that a path of fulfillment emerges through the karma you all do together. Not only through the sannyasis and their lineage, but wherever shortcomings have occurred in any part of this world, all of them should be destroyed, and in their place, goodness according to the divine will must come through the guru lineage, you should always think while doing any work. When studying children do not get knowledge in their minds, they should pray like this, not only the children of this lineage, but wherever there are children who have gone astray, you should pray for goodness to come to all of them in this way. This is the word God has given us. Boys and girls should be given special attention individually and prayed for. The karma coming from me, a woman, must correct the mistakes of the woman in this world, and the man. This should be your qualification. This qualification of man and woman is advancing together. At that time, our minds should not become bad, nor should we fall into situations that make our minds bad. Especially the men and women growing up here are the ones who have to judge the world. We must become great through this sense of dharma. Standing in this dharma, if there are any other traditional solar systems, our constant prayer is that all the mistakes that have come through them so far be resolved, and that we be given the knowledge, experience, and habit to do what we need when we need it. We got that knowledge, and when God tells us and makes us do it, we must be ready to do it. Other than this, thoughts like rubbish lying on the road should not be allowed to enter the mind.

Question: Guru! When natural disasters occur, what is the remedy or solution to remove them?

Guru: As a result of natural disasters, losses have been occurring for a long time. This will repeat again. Changing the nature of prayer and the nature of worship is the only solution for this. Not only in India, but anywhere on the face of the earth, times are coming when anything can happen. Among the notifications we get, there is one thing that has been received many times. The existence of the government will cease to exist, and the dangers that have occurred at the administrative level will seem very trivial compared to the dangers that are yet to come. We also prayed for about four years to avoid this tragedy. When meteorologists announce that there will be a cyclone, we pray. Then it will be effective. We prayed when there were difficulties in Mexico, which is called the underworld on the other side of the earth, and when there were widespread beatings in Islamic countries. When Indira Gandhi was in danger, a letter was written to her indicating it.* Notices were printed and distributed here. She lived only for two weeks after saying this. When her danger was being received here through vision, those who stood listening misunderstood it in another way and wrongly informed the Manorama office. That Indira Gandhi will not die.

* Letter to India

It was written in the same way to the Indian Express office as well. It also happened in such a way that those who heard this news felt contempt for the ashram. Not everyone, but some had it. Later, the journalists themselves said that it came because of the mistake of hearing and knowing this.

The earthquake in Kalloor was very terrifying. Later, it occurred in a minor way at various times. Even then, we had printed notices and advertised it. Many people in the Kalloor ashram had also prayed. That fear of the earthquake then slowly subsided.

Question: Guru, are you saying that natural disasters are caused by man's misguided forms of worship and various kinds of evil deeds?

Guru: Yes, if we say that, we have to look back a very long time. When talking about periods, we should at least talk from the time of Krishna. The shortcomings of the course of action that have come to our knowledge since that time, only then will we understand at least a little.

Question: Could you explain a little what you said about natural disasters occurring continuously due to misguided and untimely lifestyles?

Guru: If we start explaining it like that, we will have to go beyond the calculation of the Manvantara. When we say beyond the calculation of the Manvantara, we do not mean only the past Manvantara. What is currently in existence is the twenty-eighth Chaturyuga of the seventh Manvantara. Until the third Chaturyuga of this Manvantara, dharma flourished without any highs and lows. After that, Satyathranan of the Manu lineage, who came in accordance with the times, looked at the past three Chaturyugas. Then only the tradition of Manu is seen, nothing else. Wherever he looked and however he looked, how it came in the five senses, how it came in the five koshas, its course of action that took place as Manvantaras and what is currently in existence, that is all that is seen. As each person attains each state, there are many levels of attaining immortality in the divine nature after crossing the demonic, but the truth is that it was seen that the mind system was working in all this. The six Manvantaras that took place according to the Brahma's imagination and the three Chaturyugas in this Manvantara took place truthfully. Along with seeing all this, this sage is also seeing the history of many visionary gurus in his guru lineage. All that has come in accordance with the Yuga dharma. There is a calculation of so many gurus for each Chaturyuga, and everything has been brought according to that calculation. This sage sees all this as coming and joining him. The reason this sage sees it this way is that he somehow missed thinking about the change in Yuga dharmas. Along with that, the four sentences starting with Aham Brahmasmi that he said are still the upadesha mantra given to sannyasins after their initiation name.
