GURUVANIKAL · February 1995 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0820 to GV2:P0839
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

February 1995

Question: Guru! What is the difference between a Deeksha name and the advice given by a Sanyasi?

Guru: After a Brahmachari practices Brahmacharya for twelve years, they will become aligned with either Shaktheyam, Shaivam, or Vaishnavam. Some people mix all three and worship. Once a child is born, among Hindus, the traditions of the Shodasha system are followed. The Shodasha system is a principle where rituals are performed in seventeen stages from birth to death. This involves advising a name in the tradition of some gods or goddesses. This is called Upanayanam. While performing Upanayanam, the Surya Gayatri is chanted.

There is the course of karma conceived as Nanmukhan and the Trimurthis. When Subrahmanya got angry, he pinched off one of Nanmukhan's heads! Subrahmanya is sometimes called 'Shubhramaniyan' in Tamil. Shubhramaniyan is one who has turned his virility into a bead. The premise is that by eating leafy vegetables, using kayakalpas within twelve years, and practicing alchemy, just as mercury is solidified, this virility is made exactly like a bead. Subrahmanya's guru is Bhogar, isn't he? In this same yogic practice, the virility

Bhogar is the one who made the bead. Even today, there are ascetics who believe that through this alchemical practice, the bodily functions reached the five sheaths (Panchakoshas) through the five senses and the five elements (Panchabhootas), and the individual soul (Jivatma) united with the Supreme Soul (Paramatma). Those who find bliss in this by citing a yogic calculation (the calculation of alchemical practice) of seven thousand for Bhogar and twelve thousand for Agastya are the ones standing behind this sensory function. As an illustration of this, there is a story about Bhogar, which explains how he got the name Bhogar by turning his semen into a bead.*

Let's take the aforementioned Satyathmanan. Satyathmanan means the one who follows the truth. Satyathmanan saw that everything that continues through the Manvantaras comes to him. * The story is elsewhere.

When looking at it this way, he sees himself as everything. The tradition of knowledge, which is the five sheaths in the evolution of the five elements and the evolution of the five senses, comes to him. What Satyathmanan saw in this way is not wrong, it is correct. However, because he did not consider the chronology of the Yuga Dharmas, he saw the visionaries, gurus, and prophetic figures who had come from the beginning up to him, and then everything else, coming to him. When he saw in himself the visionary nature of everything in Manu's tradition coming to him and him seeing himself as everything, in the visionary tradition of seeing himself in himself and in everything, he felt 'Aham Brahma' (I am Brahman). Once the nature of this universe, which comes from the cessation of rotations, is seen, most people cannot think beyond it. This is the aspect of how the Jivatma and Paramatma function after the action of the five sheaths. People stop talking at the boundary where they begin to think and understand beyond that. By bringing the functional capacity of the gurus, who came according to the Yuga Dharma through the passing Yugas, to Nambuvan's karma, a conceptual form of Brahma was given using the three parts mentioned. No matter which sage you ask, there will be no revelation about this.

Question: Then how can it be known?

Guru: Through the visionary tradition. What are these sheaths known by reaching the five senses that evolve through the five elements? Annamaya Kosha (food sheath), Pranamaya Kosha (vital air sheath), Jnanamaya Kosha (mind sheath), Vijnanamaya Kosha (intellect sheath), and Anandamaya Kosha (bliss sheath). After forming these and going into the concept of creation, this aspect is about seeing the Guru, the embodiment of karma, who merges and unifies as the soul and the Supreme, which is the unique karma showing what the karma of the solar system is. However, following Satyathmanan, such a mistake has occurred in the theoretical doctrine. Due to this sage's use of the phrase 'Aham Brahmasmi' (I am Brahman), the knowledge we have received from the visionary aspect is that none of the things in the Brahman concept functioned in the subsequent three Chaturyugas (four-yuga cycles). When it came to a stage where scholars or those without an understanding of the visionary aspect were present, in the second and third quarters of some Chaturyuga, a practice of worshiping with the concept of Brahma, Vishnu, and Shiva in the path of creation of karma reaching from demons to gods emerged. When the five elements interacted with the five senses, this sensory nature was activated in all living beings. To recognize it, they mentioned Annamaya Kosha, Pranamaya Kosha, Jnanamaya Kosha, and Vijnanamaya Kosha, and stopped at Anandamaya Kosha. Thus, by the imagination of the mind and the proficiency in the path of karma, three Chaturyugas passed without even being able to take a look at the long history of rotations that came in accordance with the Yuga Dharmas. Satyathmanan failed to get the meaning of the continuous karma and the meaning of the Yuga Dharma. That is why phrases like 'Aham Brahmasmi' came to be. When all the eminent scholars and ordinary people who came after this wrote, depicted, and experienced it, as a devotee says, crossing the six steps

It reached the ritual of praising Shiva, where one went to the place they lay to see Shiva. This devotee's praise is repeatedly maintained. It became a system of worship across the entire Asian continent. Thus, even if we consider Treta and Dvapara as the yugas for that dharma, in the concept of names and forms that stand manifested and instruct, each person scholarly depicted everything up to the status of the Trimurti, or the status of Devendra, or the status of the Ganas. In name and form, according to their respective skills, the deity's mastery of all arts and adornment with all ornaments came into existence as dharma. This was affirmed even through the Puranas. They wrote, the great sages who praised what they saw day by day. But there are many examples of occasions when they could not tell or know the greatness of time. They became arrogant, stuck in the name and form, insisting that there is nothing beyond the Trimurti system. There is proof for this. A Yaksha placed a blade of grass and called all the gods, telling them to burn it or destroy it. Despite trying all their tricks, it did not happen. Because Devendra had no arrogance, he was able to recognize the Yaksha. The one who came as a Yaksha was indeed Brahman, who came by the will of Brahman. The word given there is Devendra saying that Brahman itself came as the Yaksha.

Guru Vani March 1995

Boys and girls need to be taught certain things that will be useful at respective times in the various stages of their lives. As far as girls are concerned, Kanyadanam is an important matter in that aspect. By teaching her thus, her mind is made steady. Boys also need a good life just like this. A

When becoming a householder, one must have a clear understanding of who they should be, who they were before that, and what they must do for their home and country. For girls, it is during the Kanyadaan ceremony that they are advised on how they should be when starting this life, the qualities required to become a mother, and what they should be before becoming a mother. This advice must be given in private. It is said that during this stage of instruction, some boys and girls experience visions. Some also receive visions due to their punctuality and discipline. Nothing else needs to be done for this. As proof, many among us have had visions. This vision occurred only after coming into the Guru's presence. Not before that. That is, these things become clear through the Guru. This is Savikalpa Samadhi.

One must attain Savikalpa through the Guru. A child who attains Savikalpa must reach at least the position of a Rishi under a deity. The result of being a Brahmachari during the Brahmacharya stage must be attained. A lineage originating from such a Brahmachari is what benefits the world. You will not understand this immediately. Two or three things come in order here. First, parents must impart the knowledge to the child about what they are meant to do. Then, the child will carefully absorb what the Guru teaches. The Guru must also advise them about their marriage. Then, their parenthood changes automatically, and they rise to the state of a Devi or Deva in that position. Through the Guru, their soul attains this greatness. They realize this themselves through experience. It is not something I teach them. The deities they worshipped or their ancestors worshipped are attained by them after coming here. At the time of marriage, these deities reveal the Guru to the man and woman. Here, what is looked for is not wealth or education, but a wealth of character. A wealth of character means a traditional familiarity. This is what is meant by Rishi Putri (daughter of a Rishi). Rishi Putri means one raised by a Rishi himself. According to her growth, or within the Guru-disciple relationship, there are three or four types. In Savikalpa Samadhi, before this girl gets married and becomes pregnant, she must have a vision of the Guru and her husband. What did you understand? The Guru has given advice to this child. In this child's vision, the Guru, parents, and the entire lineage will stand in a row. Like Rishis sitting in a royal assembly, they give her the wedding garland. You can go and garland whoever you like (Swayamvaram). Her choice will not be wrong. Because she has seen it. It is not doing whatever she pleases, as people assume. When it is said she chooses herself, it is true. It is destined for her soul. That destiny has already happened. Later, it is shown externally in front of people. They know what the Guru gives the garland for. The Guru, however, only smiles. This is Swayamvaram. People have not been made to understand this. All this is necessary for mature men and women.

This is a ceremony. It is by showing this ceremony that people call it Kettukalyanam. But nobody knows what Kettukalyanam is. It later turned into a celebration. This tying is done with celebrations like riding on elephants. Ten girls are seated in a row, and a groom is decided for the Kettukalyanam. This one person ties the knot for all ten girls. Look at our ignorance. Here, Narayana Guru stopped this Kettukalyanam. This is a great deed done by Swami. Thus, the Kettukalyanam that existed among Nairs, Ezhavas, and even Chandalas was stopped. Ten children, from ten years old down to three years old, are seated in a row, and one person is chosen from among them, and he is made to garland them. What kind of nonsense and baseness is this? The greatest baseness created to make their husband the rightful owner.

Before the child is born, whatever shortcomings the father, mother, and family had, are resolved through the aforementioned ceremony. Even before birth, this child is told what they should become. That is why it is said that conducting and arranging a marriage should be according to the Guru's decision.

What has been said so far is about the old Swayamvara. This is not for us. Instead of this, we have something else, the bride and groom do not need to know about our selection. The father, mother, and Guru should decide. Children should only have that relationship. There is a reason for that. Before getting married, the father and mother should instruct them. The child should be born only like this. For that, their deity connections must be cleared. This is Guru Pooja. The real Guru Pooja happening here fulfilled what was needed for that house. From the day of performing that Guru Pooja, the man and woman seeking the Guru lineage should know that they must do only this. For the lineage coming through the relationship decided by the parents and the Guru, a lineage has been molded by transforming the deity-demonic lineage into a Guru lineage. This Guru lineage is waiting for the growth of this child. For that, the child is instructed to do such and such things. The father and mother should tell them that. Or the Guru will tell them. But the father and mother cannot explain it completely. Because they will only accept the father and mother to a certain extent. What they don't listen to when the parents say, they will listen to when the Guru calls and says. It will never belittle them, but only elevate them. That is why it is said not to look at education and wealth, only the qualification of the family should be considered. This is what the Prophet demonstrated. The Prophet married a merchant's daughter to a beggar's son. And a beggar's daughter was given to a merchant's son. The command is like this; Hey, you give her to him, they will have what they need. No one else has said this. They haven't understood it.

The Prophet has said, Hey Pathu, do not do this. What does it mean? Nectar flows from our brain daily. This nectar should not go into the stomach. It should only go inside the intestines. Khechari Mudra is practiced knowing this. Under the tongue, over a long period - by slightly cutting the tongue with Darbha grass, that is what is achieved. After a certain time of cutting the bottom of the tongue, this nectar will ooze and go to another place. Khechari Mudra avoids that and indicates that it is like this. This is a state of Samadhi. The real nature of Khechari Mudra is this itself. What is this Samadhi? Without knowing the worldly steps happening in the universe, one meditates and focuses there. When the tongue is cut, the throat is closed, right? Attention is needed there. That is a Samadhi. One sitting in this state can see rays of light. It is nectar in the body, isn't it? If the Guru is capable of nurturing and developing this, he will specifically instruct. They will attain Savikalpa. How many people here have this. There is also Sanchara Samadhi. Wherever one travels, they see the place and state of travel. That is, seeing the state of travel in front as Savikalpa. Through that, deities and past Gurus come before us. Instead of that, what do those who call themselves scholars do?

GURUVANIKAL · April 1995 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0840 to GV2:P0859
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

April 1995

Question: I have often heard you speak about the Pournami Vrata. Can you explain it once more?

Answer: There are many vratas (vows/fasts) among Hindus. Among them, the Pournami Vrata is for the goodness of the soul. It is not for achieving great financial status. However, when goodness of the soul comes, financial status may follow. Without the goodness of the soul, whatever status one achieves, none of it is permanent. Moreover, it will turn into distortion.

For a long time, we have been talking about births and acquiring the merit of births. There are those who ask what births are. There is no need to ask whether there are births or not. Because the very existence of a visionary proves it. A visionary foresees for a long time, doesn't he? Then is there even a need for the question of whether there are births?

The karma of Hindus stands in the Trimurti system. For a long time, there has been no karma in this path to know and act whether there is anything beyond that. But earlier it existed. It is not in the Hindu doctrine, it is in the doctrine of the Manu lineage. But what Manu is, what the lineage of Manu is, or what each of us belonging to the lineage of Manu is, no such information is found among Hindus to know. Then scholars or similar other people say that there is mental stability and then do some storytelling. What do we need from this, or what does someone else need, has not been seen and told in the light of the times. Nor has it been told what has come and gone. The times referred to are about the Chaturyuga - Krita, Treta, Dwapara, Kali. The very last period is Kali. We have been taught that Kali is bad and that it will lead to destruction. Kali should not be taken lightly. It has special qualities and can qualify for liberation. We have known such a Kali to be bad. How will we change this perception of ours about Kali, how will we grow? How will we take it up to the doctrine? None of this has been received from ancestors or Vedic scholars. It is not that they do not know, or rather, they can only give what has reached them. We have passed millions of years. History reaches a maximum of five thousand, six thousand, seven thousand, eight thousand years. Can anything be known from an account that reaches the vision of such a short period?

But according to the Hindu calculation, a Chaturyuga itself is twelve thousand divine years. One divine year is three hundred and sixty-five human years. In such a long Chaturyuga, what all did we do, what all did we not do? Who will we ask this? Which Mahatma will tell us? No one could tell us. Because the attempt has been to know by standing within the Trimurti system.

Question: What is the shortcoming of the Trimurti system?

Answer: Five thousand two hundred years ago, Krishna came. A birth greater than Krishna's has not occurred on earth. Such a Krishna has been hidden behind curious stories and dirty stories, making it impossible to truly know him. We have not been introduced to the greatness of Krishna.

Another example of our ignorance is the story of Kamsa. We have been taught that Kamsa is wicked. But Kamsa is not wicked. Kamsa stood in the situation where the light of the times spread. The author of the Gita says that dharma is directly perceivable.

The king was present during wartime in the very manner of how a king should be. In Kali Yuga, we see this in the Yadava clan. This Yadava is a Shudra, belonging to the fourth class among the four Varnas. The Vedanta that teaches us to keep the Shudra at a distance is what we have been taught. Shudra does not mean a Shudra by caste. There is a Shudra in the house of a Brahmin, a Kshatriya, a Vaishya, and a Shudra. Similarly, there is a Kshatriya, a Brahmin, and a Vaishya in every house. We do not know this. Because we do not know about this Shudra class and Shudra Dharma, we also suffer the misfortune of not knowing about Kali Dharma.

Many great souls have struggled to remove this misfortune. But because we are ignorant of where our ancestors or we went wrong or made a mistake, the Dharma in the special actions performed by men, women, young, and old has been lost to the point where we do not know what it is. This loss will never become irreparable for anyone. This is because we are not ready to go beyond the Trinity system. Without going beyond the Trinity system, or at least reaching the state of the Lord of the Ganas, one cannot attain liberation. This is what the mind system says. We have no information about this mind system.

This is the time of Vaivasvata Manu. Vaivasvata Manu is the seventh Manu. The current time is the Kali Yuga of the twenty-eighth Chaturyuga in the seventh Manvantara. Which one should we take? The Trinity system or the mind system? This is a subject that must be understood without any confusion between the classes. What work will we do for this? Whom will we ask?

Except for our ancestors saying many things and leaving, no one knows anything clearly. No scholar or Vedic priest has taught us anything. As a result of an action that has deteriorated due to these reasons, natural disasters occur and cause frequent destruction somewhere, but we have not been able to bring any good out of this. You must always think about this. Always think about what you should become through the actions that you and your children must perform. Otherwise, this is the destruction that each of you is building for your children. You will competitively develop science and technology more than necessary, and you yourselves will be destroyed one by one. You must also think that the world itself will shine and be destroyed by a flood of food. Let us give up this leap towards destruction and be ready to work for a better tomorrow.

God has given us a path to know all this through an action. This is not a work that tells you it is like this or it is not like that.

Question: How is the path given for us?

Answer:

The Dharma of Kali is good for all classes - Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra. Brahmins should not be taken in the sense of those who perform Vedic rituals. It should be taken in the sense of 'Brahmajnaneti Brahmana'. We should abandon the myth that Brahmajnana will not shine in Kali Yuga and see Kali as a special period of time when the best time to make it shine and the opportunity to do so have come. This path of Dharma to be realized in worldly actions is given from Brahman. That is what is being told to you.

I have experienced a lot of pain and difficulties for this. When I say this, I can only say it briefly. Those of you who are interested, please inquire and find out about this.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · May 1995 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0860 to GV2:P0872
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

May 1995

The Trimurti tradition is the truth that is seen manifested. There is a course of action that reaches up to the position of the lord of the Ganas, just like the Trimurti tradition. Seeing this course of action, we believed that it was the truth. All this is correct. Because it has its own truth. But this is not enough for us.

What happens with the constellation of stars, what should be done after reaching that state - this is the boundary of the soul's good deeds. Who are we, who should we be in the course of action that stands at the lord of the Ganas? Creation will come in the world like the course of action. It is not two for man and woman; there is only one. What should be added to the children who are raised to the part of this knowledge, or what is the side that joins the part of that knowledge - today's historians do not know any of this. It cannot be known through any myths or epics either. We are standing in an action where there is no point in knowing this. You come and know all this slowly.

In the Manu lineage that has come so far, we are standing in the twenty-eighth Chaturyuga in the seventh Manu lineage. The opportunity you have got is what God has shown us to get the situation of this Chaturyuga completed in our lineage. It cannot be known so quickly to its fullness. The reason is that we have no information about Manu and the Manu lineage. We also have no information about what is said in the Manusmriti. We don't know what has been created through it either. Manusmriti is knowing what is needed and what is not needed. Manu is also a historical truth to know what the fickle state of the mind is. Through what should we know this?

There is a lineage guru (appointed person) to know this. We need to know how to reach that guru and how to follow that path. There are many types of traditional gurus. There are those among them who come only to tell what the Trimurtis are and what that tradition is. We are able to know not only such gurus but all types of gurus. No other guru lineage has such clear knowledge. Neither Christ nor the Prophet has this. It is said that the Prophet's prophethood is the last. But three months before his samadhi, he makes a begging prayer, saying, "What are you showing me, Father, who thought that there is nothing but you?" So a course of action that even great men like the Prophet could not reach has existed here for ages.

We have not known this truth. Krishna's situation is also the same. We should see Krishna as a person suited to the dharma of the time. That is what was informed before coming. What is it? If you don't rule properly, people will be sent to your house to kill you. Kamsa could not understand that because of the words ordained by God. That is, standing in the position of Pratyakshavaha Dharma

Nothing else could be known. The Trinity system covered up the truth in such a way that it was impossible to know what a Manvantara was or what time was beyond it.

If those things happened because Sri Krishna was born, then that is also Brahma's decision. How can one person change that Brahma's decision? You are able to ask if Brahma himself could not have kept that decision aside because of the previously mentioned calamity. It is not like that. There is a Kaladharma (duty of time). If you do not know and reach through Kaladharma, you will not be able to know and reach what the historical truth of this world is. Therefore, do not cross it without knowing what Kaladharma is.

There are many stories here that have convinced us that there is a divine karma behind our death and birth in this time itself. But it is not enough to just convince. It is a karma that must be experienced and convinced. This is what was said to be the thing to know without distinction of male-female, big-small. That is being done here. If you feel that those who know this and those who make it known should live in the name of God, if you think that you have a karma for that, how should you live? You must show life in such a way that it brings an element of how to take it and pass it on to the children. One thing must be careful when passing this on to girls. Girls will not stay with you for long. At most until the age of thirty. So if you do not raise girls carefully, you will get a mother who spreads destruction.

The Westerners ruled India for four hundred and fifty years. It is not enough to just say that our customs and heritage were lost because they ruled like this. We were not able to take many good things. Giving something to someone and taking something from someone is a great imagination of the mind. Our body is for taking it by growing that mind, being humble, and being righteous. So if we do something with the body, a karma that cannot do anything will come and climb on our mind and shoulder.

Those who lived under the bridge during the British rule came here and became Viceroys. They are those who studied a little and cannot do anything. If he is smart, he will be taken somewhere as a Governor. He has nothing. Not even a good rich man. He is the one who came here and became Viceroy. Those here only learned the bad things he showed. He doesn't know how to walk when he comes here. A good rich man, the biggest beggar, and everyone in between behave in their own way. What we learned by seeing this is a danger that happened to us. While we are alive, we should think that we got such a knowledge by connecting with something in the mind, and we should ache to be able to maintain some good dharma before leaving this nest. What has happened cannot be taken away. Let it be like that. When born, that life came up through the star and the zodiac. Can it be cut off? We are doing the greatest karma of the quality of birth. You do not know that. Why?

In Kerala and Tamil Nadu, there is something called Seemanthakarmam. This karma is done in the seventh month of pregnancy. The brain develops from the seventh month of pregnancy. To avoid mistakes when this brain develops, Puranas and epics will be read and heard. This is Seemanthakarmam. Brahmins will also give sattvic food like honey and ghee at this time, so that the brain and vasana become sattvic. In this place, the Shudra will mix tamarind and give it, making the brain and body tamasic. They were trained like this, calling it Pulikudi adiyanthiram.

We must know what the lives of great men are like. Similarly, we must know what the lives of martyrs are like. But we are kept in caste in such a way that we cannot know the true form of their lives. Hindus, in particular, have kept God in caste and are dancing and singing. All these crazy dances are in the name of God. Even martyrs are included in caste. It was an opportunity to be born in a tradition that came like this. On looking at it, it is good to be born like this in today's situation. As mentioned earlier, this body is to prevent Kali Dharma. Before the body, which is the instrument for prevention, is born, one must know what it is when entering the karma of birth. Otherwise, children of indescribable misfortune will be born. What can be done with these children? Many great souls came to change this misfortune. Based on Yuga Dharma, Krishna, who is seen in the forefront, was a Jnani by birth. We could not even investigate and find out his biography. Why Krishna came is still unknown. The wonderful stories of Krishna from birth are our wealth.

Guru Vani

GURUVANIKAL · June 1995 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0873 to GV2:P0882
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

June 1995

Thus, the mistake that came and went through the four Yugas is not the mistake of this country alone. It is the mistake of the whole world. The karma of rectifying this mistake will happen only in Kali Yuga. In all other Yugas, the path of the respective Yuga Dharmas is taught. If the Guru is qualified to grant liberation, he guides those who come to him according to their qualifications. To a Brahmachari according to his capability, to a householder according to his capability, to a great man according to his capability - thus when guiding him to reach the knowledge that he is doing the karma of the world, he became unable to take it. Because this glory itself cannot be known. Even though Krishna came, it could not be known.

Even though knowledge was given in advance, the ruler Kamsa could not take it - you should not rule like this, you should rule looking at the sadhus. Sadhus means the good people - you should rule only as the Sanyasis (Gurus) say. Kamsa could not take it. Because he was also on the path of that mistake that happened many Yugas ago. For those standing on that path, all aspects of pain will be shown to bring about a special feeling. And when Kali Yuga begins, these mistakes must be rectified. Then we may lose many things. Because Kali is the path that determines one of these - from Deva to Devi, from Devendra to Parashakti, from Shaktheya to Vaishnava - determining that taking one is enough.

But we have been taught exactly the opposite. That Kali is useless. We have lost everything of our own. The only thing left is the Western way. Things happened in such a way that their karma came. Religious theories also came through many great men. But we have become far from all this, or have been made far, in such a way that we cannot carry any of this. Truth has become unknown to us. Krishna came to show this truth. Not only to us, but even to that house it was not understood. Ours became a tradition that could not be understood even after getting a divine voice. Therefore, he also perished. His tradition also perished. Not only that, we lost the statecraft without understanding what it is. That is why the Portuguese, the Dutch, and the Mughals attacked us.

So a mistake happened to our ancestors and teachers. We must know how that happened. God has given us a correction for this. You are unable to understand this special provision given by God. This is the result of the karma done so far.

When our ancestors die, their souls go and stand under this goddess, god, or devil. This ancestral purification is done here so that this soul can get some goodness through birth, or to make it do some goodness after birth. If we tie up the ignorance of our parents and the shortcomings of our souls and go after pleasure, life will only be wasted. This is a time when we are made to do something by reaching some research center in the part called the knowledge series of material pleasure. But we were not taught what freedom is. What is freedom? We must remember that even Mahatmaji received the kicks, spits, and blows of those who attacked and conquered us. After that, God poured many good things upon us. The main ones responsible for that goodness are the Parayi petta panthirukulam. Especially Thiruvalluvar. Even after they all came and spoke, we did not understand what freedom is. Therefore, by giving birth to great souls in foreign lands, that lineage was given the facility to capture the government. They not only came here and subjugated us, but also created a situation where we can live honorably only if we adopt their language and dress. Today the world market is their monopoly. Kali is the time destined to know what we have done to our lives to make this ours.

Through this favor of time, we are taking over and doing the karma of our ancestors. Can we do it? The people of the god of one caste cannot go near the god of another caste. The magnitude of the ignorance formulated among us like this is what has destroyed us. An example is the Saraswat Brahmins. It is assumed that Saraswat Brahmins are the real Brahmins. To some extent, it is true. But how much respect do we give it? We consider it a joke to take some of their words and imitate and mock them under the name of Namboothiri jokes. They are the ones who started living honestly with great difficulty without using any intelligence. But there is a shortcoming that everything went through the path of tantra.

Our ignorance, born and brought up through the trinity system that has crossed many Chaturyugas, has not been able to create anything of our own or maintain what was ours at one time. At the same time, many intelligent people and thinkers were born in foreign lands, developed science and technology, and took over the fields of industry and commerce. All this was here before. Why doesn't what belongs to India become India's own? Because we can only sit around talking about the greatness of our uncle and cultural centers like Nalanda and Takshashila. We didn't do anything.

Thus we drowned in every way. To escape from this drowning, what we need is a way of life according to the Yugadharma. Everyone who came to tell us the way of life according to the Yugadharma made a mistake. This is what we must clearly know. We are sunk in the deception of the story. No matter who does good, an interest will come and destroy it. They will tell a story for that and make us believe it. Just take an example from this Chaturyuga itself to understand that. Even though Mahabali ruled very well, God came, stepped on his head, and pushed him down to the underworld.

Birthday Message

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · August 1995 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0883 to GV2:P0929
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

August 1995

All human beings are continuously receiving knowledge and familiarity from other humans and from all corners of the world. Parts of this knowledge have traveled across different times, lands, and countries. Everyone gives their own account for each part of knowledge. According to their respective ways, each is correct. Mainly, religious theorists record them by studying and teaching about a lineage of gurus, or a lineage of prophets, or an ancient lineage. Followers of the guru path also record it in the same way. If one has to bear witness to all this, or express it in a new language, there are still thinkers today who would say that it is one of the wonders. Even if this wonder is formulated or expressed in words, without the masses practicing and following it, they go about neither obeying nor making others obey through mere words. Can anyone do anything here just with language? A term for this can be stated like this: a slow denier. Nothing can be learned or taught from this.

In the world, the influence of human history belongs to Asia, especially India. It is India that gives the complete account of the Chaturyugas and Manvantaras. It still exists today in such a way that it cannot be proven wrong.

The Yuga system - Krita Yuga (Satya Yuga), Treta Yuga, Dvapara Yuga, Kali Yuga. Its duration has been determined by five gurus known as visionaries. After one Yuga and before the beginning of the next Yuga, a transition period is mentioned. The transition period which is the end and the beginning. It is not possible to determine the time of this transition period entering from one to another. But the Manu tradition determines the time for this. Manusmriti says this; the duration of entering from Krita Yuga to the beginning of Treta Yuga is as long as Krita Yuga. Just as it has been stated and confirmed for Satya Yuga, the duration of the other three Yugas has also been shown. For the Chaturyuga comprising the four Yugas, twelve thousand divine years are mentioned. This is the calculation originating from the Manu tradition.

But this calculation does not mention the period with the special rule of the transition. That is the time when many great men come and work. There are gurus with a vision of five hundred to five thousand, ten thousand years for a Yuga. When taking that in the total calculation of a tradition, it goes to ten thousand years and beyond that, some religious principles, old and new, have regulated it to twenty thousand years and a maximum of up to two lakh years.

According to the calculation of fourteen Manvantaras of Hindus, the time of fourteen Manus together is depicted as one day in the life of Brahma. After that day, then the deluge. Thus, the period of fourteen Manvantaras is depicted as the period of the deluge. But somewhere in between, it got confused and the karma called Nanmukhan (four-faced) went wrong. When that story passes through the Puranas, through some artists, this term Nanmukhan has changed and instead depicted as the face of Brahma. Telling the story through another way, many organs of Brahma are depicted like this. Thus, the imaginary story of Brahma is what completes the Trimurti system. Subramanya, who is of the knowledge tradition, is known as Arumukhan (six-faced). When it came to talking to these faces, the story of Nanmukhan imagined by Brahma itself disappeared. When imagination and poetic fancies mixed and the Yuga dharma and the Trimurti system went wrong, the face of knowledge stopped speaking. In the history of Nanmukhan, which got caught in poetic imagination or the fall of the Puranas, the face of knowledge, which is called the fourth face, was depicted as being pinched off when angry. After that, when it was depicted stopping at the Trimurtis, that face disappeared. That is, the face of knowledge disappeared. In another Puranic saying, when Arumukhan got angry, he pinched off the fourth face. This is the history. Thus, the conceptual deities Brahma, Vishnu, and Shiva were depicted and completed in the place of error.

Coming that way, the concept of the Saptarishis or the history of Manusmriti or the Manu tradition is lost without being seen. Coming back here, wherever the Vedas were written as four, the Shastras as six, and the Puranas as eighteen, the boundary of the six (river) was written and completed, and the sea was created there. Even dividing the boundary of the stream and the lake, it has been tried to be told like the knowledge of the sea. Finally, a word liked by the assembly of scholars was completed by the scholars sitting in the royal assembly as the sea of knowledge!

Thus, adding sound, vowel, and consonant, giving meaning, and giving determination to the chillu letters ള (la) and ണ (na), it was stopped at ഹ (ha), ഭൗ (bhau), മ (ma). Here, whatever meaning the scholar's

You will complete it according to the text. The ocean-like knowledge thus reached there. This is the poets' assembly of historians.

But this is not enough for us. When a mistake occurred in human duty, which should act with light and righteousness for ages, the historians mentioned earlier tried to complete it by calling it the Stone Age and so on. All these efforts have become like trying to untangle a tangled thread thrown into the sea when it gets unraveled.

Be that as it may, can we utilize the opportunity we have received or not? If so, how? The time received means the birth time of great men. That is, as mentioned earlier, the great men who had vision from five hundred years ago, and recently Moses who came after Krishna and the lineage that came with him, and then Buddha who came to India (there are accounts of many Buddhas - among them Gautama Buddha) - there is an account for all this. In addition, the Siddha principles that came at certain times in between, the various principles transmitted through them, such as Yoga principle, Karma Yoga, Hatha Yoga, Chandrika Yoga, Hamsa Yoga, also caused confusion. They did works creating various miracles. When Dr. Somervell traveled in the outskirts of the Himalayas, he saw a person sitting like a skeleton. When he went near, this figure asked: Is Henry IV ruling England now? The karma that completes that penance is amazing, having lived for centuries through rejuvenation therapy and so on, is also here. The karma that states the qualification of penance, that completes the act of penance, has been brought and stopped there. This is a kind of Yoga Siddhi.

If such a miracle is told and diverted, will the face of human knowledge turn? Or if the sutra point in Patanjali Yoga is completed and the karma difference of Yoga Vidya is completed, will it become human duty? In the story of Kabir Das, a disciple of Ramananda, a story of a Gorakh (Gorakhnath) is told. But he could not win against Kabir Das. Not only that, history says he left. Then what is the benefit of this miracle?

But let me tell you what we have been told here. The truth that what should happen according to Brahma's determination is there in the form of the Trinity exactly as it should happen has been experienced. No matter how it is elaborated, it only stands where these three faces are mentioned. The face of knowledge is not seen in it. There is no way to tell the developments when visionaries come there. For that, we need to understand something. If we study about the author of the Gita during the time of Sri Krishna, we will understand some things. History has proven that Krishna was brought deliberately by God's determination. How is that, the history that Kamsa killed the seven children born to that mother even before Krishna was born. Some stories have also been written about it. Whatever it is, a truth is evident. Believing the words spoken by the Gods who appeared, Kamsa could not know the side of the disembodied voice because he did not know the side of the face of knowledge. He could not even know whose disembodied voice spoke to Kamsa saying, "If you do not rule properly, I will send someone to your house."

This is proof that Kamsa could not grasp the truth even after crores of gods in the Trinity system came and told him. He tried mantras, tantras, and Putana to kill Krishna, who came according to Brahma's determination. But nothing worked. The truth that Hindus, who do not fail to tell the history of the Yadava clan where Sri Krishna was born and brought up even today, also failed to respect Krishna along with it cannot be forgotten.

History says that Krishna ran to various parts of India and created many war situations. It details the application of the eighteenth war by the author of the Gita and Arjuna's test of strength. The proof that the people or kings of India did not respect Krishna is that Krishna had to run away to another shore and build Dwaraka from there. Another history says that Krishna, while in deep thought there, was struck by an arrow and fell dead. It was on that occasion that Krishna instructed Arjuna, saying there is no other way to save them, at least take these women and children and cross the dense forests. Vyasa himself says the history that when they went there, in the battle with Jarasandha's men, not even one was left to be saved. Vyasa says thus: Arjuna! When Krishna, the Lord of Yoga, was alive, your physical strength and the strength of your boons bore fruit. But Krishna has passed away. Now none of this will work. Therefore, let us go for penance. Thus, history says they went for penance. Can it be said that the scholars here or anyone else do not know this?

Krishna was born as the eighth son in the eighth state. He also proved that it is the face of wisdom. It is this face of wisdom that reveals the truth to Arjuna in the eighteenth war. Although Vedanta completed many things, the truth is revealed to Arjuna, who cries aloud saying that he finds it difficult to kill great men like Drona and Bhishma and many others. Look at how the poet completes this: Krishna gave his own eyes to Arjuna. But by grasping Krishna's advice, Arjuna sees directly who these great men really are; after that, Arjuna bowed to Krishna and subdued all this. When one remembers that such a Krishna and Arjuna made a mistake, the heart cannot bear or forgive it. How can the sorrowful truth standing in this part be completed?

But look at the evidence we have for this again. The same thing that happened to Krishna has happened to great men of many justifications. The same thing happened to Moses who followed Krishna, Buddha in India, and many great men who came with and without caste and religion.

If we take about twenty-five historical figures, the Prophet is one great man among them. The same thing happened to the Prophet who came in the exact same path that Jesus came. Look at what that great man, who thought that there is nothing but You, has told us; what is this that has been shown to me, who came believing that God and Truth are only one! Jinn are ruling the world, alas!

What does anyone have to say to this? This great man did not say this for one caste or religion, he said it for the human race as a whole. What could we, who came believing and not believing, do about this? It was the Prophet, who attained Samadhi in the seventh state, who said that Jinn are ruling the world. Did he tell a lie, or did he say it for the sake of the world? What else could we do till today other than worship these Jinn? We, who worship gods, goddesses, yogis, sages, ascetics, wise men, and saints in the language of each religion, are actually worshipping Jinn. After that, how many unrespected great men have we seen till today. Radhaswami is one of them. But there is no Guru-disciple relationship.

What is the Guru-disciple relationship?

The Guru-disciple relationship is taking the waking state, dream, and vision to the Turiya state and correcting the final vision in many ways. For one state (one step), there are seven thousand four hundred and forty-four visions. The one who completes seven steps in that path is the one who has seen the presence of God. Then comes the vision of God, the vision of Brahman, and the vision of Parabrahman. A special state that cannot be described about this

It is because they exist that the sorrow of these great men cannot be removed. First, they must be liberated. After that, their lineage must be liberated. Thus, only a Guru who comes through the divine will can recognize whether the aforementioned disembodied voice is true or not, without using sleight of hand, occult powers, or knowledge. The one who rejects knowledge and ignorance, completes duality and non-duality, and makes things look good does not know this. Only a soul who is born completing the eighth state by birth, who has taken countless millions of births thirsting only for knowledge, can realize the word of Brahman.

Another proof for this: Showing in a vision who all these mentioned great men were and how they came, and realizing who stands where in the different states of those times. Our destined duty is to find the self-sacrifice that each of such great men has done for us and knock on the door. Karma is not a clown who argues and wins by saying whether it exists or not.

Vision is when the ascetic and the wise man who have put the soul on the path of virtue come together, show the mistakes and good deeds that have happened, and correct them. This vision joined with seven thousand four hundred and forty-four is one state. The completion of the seven states (steps) - that cannot be achieved by Vedanta. This is the difference in states where, having reached the state of offering the soul by the soul, the stream of light is classified by seven, seven thousand, and seven thousand four hundred and forty-four.

Believing without a doubt that we all owe a debt to the divine will that proved this to be true, one should practice as a Guru the one who corrects by reaching the self-vision of 'Jagrat-svapna-sushupti-natham sakshipoorthi namo namah'. This should not be completed through the words of the best of scholars. This is the calculation of the stream of light completed by self-vision. The language of the Vedic scholar for this is:

Antarjyothi bahirjyothi

Pratyakjyothi paratpara

Jyothirjyothi swayam jyothi

Atmajyothi shivosmyaham

This is the Tantric side. But none of this is our sorrow, at least let us start studying from the time of Sri Krishna; we should point out the Hindu dharma that has come from then till today and the Kali dharma that has passed until yesterday and exists today, respecting the words of the great souls who had the knowledge to bow down and express sorrow to that great power who sent them to work for this before leaving the body at the end after working in the knowledge and science systems that are said to be progressive.

What is written as Kali is good and not good? It is a sad sight to see scholars who logically assign meanings even to vowels and consonants falling and rotting. The wheel of time has caught us to recognize this Kali dharma given by the divine will to pass on at least to the coming generation. That is, complete the duty of a householder by presenting to our grandchildren or the coming generation the truth shown to us with cause and effect of the dharma that came, has come, or is going to come to implement the divine action denoted by the four times.

If one can present the sacrifice, love, and respect of the wise and the scientists, each one becomes a special dharma that openly shows the truth. Having all kinds of

The wheel of time has returned to us the brilliant sacrifice of the aforementioned martyrs, enabling seekers of knowledge to learn and realize the truth of everything through experience. The purpose of this is to provide an opportunity even for our successors to explore that colorful sacrifice, both existing and upcoming. Kali is the special cycle of time opened for us by the divine will for this purpose. This is a special dharma intended for the people of this Kaliyuga and all living beings to experience and understand.

I am repeatedly telling you, isn't Kali the best of times? Our life's goal should be to complete the course of dharma—described as gross, subtle, causal, and effect—by replicating in every atom and the whole, the brilliant sacrifice of the great prophets of the past who shared a noble life, or who completed their lives struggling with body and soul, and the great sages who shared their lives for us.

Let us share forever the pain of revealing the truth through time and righteous living, and the pure blessedness experienced and fulfilled. This is what I have tried to convey to you till today through the birthday message. The teaching of the Guru is to show the life revered by Guru and disciple, which is our nectar of pure consciousness and harmonious—the path of liberation of Shantigiri ordained by Brahman! The truth of truth! The source of blessedness! The pure, brilliant genius!

Guru Vani

September 1995

We fought each other caught in the system of special rules of all actions—we cannot touch this, we cannot go there, we cannot take that, we cannot see that. In that fighting action, the Varna-Ashrama dharma was lost. The caste system came. Castes cannot touch each other. In this practice, all actions were corrupted. We are reclaiming that corrupted action. Even though many tried, they could not reclaim it. The action in Shantigiri is a return to a casteless state. The goal is not just to develop a monastic order. Nor is it to search for and lift up the knees of Indian culture. There is no need to lift it up anymore. Because this is Kali. The dharma of Kali is not to uplift by standing on the shoulders of the wrongs that are standing high all over the world. Our action is to destroy the wrong part. That is also the dharma of Kali.

In this action, men and women who are householders must acquire a great position. Grihasthashrama is a great principle where a householder rises and, through his children and lineage, takes up the intelligence to rule the world and the action to impart goodness to the world, becoming world protectors or even world motherhood itself. Without knowing this, you are living as opportunists and perishing.

You do not know what worship of God is. You do not even know how to distinguish and act upon what a householder, a sanyasi, a yogi, or a worker is. None of these actions are in our possession now. Our life's quest is to bring this back. This is the first action in the world. This action has not happened anywhere else. If it comes anywhere else in the future, let it come; we have reached the humble action of God's will.

When I first came here to reach this action, there was an announcement not to sit at a junction where two people are not seen together. Something happened here then. The locals also know a little bit. But even though they knew, nothing special happened. When I first built a hut here, I entrusted some of the families here to look after it. You came after seeing the downfall of those families. What is that downfall? They have come here and seen all this. Even though many things happened in front of them, the lack of desire to take it back gripped them and alienated them. Alienated them in such a way that they could never come back or take it back at any time. Some of them say today that it was a bad action. There is no use in them saying so.

The action being done here today is to mold the children who are to give birth to the class of God, Goddess, Sage, Sanyasi, and Sage, whom our ancestors worshipped, loved, and respected. No one who has come so far has done this action. They did not allow it to be God for them. That action has been allowed to us as God. You should know that. But whatever is said, you do not understand it. Even though more than ten thousand families have done Guru Pooja in this institution, there are not even a handful of people who know and live in that greatness. Then how will you take back this action? Many children of this land who were associated with this forgot it. I haven't told them anything. When they come to me, I sometimes try to tell it like a story. But they don't feel the pain. Why? Our ancestors are the ones who made epics and legends into stories, turned those stories into plays, and turned those plays into nonsense. Each of us is the children of these ancestors.

When we say that our subject is to correct this ignorance, what is needed is your children. We are not even able to bring these children forward. Because the dharma that we worshipped and made others worship, and offered a lot of wealth of love, is surrounding our lives. That is, if a girl is born, she is made bad in such a way that her goodness cannot be known. If a boy is born, he is made a drunkard in such a way that he cannot know what action he has to do for this world. It is very difficult to change these bad things. This difficulty is also good in a way. Because at least in the name of that, you will work hard.

There is a saying among us: Husband is God! So is this scoundrel who has completed it by stealing and has never shown justice - is this the meaning of that word? Does it mean a nonsense person who lives by losing humility and virtue without learning to walk in justice? We are creating all these. How are they running this country? What all will this mischief not do?

The first action I am doing for you for this is the Guru Pooja here. This is not about my greatness. I don't know greatness like that. I am not an educated person. I am not great by wealth, ability, or education; God has not brought me to any of that. But there is one thing, God has given a field that anyone can reach. Through this

Here are the children who are meant to give birth to many great souls capable of doing what is needed not just for this country but for the whole world, passing it down through you, your children, and your lineage. We do not even know how to create this. Seeing the gods and goddesses and everything that has come according to the law until today, giving them what they need (not rejecting them), and seeing them in the path shown by God to claim victory or liberation, we are doing it merely by the resolve of 'Parameshwara'. To do this, we are handling the divine voice we received from God exactly as it is.

This is not, as some people think, done by chanting with a lit fire and capturing some ghost to do the work. Nor is it done by bringing a root from the places where I have lived. I have not taken anyone's root. I have not found any situation suitable for taking this root. There are no suitable roots for it either. By hollowing out all these stumps, setting fire to them, filling them with good soil, planting a sapling there, and nurturing it to grow. For that, suitable children are needed. You do not know how I am doing this, and that is my sorrow.

I am making you perform the karma that elevates them to a great level by rectifying the flaws in that tradition, so that the karma in the lineage of gods and sages that has come from the beginning until today does not break. Then what should be your greatness? It is an acquaintance that humbles by bringing into you the right to be born for ancestors, gods, goddesses, sages, and even some ghosts standing as eunuchs. Maintain the truth in that acquaintance and transform yourselves into karma combined with merit originating from that truth. This is the goal. I have no other logic or theory. I have done this for fifteen thousand families in this manner. They are unable to take it. Moreover, its adverse effect is built into their karma, causing it to become a reason for destruction. About fifteen houses in this area, which made prayers and resolves and sacrificed a lot for this, have been destroyed in that state; and the remaining houses have left this area.

There is right or wrong in the ancestors. The difficulty encountered when trying to remove this is very great. To know that, you will understand if you look at the great souls who have passed away. But the biographies fabricated about them are not suitable to be examined in that way. An example is Gita Govindam. Why are children made to perform Krishna Leela? To impart more emotionalism than what is in them, children are decorated in this manner, degraded, raised, and the completion of that karma is added. This is what the Shakta, Shaiva, and Vaishnava here have done. You will never be able to change this. This is the matter behind the arrival of so many great men. We do not have the experience to change this within a lifetime. But there is luck.

You will not be able to see such a thing in any part of the world. Such a karma has never happened in the world until today. Your stories talk about the Bhagiratha effort. Bhagiratha was a king. He did many things. But even in that, this was not achieved. Here, knowledge is being given to you in such a way that you can see and understand it yourselves. That is what God has destined for you. It is in this knowledge that the god, goddess, sage, ascetic, wise man, devotee, and father are corrected of their mistakes and placed in a good position. Your children are living truly and entering into that position. Even if you could not become like that, you should at least be able to make your children do it. This is the great karma you do to the world. I am telling you how you should be from the time I do this karma until the children are born. You do not understand that.

Instead, by telling them something else as a big deal and making them listen and obey, you do not allow your children to come forward in dharma.

GURUVANIKAL · October 1995 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0930 to GV2:P0941
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani October 1995

You do not know an action to earn merit through birth. Therefore, children without dharma are born to you. Children without dharma are not eligible to earn merit. Children who are not eligible for merit never become heirs to fortune. We have all fallen completely into such karma. The intended action with your children is to liberate them from this condition.

The earth has a magnetic field. Similarly, the nine planets have magnetic fields. It stands as the solar system. How have we understood this? Very few people have understood this properly. It is not good to apply knowledge in this. There is a set of people who do not have the innate tendency to believe whether God exists or not. They are the ones who apply yoga, rituals, and knowledge in this. The state of light inherent in the body is brought about by bodily exercise according to the rules of yoga. That is something that only suits oneself. It is of no use to anyone else. Some words in that rule will match. Some words will not match. That is their own shortcoming.

Some people get thousands of visions. That is, they come in a way that cannot be expressed in words. It can be seen whether the eyes are open or closed. It is an action where the mind ceases to function. This

Karma comes to the purity of man. There are those who get visions at every border without continuous vision. This nature as a whole, that is, the vision that shows what this worldly nature is. For that, he only needs faith. Faith in a word. The word of a clear living soul, like Jesus, like the Prophet, or one who has moved away from sectarian ways like Shaivism, Shaktism, and Vaishnavism - faith in that word. This should be a soul who has known at least up to the state of the Trinity. Or else, they will be taken to some corner and left there. The vision will become a mad vision. That is one. Another is seeing karma standing in a subtle state and how it is done and made to be done in the gross state. There are many such ways of vision. Whatever the way, one should proceed without thinking for oneself, holding on to the Guru and taking refuge in the Guru.

Visions will go on without limit. It will go on in a way that cannot be expressed in words. For example, if we start on a journey, what all will we see until we reach that destination. So, what all would one see if they were to travel around this whole world. There is such a vision. Having seen it like that, one will meet the Guru again. That is its theory. If we set out on a path, we will focus only on the main path. Other things will keep passing by. Another thing is, even if that person is a visionary, Guru, or prophet, for how long and who all can use it. Who all will get this kind of vision and how. This is the vision that shows madness in return - mad vision.

There is a vision that shows many past lives. A vision where everything done in whatever period in the relationship between disciple and Guru comes in sequence. If the disciple is not that knowledgeable, he will be given a vision in a way of lesser knowledge and brought up. An knowledge will be given showing what the disciple's soul has been purified of through many births. For man, there are colors that are knowledge and non-knowledge within the five senses themselves. Standing on that path that goes through the five senses, five sheaths, and five elements, one must go to another path. Whatever the disciple is entitled to will come. The Guru will not ask if you did this or saw that. That is the Guru's qualification. The disciple must also have a qualification. Otherwise, it will stop after seeing one, two, or ten visions. Even if the disciple comes as one with no qualification, if he takes refuge only in the Guru, he will get the prescribed knowledge of what the movements of the soul that has lived and passed as many signs with his life are, knowing that, what the karma following that knowledge is, and how the hymns of karma are. Even in this, there are variations. One variation is a way where one sees all the attributes of nature, sees the nature of this earth, and sorts out how the action of all these souls is. When speaking of special rules, it is the evolutionary difference of the soul growing from an ant (an ant is a soul that has learned to observe according to its knowledge for its own life) up to an elephant, lion, or rhinoceros. The nature that each must receive for the action of all these souls will be contained in that soul in every way. For example, a theater prop. How many props, big and small, can there be for the need of a play. Similarly in the cinema system. All these are courses of karma captured from this nature. If Vedantins say that all these courses of karma are non-existent, will they cease to exist? Should Vedantic words make them non-existent? Will the good and bad karma experienced cease to exist? There is a capacity for action where the body, mind, and intellect work, will it happen if one says it doesn't exist?

Similarly, what kind of conditions this body has accepted can only be known through the evolution of this soul. One must see the activities of one's own soul, as well as those of many other souls. We see male, female, small, large, and so on. How these bodies change roles according to age - father, mother, husband, wife. Is all this an illusion? Or else a soul should be able to rise to the level of that karma. That is, the soul's capacity for transformation came about like this because of a master plan. It is the special transformation of the soul, taking thousands of births over many periods, that is contained in them. As one grows up like this, a vision of a demon comes, and the thousands of activities of many kinds of demons - one becomes convinced of it oneself. All this is coming before us. Or else those concepts that we and our ancestors worshipped, those roles, their creations, their light, all this comes together before us. Then this karma, which is imagined by Brahma, this cause which is gross, subtle, and causal, is known by the soul. When we close our eyes, we see a model of thousands of births.

We often say, I slept well yesterday, slept without any movement, and so on. Because of sleeping, the body gets rest. Keeping away the karma of the many natures of the soul, its activities, or doing nothing. The soul only knows that it just lay down and slept comfortably like this. That is rest for the body. What if it is a subconscious sleep? Went there, went here, that came, this came, saw like that, saw like this, I became a slave to the police, a slave to the king, many mischievous people harassed me, they all caught me, tied me up and threw me in the forest. Some were received very happily. After all this, then only oneself and the Guru. This is the drama. The special rules within this drama are all these things seen.

Farming in one place, many kinds of farming in one place, a desert with nothing in one place, thus travelled and saw many lands. Saw south, north, east, and west. In some places, somehow - getting on vehicles and not getting on. Travelled even in parts where there are no vehicles. How it was when there were vehicles, how it was when there were none, how it was when there were no objects..... Seeing that it is a karma that one's soul has passed through like this, again only the Guru and oneself.

One might have done many good and bad things with one's life. Seeing a karma that cannot be told to each other, even to oneself and to those who can be said to be the most dear, again only oneself and the Guru. What was the evolutionary state of one's soul in. The soul acted in what has life and what does not, the soul has experience acting in moving and non-moving things. We have also seen that subject of moving the non-moving. Saw that it is like this, its life is in this order. Saw it acting in everything from ants to elephants, reptiles, many birds, thousands of flying creatures. Saw why these reptiles, flying creatures, and birds lived that life.

Saw thousands of patients and many karmas nursing the disease, many karmas finding medicine for it, physicians bringing treatments. Saw that in the field where poison should not be given, that poison itself is prepared and given, and similarly everything is prepared and given. Then, from these leaves that are prepared and given, to whatever is in nature, utilizing all of it, it is about the virtuous aspects of that karma that is experienced and finished as one's body and one's soul, this is, in short, what can be called vision.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · November 1995 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0942 to GV2:P0955
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

November 1995

To explain and see nature, one needs seven thousand four hundred and forty-four visions. There are seven such worlds. One group calls them heaven, the netherworld, this world, and the other world. Another group dismisses this saying no such thing exists.

Karma is said to be the active capacity of life. Karma and the vision originating from it are connected to the worlds. So, for the worlds to cease to exist, shouldn't the active capacity of life come to an end? How long does life function? Or, one must look at how long it has been since karma began to cease, like a dried-up tree standing still. Saying it doesn't exist won't make it so. When that tree lying in the soil completely decays into the soil itself, that karma will end. Similarly, no one has the right to say that this karmic world—where humans are born and raised, ruling over and being ruled by thousands of people, and performing karma through life—does not exist. Can the one who says it doesn't exist refrain from using this earth? If one wants to argue with words that all this is an illusion and none of this exists, such a person should stop using this earth. One walks around without wearing clothes. Another dresses up in all sorts of costumes and walks around like a clown. One walks around showing various antics like a madman. Some walk around showing all the qualifications that the civilized world calls merit, while having no actual merit at all. If one is perverse, another engages in the karma of eternal existence without any perversity whatsoever. Who can say that this karma, which exists in thousands of such ways, does not exist?

Why is he born who says he is neither born nor dead? Why should he eat, not eat, speak, or not speak? Vedantins say that there is only a feeling that 'I am'. Then let his body be cut down. Does he who says 'no' not think that he has to eat, dress, bite, lie down, and walk? If a person - a sannyasin - uses various medicines and takes care of his body, then he does not need food or anything else. He goes to some place and sits there. Even if a snake bites him, only the snake will die. If he goes and sits in a place like this, he can sit there for thousands of years without knowing anything. Even if someone who has been sitting like this without knowing anything for six or seven generations wakes up now, he will ask if the king who ruled then is still ruling today.

Someone from Mexico came and told me, Carlos, you saw there, saw here, did such and such things, I have done such and such things for so many births. This is a vision. Thousands of people come and tell me what they see in thousands of ways. They have done good and bad things. He experiences all these karmas or, in the form of maya, surrounds him and disappears. Or this body is the evolution of the jiva that has been surrounding him for thousands of births. He will talk about the many births he has had with that body and the thousands of karmas he has done.

What is all this seen? Waking, dreaming, deep sleep. In it, you can see a lineage that believes in a guru. If you ask standing in that lineage, there will be people capable of explaining. That is called the traditional guru-disciple relationship. Didn't I give a proof - Carlos said thousands of births. That is the guru-disciple relationship. If this guy sitting in Mexico saw this sitting there and had to tell it up to here! So many people like that.

This earth has a universal truth, a special power. The earth stands as a sphere. Similarly, this sky filled with thousands of spheres. The sun and the other nine planets circling as a solar system. These nine also rotate. Only the sun remains stationary - the solar system! Connecting with this solar system, twenty-seven stars, twelve zodiac signs, and nine planets have been calculated. There is no need to do anything to the person mentioned by measuring, adding, and subtracting its size and distance. He didn't come for any of that. What has he become? How bad has he become, how good has he become - he only thinks about this. The guru only explains this. There is a secret of life standing in the nature of many jivas, knowing and unknowingly, in the measurement and weight of this star and in its cause and effect. This cannot be known in one lifetime. This is where the guru's importance lies.

Seeing such worlds and paths of travel, he and the guru are talking. He and the guru are talking about good and bad. This is the journey to the path of truth. The guru will not ask if you have had such experiences in life. The disciple must tell the guru. Then the guru explains this secret of life, like Sri Ramakrishna to Vivekananda. Sri Ramakrishna reached a certain position and showed that position to his disciple.

In waking, dreaming, deep sleep, and then in both states of consciousness, one will see without any special rules. He is seeing his life and his karma-residue, in waking, dreaming, deep sleep, and in sitting and in both. It is in sitting that this turns - what god, what goddess

What is it, what is a sage, what is a sannyasi, what is a guru, thus the difference in gurus becomes clear. When it comes to that, the guru must teach it. Not just seeing, it can be seen clearly. Then, who is Vivekananda, who are the great sages who have come so far, who are the prophets who have come so far. All this must be included in this guru. One who is not like that is not a guru. Everything will be clearly obtained.

Thus, in this guru-disciple relationship over thousands of births, thousands of people would have been seen. There is no need to say whether it exists or not. It has been coming and going for many births like this. But the only relationship one has - only oneself and the guru. Even though one has thousands of relationships over many births, in the end, one sees only oneself and the guru. Therefore, no one else but the guru can fulfill this life of one's own.

The course of karma of one person is not like the course of karma of another person. Therefore, the guru does not interact with another person in the same way as interacting with one person. We will see ourselves involved in the karma of the whole world. All this will be seen in dreams. After the guru explains and teaches about the waking, dreaming, and deep sleep states, he will then pass on to the turiya state. The nature of the guru is to come in many characters, removing and taking up, doing and making do, knowing and making known, all this course of karma in the nature of all the prophets and all the gurus who have come so far. Vedantins ask why one looks at all these coming as mother, father, wife, children, husband, and so on in thousands of ways. If Vedantins interpret with letters, will this karma disappear?

Guru consciousness is of many kinds. One has to learn from the guru what a solar system is like. Thus, when passing through the seven spheres, form is lost. Then there is no form. Jesus talks about the third sphere in it. That an angel comes among the silver clouds. Thus, after each sky sphere, when passing through six sky spheres, form will disappear.

Now, this earth has a sky sphere. The sky sphere is called the open space. Thus, all seven sky spheres have open space. Thus, all nine sky spheres have open space. These differences must be corrected by the guru himself. There is no other medicine for it.
