GURUVANIKAL · December 1995 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0956 to GV2:P0970
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani December 1995 We have received a traditional knowledge. That knowledge is different from the way we have traditionally maintained it. Our shortcoming is the ignorance that occurred as a result of our highly uncultured worship, separated by caste, religion, and class. Because it came traditionally, we have not been able to have any realization about it or know the reason why it happened.

Many great souls came among us and tried to retrieve this by practicing and doing karma. We have been told that two thousand four hundred and forty-four (2444) gurus came for this purpose in this Kaliyuga itself. This five thousand two hundred

Even though so many masters, including Sree Krishna, have come within these years, we have not understood what this karma is. It was also a great mistake that they wrote stories and taught us in a way that concealed that great karma. Then there is only one family tradition to say that there is no mistake. The Yadava clan.

There is a family tradition called the Yadava clan. It came from the Shudra class. Coming from its lower strata, even now that group has a family instinct to live with the cow and its kind, not only in North India but here as well. We do not think about why this Shudra class was given royal status. The reason we cannot think like that is because it came in the nature of the completion of the mistake that has occurred over a long period of time.

Without completing this Yuga Dharma, no other classes can do anything. How will the Yuga Dharma be completed? Not being able to know this is the biggest mistake that has happened to us. Satya Yuga (Krita Yuga) is spoken of as an era where everything happened truthfully, without caste, religion, or class. But when Treta Yuga came, we were not told how the nature of Treta Yuga should be. A little of the tradition of the karma done by man in the beginning of Treta Yuga is still among us today. The very practice of going to the forest as sanyasa is the greatest example of that. We have not been told anything about the nature of Dwapara either. Going and sitting in the forest is not sanyasa at all. And one should not assume so. Sanyasa is something that should be done in a way that suits the times.

We are reclaiming the Yuga Dharma. It is said that a quarter of that era will be spent just for reclaiming the Yuga Dharma. But we have no information about it, or if there is anything, everything is God! We do not have the knowledge to say anything beyond God's illusion. This is the current state of Hinduism today. The situation of other religions is not different either. People of other religions also believe that they were born in the homes of small people, or that they should come like a king. We do not have a sense of certainty about it. The clarity in the belief that God's arrangement will come in a lower class, or like a king, or in Indian culture, or in other cultures, is the sense of certainty.

Because we did not have this sense of certainty, we have passed on absolute ignorance. Sree Krishna is one of those born in various positions to remove this ignorance. Krishna was born even before the beginning of Kali Yuga. He was born like that thinking that if Kali starts, the order will go wrong. But it was not possible. Nothing that came after that could achieve it either.

So the ignorance of the Dharma of the era exists among all humanity. Moses came. Jesus came. None of them came to create caste or religion. But what do we do in the nature of worship? First, the church nature came in eighteen forms. Now it has split into sixty-four and exists. Some worship with a cross. It is said that this itself has split into small parts and now there are more than two hundred forms. If this happened in a religious theory that came within just four thousand years, what would not happen with the passage of time!

Whichever way you look at it, this has come as a flaw that happened in the beginning itself. Therefore, we must get the original knowledge entirely from God Himself. Then one might ask, were none of these who came born from God? Or was that knowledge hidden because of their mistake? It is not because of any of that, when they grow up, they

It takes us time to understand. Once we understand, it takes time to obey them. As for those who have obeyed, they begin to think scientifically about what they have known and seen after some time. Even if we look at the history of great souls, the knowledge we have acquired about them is like this.

What is needed to bring about a change from this knowledge? This is what is called Pratyakshavaham Dharmam. But our theoreticians will say that getting the vision of God is enough, everything is done. It is wrong to assume so. For that, completing the Deva tradition entirely, then completing the Rishi tradition, and only then comes the arrangement that shows what Sanyasa is. That is its path. When such a great soul is alive, or in the actions that have come by relying on and rooting in that reliance, what all were we able to take? We could not know any of this. So, going into the profound depths of knowledge that we have absolutely no realization of, we portray it all as miraculous powers. Without reaching a clear understanding of a great soul's life, turning magical powers and mantras - then tantra and yantra - into tools; what happened at the beginning of that life, what happened in the middle, what happened at the end, and how this life duty should be repeated to obey the Yugadharma - we have become ignorant of all this.

In this situation, what can we impart to our children? You are householders. Each of you has a course of action that enables you to grow with the home and live with the land. Can children grow up knowing the danger that has befallen this home, land, and country? Apart from them and us living according to the course of action prevailing in the world, what do they get? Then all they get is only what those called historians have written and piled up. There is no time to study everything written. Then what will they study? What can we, who do not have enough time to study and teach about each era like this, do? Fighting over caste, religion, and class, standing in actions where we do not even live together with our children, not knowing the Yugadharma, not knowing and following it, standing in the denial of God, what can we do?

If each caste has a different God, how can you say that God is one? If you and I have two Gods, what is the significance? Or we all have God, but all these Gods are on different paths. Then how can we unite? Learning the actions of each caste as opposite to each other, turning opposite actions into caste, creating actions through caste, thus everything is done in opposition to each other. However, there has been some change today.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · January 1996 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0971 to GV2:P0981
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

January 1996

Today everyone is getting the karma to be free. It is the special rule of the time that has come for five thousand years. Anyone can do anything from carpentry to Vedic karma. The government itself is making it happen. Everyone can get an education. Because we did not have an education, we had the ignorance of calling upon God by talking about caste and religion. The serpent worship that originated in ancient times, the kalari that originated in various ways after that, deity worship, the rituals and karma to gain strength and ferocity in various forms of worship, and other special rules - how will our children understand this tradition that we have gained? What do the father and mother teach about this? If we can select and study the things taught by the father, mother, and others, or the right and wrong that have come in the Vedic system, can we achieve anything? Since we are affected by all kinds of shortcomings in this way, it will take a lot of time to fulfill the Yuga Dharma. Moreover, once a Yuga Dharma begins, it will change into another.

It is seen from the calculation that Kali is the shortest period. Kali has four lakh thirty-two thousand years. Can we call upon God after this long period is over? The word is that Kali will not be accepted. How did Kali become unacceptable? No one should assume that Kali will not be accepted. Kali is good for everyone. Our ancestors have taught us till today that Kali is destruction. It is because of the evil of Kali that children behave incorrectly. Thus, completing the good and bad of Kali is what has been known till today. How can we be complete in this knowledge? How can we know and act upon at least the Dharma we have? This will become a karma passed on by the ancestors, which will never be suitable.

God has given birth to many great people here and there. You should respect the people God sends to fulfill the karma mentioned earlier when they come before you. But you have not been able to do it so far. You did not respect Krishna, who came as a born wise man. You covered it with a story, turned it into the most corrupted story; turned it into a story and a play, and it is shown as Kathakali or by imparting all the madness in a drama. And how much can we gain in the karma of showing and informing that this is the special intellect of the time? When we look at each of these like this, we have not understood at all that God has been working for us for so long. Then you can ask, it is said that God is everything, then why not just die a single death. God will not die like that. You have heard a calculation for a long time - Kalpa. If you look at it with that calculation itself, it is talking about the karma that has come for so many Chaturyugas and the Kalpa that has been acting and not acting for so long, or the karma that has come as Manvantaras. Can we just go straight after studying all this? No. Then what should be done? If a prophet or an Acharya or any such Mahatma comes according to the times, how will we know about this person? This is what is called everyone's lack of knowledge.

In the past history, Kali Yuga is the shortest. How can we, who cannot even take and see the past three periods, know that long history about this Kali Yuga? How can historians who have no clear understanding of anything, who have taken something said by someone and written something, create a clear calculation and tell the history of a long entity? No matter how much it is extended

In short, all they can say, and that too unclearly, is just the story of the last ten thousand years.

Even the great souls who came after Sri Krishna were born in very low categories to try and reform us. But we didn't understand it. We each fell and rotted in a course of karma where we couldn't respect anything, draining the good essence and becoming like gathered trash. In that state, we have become bad.

How can this situation be corrected? A great soul himself must tell you that. No one else can do it. Only the individual who has earned the right over the ages can correct you. How will you recognize the person who comes to correct you? How will you know that it is the truth? The karma you heard, saw, knew, and worshipped has reached the most corrupt state, turning into numerous seeds of evil that we ourselves created to prevent our own betterment, and we are each the remnants of that. How can we become better? Through what will we improve? We don't even know this karma. Then how can our father and mother explain it to us?

Look at a recent picture, of a Pandya king. The description of this Pandya's wife is like this: This Pandya's alliance is from Kanchipuram. In Kanchipuram, there are massive carvings and similar grand things. In Sanskrit, it is said, 'Nagarishu Kanchi' (Among cities, Kanchi). Thus, having studied about Kanchipuram, the 'Nagarishu Kanchi', we keep praising it. Whatever it may be, regarding the educational wealth of this city, we can agree that it is the greatest thing in our country. It is said that the Pandya who married and brought this woman built various kinds of temples. When it came to the karma of what is called her favorite deity, the Pandya built the Madurai Meenakshi Temple. When the temple was properly finished, the king asked this woman: Now, who is greater, your father or me? The woman said, this doesn't have an inch of the greatness of what my father built. It is said that the woman's head was cut off. The beheaded woman and him have been sculpted and placed there.

How do we know history from this? Because we have been rolled through such untrue history and fabricated stories, we have no information about anything. With that, a narrative of this kind emerged. The first thing that happened was that if one needed to cut, dig, and eat, iron tools had to be invented first. We know how long it has been since this iron thing was invented. But cutting and digging were done on this earth long before that. How was that done? They have deceived us by saying this. All these stories we see today did not happen in one or two Chaturyugas; they have sprouted and flourished over thousands of Chaturyugas. Occasionally getting destroyed, hidden, overflowing again, and flooded with water, it has evolved to this state. Thus, changes will come according to the times.

But this is not what we need to understand regarding ourselves. We must absolutely find a special dharma to act for their good and our good, after knowing how our ancestors lived and the sad stories of their lives. Because we don't have this special dharma, we don't know about karma. Therefore, we internalize all the madness shown by these caste fanatics, and in that internal struggle, life becomes a competing exhaustion. To give strength to this, some bad things are fabricated into stories in the name of God.

GURUVANIKAL · February 1996 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P0982 to GV2:P1008
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

February 1996

The mistake that has occurred due to our system of worship is very great. The malpractices that each of us is caught up in - celebrating these malpractices, showing them as special, our ignorance written, studied, and passed down through generations - the harm cultivated is even greater than this. What happened because of that? Class hatred emerged, and in everything, sowing this destruction as lord and slave, reaping it has become our lot. How can it be right to say that all this happened by the will of God? How can this be handled?

Today, we must think about where we stand in the act of worshipping and making others worship. For each religion, their own God stands great as the author of the Vedas. For each caste, the God belonging to their caste is great. The lower caste has no God. As a result of building slavery in this way, we lost the knowledge to worship God. This is the truth. So, we should at least have the knowledge that worshipping God like this is not enough. Understanding this, we need an introduction to recognize what God is, what a god and goddess are, what a sage is, what a sanyasi is, what a wise man is, what a karma yogi is, and what a devotee is. Can you be led straight by introducing all these one by one? In a way that it cannot be recognized why we were corrupted by being taught like this, we acquired vile knowledge by studying and teaching grammar and logic. Therefore, something came here that makes you unable to believe that the good knowledge was not what existed in our country.

We say that the first university in the world was in India (Nalanda, Takshashila). Thus, we must take a look at the state of the country. We have a wretched habit of not being able to think and understand all things like that. Therefore, if there is a familiarity among us of thinking and understanding or being liberated and understanding, we must try to understand by keeping it in the forefront. Otherwise, we will not be able to understand anything. Because you did not do so, you fell behind in the act of preserving knowledge. A historian or a person born only for the dharma of an era cannot preserve this knowledge.

In the act of preventing Kali, who is each of us, what is the reason for this knowledge, and how do we stand with the effect in that cause - we must reach this sense of action. If we speak highly while supporting the fallen historical objects, where will we end up? If we insist that the history since the time writing began is the truth, when will we be able to know about the Yuga Dharma and the Chatur Yuga? To know about the Chatur Yuga, it is impossible without the knowledge of the Yuga Dharma. First, one must know the dharma of the Chatur Yuga. After that, one must know the dharma of each Yuga.

How should the dharma of Kali Yuga, which stands at the fourth quarter of this Chaturyuga, be imparted? We have no information about this. Because we have lined up behind the custodian who lived by following someone else and made it into a science. The technologies that create today's war materials were learned from the logic called Nyayashastra. If aviation science was first developed in India, why couldn't India take it? Indian science has thus gone to many places. They are selling it for money. India, however, could not make anything, not even a pin. We have only had a history and a government that could only degrade us. What else have we been able to do here other than fight with each other? Have parents been able to give at least one truth that our future generations can respect? In this state, everyone has become one born in the lap of dead and decayed ignorance.

God has given you an opportunity to think about what is needed to change this. Today we have many good insights of modern science. We do not think about why it came about in this era. Or even if a habit of thinking develops, it is only capable of thinking about some histories and nothing else.

We have a path of dharma to inquire whether anything has extended beyond this stream of thought. Neither the people of this country nor the people of any country are able to take it. The result of this is the unrest seen today. The government has sown the seeds of destruction everywhere by playing and making others play. The people reap them. Historians also travel along that path but are unable to convince the people about its ravages. Moving forward in this state, we have no right to speak a truth about the world, the country, or ourselves. Whether educated or uneducated, a personality is one who can complete an action to see the truth during their lifetime.

What should those who are alive now do? Is it enough to pass on the rest of the story woven by our ancestors to our children? If so, we have nothing more to say than what our father and mother said. It is not in history either. Then where can we find it? Will we get it if the great uncle comes dancing?

The people of Ambalappuzha and Cherthala have a little more of this kind of work. There is a Satyagraha Ashram there. I think it is a school now. Why was it built? Why did your ancestors conduct satyagraha there? If they fought so much for the freedom to walk outside the walls of that temple, for the freedom to walk on the road, we can think how great we are. Instead, big stories have been fabricated. If we cut open the root of this ignorance we are holding onto, will we get any knowledge from it? This is what our contemporary history has shown us. We have studied that history without knowing its reality. You must think whether we need this history or not. If you feel you need an alternative, you must have a dharma for it, if not directly, at least by hearsay. And an idea to act on it. There must be a purpose of action that imparts mind and intellect. It will not work if you try to complete your life with any idea you get.

Each of you is a worker of day-to-day life. His loss, profit, digging up the roots of the working class - digging up the roots of the capitalist by making them pay wages without working - this is all the knowledge this world has given us. Similarly, what all works have been done as caste, religion, and class. Standing in this familiarity, any

Having learned about good things, what will you pass on to your children? How do you make children owners of a good personality? Have you ever thought about the reason for this? They took away all the good aspects from here. They developed it, made it into a creative science, and gave it back to you. You are parading it around as philosophy. Through this philosophy, what will you achieve for the fulfillment of your karma and life?

Guruvani

March 1996

You who study and teach philosophy have not seen what the ancestors have done. Many great men came and spoke to honor the path of karma mentioned by the ancestors. We have the rest of that history in our hands. But today it is not possible to know any of this. If you follow historians, you will not get any of it at all. You will not get a single word through history about the path in which the great ancestors and your contemporary great men have worked and failed. Contemporary great men are leading you to the path of knowledge by seeing, knowing, and acting. There is no other justification for it. If another justification is attributed to it, it is contrary to the truth. Without exception, the knowledge we have discovered so far has been handed over to people of other countries. The sorcerers who taught us to give it, get money, fight, win, and live are with us in the guise of guides. What else do we have, who do we have other than karma that has pushed so much tragedy? What do you have to give into the hands of your children who are waiting to know, study, and teach by following your path? So this is how you should understand it. Have the great men born since the beginning of this Kali Yuga experienced happiness or sorrow? Gandhiji was a great man who worked in materialism. Gandhiji did not have any spiritualism, did he? Yet how much sacrifice he has experienced. But not only us, even his followers could not grasp the magnitude of that sacrifice. We could not walk on the path of dharma and grasp the aspect of virtue. This subject should be understood in this way. We have come to a predicament where we cannot give contemporaneity and nurture people who have kept such a sacrifice-rich lifestyle.

Personally looking at it, Gandhiji is a spiritual person. But there is a flaw that it is not suitable for the times. Because he has advocated for Ramarajya. Which is this era of Rama? How many Ramas have come after that? Where do we stand in the karma that teaches Ramo Ramacha Krishna Kalki Janardhana? If so, where can we reach if Ramarajya is brought? There is a history that Gandhiji participated in the Vaikom Satyagraha. Vaikom Satyagraha in the temple

It was not possible to enter. It was to walk along the road around the temple to reach the boat jetty on the western side of the temple. How have you understood this history itself? If this is the distance of our contemporary history, is it easy to learn the truth from ancient history and move forward? Now let's take another history. A Vamana avatar came and trampled down a king who was ruling the country well! Why trample down someone who rules well? Because he is an Asura. If he is an Asura, what imparted Asura-nature to one who rules exemplarily? Does history have any other information to tell us other than the complexity of such myths?

This is the state of our history. How can we detach the contradiction of action that is praised and glorified without being able to know the true state of anything? What do we have to tell our future generations if they stand up straight and ask? Let that be.

How can we see Sree Krishna, as a historian or as Yogeshwara? When we praise 'Yatra yatra Yogeshwara Krishna', this author of the Gita, who is Yogeshwara, came and we could not know him. Is there any meaning in such people saying that Krishna is Yogeshwara? If we speak bluntly, you will say that Krishna has not died, he is merged in the fire of Yoga. Then we all got salvation, right? Each one blabbers such justifying misdeeds, telling stories, enjoying and playing around. Let us change from this storytelling. Let us try to know about Krishna. We cannot move forward without thinking, knowing, acting and suffering as much as that man's sacrifice. To know about that sacrifice, there must be desire, determination, dispassion and love to know about the state of any action that aligns with truth. Dispassion is the respect of love.

There are some things within ourselves that show us how the dispassion of love is. A sacrificer can show us those things within ourselves. It takes a sacrificer to show the sacrifice of Mahatmaji. Even after being kicked by soldiers, without taking it as a big deal, that man is showing what the richness of sacrifice is. How have we seen even that richness of sacrifice? A lineage did not grow to a level where we could take up his steadfastness in dispassion. That became a mess. An unjust society or government did not come forward and show us the history of the contemporary era. How far will we go if we study the history chewed and spat out by a handful of people who waste time talking about some rationalism and liberation without even the slightest connection to sacrifice?

Therefore, we do not need to talk about liberation and sacrifice, nor any nonsense. What is the action suitable for the Dharma that we need to achieve in our lifetime? How much weight of sacrifice is there in that action? What hardship does that sacrifice have? How should we experience that hardship? At least we should know this much. This is the aspect that should make great men worthy of worship. This itself should be the history regarding great men. What have the sacrificers we have celebrated and presented as the greatest sacrificers today been able to achieve? What has the Prophet's last prayer in that graveyard shown? True Muslims cannot deny that. We have denied all the actions that have occurred to recognize such a sacrifice. This is the reason for the downfall of the people.

I am saying what I know about what should be done to bring a change from all this. That is my knowledge. Saying Krishna's sense of Dharma

In Karma, Prathyakshavaham Dharmam is mentioned. That means seeing Dharma manifested. Manifesting does not mean the manifestation that today's poets and scholars talk about. What is their theory of manifestation? For example, how do they talk about the sun rising? It bursts and blooms. Where is the sun that bursts and falls like this? If these mischief-makers can lean another ignorance on top of your ignorance, where do you stand? Where is the sun supposed to burst and bloom? If you are taught like this about this special rule that revolves within the paths of the sky, witnessing a dharma of time, who do they think you are when they say this?

What is Krishna's Prathyakshavaham Dharmam? Krishna is asking, why don't you kill them? I don't know any of that, I can't fight them this way and kill them. I only feel this way about the act of killing them. Do you believe what I say? Definitely. Then why don't you obey that word? Now, I only have doubts about killing here. But you just think about this. Then it is said that Arjuna sat on that chariot with his eyes closed. When he closed his eyes, Arjuna himself sees. There is no harm in killing them. That is how God appears before him. God teaches to kill teachers and relatives. How might God come in your logic? How can you, who deserve compassion saying that not even an ant should be harmed, feel like killing? I am asking the historians. How can you feel like killing? Didn't you see the logic of the Jain theorists, covering their mouths. That is, even germs should not be killed. If germs cannot be killed, how will this earth survive? Then are you and I great sinners before God? How many creatures have we knowingly boiled and eaten? If we can plant and sprout all these seeds, cut them and eat their leaves and roots, isn't that loss of life? What dignity are you establishing with this Advaita word?

We must be convinced of one thing Arjuna saw. That will be your goodness. Where did you see it to say that you are not killing anyone, nor are you causing anyone to be killed? Arjuna then bowed and took up his bow. Let it be true or good, right or wrong, whatever it is, it is manifest. The Karma-vadi Krishna asks Bhishma, who fought and lay on the bed of arrows. You could have gone before this, then why are you lying like this? Then Bhishma says, let this go, Krishna, this will go. When looking at this historical truth said by Bhishma, who are Bhishma and Drona? What was the reason for them to fall like that? Thinking about why Krishna himself stood in this path of Karma, standing as Guru and disciple, and caused them to be killed, we get the meaning of the word Prathyakshavaham Dharmam of the events that happened then in human dharma and those that happened contemporaneously.

April 1996

To be able to see in that form the realms where our ancestors were stuck in the course of life. Here our children are able to see that. That is the directly perceivable dharma (Pratyakshavaham Dharmam). There are no other justifications for it. There was a dangerous reason in what Krishna realized and told Arjuna. That reason is revealed through Krishna's life itself. Krishna falls down, struck by a hunter's arrow. Having heard, known, and studied the part where Krishna says that there is no saving him anymore and asks to take the women and save them, why can't you grasp the truth of those words? What have you understood when you explain these causes and effects? That is, you are unable to grasp as much as you see. Then what should be done?

To us, things are experienced here as directly perceivable dharma in the waking, dream, and deep sleep states. This directly perceivable dharma should not be just like telling a story. If waking, dream, deep sleep, and Turiya have come into your experience, there are some people you see during those times of experience. Who is that? Those are the people in your house, not the people in my house. But the people in your house and your land are the people in my house too.

This closeness is what you have with me. Only this bond can bring you close to me. And this is exactly what you get as a reward for coming close to me. This is how you should study what Krishna is and what Krishna did.

You should grasp what it is that you see manifested before you. That is, what is Shiva, what is Brahma, what is Krishna, what is God? Through what is seen manifested, you must understand well what this is. Otherwise, there is no meaning in saying that you saw Shiva, saw Krishna, etc. This vision (the experience of seeing it manifested) must be brought into the path of action of what these people, who speak of the past state, did. We are not realizing a dharma that settles things through action after putting an end to pretenses. We should not practice such a dharma. God has given us the opportunity to do it completely truthfully.

How do these children perceive it directly here? The great ones who came and went as our ancestors are coming before them. They come and tell themselves what the historical truth about them is. To know if what they say is true, you must tell the Guru. That is how their history can be known as truth. Knowing this in this state, you are not sannyasins, but householders. It is your children who are shown this. There is an action here that can show it like this. These children who see like this are many in this lineage spread across this entire country. To know and grasp this, how should you, who have known this, approach the Guru?

Gold must be rubbed on a touchstone. Grinding it on a normal stone will not reveal its purity. Without shedding the ignorance of grinding gold on a grinding stone, can you know your own purity? There are many in this lineage who know how to test purity like this. What they say is the truth. They may not be able to recount history. But they must know how to speak the truth. Those who do not know should learn through those who do. Having known it, you should desire to attain it through your effort and your love. There is much to say about this. But it shouldn't be said just like that. This manifestation does not discriminate between male and female. It does not discriminate whether one is a drummer, an elephant mahout, or a rogue. Whoever they are, if they are children of God, they will receive it. So, what should one do to become children of God? You must think about that. You should not abandon history or truth. Waking state (Jagrat), dream state (Swapna), deep sleep (Sushupti) - first, it must be seen like this. Just as it is seen in the waking state, it must be seen in the dream and deep sleep states. Similarly, after seeing it in the Turiya state, one must journey to the Turiyatita state. You have faced defeat in the course of that karma's journey. Now you must think about what to do to reclaim this fortune. Realizing this and acting upon it, your resolve should be to strengthen the consciousness of truth, no matter what we lose. That is your karma. That is the truth that must be elevated within you. Prepare for this truth. This is all I have to say to you. If Jesus could be persecuted like this, there will be people to persecute you in any way. Those who run away saying, "I cannot die," are not worthy of taking up this fortune.

So, you must attain the subtle body of the ancestors who have passed away. From where and from which realm does this subtle body control you, what is the shape of that realm, what all happens there, and how will you be taken to the story happening there? The one from whom you receive these answers is the Guru. If you search where you ought to search, you can find yourself and your causes. You must instill this inquiring mind in your children and make them everything for the nation. You should not make them belong only to your home. If they are made to belong only to the home, when everything earned is eaten up, they too will die. They and you will all die. In the end, as the master said, you shouldn't ask for anything other than a single thread, a rosary, and an upper ear ring. In this state, we need to move completely towards the side of knowledge. This is your need today. To fulfill this need, losing your properties, house, or other things is not a loss. This cannot be brought about through money. It means that we cannot travel on this path holding onto the pride of money. Money is needed; I am not saying you do not need money. Do not use this money to act and show off the arrogance of a non-story that writes and completes stories to show that there is no God just because you have money. The rich, the beggar, and the poor must have this knowledge. Being poor does not mean being a great sinner. He is one who has swallowed sin like a bark. Even a local person walks around to reform the poor. That will not happen. Because, you do not know who these poor people are and what to do with them. If you dress up these poor people and make them speak ill of you, a situation will arise where he becomes a great sinner and walks around extending his hand before you. You should not go into that karma. That is the path to your destruction. The poor should not be reformed in this style of yours. Let him have the knowledge to buy and eat. What and how does he buy and eat? If someone sees the one you teach and send out, anyone should feel like giving him what he needs. Having acquired the means to eat and dress, wealth, capability, respect, and humility through you, children who come to this nation to implement the dharma of the nation must come creatively through your hands.

GURUVANIKAL · May 1996 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1020 to GV2:P1029
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

May 1996

When visiting certain places, it seems as though a certain muteness hangs in the air, as if nature itself has fallen silent. I often wonder about the reason for this right then. I am sharing some things I happened to observe. In the practices where locals worship and lead others in worship, invoking God by naming various castes and religions, we have taken divine worship forward in a completely ignorant manner. No one authoritatively explained to us the reason for this or its root cause. Ever since I realized this, I have spent many days reflecting on these matters, and as a result of a strong desire to highlight them repeatedly, what I am going to share here is a summary of what I have been able to observe and understand.

There is evidence that we had a wretched habit here of claiming that all human beings are one, irrespective of caste, religion, and class, while practically dividing ourselves and behaving based on these very differences. However, we never received a profound and far-reaching understanding of how or why this came to be. Furthermore, when speaking about the Vedas—specifically about the great sage who divided the Vedas into four—he is praised with terms like 'Krishnavarnam'. It is even extolled that this great figure is an incarnation of Krishna himself. But when we look at the 'Tamoguna' (mode of ignorance) that created the caste system, the destructive act that tore apart and ruined the culture of that great man is a terror beyond imagination.

There are Christians and Muslims in India. Or many other doctrines have arrived. It is purely out of India's ignorance that a group of people converted in such a way for the sake of salvation and survival.

Other than that, there is nothing to say about it. One must think that they had at least this much goodness because of that change. The decay that has accumulated over the ages of life is what affects everything. Our ancestors, the forefathers, are the remnants of that decay. When looking at the objects worshipped by these forefathers, it is a sad truth buried in the dirt that we cannot help but say they are completely unknown to us. This truth took on various colors, became the four varnas, and then caste. This is the origin of caste.

Varna protection has never taken place here. What is varna protection? How is it done? Even the thinkers among us do not know this. Even those who say that such things have happened according to the dharma of the ages do not have the knowledge to say what actually happened. The reason for this is the incorrect practice of worship over time. When looking at this very old history one by one, it has been seen that a large population lived and died in a way that is unimaginable in terms of the degradation of life. This population is what worshipped and was worshipped here. Nature has created such a situation for the many crores of people who have been lost for ages without attaining merit through birth or liberation through death. When we go to a place, these ancestors and gods will come running in the hope of getting something. They will only leave after getting something. They might have been trying for such an arrival for ages. With this arrival, it is fulfilled. Over three lakh crore ancestors and gods have achieved this in this way.

The authoritative ritual we perform for this liberation of ancestors and gods in another way is Gurupooja. In fact, it is the Guru who is worshipped. Then why is it specifically called Gurupooja? When a family approaches due to some kind of illness or other afflictions, when looking at them in a vision, it is found that their ancestors and the life forces they worshipped under the Trimurti system, such as Shaktheyam, Shaivam, and Vaishnavam, have been lost in a way that they cannot attain liberation, leap towards goodness, or come to an understanding. What is the subject that you parents and the children you raise need to know to recover what has been lost? Our home should have prosperity. For that, children and women should be good. Moreover, when looking back at each thing in the state of the country, do not lose the opportunity to realize what happens to us when we call upon the god created by caste and religion. You should have a determination to uproot and throw away the roots of destruction that have come upon your land, country, and the world itself due to the mistakes that have occurred in you, your children, and your brotherly love, or due to the mistakes committed by your ancestors, and to establish the truth in its place.

Each of us should have a sincere regret that we have not been able to show in our mutual love what the great men, whom some religiously call saints, apostles, and Imams, and whom Hindus call by many names, should be to us, other than dressing them up with books. From that regret, you will become good. When you become good, you will be convinced by the divine presence coming into you that the actions you are showing off today by fighting are not what you absolutely need. With that, you will have the realization that we are the children who have the authority to remove the past mistakes from this world. Thus, you will never be able to stay away from the karma given by God. This is dedication. In this dedication, to completely remove the sorrow that caused the downfall and to bring goodness to everyone regardless of caste, religion, and class

We can also act in the name of God. God has given us the principles of karma to do what is necessary in that regard.

The accumulated karmic result of a few of our ancestors, whether they were yogis, karmis, bhaktas, jnanis, or none of these, inspiring crores of people to behave badly, is what has led us to turn towards actions that invite all this destruction. A change from this is indeed God's will. Instead of bringing it to the level of human love, we have only covered up the great souls born for the whole world by telling stories to implement this divine will. We are not even able to accept Sri Krishna, who was born in India in recent times, that is, within a mere five thousand two hundred years. Not only that, we have understood the glory of such a great person in exactly the opposite way. What all fabricated stories have we been taught about Krishna, who came as the first guru of this era or this Kali Yuga. After that, Moses, Jesus, and the Prophet, though their era's influence is comparatively less, no one sees the karma they undertook with sacrifice for us. None of them shared their sacrifice for any caste or religion. They strove for actions that confer liberation to the source of human birth. But what has their lineage shown us? They have made us successors of a lineage that sowed the complete destruction of karma through words and deeds. To change from this, you must become truly blessed in this time ordained by God for you. For that, God always shows compassion to you. By completely fulfilling that compassion, you should be able to bear any sacrifice for the good of the world.

GURUVANIKAL · June 1996 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1030 to GV2:P1038
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

June 1996

All those who have come as Hindu religious theorists have behaved in such a way that we cannot know each other. Even today, the situation is no different. Calling it a secular nation and fighting for religion. Today, we have reached this kind of action. If we fight like this, will we ever be able to stand together? We have desired for the good. In reality, what we are doing is not good. My God is greater, others' God is small; behind this attitude, small castes got small Gods. Behind this difference, everyone has been working while staying away from each other. Therefore, if any good action is seen from a person or a society, it is usual to move away from there. It is the perspective of that person or society that has changed. It does not mean that we have changed from where we stood. We cannot change. Moreover, we are only creating scratches in that society and causing it trouble, rather than being able to impart any good from it. Or if anything

If anything has been done, it is merely some inadequate actions. No dharma has been practiced otherwise. As long as the human world does not think about how dharma will and should be practiced, no good will come.

Going after angels, saints, cardinals, and apostles, and believing it to be monotheism. Believing that so much was done under the same Pope for so long. Although it is true that this has managed to unite us to some extent, it has not been able to impart anything good. Similarly, Islam, Jainism, Sikhism, and then the Gurudharma Pracharana Sabha - there have been countless such things in India. Has anyone been able to say a definitive word about what has happened in these states, from devil to god, from god to sage, from sage to ascetic, from ascetic to guru or imam, and what harm has come to us from any of these? Or has anyone imparted the understanding that this is the harm that has happened, and that we, humanity as a whole, must change from this harm?

Many people have wished to see Kerala as a model. Because, unlike other small countries, Kerala has a virtue. The fact that agricultural land is less in Kerala, which has more plows, is not an inadequacy, and there is enough water and organic nature to preserve them. But we have not done that and set it right. The reason for this is that a great tug-of-war is always maintained between the workers, laborers, and the capitalist among us. This is a continuation of the old fight. Even though we are ready to shift conceptually to the path of modern perspective, there is no need for more proof that we are unable to abandon that old tradition of intellect.

Now take the children in our homes. Until what age can we keep girls in our homes? When we send these girls to another house, we do not know what virtues we should send with her. Instead, what do we do? Bargain for money and sell the girl and the boy. This is completely unnecessary. We don't think about what qualifications this girl should have to become a mother. Can they add anything to dharma other than wasting the children's lives by imparting undeserved wealth, lordship, and suitable boasting? Look, how has life been destroyed without giving an opportunity to understand the meaning and depth of life by knowing the hardships of life and the transformative nature of life? Therefore, it is like establishing the foundation of loveless action by adopting the dress and language of foreign countries.

I am not saying that you have no love for your children. You have love, and you have thoughts about what should be in the course of action. But beyond all that, some things need to be changed through the children. That is, through all past religious customs, our ancestors have brought some rituals. There is nothing in it that is sufficient for the completeness of life. Therefore, nothing is complete for us. How are we standing in an incomplete state? Calling upon the god of caste, it crept into religion in another guise. When the god of religion was placed, the fight began. This fight has gathered and stands today in a system that falls and breaks everywhere.

Everyone should think about why we, humans, need this system. Instead, you should not be ready to say that my god, my guru, and my imam have more strength. Purely as humans, as men and women, we

We must live by giving love to the One. Do those who speak of caste and religion use the five elements differently? Is the nature of the five senses different? When we act through the five sheaths (Panchakoshas) and reach that state of love and respect, we will realize that love is the greatest of all. It is this love that is offered to God. Then, having received God's love, humans should share that love among themselves and raise their children in a way that sustains Dharma. There is no greater duty for a human being than this. Only when this happens can we truly love God. That love for God will bring purity to your mind. If this purity of mind is shared for the goodness of humanity, you need not doubt it—you will reach the highest pinnacle of Dharma. Otherwise, if we raise children without imparting anything that contributes to the good of the world, they will bring disgrace not only to their homes but also to the land, lose their Dharma, become devoid of merit, and end up as pathetic beings. You who loudly proclaim that there is no caste, no religion, and no class are the ones who have the tendency to assign castes even to animals. You must understand that this casteism will not only destroy you but will also turn out to be the greatest disaster for life itself. The body will perish anyway. This body, which is scrubbed, bathed, dressed, covered, and carefully carried around every day, has no real greatness to show off. All that truly exists is the life force (Jiva). Yet, nothing is being done for the glory of the life force. It is a special manifestation of love. For the sake of that love, let us forget caste, religion, and class, and unite.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · July 1996 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1039 to GV2:P1050
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

July 1996

If you look at the ideas of any Acharya, you will not find anything bad in them. Followers of Jainism walk with their mouths covered. The compassionate intention behind this is to avoid destroying even microorganisms. Can we say that is bad? How many microorganisms within us are destroyed? How many outside are destroyed? I don't feel that the work of modern science, which has the ability to detect these, is any less. The content of a theory that has repeatedly established a ritualistic rule that imparts much more love than this modernity is what was indicated above. How much have we and the crowd that includes us loved to imbibe this love? If we can pass on this loving thought to our children, it can be seen as a source of great love.

We are raising children to become the owners of the karmic destiny of offspring who have imbibed sin in various ways and compete in various ways. How can they take on this source of love? Instead of teaching children to repeatedly follow a ritual that imparts love, we have only imparted conflict in them. In this disobedient growth, in this loveless respect, we are completely the culprits. Loveless respect is mostly seen among those who think they know something among us.

There is a greatness of every kind in respecting each other. In the matter of respecting each other, Kerala had a blessedness compared to other places. But today it has been completely ruined. There was a time in Kerala when uncles and other women lived without conflict. If you nurture that unity, you will not be able to reach and act upon any righteous karma or dharma.

What do we need today? If there is sinful power in the karma done by our ancestors, it is a theory to destroy it and cultivate merit instead. Dharma is respecting how much of a share of fortune there is in the theories stated so far, how much of it we can take, and how we should be in that karma. We have not reached anywhere in that dharma. In the karma thus respected, love is at the very forefront - love for God. Just as we impart love to a plant we plant, humans have a karmic inclination to impart love to everything. The respect for human life is a great meritorious act that voluntarily takes on the state of dharma. If we are to be able to bring children to a dharma theory steeped in respect, we must know what we have done, or what society has done - what the ancestors did, who claimed to belong to the same society while behaving with enmity towards each other. For this, not only do those who are said to be knowledgeable do nothing, but they also deceive us with caste speeches and by showing love like vote-catchers pinching a child's nose. This deceit of the Lord is destroying dharma. Does this Lord, who has lost dharma, have any love for karma? Any loving respect? Where have we, who have lost humility, reached? We have simply decayed into a state where we cannot find the functional capacity of a life.

We can do a few things to change this. For that, we must know about the four yugas - Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, Dvapara Yuga, and Kali Yuga. We have been told that Kali is very bad. But it is not so. Kali is completely good. Everyone who has come to say that Kali is good has been hurt. The harm we have earned through the repetition of the dharma of other yugas in the current Kali is beyond even thinking. Because if what has passed is repeated again, it will become a meritless decay. It will be seen put in a rotten thing. Reaching a karma that gives mind to it in such a way, even self-awareness will be lost. A reform for that

If you need it, you must find out what the flaws are in the gurus who have come so far and in their religious doctrines, what they have done, to what extent they can be followed, and where the destructive nature of hatred will lead. If we can discern this, we will become completely good, no doubt. Then you won't have to share anyone's property. You will be able to guide your children to the virtue where dharma resides in the act of sharing love. Thus, by coming to knowledge, you will reach self-realization from knowledge.

Previously, Karinkuttichathan was worshipped in this place. That is not just hearsay for me, but something I have seen. Besides that, there is another special thing here. A sick child was recently brought here from Vaniamkulam. When we looked into the illness, this affliction was caused by Odiyans. If asked why these Odiyans do this and complete it, they themselves do not know. This karma exists not only in one doctrine but in all doctrines. Bhajan math, temple, vanakkamasappura, church, thaikkavu, mosque - we speak giving respect to each of these. Is this respect enough only for us, or do others need it too? Or is the God of Hindus not acceptable to people of other religions? If so, why did Hindus convert to other religions? Everyone talked and talked about this until it became like something wrapped in white and put in hot water. You need to reach a knowledge that you can grasp without the doctrine flowing away unnecessarily like this.

In Kerala, recently three gurus have come among the Hindus - and then some as contemporaries. Anyway, none of them came to ruin you. They have done whatever good they could. But you don't know how to take this goodness. You have tried to take this by following the footsteps of the religious doctrine written with this conflict. That is not how it should be; you should take this from the knowledge that destroys the conflict from the doctrine and takes only the goodness. In that karma, you will change to the dharma seeking virtue. It is not enough if someone else says this for you, we must know and take it ourselves.

Imam means guru. The guru who can tell us about our spiritual matter. How is that? Jagrat, swapna, sushupti (waking, dreaming, deep sleep). What happens in sushupti, what happens in the sushupti while in jagrat - this Imam must tell us. Hindus show another guise for this. They say, when the mind gets peace, its strength will decrease. Then the body will not be able to sit straight. Therefore, a device is needed to keep the body straight - in Tamil, kannakol - so that it doesn't droop. But what is the use of just putting a kannakol on the neck like this? This is the truth that has come in the special love of the life where consciousness is merging. Karma is needed to take back this special love.

We must know from an organizational traditional nature what all is done here to raise the younger generation in that karma which has been known and made known through our ancestors. Then we will understand, no one is bad, everyone is good in their own way. But we have to go very far forward. Why? There might be tied-up demons in what they worshipped. This leaves the complete form of thoughts about God and shows the noise of a chaotic dance. Through this chaotic dance, they actually spend money, complete it by telling fabricated stories, and make it seem that their God is perfect. When we try to change this from such Hindu logic and other logics, we must remember the depth of the word spoken by the Prophet, the matter spoken about the samadhi of Sree Rama, Ramatirtha, and Shankaracharya, and similarly many other things deceived by those established in yoga. There is a practice to take back these karmas. The subject you need to know for that is not the logic that Hindus strongly speak up to heaven. Saying that everything will be alright by the grace of God is the hollow word of a person without substance. Nothing will be alright like that.

This is not the issue. Can the story of karmic successes and failures over a very long period be fixed with just a single word?

To prevent this mistake from happening to at least your children, we are purifying the karma that has reached an impassable state, which your ancestors accumulated as demons, gods, and goddesses, and which has now passed on to sages and ascetics. That flawed karma is as vast as the sky. There are such accounts in all religions.

Birthday Message

GURUVANIKAL · August 1996 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1051 to GV2:P1071
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

August 1996

Like the astrological predictions that are sometimes written to make fun of you, I am also making fun of you. But I am not joking, I am speaking the truth.

There is an old saying that if you earn money even if it brings disgrace, that money will erase the disgrace. Both the knowledgeable and the ignorant have no difference of opinion in this matter. I am also one of those who belong to that group. The word 'knowledgeable' came into existence only because there are 'ignorant' people. But the reality of the ignorant is true as far as the knowledgeable is concerned. At the same time, it is taught that the ignorant is the knowledgeable. What kind of nonsense is this? It is certain that if ignorance is let loose, it will drown in the procession of the knowledgeable. Therefore, we need a medium that drives both of these. In this, knowledge should be driven first, then ignorance.

The fact that the Karma Yogi becomes an ignorant practitioner itself makes us understand that both ignorance and knowledge are equally tiring. We have to see the knowledge-ism that brings up the useless here. Like the import-export* system, the incoming politics has burned here. The politics that existed in India before Mahatmaji was created by A.O. Hume. The wealthy people who claimed the succession (Indian National Congress) of that politics taken over by Mahatmaji and reached anarchy should have known one thing first. We must first find the root cause of whatever we are focusing on. There is a reason to say this. Caste, religion,

*Import - Export

prophetic religion and many other things have been nurtured, and this politics that has spread like a parasite in all of them will also die when those trees die. Indian politics has reached this state today. We all should be able to recognize what we gain and what we lose from this politics.

In the same way, when a procession serving destruction in the name of spiritual and material things passes before us, we may doubt whether it is our human brothers themselves, or the demons of the past, or the illusory forms of the divine elements that are said to come in the future. There is no doubt that it is an illusory form. But is illusion non-existent or existent? If it is existent, in what form can it be shaped?

If these are only some aspects of the contemporary chain of mistakes, before all this, we have made a mistake somewhere as the cause of all this. What is it?

There is a puzzle called Yugadharma in Hindu history. Even if we study the Ramayana, Bhagavatam, eighteen Puranas, four Vedas, and six Shastras altogether, the complete picture of Yugadharma is not revealed to us. None of those who have tried to correct us by counting and scientifically explaining these Vedas, Puranas, and epics one by one are bad. In addition to all this, many secrets of Yugadharma have been indicated here and there. But nothing can be clearly understood. There have been people among us who talk a lot about the Yugas and those who have stopped by giving accounts of up to five thousand years. A

In a short period of time, India and Asia have a history where Sri Shankara and many other great scholars, sages, prophets, and gurus came and went, attaining scholarship and greatness among Hindus. But whether it is because the succession of what was written in the scriptures went wrong, or because of various karma doshas, or because of bad luck, there is no way to know why - our ambiguities regarding the Yugadharma are very large. But there is a way ordained by God for us to know all this clearly here.

We don't even know what came and went where in the Asian empire. Can we know what the progress of scholars and commoners is just by writing endlessly about this arrival that has happened to date? Can we determine the duration of the influence of the prophets and the small gurus who followed?

Now what is this duration?

The duration refers to the long Manvantaras and the Chaturyugas contained in the Manvantaras. Our effort is to find out which Chaturyuga of which Manvantara this is. Not many people can participate in this effort. There is clear evidence that they cannot. How many prophets came here? How many gurus came with an influence of a thousand or two thousand years? This is the evidence. What can we do by counting such endless numbers? Is it enough to find this out in some way? You must be aware of the effort or endeavor to complete the story of the god and goddess and try to complete everything with endless words like God's Maya or Mayavilasam. The truth is that none of this has influenced us, or rather, it has not taken us anywhere. It is also true that quite a few efforts have been made here as mentioned above. We have also experienced the results of those efforts to some extent. There is no point in arguing about what is there and what is not.

Here, an easy way has been gifted to us from that Brahma's decision. On the cover page of an almanac among us, we can see a female figure blindfolded with a black cloth holding up a weighing scale. How can one know the accuracy of the scale if blindfolded with a black cloth? What should the symbolism of the effort brought through it look like? Its meaning is that even if blindfolded with a black cloth, if there is effort, the accuracy of the scale can be shown. Although we all know its meaning, the misfortune of not getting the fulfillment of the meaning has existed for billions of years. The earth and the moon belong to the orbits of the solar system. Although many people calculate and research the science related to it, no matter how one calculates the nine planetary positions, no one is able to see and say beyond the subtle results. What is said is in the manner of sometimes it works, sometimes it doesn't. One of the many reasons for this is this. If ten people in the same star gather, ten characters, if a hundred people gather, a hundred characters. If a thousand people gather, a thousand characters, there are many reasons to say. Therefore, it is not possible to just say it like that. We have the luck to get the knowledge according to Brahma's decision that there was a mistake in the dharma of the times. Therefore, everyone living in this opportunity is lucky. No one should ever let go of this luck that has come to us. Kaliyuga has the special quality of being the period ordained to be born in Kaliyuga, the fourth quarter of Chaturyuga, and to know what karma and dharma are conducive to liberation. The prophets who worked for the good of millions of people and

You yourself can see through vision that the glories of the Gurus are showered in this. The dawn of that destined fortune has also happened among you. Through this, any Shudra born in this fourth quarter has the fortune to reach the state of liberation through karma,

* Subramanya's Navagrahas.

merit, and fortune. This fortune, where the fortune to be born in the Kali yuga where that dharma is practiced and the fortune to be eligible for liberation come together, is the greatest fortune of us all. The countless thirty-three crore gods of the Hindus. This earth where we are born and live is one of the lands where millions of Gurus, prophets, and countless sages who came along with them have come and gone. This fortune cannot be postponed by setting a deadline. Therefore, let us all work together. Without perishing by talking about the vanity of caste, religion, and religious belief, without falling into the whirlpool of blindness and rotting away, let us share the fortune given by the divine will with everyone. The vision is what shows all the blessed souls called demons, prophets, great sages, yogis, karmis, jnanis, and bhaktas till today in the five states of waking, dreaming, deep sleep, turiya, and turiyatita with the power of the yugas. It is by seeing through this vision that the Gurus who came according to the laws of the yugadharma have showered their glories. If you want, this is an opportunity for you too to see it with your physical eyes and attain fortune. Kali is the time given to take up and practice this karma ordained for each child, without perishing by babbling about male, female, caste, and religion, and without getting caught in the whirlpool of the learned and the ignorant. You can believe that this is not just a dharma but a procession of dharma. Let no one doubt that this path of dharma is the same for all of us who got the opportunity to be born together. See, know, and make known all the above-mentioned good and evil with your physical eyes by holding onto a Guru. Stop harboring the ignorance of playing by talking about the greatness or smallness of any god. God will come if called in any language or form with the intention of God. No one needs to doubt this. That is the Guru - Salutations!!

October 1996

What should faith be for a human being? There are many occult powers and sleight-of-hand tricks. Believing in any of these is not faith in God at all. Should God show occult powers before us? Should God immediately give money to someone who prays and then discard them? We can take many such examples. The virtues of our women are within us. Looking at it that way, father, mother, children, and everything else are within us. Everyone is seeking condition after condition in faith. How can we change this? How can we hold on to the truth in this action? We, who can see it through experience, know what it is. If you take great souls as evidence, you will understand a small thing. Gandhiji himself is an example. What happened to Gandhiji, who tried to instill national consciousness? National consciousness cannot be instilled just like that. Why? As long as we do not see whether there is an industrial connection behind it, that national consciousness is inadequate.

What can we do when the dynamics travel inside and outside us and get destroyed in such a way that one person cannot adapt to the situation of another person or country among us? We can do nothing. How can you, who speak and act from the midst of various kinds of ignorance, correct yourselves? This is what you should think about. A house cannot be run without the involvement of husband, wife, children, father, and mother. We should know that there is a danger zone with us that these children cannot be protected. Whatever antics the father and mother show at home, the children will copy them. This situation has spread to the neighborhood and the whole world.

Faith will save. Again, what is faith? If any offerings or vows are offered in the name of faith, it is attached to our life, mixed with merit and sin, is it sufficient to remove the negative aspects of action? Neither mantras, nor tantras, nor knowledge conditions match our life. You must understand that this is purely a birth quality that we had when we were born. We must fully accept it and act upon it. There is no point in father, mother, husband, or wife saying this. This is absolutely true. Then they don't think about any of this. Don't they have education, wealth, and ability? They do.

The Mughals came and ruled this country. The Christians came and acted in such a way as to make people believe that they are the original form of this world. Can anyone find fault with the Hindus for claiming the greatest antiquity by saying that they are the descendants of Manu?

Manusmriti belongs to the world of the Guru. What is the reason that we, who rose up carrying the Manusmriti of the world itself, go nowhere? Manusmriti is the knowledge of the world itself. If you study and study up to a great encyclopedia, up to an encyclopedia of all knowledge, should you think that you know everything? As long as you, who stand on the side of knowledge, do not know that knowledge is the knowledge of the dynamics of life, you cannot do anything. It is not enough just to know; one must be able to go as far back as possible and acquire knowledge. Another thing, if we mention all the things of many siddhas and prophets who have worked at the forefront and settled down, it does not mean that we have swallowed all that knowledge. There is no point in any of this. There is no point in playing with the meanings of vowels and consonants combined with rhythm and beats, if needed, to speak like a storyteller and enjoy standing aside.

This is not what I am telling you. I haven't even gone to study like that. I am not speaking about something I have studied. But it is also something I have studied. That is the only way I can tell you. That is, I can only give you an explanation of this. I cannot even point to a single house to explain this subject completely. Because you have not understood it that way. So this untouched knowledge, but in Narayana Guru's language, or in Advaita language, touch and know. Touch and know one by one. If you spend your whole life seeing, knowing, tasting, and acting upon every tree and plant, how will you study humans? Therefore, you cannot study and complete anything. There is no point in going like that.

What is belief? How many churches do those who believe in Jesus have? As how many churches are they operating in the world? What is the situation in the Islamic tradition where one had to ask if Jinn rule the world? Why do we have this fight among us? Are they senseless? They have sense. But the misfortune of not being able to tolerate and forgive has gripped them. In reality, there is no caste. Then what is there, Varna. In this Varna caste, they finished writing stories without knowing where Varna is mentioned and went after Puranas. These Puranas, in Tamil language, mean old things.

The karma of old tendencies has come to each of us as a body containing the movements of acquired merit and sin. This is destiny. No one else can say anything about this. When we try to implement this destiny, our relatives, our mother, our wife, nothing will align. Or if they align, then the house will become useless. Understanding this path, everyone is slowly stepping back.

What will we do with them? The cause of this trouble stands with the world. It is impossible to measure and settle the right and wrong that comes to us in connection with this matter. And no one should think that it can be measured and settled. Then, there is a knowledge that belongs to human creation. But it has no closeness to our life. It is just like seeking a way to live a mere life. Like a merchant, a spinner, a weaver - like many things.

But a person cannot do anything by learning all these jobs together. Learn and teach according to aptitude. Whether female or male, teach according to aptitude. That is right. When teaching this aptitude, money and capacity increase for our home and land, and it becomes useless only for the home. Everything else is fine. Thus every house is being destroyed. What can you do for a house that is being destroyed like this? You must be careful about the situation that may arise when holding onto belief tightly. Because you are the ones who lived fighting, divided by caste, religion, and class.

If you ask anyone, no one has a caste. If there is no caste, then why behave like this? Another thing is that those who speak big things have no answer about what they say. Thus you are growing as the heir of the kingdom of heaven who has found the complete decay of a bad life in every way. How can this be changed? It cannot be changed. Then what you can do is hold onto one thing tightly. That's all.
