November 1996

Jesus said, I have much to tell you, but you will not understand it. That is exactly what I have to tell you too. If you look at each one of you, one thing becomes clear. None of you are able to take this. You may ask why carry around things that you cannot handle. You, who have understood through theory and tradition that if you see something, you can just go with it completely, will never be able to take this. Not just you, not even a child in the world can do it. You must be able to insist that even if you lose your wealth, intellect, and education - whatever you lose, you will save your life. You, who are under the illusion that life can be saved with money, ability, wealth, and all their decorations, are not attaining salvation, but rather the complete form of failure. What would be the mental fortress of yours that is overflowing with these special decorations? Then how and with what will you seek a solution for this? You have a head or a brain, you have thought, you have everything. Then why can't you think it out? Having learned, spoken, babbled, and traditionally practiced everything in a contradictory manner one by one, what can you hold onto to move forward?

There is faith. But you do not know how to believe. This is where the problem lies. All that you have is not for trading in front of God. You may earn for one generation, for your father, mother, and children. You should not earn beyond that. If you earn like that, your children will become the escorts of the aforementioned education, wealth, and intellect. Thus they will perish. This is the logic of the Westerner or the capitalist. When you placed this logic inside your faith, you did not see divine justice.

You must understand one thing. Nature has a far-reaching knowledge. From this knowledge that stands near and far, you can understand everything. It is not that easy to take up this subject. But it can be taken, how? We are applying here that knowledge which says your faith will save you. What exactly is that word faith? When that faith is believed in such a way, it becomes useful for the home and the land.

We must look after our home, look after our land, look after our children, look after everything. When looking after and nurturing like this, husband and wife will not get along, father and son will not get along, mother and daughter will not get along. What can anyone do about the karma that comes without anything getting along like that? Is this contradictory quality suitable for the land? Is it suitable for the home?

You must note what the great souls who came and worked on this earth had to say in the end before they left. What was the last word Jesus said -

Father, have you forsaken me? At the same time, what are we being taught? That the sins of the world are completely washed away by the blood of Jesus. What answer does the world have for this?

The Prophet said that this world is ruled by Jinn. Sitting in the very same graveyard where he saw this, that great man attained Samadhi. For the last three months, he did not get up from where he sat. Can any religion or ideology provide an answer to all this?

So, how will you take all this? Therefore, what should we do? We should only take the path given by God. Do not take any other path. If you take another path, it will be harmful.

Sri Krishna is the great soul born at the beginning of this Kali Yuga. Even before Krishna's birth, that family and the government had received knowledge. The ruler had received knowledge. He was being instructed on how to rule. When he did not obey, he was told, "I am sending someone to your house," speaking to Kamsa. Then what did the priests advise? If so, none of the children born should live. Thus, seven of them were killed. How were these children, who came by God's will, destroyed? Who has the authority to destroy these children who came by God's will? But then, how will we believe this truth that is being told? - You have the right to ask anything like that.

But this is not what I am saying. We can believe or not believe. That is not anyone's property. But it belongs to everyone. We are the owners of karma, taking up the leadership of the house as father, mother, children, husband, and wife. This is completed by both man and woman. Children are the completion of karma for man and woman. To complete that, you should not go after karma that wanders aimlessly somewhere. How do you think you will achieve it by completing it through business and various other ways, learning and teaching deceit? Therefore, that is not how it happens.

Who are our ancestors, our prophets, who are our gurus, what are the different types of conditions, organizations - all these must be clearly understood by us, or rather, in an individual character. It is not possible for us to understand each of them in an individual character like that. All these are indeed great. Everything should be respected. But one thing. Belief will keep coming. But where should belief begin? Where these mentioned ancestors, prophets, and acharyas believed and ended up in a mess - that is where belief should begin.

Sri Krishna did not worship anyone. He worshipped only one thing. Krishna is the owner of such a personality. Moses, Jesus, and the Prophet, among such prophets, twenty-five have come. Hindus are those who believe that God exists in every pillar and speck of rust. Standing in that course of karma, what right do they have to speak of Advaita? How can one agree with a philosophical prophecy that says it exists in every pillar and speck of rust, and in the same breath asserts that none of this exists? No one can agree with that. There is no point in going and talking about this philosophical logic and the meaning of vowels and consonants. It should not be said like that. That is why it is said, "Anubhavamadhilonnilikkille/tanamathilliyu moonnamakshiyale". Can you tell the name of a thing or a person just by seeing it with your eyes, without asking? What if one thing has four names? A person with a name in Malayalam gets another name when he becomes a person in Tamil Nadu. If four names from four places are combined like that

How can one recognize a person? These are all various contradictory qualities of the world. You cannot reclaim these paths.

Again - what is faith? To what extent have great men spoken about faith? To what extent did Sri Shankara speak? Many such great souls came. But no one was able to illuminate our path till the end. After the last five thousand years, no one has come until today. If Advaita is preached and established, will we all become Advaitins? If asked to complete the dualities, can you?

Even if counted one by one, what is that One which remains countless? What do you mean by that? Can you ever take up the karma that stands exhausted after counting one by one again? Yes, when it reaches its defeat. The great sages who praise what they see day by day. You must stand there. After becoming a great sage, what is the next thought? So, across what periods of time have these great sages been born? They have been born across eons. Those born in such a way have spoken about the glories of the age. You have not been able to learn this. Because there has been no opportunity for it. If you are to grow in that field, you must have ancestors who grew up and attained liberation in that field.

December 1996

It is the great men who have praised the glories of the era. Only these great men (sages) can speak about the glories of the eras by realizing them in the dimensions of waking, dreaming, deep sleep, Turiya, and Turiyatita. The foresight of the sages is true. The sages have said that the greatness of the Kali Yuga is not the greatness of the Treta Yuga and Dvapara Yuga. Sri Krishna clearly saw the greatness of the Kali Yuga. You could not see what Sri Krishna prepared to show you after seeing this. Because your belief is brimming with foolishness.

Standing at a karma where the heavenly world can be seen, you are acting stubbornly after seeing the heaven of great fools. You should not desire such a foolish liking. Then what should you desire? A worthy karma that both you and I can like equally. You and your children should be able to take up that karma. Whatever theories come, man can only act and make others act by imbibing this karma. If acting otherwise, it is not good either. You have got an opportunity to handle a theory of this level. It is said that by fulfilling this dharma of Kali and sanctifying this Kali Yuga, each of you will become like a god. Beyond that, you will become eligible for liberation. It was your ignorance that you could not become entitled to this dharma of liberation.

How did you get this ignorance? By fabricating Puranas and epics, hiding tricks behind them, and worshiping and making others worship based on them, you not only distanced yourselves from each other in the sin of karma, but also became unable to ever get close to the virtues of humanity. This incorrectness is completely present in Hindu philosophy. We ourselves have spoiled this. Due to our wrong understanding, when we followed the theory by adding unnecessary things like untouchability, we had to speak by changing the nature of God. It is through this difference in nature attributed to God that you drifted apart. You should think that this will only serve a karma of making you kill each other.

We know that it is Guru Dharma that gives light to religious theories. People belonging to all religious theories stand included in Guru Dharma. For all these theories...

Guru refers to the Guru Dharma, which is capable of spreading light, as the soul of all dharmas. That is, if you examine this Guru, you will see that all the gods and goddesses, acharyas, and prophets who have come so far are united in this Guru. There is no other logic to it. If that is the Guru's spiritual merit, it is meant to be received by the disciple. However, not everyone will get it; it is the destined disciple who gets it. This destiny is not limited to one caste or one religion. Destiny is the time of birth. That is, creation will happen in the world according to the course of karma! The theory of destiny is the theory of the time of creation. Householders should act knowing this theory. Only then will they have good offspring. Offspring who are pure in karma and holy souls qualified to rule the country. That is how any karma gets its power. But we have not been trained in any of this. It is as if we are determined not to learn. Whatever the special feature of the Yuga Dharma is, a karma has been brought in that makes even a Chandala rule. We cannot step aside saying that a Chandala is ruling the country. This is how God punishes. Because of this rule, since it is impossible to keep people away by citing caste, a complete independence was brought about and this Chandala himself was made to rule. Is there a need to lose oneself by talking, getting acquainted, and getting used to those ignorant theories?

The dharma of Kali is exactly the same for the Brahmin, Kshatriya, Vaishya, and Shudra. But here the Shudra has come with eighty-four types of karma in eighty-four castes. If the Shudra is kept away like this, the Kali Dharma itself will be in danger.

Where did it go wrong? The mistake of denying Guru Dharma in the name of Vedic Dharma. Or the ignorance of behaving in an unconscious state, with dharma covered up without knowing the glory of the prophetic dharma. The masterminds of this ignorance are trying to escape by performing tantras and mantras. This is my experience. This is what I saw.

I had the experience even when I was a very small child. But I understood how to realize the experience through an experienced Guru. At first, I traveled along the path of Advaita. But that path did not help me reach the karma of realization.

How should realization be seen? Vision - vision in the waking state, dream state, and deep sleep state. Later it becomes transcendent. What is meant by transcendent? When meditation and chanting continue, we can see the light of life without our knowledge. We will see all the ways the grandfather lived in this world, all the ways the sky exists. Seeing that, he will see his Guru and himself. This will be seen without any difference between male and female. After that, the deity, or the path of the acharya that has been practiced, will be shown. This is the truth of vision. After seeing all this, one can bring oneself to the state of talking. Once the state of talking is reached, then doubts can be cleared. This is the dharma of direct perception. The core of the truth that is spoken as a word after being manifested like this is God! The one who reaches the form of sound is a Vedantin. The coming together of such a Vedantin and a prophet is the door of God. If we do not work and submit under a Guru who knows this, the sense of difference will always remain among us.

GURUVANIKAL · January 1997 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1111 to GV2:P1121
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

January 1997

Even if one does everything with the complete understanding that God is the core of the truth being revealed and taught, mistakes can happen - errors will occur. There is a reason for this. When we believe in a prophet or a Guru, by taking refuge in them, we are reaching God. As the disciple advances a little further, he feels that he and the Guru are not different. This realization will pave the way to the soul of truth. Even the different phases of life's journey can be seen. The variations in the journey of the soul, seen in the states of waking, dreaming, and deep sleep, and departing from the waking-sleep state to the Turiya state, this journey makes everyone alike. Thus, the Guru, who is the essence of truth situated within the bliss of the soul, encompasses countless formless skies. Whether one is a sectarian, a follower of a prophet, or a follower of a Guru, those who do not believe that there is only one God will have doubts. That is why it was said that mistakes will happen.

Even if one believes in the truth of the Guru, mistakes will occur if a situation arises where there is a lack of awareness about what needs to be observed and known. This is the danger of having a blemish (doubt) in faith. Such people are ignorant of Guru Dharma.

If faith remains unblemished, one will not be deterred by any circumstance. They do not care about wife, husband, children, wealth, power, or education. The theoretical ignorance that has existed in their family until now must change. With love, respect, and humility, it

What he needs is an act of redemption. This is all he requires.

This is the ideal model of the Grihasthashrama (householder stage of life). Such a householder has no need for caste or religion. There is no need for knowledge belonging to any sense of justice within the human system. It will not become alien by gaining strength in the ignorance that speaks and alienates each other. This is the extent of saying that the guru and the disciple are not different, but one. In the end, there will be no one to come along. When there is no one to come along, only that truth comes along.

I have come to tell this truth. I will not accept the guise of any theory. I have not come to gather people. Seeing your right and wrong, if you can correct the wrong in any way, I have come to fulfill that. You should know the Guru in that way. Otherwise, mistakes will happen. It will be difficult to even spend anything in the name of the Guru. Without knowing what the Guru is, that difficulty will not go away. Let me give an example, it is the history of a great royal family. For that, a story has been fabricated that he cleverly left his house. He did not go cleverly like that. He was a person who lived according to the Guru's principles. His own Guru told him - you have everything, but a situation will arise where your children will be of no use. What should be done when there is no fate for children? Karma can be done, all karmas can be done. He often asked his wife - what will you do after I am gone? I will just sit. Therefore, I will also die with you (it is a matrilineal system, isn't it). Alright, let it be so. He decided to test this. He went to Bhartrihari and practiced yoga. Through that, he trained to hold his breath from one yama to another. Thus, one day, after eating food and folding betel leaves, he sat like that and said again - the Guru has said that you have no fate. He is a king. Being a king, when he had no children or anything like that, he did a trick to know if what his wife said about dying with him was true. Because he had practiced yoga, he held his breath and lay down as if unconscious. Then the wife thought, he is gone. They did not beat their chests and wail. Because the husband's sister is nearby. The sister and children will come and take all the savings. Therefore, deciding that there is no need for fighting and grabbing, holding back the crying, they loaded gold coins and valuables into a bullock cart and sent it away. A gold rope was hung above where he was lying. It was impossible to take this. She found a way for that. She took a thick blanket, covered this body, stood on it, untied this gold rope, and put it in the cart. After that, she came back and started beating and crying. Then he also regained consciousness.

Later he took a piece of cloth, tore it into two, made one piece a loincloth, wore one piece around his waist and walked away. He came back again and sold everything he had for whatever price he could get. He gave the gold coins and other things to his sister. He gave to other relatives as well. He gathered the rest and gave it as alms. Giving alms means giving to the poor. Alms should not be given to the poor, but to the mendicants (sadhus). He does not know. Then he walked away. He wandered around like that for a long time. In the meantime, he would also go to his sister's house for alms. It was a shame for them that he came to the door as a beggar like this. Therefore, deciding that he should not live, they made an ada (a type of sweet) and gave it in his hand. It is said that he bought it happily and said this. The bronze ada will burn the house. Thus, it is said that this sister's house also burned down in the end. There is such a history.

What should be understood from this is that no matter who says they will die with you, or whatever else is said, things will only turn out like this. When there is no one to come along, only that truth comes along. My job is to say that whatever anyone can do for that truth, do it without any difficulty. Only in this way can I resolve your bad things.

One's own parents and ancestors are wandering without any means for birth, karma, or liberation. Giving karma, birth, or liberation to these individual souls

Self-action is what needs to be fulfilled. There is no point in saying that the Milky Way flowed for this fulfilling action. No matter who, what, or how it is made to flow, it will not flow. Because each soul is the remainder of the course of karma that has been acquired in many births. The effort of a householder is to describe the completion of dharma while standing in the attainment of such a birth. It is through that effort that one must reach the action to liberate this world.

This is the dharma of Kali. Will this change if you cry out in pity, holding on to the bottom of some story told by someone, after destroying a household dharma that is to be fulfilled one by one? You will not be able to extract anything from this story. What you need is to become the owner of a completely perfect color (varna). Become a soul that brings blessedness to it. Your lifetime should be the holy days of the soul developed as father, mother, children, husband, and wife, who are destined to bring blessedness to this soul. If you do not have the determination that the aspect of that color can be completely attained by you, such a life itself is in vain. The purpose of life is not to clear the doubts of the mind. What will you gain by clearing doubts like that?

Where does my soul stand in the action of attainment, how should the soul merge in Turiya after passing through the waking, dreaming, and deep sleep states, and how should that course of karma be broken later? This is the dharma called our field of action, which is completed by the soul. No one can break the course of karma except through the dharma of the Guru-disciple relationship. What is the Guru-disciple relationship? The Guru's sense of dharma that liberates whatever deity traditions have come and gone in the disciple's lineage. This relationship should not evolve for money and power, for the sake of the world's verbal state. The success of this relationship should be to acquire the state of color completion in the merit of the action that travels with difficulty and pain through anything. What should be copied into your children who take this up? Seeing the completion of the three states of waking, dreaming, and deep sleep, the responsibility of untying the knot to Turiya is handed over to the Guru. When the Guru unties that knot, the disciple's soul becomes completely free. This is freedom.

GURUVANIKAL · February 1997 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1122 to GV2:P1145
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani February 1997 When we say love of God, we must imagine a form for God in our mind. Because God is indeed a form. Otherwise, if we say God is omnipresent and give some other meanings, we will never understand God. Haven't we been worshipping the form itself for the past two yugas? When that familiarity exists, will you be convinced if it is said that God has no form? Moreover, it will be difficult to grasp.

Form does exist. Just as we see the form of a child or an adult when we take a photo of them, everything in this nature has a name.

That is not our subject. The form must be severed. That is, even if we reach the light of the past times, the subject is how it should be severed. From the time the past yugas (Treta and Dvapara yugas) began, there has been form meditation up to this day. There is also formless meditation. But we can

It is difficult to be convinced. But it is right next to us. We cannot understand it clearly unless we understand this formless form.

How should we understand when we say formless form? We say that God is formless. It is true that it can be seen as formless. If it can be seen, it means it has a form. That is, even an atom has a form, and what is seen in atomic form is also a form. But it has no specific form. But when it is seen in everything, it can be found in its color and qualities. The Guru is the one who recognizes these differences in color and quality. This is what we do not understand.

Placing an idol, placing a lamp, or even lighting an incense stick is done after thinking of something in the mind. This form will also keep changing according to the concept of the thinking mind. That is, the form will keep changing just as the concept of the mind is. But these forms can only be recognized by vision. How can we make the form known by such vision our own? Or how will it become ours? This is the subject. So, whether it is a form or formless, if we move forward believing that it is us, we will not see the form later. All this is one. We do not see anything else separately. For example, suppose we take a photo and see that form as large. But if you look at the size of this picture, it is only enough to be contained in a piece of that form. Then it has a complete form. It has a specific form. Seeing it suddenly like this, when that perspective also runs into it, that form is not seen as large or small. Then the focus is on a journey beyond this. Then everything becomes ours. It should be seen like that - everything as ours.

When we say that everything should be seen as ours, it is not like saying 'our property' by talking about the universe, it is exactly what we have seen. Everything we have seen is ours. But we see many things. From dawn, what all falls in our sight! Will all that become ours? It is not only ours, it belongs to many people. Or it belongs to many natures. How can we take back each of these things that belong to many individuals and are in many natures? A knowledge is imparted for that taking back. That action is what needs to be known. It is formless; at the same time, it has a form.

Whether it is formless or has a form, it is seen by giving it a form, just like seeing a picture of an atom. Similarly, even the absolute can be shown in a picture. Suppose a picture is taken of the solar sphere or the other nine spheres. But we cannot know what is contained in all these pictures. Similarly, we cannot know what is contained in each individual we see, where they have reached - we cannot know this either.

The thing that has been achieved at this end is something that has been implemented since the world was created. Do not say it does not exist, it does exist. But when we bring it up by giving it a form and divinity, because we cannot determine its measure, we give it a meaning in an undeserved extent and we become nothing. For example, can the person who saw Krishna make a mistake? Did they see Krishna as big as Krishna's size, or did they see Krishna as big as the absolute of the person who saw?

What we need to understand here is that every object has an absolute. Every atom has an absolute. In whatever way there is an absolute, there is also an end in that way. Thus the absolute of the earth is the absolute of the solar system known to us. The presence of a Guru is essential to understand in this way. What kind of Guru? Each subject and its completeness and specialty - thus recognizing the completeness, specialty, quality, color, and experience of everything, and recognizing the special quality of Om, that is, the long form of the letter consciousness of Omkara!

By writing 'Om', you see the Omkara. You also see Omkara as the embodiment of consciousness in your vision. You see that form into which the sound of Omkara merges. You attribute the distinction of a complete form to Om. Even when it is a formless sound, it has a form. You also see this complete form of Omkara contained within something that is completely formed. Don't you see that action, which makes many things that are complete forms into complete forms and sustains them, also as a form? Therefore, even though God is formless, He has a form. This is how it should be seen.

Seeing everything in the position of a Guru, and seeing it contained within that Guru, the Guru's perspective should be taken as the author of the Gita says, the Guru is the embodiment of all Dharma. What is this embodiment of Dharma? One should start seeing it from the cause of origin. It is not enough to have an origin revolving only around one earth. It is not enough to state the cause of this solar system, speak of Vedanta, and add wholeness. As Vedanta says, gross, subtle, cause, and effect come, and in the part that says cause and effect, what is in the subtlety? Increasing such subtlety, reaching from the atom to the whole in the nature of light, thus one can see the action in the Dharma called gross, subtle, cause, and effect. That is what we should call karma. How will that karma and the doer unite? Keep a medium, be it a Guru or something else. Realize the Guru as all these coming together in that medium. This is the vision!

Guruvani

March 1997

If you look at human history, everyone passes away confined within a form. The form of a place, the form of an ashram, thus everything is a form itself. Our subject is how this form will end. Triputi

It is said that when the end comes, the light of one's true nature (Swaroopa Deepam) will shine. That means, when the actions (karma) that exist as the triad (Thripudi) end, the light that is the true nature of the soul should shine at the end of life. When we say Thripudi, our understanding is Brahma, Vishnu, and Maheswara. The reality is not like that understanding. There are many types of Thripudi. The light that shines when Thripudi ends is also of many types. That can only be determined from experience.

It is often said that there is a witness deity (Sakshimoorthi) who is formless-form (Aroopaswaroopan) or of the form of Omkara. It is said in connection with Krishna and Rama as well. It is said in connection with all gods and goddesses. Om Tat Savitur Varenyam Bhargo Devasya Dheemahi.......... Here too, the form is added. Some people will explain the meaning of Om Swaroopa and dismiss it. What is needed is not to explain the meaning and dismiss it. How can one explain the meaning and dismiss the formless-form? It was seen that even the Gayatri mantra has a form. Then for what is the meaning explained and the form made non-existent? Therefore, such an imaginary commentary is not what is needed. When devotion grows, when the form grows through devotion, thousands of such states will be reached. In many places during these changes of states, deviations will occur. Someone standing next to me, appearing in my form, tells me to apply Dhanwantharam Kuzhambu on my head, applies it, and moves away. What can be said about this? The belief that 'it was the Guru who said it' is the most necessary thing. But what if one cannot recognize the Guru's words? It will take a long time to recognize it with perfect examples like that. It cannot be recognized by small methods.

One must know, one by one, crossing the changes of states, how this Guru has reached all moving and non-moving things, how he has reached all forms. Then the disciple himself sees a witness deity changing into a formless-form. That is, seeing it as a formless-form, seeing the form in the formless. But it also has a form. Like a mirage - like an illusion. In short, whatever objects exist as formless-forms, the Guru is in all of them! This is the concept of the Guru.

You have a flaw. A tendency to easily dismiss things saying, I have known all this, I have already known it. This complete concept of the Guru is not something you have known, taken, and thrown away. The variations of the Guru's form have changed billions of times. If one sees something that stands beyond time and space according to human capability, and dismisses it saying, Oh, this is not like what was seen in the old days, what can be done? This understanding is due to a lack of knowledge. This lack of knowledge exists in all subjects. Therefore, all knowledge must be acquired from the Guru. In billions of forms from the atom to the infinite, their respective knowledge is contained. How can this be known? It must be known through those who have experienced it. The knowledge seen and known in that way is what is here. From the smallest to the largest, whatever objects exist, whatever reality of form exists, see the Guru in all of them. The Guru seen in that way is the formless-form.

Then the Guru moves and stands as a form within one's own form. The Guru will be shown in the nature of whatever thoughts and imaginations one has. Whatever exists where names and forms merge and stand in the form of sound and point, all of them stand embraced in the Guru.

With us, there are men, women, big, small, father, mother, wife, husband, everything. But we have also given a place to each and kept them apart. Similarly, there is a different imagination for God as well. Everything that comes as the nature of the soul, the nature of the universe, or the goddess of the universe, comes through the Guru. Even the smallest people have experience here in this state. But everyone will not have that knowledge. There are those who do not get it even if they ask the Guru. How many people have cried lying down, saying it is enough if I get it one day. It will not be obtained like that. You must understand that too. For example, there was a person in Kadakkavoor. He used to struggle a lot to get this. He would wake up at 3 in the morning, bathe, and walk up to Mel Kadakkavoor to do poojas and other things. Those who do not struggle and desire in this way, those who do not struggle at all, they are getting it. What answer can be given to this? You should not think about whether someone gets it or not.

Whether you get it or not. Getting it also has a meaning, just like the relief you feel when applying Dhanwantharam Kuzhambu, as mentioned earlier. You can see it in all your worship deities. You will see someone; you will see it in someone. Everything must be given to you by making it formless and with form. It is the Guru who gives. Along with oneself, in one's own form, that is, exactly as one is, so is the Guru. It means the Guru matches one's imagination. The Guru will lift you out of all these imaginations. Moreover, only the Guru knows what you are.

Don't we say we had a dream? We are actually seeing and experiencing the dream as if it were real. But we only realize it after experiencing it. That it was a dream. Another thing is, after seeing the Guru, whatever appears and disappears in a formless state, all of that should become the Guru. We should be fortunate enough to see everything that appears and disappears in the name of the Guru. Bringing one to this fortune is called 'correction'. There are millions of such corrections. Therefore, it is necessary to call everyone and say it generally, and also to explain it individually to isolated people.

The experience of a thousand people is in a thousand ways. Why does God show it like that? Isn't it enough to show a truth in the same way? What is being shown to you is the repetitive power of a life that has come and gone in this world for so long, both formless and with form. That is why it is shown in different ways to different people. That change acquired in life is indescribable. This is how God's love is. God comes as formless and with form, and has transformed everything that sprouted, grew, and perished in this nature into the Guru. That is how everything in God's imagination has been transformed into the Guru. Therefore, the Guru must be present in everything that has come and gone in the universe through the ages. Otherwise, that Guru is not complete. That is why it is said that the seeker must get the Guru in many ways, both formless and with form. You must get this understanding firmly.

When it is said that God is love, we understand it because we have love within us. The form and nature of that power of love will keep changing. No matter how the form and nature of the power of love are changed, the Guru is beyond everything. Then it must be understood that whatever formless and formed natures God has, the Guru is the light of all of them.

What is meant by the repetition of the Guru? Suppose a person meditates in a cave for a long time and then dies. After that, in what form did that great soul come and be born? In what forms and natures did he come over the ages? This is repetition. Then one must know that the Guru is the lightness that recognizes each of these. It is this lightness that has entered as the core of bliss within our minds. It is out of our love that we give it many forms and call it. You cannot even imagine the magnitude of that action which we come to know one by one. It will not be enough whether you see it as one, a hundred, a thousand, a million, or in countless ways. This is where the Guru is needed. The Guru will point out that this is what it is. And then merge you into it. Thus, it will be merged into everything. Then only oneself and the Guru. This is how that lightness should grow. It is growing within our minds; as the bliss of life! Once bliss spreads into everything as love, then there is no form.

Guruvani

GURUVANIKAL · April 1997 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1146 to GV2:P1156
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

April 1997

There are quite a few people who have had the opportunity to come and stay in this ashram. After some time, they have a lot of sincerity, love, and respect. But there is a task that needs to be completed in our imagination. Until it is completed, everyone should have a restlessness and a conflict regarding it. How long have I been coming here, why did I come, what were my thoughts when I came, who would I have become by now, what is my duty towards this divine work having undergone such a transformation - matters like this should make everyone restless. That conflict is goodness.

Most of the activities here are done by householders. This uniqueness brings a part of a new knowledge. What is this new knowledge? In the past two eras, crores of people have come and gone to determine the fate of the yugas. The past two yugas mentioned are the Treta Yuga and the Dvapara Yuga. We don't need to make any major evaluations regarding the Satya Yuga. Because we have heard that the dharma of the Satya Yuga happened according to God's will. Therefore, it is enough if we evaluate the Treta Yuga and Dvapara Yuga. Many such Treta Yugas and Dvapara Yugas have passed like this. India has the authority over all these yugas, their actions, and their evaluations. No other place in the world has the authority to evaluate the actions of the yugas. The reason why India alone got this God-given authority is because of the actions of the great sages here. The great sages did not work to fight and win against anything. They were the ones who strove to bring joy, respect, mutual love, and a sense of cooperation among us. One aspect of the action they did with that desire passed in the Satya Yuga. However, in the Treta Yuga and Dvapara Yuga, it was not the action desired by the sages that took place. Thus, it has been going wrong for twenty-five Chaturyugas. The fundamental mistake has happened in the order of worship.

When we say worship, it should not mean just the worship in our country, but worship in all parts of the world. Worship is an aspect of the greatest freedom. When evaluating freedom, our country has often been unable to maintain that freedom. People from many countries have attacked and subjugated our country and destroyed our freedom of worship. It has happened so many times. The reason for this is that we failed somewhere. For these twenty-five Chaturyugas, we have not been able to find out where the real cause of this failure lies. Moreover, it has not been proven how the knowledge and familiarity of the Yuga Dharma have been lost over so many yugas. Highlighting the miraculous deeds of sages, telling wonderful stories of yogis who live for many years giving up food and water, talking about the valor of princes - it is by saying all this that we take pride in our Arsha culture. Finally, these celebrations come to an end by jumping and dancing in front of some devil. We have been taught that the God who shows this test will save us. Let this God save us and straighten us out or implement our duties and dharmas, but because such a familiarity has come among us, neither a man nor a woman has been able to bring about a truthful life. So many kings ruled in a state that cannot be fully depicted even by a single language existing in the world. They changed and established culture and language in their own ways. As a result, India was left without even a single language to stand together. The plight that India has reached now, to unite the underlying aspirations of the people

It lies in the inability to transmit it. Even if the 'unity in diversity', which is described as the unique heritage of India, sprouts up somewhere, we have the helplessness of not being able to transmit it into our lives.

Before speaking about realizing this unity in people's lives, and the action and culture required for it, I need to reveal some truthful aspects. There are some important occasions in my life, and the knowledge and pains that have come together during those occasions. Since you do not try to evaluate and understand these insights and pains, I am recording them again in another way. I did not live my life thinking that I would have such a mission or such a sacrifice. All I had was an imagination of love. As that imagination of love transformed and began to spread among the people, I wondered if this worldly life was this painful. Even though I lived among ordinary people for six or seven years with the burning of this pain, no one could understand that there was such an ache beneath this life. No one knows why fellow human beings who do not experience this level of pain fail to understand it. But this pain is a tradition.

Generally, we hear many things about the greatness of the guru-disciple relationship. That is somewhat true as well. But no one has been able to understand that the goodness in the guru-disciple relationship only reaches a certain part of the truth. The goodness planted by the guru in the disciple grows, and the fruit of that goodness is determined through the experience that comes from the disciple. The experience where everything grows in the disciple and becomes material wealth, knowledge, mastery, and enlightenment. The blessedness cultivated in him in this way is for the survival of dharma for all times! The pain when none of this aligns with the guru's expectations is unimaginable. Thus, life itself becomes a struggle for life. In a life lived as a synonym for struggle, the very course of a tradition—the tradition mentioned earlier—grows and stands tall. The unchanging course of 'I am big, you are small'. This course has brought about a series of pains in every way.

So, what has society gained? Only an asset of not being able to love anyone. This asset has expanded and reached the familiarity of a land and the familiarity of a country. This can never be set right. This lack of love has become an action through many ages. Our effort is to bring a change to this. This life of effort is what is called ashram life.

I have convinced you of this many times. I have made you experience it truthfully. But what I see in you is a familiarity that cannot be transmitted in your home. The character that cannot be brought about in your home cannot be brought out outside. Even as father, mother, and children, you do not unite. This lack of unity has come to your neighborhood, the country, and all parts of the world. It is not easy to change this. Because it is a familiarity that has been respected and worshipped for so long. It is a familiarity that has been passed down through lords, kings, emperors, and various other forms of government. People have even assumed that this is truthful knowledge. Now there is no need to abandon that knowledge. Because we have the freedom to live in a better way based on this knowledge of ours. Moreover, if knowledge and wealth can be tied to this government, there is a situation to achieve anything. Therefore, in front of knowledge, in front of truth, we are mute and blind. Reaching all the systems of life, a large government has arrived that does not know how to behave with whom, and it is deceiving us with the size of that government. Staring at such a system, what, where, and how can anything be done?

What has come down through the ages must change completely. That is, what has been worshipped ancestrally must change. What needs to be changed and re-established in every age has not been changed for many ages. Thus, the dharma of the age has not been carried out. That is the root cause of our destruction. Our efforts must reach the extent of establishing this dharma.

GURUVANIKAL · May 1997 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1157 to GV2:P1169
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani May 1997

When comparing our knowledge with the knowledge of other countries, we can agree, if we want, that it is a very slow knowledge. However, because our ancestors did not handle it properly, we could not truly bring that knowledge to the side of knowledge. Therefore, we have not been able to take the knowledge that is our heritage. Nor have we been able to give it.

There is a knowledge—a vision—in the nature of our ashram that is not usually heard among us. It is often referred to by names like pratyaksham (direct perception) and asareeri (disembodied voice). But for us, vision means what has happened since the creation of this earth, or the solar system itself. What are the causes and effects of such events? Has the true knowledge of the vision been passed down through the lineage of the Trimurti or the lineage of Manu? Or is the vision its true aspect in the division of philosophers and ideological theories? It is a glory granted by God to bring peace to the arguments that have come through these various aspects. Through this glorious visionary aspect, remove the ancestors who were the cause of the birth of individuals from that position, that is, give them compassion in that nature and keep them aside, and then give birth to them when they are to be born. This vision is what God has allowed us to bring such peace. It is the duty of you who are living today to raise children who are worthy of giving birth to the ancestors who are thus kept aside with compassion at the appropriate times. But the living do not seem to have that familiarity. Prior familiarity is the growth of familiarity. There must be an ashram to develop prior familiarity.

To develop this familiarity, God brought many ideological religions and many prophetic religions. Various types of Vedantic programs were introduced at different intervals. But nothing has been able to unite these diverse traditions. You do not even know that such a mistake exists in human life. Therefore, you have not been able to respect each other so far. You have fallen into such a chaotic path.

You cannot unite by recognizing the lack of knowledge that existed in the beginning regarding this and its good and evil. For that, this life itself is not enough; today we have living conditions that are much more developed compared to the past. Despite all the greatness, we are running competitively after education, wealth, and enlightenment. No one is trying or desiring to foster human love among us. What is the reason for this? The same as said before. That is, those living today are the remainder of the mistake of establishing the foundations of life without recognizing ancestors, gods, sages, and ascetics, and without knowing what the state of yoga, the state of education, and the state of wealth are. Therefore, you do not have the knowledge to love, to get close through love, and to handle anyone. What you have is the knowledge to return to the barbarism that has been accumulated under the name of a new culture. What can a language, a country, a religion, or a culture do to resolve this?

We must be able to do that. God has given us such an ashram familiarity. An action to unite your faith, your love, your respect, and your humility. What is faith? There is a faith in mother, father, wife, and husband. Let that faith become straight with knowledge. When that becomes straight, whatever we have in the land, all of that

It can be straightened out. What good is it to learn and live by looking at the hypocritical practices outside, thinking that our house is no good and everything else is fine, and then get ruined? How can we get ashore? What good has the human race gained by worshiping God in many ways and showing off with drums, songs, noise, and respect?

We cannot take one good thing by separating these mistakes one by one. God has shown us an easy way for that from the past. God gave knowledge to the king for the birth of Krishna and the birth of Jesus. But the scholars or leaders of that time did not allow that knowledge to be believed like that. Not only that, they also encouraged to take it in the way it happened before. Therefore, with the life of those great men, their idea went away. Or they fabricate a theory suitable to degrade the people and teach those ideas through it. When that happened, there were neither the ideas of great men nor the fabricated theories. What do we need now?

An idea suitable for this time, which gives the right to all the varnas in the varnashrama dharma, the Brahmin, the Kshatriya, the Vaishya, and the ignorant Shudra born in the fourth quarter, to grow up and reach Brahminhood together and get liberation. No lineage has been able to give an idea based on this knowledge till today. That is not because of the fault of the ascetics who came with the ideas. What do the ascetics know? They think that if God is manifested, God will come here and beat many to death and make some others good.

The idea given by God for the human world to change from this ignorance is what we are developing through this ashram acquaintance. We have received this with all the special qualities according to the time. The ascetics, the rulers, and the people have desired and suffered for a long time for this arrival. There is a big story behind this of desiring and suffering for a long time. We do not have the qualification to work up to its qualification. A situation has happened to our country and house that we cannot go outside our country. All countries have this situation. Because no country has the experience of taking back the idea according to the qualification of the time.

No matter through which time cycles it comes back, an idea reaches its complete form in Kali. Kali is more important than Satyayuga. If anything has gone excessive due to any lifestyle in Satyayuga, it is Kali that straightens it out through realization. The lifestyles of Treta Yuga and Dwapara Yuga are also straightened out in Kali.

This Kali has another special feature. From the beginning of this Kali, God has desired and worked for many times. It may seem why God has this desire. It has been said and convinced that God has no desire. God has the will of what should happen and what should not happen. It is only that God does not need the result of karma. It is enough to understand it like this.

Because the language, dress, lifestyle, and nature of each country are different, each one can only learn to live from a distance from each other in the acquaintance of each other. In this lifestyle of man and woman, many mistakes have been made. That is, when the time of the god and goddess was kept and grown, it was thought that it was God's play. It came to the place where God took it. We could not even recognize whether we are killing God or God is killing us. What else can we do other than fight among ourselves when we have reached like that? Some people should have a desire other than this. That is some ascetics or prophets or people. Otherwise, it cannot be implemented. Because the people as a whole are caught in negative qualities and are looking. Everyone is trying to be stubborn by saying their side. Even if it is like this, we have a favorable situation that this is God's decision. That is, whatever our ancestors worshiped and did and spoiled in whatever way, liberating all of them

We have a task given by God to hand over to the children. We have built such a path.

GURUVANIKAL · June 1997 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1170 to GV2:P1194
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani June 1997 What mistakes have crept into this worldly philosophy, how those mistakes have manifested in action, how they have become mixed up in the paths of the avatars and their followers - it is very difficult to retrieve and correct all this. But God has given us a way to retrieve and correct all this. We also have a practical method in that regard. This is being truly experienced in our lives. We see it in the states of waking, dreaming, and deep sleep. God has also given you an experienced Guru to distinguish the truth and falsehood that may arise in this. But you are unable to take it. There are many children here who have grown up in that path. None of them have been able to take it. Nor have they been able to train someone else. Or if they have somehow grasped it, they have only destroyed it by overthinking it with their intellect. This situation is extremely regrettable.

To change this situation, you must sit together as father, mother, and children and think. You must have a determination that this subject, which I received as a gift from God, will be shared and developed together and returned as God's own. How will you give it as God's own? You understand and practice it to some extent. But you are unable to practice it with its true value. For that, this is what you must do. No matter how hard it is, unite the home. Do not try to reform the land without uniting the home. That will never work. Home, land, then country - the nature of unification should be in this order. Otherwise, we will always be running after someone - politicians, looters in any kind of administrative field, the aggressors we see and hear every day. The entire Shudra class is ending their lives running like this.

Shudra does not mean a specific caste. In a world where actions are performed without the right to Brahmajnana, whatever classes exist, those classes are called Shudras. We believe that karma is completed with drumming, singing, noise, and taking a vow on a specific day. Not only can karma not be completed with all this, but these are also thorns on that path. The worldly philosophies that humans have adopted for crores of years have gone wrong like this.

As a remedy for this, God has given us this subject and path. But the world is so fierce that it cannot be desired and taken. Our birth is in such a time. Yet we have nothing to fear. Because this time we are born in, Kali, is better than the Dwapara Yuga in which Sri Krishna was born. Since Sri Krishna was born at the end of Dwapara, which came after the period when Treta Yuga had grown, he knew how to fulfill the parts of Treta Yuga and Dwapara together. But Krishna

Despite his efforts, he could not get the lineage to do this. There is a reason for that. There is a truth that when a person is born, the part of raising that person, making them reach a certain level, and making them act is developed through their successors. Krishna also struggled a lot to bring about that truth. But he couldn't. Even after having to sacrifice his own children, he couldn't. What is the value of Sree Krishna? You are seeing what God has taught us about the value of Sree Krishna. That alone is enough to know who Sree Krishna is. The situation was such that he could not even live in his own house after he was born. Whatever the truth or falsehood of the stories circulated in connection with Krishna's life may be. Today's need is not to evaluate it. Krishna came after announcing in advance that he would come at this time. Unable to cope with the house where the children born before him were beaten to death en masse, he ran somewhere. Even after running so much, he could not survive. Forgetting all the actions done by directly confronting, clashing, and establishing alliances with all the kings, it is imagined that he goes into everything by singing hymns of praise to God. How can one imagine without action? No one knows this.

What is karma? It is the familiarity with karma, which is a mixture of good and bad, past, present, and future. It is the guru, the king, or the government that should prepare this familiarity with karma for the world. It is the contemporaries who should do this. Do we and the rulers think so deeply? Only a person who thinks and acts on an idea at this level becomes a believer. Belief does not mean my belief, your belief - it is not like that. Do not taint belief. Do not taint belief for any reason, not even to gain someone else's love. Because we must value our love for our lives and our love for our lineage.

What all sorrows will follow us when we start acting. Let it be happiness or sorrow, good or bad, whatever it is, it is ours. We have not thought of rising to the position of such knowledge. Or if anyone has done so, we have only destroyed them so far. Not only that, by destroying homes and lands so that neither men nor women can grow, the human race has reached the position of ignorance and is testing each other's strength. What will anyone get from this test of strength? Suppose there can be achievements according to each of our desires. But what will those achievements become when they come to our children? We can see that fighting, murder, and robbery are sitting right in our house without going anywhere looking for them. The misfortune arises that we can never raise a good girl, or when she becomes a mother, she cannot impart the father's knowledge and the mother's love and affection to the children, and thus give birth to offspring who bring light to the world. Because the mother says, the children should be like what the mother does. The father says, the children should be like what the father does. Thus all mothers and all fathers are fighting.

What will we do here? If we end up in an action that only fights and wins, we will not be able to do anything. To change this, see what happened behind us, and move forward without tying ourselves behind it. Father, mother, and children should try to move forward together. If each of us does not take care, we will never be able to escape. We should not take together the good and bad of our past miseries, the course of action done by governments like the imperial position, but rather think and know what the existence of good should be without evil coming into our hands. For that, it is not just necessary, but essential to have a care about what should be when becoming parents. As a basic element of that, you should know what the action done in this ashram is. By removing your deity, ancestors, and sage from the influence of your action, your action is brought forward in that place. But you do not know that. If you have ancestors who corrupt your tendencies, from that the ancestors and thereby you

It is liberating. Is this something that you, your home, your government, or the worship you have been following can do for anyone? This ashram dharma permanently changes you from the course of action that destroys you, and blesses each life by placing it in virtue, fortune, and dharma.

You know nothing about a sannyasi. Not everyone has grown up to be a wise person. But if they grow according to their own knowledge, we are unable to recognize them. This is the truth of it. If we sit around just to find fault with them, how will they correct their mistakes? Let them correct their own mistakes. This is the aspect of it. Otherwise, the mistakes of the world will not be corrected. If said in the language of Vedanta, it is not something that is going to happen in any other way.

Guruvani September 1997

When we lead a life, there should be a caution. Two things are required to maintain that caution. The ashram experience we have had so far is modeled on the Treta Yuga and Dwapara Yuga. Such a model will not work in Kali. Kali has its own dharma. The dharma of Kali is to act knowing the time of the Deva and the time of the Rishi. For that, one must cross the state of the Deva and attain the state of the Rishi above it. Regarding India, that achievement is never right. It cannot be said that it is because of India's fault. But it is a fault. We are unable to cross the state of the Deva and attain the state of the Rishi in the Indian way. Or if any ashram character has worked well in that way, it will be brought to some bad system. Most people have not understood this.

Then the prophetic religion. We have not practiced the prophetic religion to date. What is ours is also not ours. Because we don't have it, what have we become? An inability to act on the side of goodness has come. This inability has come as if making us all ignorant.

This must be changed. No one understands how to change this. You must have the ability to understand this and live. If you do not have that ability, even if you live with me, you will become bad. That is, you will have a misfortune where you cannot know yourself. Therefore, there must be a decision that we will live in this way. Only if there is that decision can you know each other. Otherwise, no one should have the idea that this can be brought from somewhere and fixed.

My desire is that you should have a determination that you will achieve something with a life. If you do not have that determination when living with me, then I should move from here. But because it is not suitable for a life, I cannot do that. You must make a decision that you will live in harmony with this divine will. Otherwise, you must take a precaution to live on your own. Otherwise, you will be neither in the ashram nor at home. If you feel that what I am saying is not for your need, you should do it at least thinking it is for my need. Either something good for you, or for me.

You should never lead an ashram life looking only at the good of the ashram. You must also see your own good. If you do not prioritize your own good, none of this is going to happen.

Why is it that not even one person among you has risen up as an exemplary model? I have often told you, if you wish to live associated with me, either you live as belonging to the ashram. Or stay away from it. When I say stay away, it doesn't mean there is a hindrance to coming and going. I have told you a lot, and have a lot more to tell. But why do I say it in vain? Say it and cause pain? This is my pain. You don't understand that. No matter how much is said to you, you prefer to go along with the old habits of your life.

You do not have the knowledge about the greatness of this ashram and this idea given by God. That is not your fault. In terms of an ashram system, this is an ashram system of a kind we have never seen before. An ashram system that follows the Yugadharma has never come before. Therefore, no one has been able to explain it back to anyone until today. No one even knows what Yugadharma is. Other than the fact that it is written about in the Puranas and such, no one has any other knowledge about it.

In the Chatur Yuga, the nature of Satya Yuga is of a special kind. Kali is the time that resolves the negative aspects that have been repeating for three periods. If any flaw has occurred even in Satya Yuga, Kali is the time to resolve it. Otherwise, Kali is not bad. Saying Kali is bad is meant for the Deva, not for humans. What is this flaw? Now, when looking up to the time of Sri Rama, it is seen as a long period - eighteen lakh years. Taking into account the heavenly pleasures of the Devas, worshipping them and living in that state, their merit has been exhausted. Since the merit has been exhausted, it means they have nothing left to do. That is the truth of it. If they want, they can take birth. Or if they do not want birth, they must have received the teaching conducive to liberation. Without receiving that teaching, they cannot do anything. When a Guru of that stature comes, they want to get a birth. Otherwise, do not assume that the Deva has any other ability or knowledge. It is not right for us to get any such notion. Another thing, what the Deva has is a demonic quality. However, the Deva's life is one that has stood in many ways, danced around in many ways and perished. So what that shows would be the quality of a demon. Similarly, Yakshi and Gandharva (Akasha Gandharva) and so on. What harm do they all do? Haven't we heard that if this possesses, it won't leave. So what good is such a demon going to do for us? How many such entities have been seen right here? What all nonsense have they come and stood and said during Darshan here. You all have seen and experienced all this. Therefore, you should discuss all this among yourselves and decide that we should not go that way anymore. We must tell each other and realize that this is why we are doing all this. We must live and learn in that way. If the continuation of the old is what is destined for you, then what connection do you have with this? Without knowing this, how will many people, using their knowledge and maintaining the ashram and temple practices they have seen, and speaking Vedanta, become acquainted with this? The situation is such that nothing can be clearly told to you. Because your knowledge is knowledge from the Deva, the Devi, and other such demonic forces.

If science is false

Look at the eclipse

There is a saying in Tamil. The scientific conclusion on how an eclipse occurs is not completely correct. Purana scholars, Veda scholars, and science scholars in the sense of Indian scientists have a misfortune of not being able to fully grasp anything that has been discovered scientifically to date. The reason for that is for many crores of years

is happening. Only if we can see the reason for this, can we understand what the matter is.

The calculation of Kaliyuga is stated in different ways not just by many scholars, but even by many visionaries. The reason for this is that the calculation of Manvantara was mistaken. The calculation of Manusmriti and the current astronomical calculation, which works by comparing Indian science and Western science, cannot be reconciled. As long as we do not see what mistake has happened not just in India or the Earth's orbit, but in the solar system itself, any knowledge can only be stated for a short time.

This knowledge is not our knowledge according to God's will here. We should be able to live by submitting to this knowledge. Otherwise, each of you should not be seeking one thing after another. Those who are interested in this, do this. I mentioned earlier, it cannot be explained and convinced. There is no question of whether there is authority for this action or not. God has given you the fortune to come here, obey, and make others obey. If you throw it away and show something else, it is not possible for me. You should live by deciding what you are going to do next, after each of you has understood it.

GURUVANIKAL · October 1997 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1195 to GV2:P1206
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani October 1997

There are quite a few people here who have got the opportunity to come and stay in this ashram. All of them have sincerity, love, and respect. Those who stay in the ashram and those who stay in houses outside must remember some things very seriously. Our mind should be full of conflict until we can complete the action that comes through our imagination. That is, how long has it been since we came? What did we come for? What were we before we came? The mind should always be full of conflict with the thought of what we have become now.

Most of the activities here are done by householders. Through the action done here, a part of a new knowledge is being brought. What is this new knowledge? Before this, two eras have passed - Treta Yuga and Dvapara Yuga. Like this, how many Treta Yugas and Dvapara Yugas have passed. Similarly, how many Satya Yugas have passed. But we do not need to make such big evaluations. We have heard that each era happened in a special way earlier. Crores of people came and went to settle these two eras. The authority to conduct these Yuga Dharmas belonged to India. The reason for that is that it became the birthplace of sages. The sages came and worked not to fight and win against anything. It is to develop our happiness, mutual respect, and sense of cooperation. That desire of the sages and the sense of dharma they developed through us existed in Satya Yuga. But Treta Yuga and Dvapara Yuga have gone wrong. It has been wrong for the last 25 Chaturyugas. That mistake that has come through the system of worship has affected not only India but the whole world. It has brought people to a false sense of freedom.

When talking about freedom, we need freedom. Our country was captured by people from other countries. This has happened many times. We do not know where the flaw that caused this lies. Even if we might know, we do not have an understanding that clearly shows what the knowledge of the Yugadharmas that have come down to this day was like. Telling stories about the miracles of some princes and ascetics, and about yogis who lived emotionally without needing food, and finally dancing in front of a devil sitting in our courtyard is the familiarity with the knowledge we have gained. Then they have shown knowledge by showing tests. Will the God who shows this test make us right?

Because such a familiarity came and went among us, we could not bring a true life to men and women. Many kings ruled in a way that no language could express completely. Because their language changed and returned, India lost a common language to speak together. As far as India is concerned today, we are unable to express our desires to each other, develop them, correct them, and share them. Even if we try for that, it is not easy.

Before detailing all these flaws in this manner, I have something to inform you. I have had many opportunities in my life to say what I have said to many people many times. During those opportunities, many pieces of knowledge and many pains as a prelude to them have occurred. The cause of those pains has also been explained. But you have not wished to evaluate it. I had not prepared anything for that knowledge. What I had in my mind was only the imagination of love. When this love was shared and shared among ordinary people, experiences occurred that made it seem whether this worldly life is so painful. This excess of pain occurred through the guru-disciple relationship. Many people talk about the greatness of the guru-disciple relationship. There is some truth in it. But we have not been able to understand that its good and evil are only up to a certain part in the completion of truth. One can plant and grow a plant and see its goodness and experience it. When each such growing thing is acquired as property and grows and spreads as education, wealth, and lordship, we are unable to know for what period this growth and existence is. This is the truth regarding our life. That is, the shortcoming that has come in us is a course of the tradition in which we lived and grew up. That course is the knowledge that knows that I am the tradition that includes all the pains lived as I am big and I am small, and wealth, and whatever can be acquired along with wealth.

Therefore, what we have gained is a capital that cannot love anyone. That lack of love has come through the familiarity of a land and the familiarity of a country. We can never correct it. You should know that this realization is the light of a knowledge that has come over many periods.

What is the condition of every child in our land? We should be able to bring a change to this condition. That is what you get from the life in this ashram. I have informed you of this truth in many forms. I have also made you experience this truth at least in a small way. Due to some familiarity with which you grew up, you are not even able to bring this experience together and show it in your own home. This is what I have been able to understand from living with you. You can only show outside what you can show at home. You have become father, mother, children, land, country, and thus the world in such a way that you cannot be united. How can this be changed suddenly? Because of all this, it is a familiarity that came and went being worshipped and respected ancestrally. The result of that familiarity changing into many over many periods is a familiarity shared by capitalists, kings, emperors, and all such governments. All these children are the ones who shared it thinking it was the knowledge of some truth. None of this is what we have given today.

If freedom is achieved as if by capturing and tying it up, that freedom is not conducive to living without fear. We are not the ones who achieved freedom that allows us to live without fear. What we achieved was freedom gained by a people lacking knowledge, wealth, valor, and enlightenment. If some valor and wealth are tied behind the ignorant, what will we become? Therefore, we have become a silenced race that plays and grows. With the way of life we are accustomed to, we can only live without a sense of purpose about what we should become. We have only had governments that do not know how to behave with whom and in what manner. No matter how big a government has come, they have all committed their own thefts. Which model will we look up to and live by?

I am not saying this is anyone's fault. It cannot be said that it is due to a lack of education, enlightenment, or wealth. What our ancestors worshipped must be changed in every era according to the dharma of the time. The failure to practice Yuga Dharma is the reason for all shortcomings and faults. When we compare the knowledge of our country with the knowledge of other countries, we can agree that ours is a very leisurely knowledge. But our ancestors did not reach a point of knowledge that could handle that knowledge. Therefore, we are unable to take and give the knowledge of our country.

Since coming here, you have understood a knowledge that you have never heard before. That is Darshana. Some have called this direct perception and disembodied voice. But what is called Darshana has very large dimensions. What all happened since the time this earth or the solar system was formed? What is the reason for such things to happen? Are these causes and effects in the lineage of Manu or the lineage of the Trinity, or is it the result of many people coming together? Darshana is the spreading light of this knowledge. How can what came through many be brought together? But God has given us permission to bring a stability to this. What is that? Our ancestors, the ancestors of our individuals, are changed, given compassion in their character, and set aside. Then they can be born at the time they need to be born. The sense of duty of the people living today is to raise children with the qualification to give birth to those glorified ancestors. What experience have these people gained to raise such qualified children? Past experience is the growth of experience. The Ashram is the bridge that connects past experience and the growth of experience. That is, the place that radiates the true light that determines and nurtures the course of past and future eras!

Guruvani
