GURUVANIKAL · November 1997 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1207 to GV2:P1243
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

November 1997

Sivagiri Liberation March at Santhigiri

What the Guru spoke to them when they arrived

The story we know about Sivagiri and Narayana Guru is, to some extent, complete ignorance. The reason for this is that we have fallen into a miserable state where we cannot recognize and act upon what Sanathana or Hindu Dharma is. This misfortune did not start today or yesterday. We have no realization of when Sanathana was abandoned, what caused it to be abandoned, or how the Trimurthis came into being. When the Trimurthis arrived, there is a predicament where the face of wisdom does not speak. When we say Sanathana, it is a course of action that utilizes four periods. The four periods are Krita Yuga, Treta Yuga, Dwapara Yuga, and Kali Yuga. We have not understood the concept of 'Kali Shudrakal' mentioned by the author of the Gita or those who respect and know about it. That matter of Shudrakal is what we do not understand. It remains the same even today.

We must consider at least from the time of Sree Krishna. The ignorance of not even knowing that Sree Krishna was born as such a great soul is evident as far as we are concerned. That is, the opportunistic conditioning that taught 'Kali is destruction'. Therefore, spreading a notion among us that Kali is useless, as far as the family members are concerned, the entire knowledge of the house became Kali destruction. Coming along that path, the knowledge of the house, the knowledge of the land, and the knowledge of the country became like that. We have not been able to lead a course of action that respects the great souls who have come until today. Proof of this is that Sree Rama descended into the Sarayu river, or took Jalasamadhi

It is used in the sense of having attained. In truth, we have never been able to investigate the reason why he had to attain Jalasamadhi (water burial). Since then, how many great souls have come in this Kali Yuga. How many crores of great souls have come in the other two yugas. There are many sorrowful stories of us not being able to understand what anything is and act accordingly. We have never searched for this, known it, and acted upon it. We don't even know it. We turned towards the path that the Puranas and epics led us.

Three periods are repeatedly mentioned. It is also said that the dharma of Kali is the Shudra era. What is a Shudra? In the karma practiced as four varnas, the dharma is that the Shudra varna is fully entitled to knowledge. What is called Shudra was changed to 64 Nairs in Tamil Nadu and 84 in Kerala. Even these 84 castes do not know what they have done. This has not appeared in the history of the great country of India to date. Isolated gurus and some people who practiced yoga, jnana, karma, and bhakti went their separate ways. Therefore, caught in a dilemma of not being able to distinguish what karma and justice are, we became completely like strangers. This is an ignorance that cannot be corrected in any way.

Narayana Guru worked seeing all this. He is not talking about caste and religion. Many people say that Narayana Guru's ideology is socialism and so on. He behaved with the desire to give a karma similar to knowledge to the Shudra class as a whole, to these 84 castes, and through that karma, to attain knowledge. But there was not even a single eye with him to see it in that way. Therefore, the destruction of Kali, which acted without recognizing this Yuga Dharma, is being sown. This has been going on for so many Kali Yugas.

According to the teaching that Kali is destruction, how many people have seen Kali as destruction. Kali is not destruction. The karma given to the Shudra clan is the knowledge that was mixed with the Vaishya, Kshatriya, and Brahmin over three periods, and this belongs to the fourth quarter. So, an inability to act, not knowing what to do in this fourth quarter, stands bewildered before us. This is due to the misfortune that occurred because the dharma of the time was not known. However, some people say with some words here and there. In Jnanappana also, these words are highlighted, asking if Kali is not the best among the times.

The ignorance that sannyasins are mere beggars, or mere mendicants, has placed a completely great flaw among the people. Sannyasins are neither beggars nor mendicants. Besides, I am speaking in the sense you call a sannyasin. Our people often ask if he is a Siddha. But Siddhi does not mean sannyasa or jnana. By worshipping each god and goddess and showing some of their respective feats, we see a mark of magical powers. We assume this is sannyasa. No, this is not sannyasa. A sannyasin is one who has passed through the Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, and Dvapara Yuga over many ages and goes on completing the dharma of Kali. If such a great person is born and comes among these people, what we see today is the remainder of those who traveled with many evil desires, unable to recognize it, and many householders who thought that no one should be greater than me. This ignorance has not affected one caste or religion; all castes and all religions have fallen into this path. So, an understanding about this did not fully come into the guru's paths. Nor was it recognized what the guru's path is.

A Guru is not one who comes and seeks knowledge. A Guru is one who has seen and acted upon what the evolutionary function of life is over many ages. No individual knows about such a Guru. Since Sree Krishna, that is, at least within the last five thousand years, not a single scholar or layman has said what the ignorance that happened to India is. But Narayana Guru Swami wanted to say this. A few family members and a group of attendants who stood with them created a misfortune that could not be told. They are evil-doers who think that others should not rise at all. That

The negative effects of karma began to affect Narayana Guru from the moment he started his work. The people have not understood this. The truth is that there was not a single sannyasi or follower to lead him to the realization of the fruits of his karma, which he had worked for with immeasurable hardship. It is not their fault. The reason is that it is an experience that has passed through twenty-five chaturyugas.

No one knows how or where to correct this familiarity. If any sannyasi comes, they go after him, inquiring whether he is a siddha and clinging to all that. This is not something that a sannyasi or a siddha can do. The prayerful nature of the people was not right to get such a great person. Saying that everything is the play of God, they are drowning in it. While drowning in that path, they even mocked Sri Krishna by calling it the illusion (maya) of God. Then no one understood how to take the wisdom, that knowledge of Sri Krishna, the author of the Gita. Filling that refined karma with Hindu puranas and epics, and not knowing about the four Vedas, they did not know what the Veda in Kali Yuga is like. In that state, Kali was destroyed. It has the antiquity of twenty-five chaturyugas. One chaturyuga itself is twelve thousand divine years. One divine year is three hundred and sixty years. Seeing this, what can the people do? How should it be taken among the people? If a rich man or a scholar says that they have studied till today and got a true scientific sense, how will they correct it? It cannot be corrected like that. This is the biggest mistake that happened to India. This is exactly what happened even when Gandhiji came. You should understand at least the position where Sri Krishna stood, seeing this at least from the time of Sri Rama. What we know about Sri Krishna, we mocked, distorted by telling Krishnaleela, and kept it as stories and plays, making it an embellished mind. Then, when it was explained one by one, it became complete ignorance. This is not a mistake made by a mere scholar or a layman. It is a great blunder committed against the world. If you want to recognize this blunder, you have only one way. Other than that way, no purana, epic, or history will correct you. This is the truth. This is my experience.

There was a time when each of us thought that if the Trimurti appeared visibly, everything was achieved. We never got an opportunity to know and make known what the knowledge-action that came from the face of Brahma is, seeing how the karmas of Shaktheyam, Shaivam, and Vaishnavam came about and what they are, rather than the Trimurti. If someone comes to tell, they will not let him live. An example of that is Dayananda Saraswati. Those with some knowledge know what was done to Dayananda Saraswati. He was born in a Brahmin family. He is a great man who fought for it, asking what you are showing. Among the many songs in Tamil, there is a history there that said 'One caste, one religion, one God'. This is what Narayana Guru said in Malayalam.

Thus, we did not have the knowledge that the four states of South India were ruled by the Shudra class. You might have heard of Cheraman Perumal. Similarly, Pandyan, Cholan, and Pallavan. Because it was not known that these four states were ruled by the Shudra class, they repeated the mistake that occurred across ages by singing their praises. But we do not know in which era Shiva was a human incarnation. By repeatedly saying that, they elevated him to the level of Kailash, making it impossible to know how this should work. When it later came in another way, there was a karma adorned with Shaktheyam dancing up to Kalkudakali. This is the favorite deity of the King of Mysore, known as Chamundeshwari.

So, all the kings who have come to this day have made this mistake. The same happened to the Maharaja of Travancore. It is not known why this said matter was given into the hands of this king. Instead of explaining what the path of the Shudra is in knowledge, complete foolish answers, ignorance, and lack of love transformed it into how many castes and drove them far away.

The negative karma of confining the refined language to the temple compound, calling it Koothambalam and Pattambalam, and bringing it up to the Oottupura, has reached all people. But how this

How will the people recognize it? It is not the people's fault either. Even though many great souls have come, they have not been able to correct this and hand it over to the people. It was with this understanding that Narayana Guru started by saying 'One Caste'. The reason is that Thirunelli Krishnan Vaidyar, who was considered the greatest scholar among those who stood near the Guru at that time, and many others did not understand this. Even after understanding it, they did not know what to do. Those who know the establishment of Sivagiri and the quarrels and disputes at Aruvippuram closely will know this. He went from there, had the Kannampara temple built, and seeing that it was not right either, went to Aluva. When he made it right and returned, he saw that a Damodaran and a Kunjiraman, who pulled the Swami's rickshaw, had been beaten and left on the road. That is a caste color there. Seeing that, he said not to fight with them, took Damodaran and Kunjiraman in a boat, and brought them to Kollam.

From there, according to the convenience of the time, they were brought to Sivagiri and treated for three months. They started to get up and walk. By this time, there were those who had caused great sorrow to the Swami. No one will say this. No one knows that the Swami grieved and worked hard for that history. The people of Varkala know nothing. The people of Varkala just fell down; the place that was said to be where the bark fell became the place where the slippery pot fell. They became completely ignorant. To prevent the knowledge that came from Koorkanchery from staying, I wrote a letter saying that I resign from the SNDP in thought, word, and deed. In those days, no one would enter Sivagiri. Poor ascetics would only sweep and clean. If this is said today, they will not understand. Others would not enter either. It was Dharma Theerthar who suffered the entire sorrow of this. Dharma Theerthar's name was Parameswara Menon. He was a B.A. B.L. degree holder. It was he who brought this ashore. After him came Krishna Gurukkal. He was called Atmananda Swami. Along with him came Bodhanandan, Krishnanandan, and Ramanandan. The last to attain Samadhi was Sankaranandan. They were all disciples of Alathur Brahmanandan. They came after seeing the goodness of this. Alathur Brahmanandan has written extensively against idol worship in many ways. After all this, a scholar from Thalassery named Vagbhatanandan said a lot about logical matters and turned many people in the Karthikappally and Karunagappally areas into rationalists, liberationists, and unwanted debaters. In this situation, the Swami had to endure sorrow from all sides. But is this something a knowledgeable person can do? Why was this done? Why was the casuarina tree cut down and chased away from Aruvippuram, chased away from Kannampara, and left without refuge in Aluva? From there, he came straight to Sivagiri and then stayed there. It was then that they smelled it after knowing what happened in Koorkanchery. They said that this belongs to the Yogam and must be signed. Finally, the Swami had to say a word that should not have been said, 'Hey, I came after doing witchcraft and astrology. Won't you even let me sleep?' When he said this, they left. This is an act done by the Yogam office bearers of that time with Judge Kumaran.

On the third day, the Swami attained Samadhi. Three days later, Bodhananda Swami also passed away. Can a few people who do not know any of this achieve anything by just spilling ideas? Sanyasa is not something that is gathered in any way. A renunciate is one who strives for ages, implements all the dharmas of the Yuga Dharma, brings them as the authority for all ages, and completes the dharma of Kali. The Guru is the one who does not reject even a single demon or even an atom that has come so far, gives each atom its own place, sees that it stands in the constellations, sees that it has reached the dharmas of the ages, and corrects it. Such knowledge is not completed anywhere in Hindu mythology. It is just mentioned somewhere and left. When it came to the Trinity system, it had to be written down as the destruction of Kali. We can see that karma dosha, which has increased over a long period, in two ways.

A sanyasi is often misunderstood as someone who wants nothing, wanders the streets, a mere beggar, or one who lives on alms. That is not the case. The embodiment of all dharma - this is how you should see and understand a sanyasi. That is the mark of a guru. Having understood what every dharma is, they serve that dharma to the world through their own followers. But the great souls who have come so far have not been allowed to serve it in that way. Jesus was not allowed, and none of the prophetic religions could be made to obey. That is why all this has changed into so many sects today. Saying Shia and Sunni, it has turned into a terrible fight.

As far as India is concerned, it has become impossible to even recognize what Sanatana is. Whether you accept the Ramayana doctrine or the Vaishnava doctrine, this will not be achieved. Where did Vaishnavism come from? What does one do regarding a Vaishnava? Vaishnavas completely removed the dosha that came to the Shudras, and the Shudra class, all eighty-four castes, are the ones who should become the faces of knowledge. By turning them completely ignorant, bringing in arts and games, showing everything up to Kunchan Nambiar's Ottamthullal and turning it into Kathakali, does this Hindu religion have any doctrine? If you try to explain it completely, it will just stop there. This is the time God has allowed you to have some knowledge about this. If you can grasp this, that is what parents in the family should repeatedly fulfill. That should be directed towards the knowledge to be acquired from the guru. That knowledge is not in this manner, but it is telling what happened in each Chatur Yuga. It is the ignorance of a large generation raised without knowledge and love that is jumping around here. Because it happened that way, no family paying a tax of five rupees ever called Narayana Guru 'Swami' back then. They only called him 'Nanu Ashan'. Chattampi Swami could not study here because he was a Shudra. So he went to Papanasam, studied, and came back. He was a Chattampi back then. Chattampi means monitor. He has held the position of a monitor.

During Chattampi Swami's youth, there was a great householder in Kannammoola. When he was standing on the ridge of the field having farming done, Swami walked by. 'Chattampi...' he has called out like that many times. One day he said, let the lunch be at home tomorrow, okay, there will be a few more people. Okay. Swami went. There was no one with him. He asked where the others were. He said they would come. It was time to lay the leaves. Swami said to lay the leaves and serve. It was served. Swami went and sat at the head of one leaf. Then the others also came and sat. Those others were dogs. All these dogs are just sitting there waiting. Without eating. Swami started eating, and said, now you can eat too. The dogs ate. Swami ate, and suddenly got up. Then he asked the elder. 'Do you know who these dogs are? This is uncle, this is the other elder' and after introducing them like this, Swami slowly got up, and then said to the dogs. Now let's see your stubbornness, and they all started biting each other. This has been taught as a great siddhi of Swami. Actually, those who heard and knew it never even thought about why Swami did this. Its meaning is this. You who are so ignorant will do many things like this. This is a denial. Swami was showing that these things sitting here are your uncles. Holding it up as a big deal, saying he is great, if he opens his eyes it will burn, the doctrine taught like this is the root of a lot of ignorance. If we say that all this is what we serve instead of knowledge, what is it but the fault of karma? Without respecting all this, it was studied and taught and turned into a denial. People like this have come in all nations. So whoever has come so far, they are all fighting each other. They cannot do anything for this disease. As long as they do not understand this, they are nothing, and they are not going to be anything here. So if you live without understanding this karma!

When I was coming from Aluva, I had to visit Dr. Ayyappan's house at a place called Kathiyathodu. I came with the head of the Mutt. They are chatterboxes following a heinous act. This political turmoil, which has grown so vile and destructive, must be destroyed. They will never become straight.

If this service, which is supposed to bring good to man, is messed up in this way and given to these young people, it will bring destruction and keep it here. Can anyone remove this bad karma? For whom is this government, which stands 'righteous' by serving and experiencing such injustice?

That hardship in Sivagiri lasted for ten years then. After the Guru's Samadhi, no man would look back there. Dharma Theerthar went here and there, met some people, and fought the case for ten years. The case came up for hearing. It was a case filed by C.V. Kunhiraman - when it finally came up for hearing, there was no way out. There was a table there then. It is still there today. Dharma Theerthar sat and wrote for three days without eating, bringing bylaws from here and there. He pointed out the rules in each bylaw. With the sorrow of what to do, he pulled open the drawer. An envelope was lying face down. While he went up there and was talking, a guy came and asked whose car had come. He said the head of the Sivagiri Mutt had come. He said this car should be set on fire then. Because it was Narayana Guru who created the S.N.D.P. So it should be set on fire now. That was the time when the Vayalar-Punnapra struggle was going on. After that, a meeting was held there. At the meeting place, Narayana Guru's picture was taken and turned around. Sankaran was garlanded with a black chain. Sahodaran Ayyappan was the president.

The meeting dispersed. This is a scene where a gold chain should be given. Sahodaran Ayyappan said, "Don't do this, it's a pity." A few days passed. And it happened exactly like that. What is the embodiment of this ignorance? It was Swabhavu who played it. When you say Swabhavu, everyone is Swabhavu in every way. Everyone is Swabhavu. Finding that no one is in front, the subtlety of an ignorance grew and reached like this. This is today's government.

This government will do anything. But they can't do anything. Because they don't know anything. They are the seat of injustice. Dharma has never been told to them. That is a copy of what Swami wrote in Thrissur and posted to S.N.D.P. I have resigned from the S.N.D.P. Yogam in thought, word, and deed. This is that envelope. A copy of this was given to Sankaran Subbayyar's bench. The case was dismissed. Not only that, it was also ordered to pay the expenses incurred by the monks. Then they all went together, pleaded, and did not give a single penny to the monks. They just let it go like that.

From that day onwards, without allowing even a forty-kali, these subtle streams were covered. Even when I go there, it's like that. Going down through this paddy field, they are shouting rotten abuses. The people there are just shouting abuses. There was a man there who made toddy. It is his own child who is still handling this. They call it Thachankonam. That child has entered inside Sivagiri and hacked it open. Even then, his intention was to shake this off with black magic. By doing black magic, this man made toddy from the settlement. Down there, Kesavan Mesthiri - there is a set like that. When their children climb up and the toddy is ready, they will cut it off. Then he went and went, went to Madurai and died there. I know that even today, black magic is done by bringing that book. If you say that the children of Varkala are the ones who have suffered such bad karma, what can be done? If you say that young thugs, Matadevan (Madhavan) and such thugs even tried to make Swami drink toddy! Not a single child in this community knows this. How much ignorance is this? This is the climax. Or is it suppressed for the destruction of progressive action? Or is it for all of you to perish with the world in this way, or to seek the subtlety of goodness?

I am not scolding anyone. I don't need to scold anyone. But I lived there for seventeen years. I know the details of those seventeen years. What all they did. It was then that this case was conducted for the second time, and seeing that this sanyasi was not right, E.M. Kovoor wrote on his bench that these sanyasis were useless, and then gave the receiver to the head of the mutt himself, bringing it under the President's rule. With so much story happening in this, today's reply is given by turning it upside down. If you are convinced that this is doing good to you or to any human being in the world, I have nothing to say. This is exactly when man has reached the realm of knowledge, not knowing how to preserve this dharma, in what way it should be done, neither the S.N.D.P. nor the N.S.S. nor the Vishwakarma Mahasabha nor the Brahmana Mahasabha is going to do anything here. And today's politicians are not going to do anything either. This is the knowledge of a truth. I said this so that you would feel it because you came here. If you feel it, take it. If not, don't. But you must think about who you are doing this to. Bringing the forces fighting against God and making them do karma without staying in the land for ten years, starting from C. Kesavan's time, one by one, up to Sahodaran Ayyappan. What is this rationalism and liberationism? Did anything happen? If you try like this from then till today, someone has something to say. Whose path are you standing on? And then these people say, 'Santhigiri is against Sivagiri'. What can be said to people who keep such vile practices? Swallowing the feet of the Guru, the verses of the deniers. Those who burned the path of dharma without ever having it. That is, they took this box to the rock on the south side of the Sivagiri High School where a watcher named Kunjiraman lived, and burned it. You don't know all this. You are showing something. They completely corrected even Kumaran Asan. That is why Kumaran Asan could not come straight. It is after understanding this that Kumaran Asan wrote 'Gramavrikshathile Kuyil'. What verse does this lineage that destroyed in this way have? As long as you keep this vile practice for this mischief that completely highlights denial, it is destruction for you. You will not be saved under any circumstances. This book knowledge, which is hung back as upper caste and lower caste, is not just you, but the verses of karma dosha. Each of you sees and does something. If asked why, you don't know what to say. What is going to happen to you children who have suffered such a plight? You are never going to be anything. Now whatever anyone says, this is what I have to say. I am not doing anything to improve anyone or to improve Narayana Guru or to improve others. If you do not understand this known acquaintance in that 'staff' of antiquity, it is the violence you are doing to your children. These children will also be destroyed in an unwanted way. There is no change to that. It has happened in many homes. If you haven't seen it, see it. Even today, if you come with me, I will show it to you. I am saying this with that acquaintance. No dharma should be propagated or conducted here by opposing anyone with arrogance or realizing and destroying with humility, involving in karma without dharma. If you feel it, if there is any child who feels love for God, they should understand this. There must be a solution for this. This is my word now. Now what are you saying - 'synthesis of ideas!' How will that happen? How will you make it happen? What are you going to do by keeping this kind of dosha? You will never be able to do this. Oracles without dharma! 'Swami's feet must win' with the most vile practice inside, having lost dharma. How will it win? It will never win. If you recover this injustice, you will soon know what you have become. Because this Kali Yuga will not wait for you. Just as it was washed away in each place, whatever is supposed to happen due to drought, all that

happen. If it is seen that you are not observing that justice, it will completely destroy you. After all these arguments and uproars - what should we do, is it enough to pass this on to our children, is this our ultimate goal, can we do anything about this - if you have the opportunity to think about this, think. If you have the sense of duty to do it, you must do it. If not, don't, you can do as you learned. That will be the undermining of the foundation of the coming generation. This is the greatest bad karma you are committing. What can anyone do about this? Gandhiji spoke of Ramarajya here, is it possible? Moving away from that, it was brought like this. Can they do it? Thus each one was brought. Who can do this? This is not something to be won by fighting. You are nobody in the duty of giving love, respect, and humility by making them righteous. You are the oracles of negative mischief. You must go forward destroying each child by serving this dharma to them! This is what I have to say? Do not do this. This is the greatest juncture of danger. Remember.

GURUVANIKAL · December 1997 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1244 to GV2:P1323
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

Guruvani December 1997

When we say an ashram, it needs an ideology. Many theoretical religions, prophetic religions, and various types of Vedantic programs have come up in many places. None of this can be copied and taken together. This is not enough to recognize the lack of knowledge that existed in the beginning and the good and bad in it and take it forward. This is all the remainder of a relationship established without distinguishing between pitru, deva, rishi, and sannyasi, and without distinguishing between yoga level, vidya level, and dhana level. The knowledge we have today is one that can take us back to the savage class by carrying it as new knowledge. An ashram nature should be able to change this. For that, a karma is needed to unite your faith, love, respect, and humility. What is called faith? Faith means we have a faith in our mother, father, and wife. Straighten that out. When that is straightened out, everything in our land will be straightened out. But what good will come to us who have learned to live in some Kapalika traditions by looking at the smells outside in such a way that everything in the land will be straightened out, but nothing in the house is straight? How will we get over this? Through this savage nature, it has only been possible to become the owner of a humorous life by showing drumming, singing, noise, and respect by conducting divine worship in many ways. When we take and look at each of these like this, what is the item that has come into our hands? An item that cannot be improved in any way. But the easy way was brought as God. For example, the birth of Krishna. Knowledge was given to the king before birth. But scholars and contemporaries did not agree to believe that knowledge just like that. Decided to go the same way as before. What happened because of that, to that great man

It could not even live wondering what to do. Finally, it went somewhere. Right there, that life came to an end.

The Shudra born in the fourth stage of Brahmin-Kshatriya-Vaishya-Shudra grows up and reaches a state where he can impart all the knowledge necessary for even a Brahmin to attain liberation. No sanyasi has been able to impart the knowledge that this is the true Varnasrama Dharma. That is not their fault. The things these sanyasis have known till today is the ignorance that God appeared, came here, beat to death some people, and reformed some others. What does a mother teach her children? "Children, do not do that, your father will kill you." Each of us is an expression of men and women who have grown up in this manner.

We have an easy way for this here. It is given by God. That path is built in a special way. It is this: building a path where children are raised in such a way as to grant liberation to everything our ancestors worshipped. The course of karma of the people who passed through the Treta Yuga and Dvapara Yuga is being shown to you as God. What mistakes occurred in this worldly doctrine, and how those mistakes have operated - this too is being shown to you. It gives us a practice to reclaim our path, which had gone astray talking about avatars and other followers.

We are seeing all this as truth while we are still alive. This comes in the states of waking, dreaming, and deep sleep. There is both error and correctness in what comes like this. To reclaim these errors and correctness, one must get an experienced Guru. But even if you get one, for how long will it be useful to you? The experience here itself is the example. How many highly grown children were here. They could not take this up. Nor could they train another person to that level. Or even if they got something, by thinking about it in their own intellect, it will disappear. Thus, it has reached a state where nothing can be done.

This is a very sad matter. Therefore, you must discuss it as father, mother, and children, and each of you should share and cultivate this subject, which I received as a gift from God, and give it back as God's own. For that, we need unity. But as far as you are concerned, you are people who cannot even unite a single home. How can such people like you unite the land? Therefore, no matter how hard it is, you must unite your home. Through that, unite the land and the country. Otherwise, we will run along with the violent tendencies of some politicians or similar thugs and perish.

So what do we need? We should stand exactly where we started and gain knowledge. That is, the Shudra who has no right to Brahmajnana should gain the knowledge conducive to Brahmajnana. But the world is standing at a peak where it cannot recognize that goodness which God has given us through crores of years. Still, we have nothing to fear. Because we are standing in a better time than the time when Krishna was born. Krishna was born in the Dvapara Yuga, which grew out of the Treta Yuga. Krishna knew how the parts of Treta Yuga and Dvapara Yuga should change. But he could not make the change. The part where we grow up and act is the truth that grows through our successors. Although Krishna worked extremely hard to bring that truth into the Yadava clan, he could not succeed. Krishna even had to give up his own children to be killed. These are the things we need to evaluate and know about Sri Krishna. Instead, what have we been taught about Krishna? It was made so that he could not even stay in the house where he was born. Where all did Krishna run? He ran to the places of all the kings. Even after running so much, he could not survive. Hiding all these aspects of sacrifice, they have merely imagined him by singing hymns of praise to the Lord. But without karma, no matter how you imagine, it will not work out. We do not know this.

Karma means an acquaintance with the flaws of past karma, what exists presently, and what is yet to come. This should be done and provided for us by a Guru, a King, or the Government according to the merit of the situation. Instead, how is our government functioning?

How are we? What can we do in this world in this condition? Getting an answer to this is the idea. The idea develops along with the power of faith.

Guru Jayanthi Edition 1997

The mistake made in the evaluation of India To establish the value of India as we know it, we must state what the function of Manusmriti is. We have lost the capacity for action that is often called Sanatana: we do not know where and how it was lost. But we know today. The fact is that originality was lost due to trying to maintain a cultural way of life by adding various stories. Not many people know that. It shouldn't be blamed on anyone. But today we see thousands of verses of the Upanishads. When other Upanishads encroached on the Karma that entered the intellect from the path of philosophy, the scholarly withdrawal and translation was formed by adding the meaning that counts from 'Om'. Then the righteous indignation that has reached even the cinema, which has imparted the humor of Hindu verses, is a return to the path that came again. This has been developed, the rhythm of Advaita has been completed, and the singing about the four Yoga theories done by the monks who have attained the power of mantra, mantra, and sleight of hand has been completed. If the new eras are made into new stones and used in that knowledge, how can it be included in this theory of evolution? As long as the knowledge called scientific theory violates the duty of the age to the true theory called the philosophy of the four ages, which is called the time of birth, death, and disease, it is never possible to know what the need of Kali is. The duty of this Kali is the translation capacity of the matter when it reaches the cause, both macro and micro, not only of today's humanity but also of living beings. The time determination of each age and Manu in biological evolution has come that way. It is about the period of seventy-one four ages that is noted in a Manvantara. When this goes scientifically, there will be a misfortune of not being able to quickly control the atomic radiation that occurs in the crucible of experimentation and observation. In this death race of governance where every country runs, if the truth called Dharma burns and leaps through the ages, hides inside the framework, attains the salvation of life that touches the heart of the love called God, and writes with science, will this salvation of life come? Think.

The part that discovers the secret of evolution is the source of the Guru-disciple relationship. It is fulfilled by four times. Four times - Satya Yuga, Treta Yuga, Dwapara Yuga, Kali Yuga. When repeating in that way, the time of a solar system will have to be determined. The philosophy in that way is the true philosophy. A Manvantara with one solar system and a deluge with fourteen such Manvantaras have been mentioned. This calculation can only be told by the groups of sages above the Trinity. The Sanskritization of the scholars who wrote in detail to tell this calculation stands attaining blessing and destruction in the uproar of the lineage of gods. Due to the aforementioned mistake, the duty of Manu could not be known and to return to the lineage of Manu

What we are giving to the light of the world today is an action that corrects the mistakes we have realized today, which could not be done earlier. Accordingly, for each era, there should be rulers who perform world welfare through the knowledge of this lineage of Gurus. That is why we have started the Gurukula Brahmaniveditha Karma today. The reason it is called Gurukula Brahmaniveditha is that the future generations of men and women belonging to this should be able to determine and state what the dharma of the time is. The beginning of such a generation is what we mark today with the final philosophy, which we call Darshana. Otherwise, it will not happen if we just do it. It is an open secret known to all of us that Rabindranath Tagore, in the guise of a sage, sat under a tree and at the base of a banyan tree, studying and seeking the secret of scholars. What we are studying today is a scholarly secret like this, which has crossed many such eras. Even so, where can we reach? As a recreation of this, we are giving to the public a traditional theory about the period when six Manvantaras and three Chaturyugas were completed, by studying what the journey of the first Manvantara was. We must see and know what the body made of the five elements, which is right and wrong, is. We know that this reaches from the termite to the winged termite, and they are all seeking light. The Manvantara Nivruthi, divided into four periods, is the guidance of each period that reaches up to the human race. Thus, after the completion of six Manvantaras and three Chaturyugas, it could not be completed due to the mistake of a Kalantara Guru who came in the lineage of Manu.

What we have started today is to correct the changes that occurred in each Yuga of each Chaturyuga. To bring about the calculation of time according to the determination of Brahma, the evolutionary stages mentioned earlier must be crossed. There are records somewhere stating that three hundred and sixty human years make one year of the Devas. We don't need to bother searching for that. Today, in the path of vision we have received, Gurus must be born who can tell at least the secret of one Chaturyuga. It is this Guru who can be decisively called the Kalantara Guru.

This calculation we have received today, which was copied by a Brahmin without divine atomism and experience, following the class hatred called Suryavansham and Chandravansham, has been studied, spoken, and taught by this ignorant Shudra class. The untimely theories combined as Chandravansham and Suryavansham are the death trap that attained the good and bad deeds of birth and death called death in this lineage formed by human Gurukulas speaking, teaching, and playing. This is part of the shorthand called Panditha Pamara Moodha Rahasyanjali. What do we need? We should be able to bring the Namaskara duties and Yama accounts practiced in the four periods to at least one Manvantara or one Chaturyuga. The evolutionary theory in that direction is Darshana. Through that evolutionary theory, where it went wrong and where it did not go wrong must be informed by the four periods that come under the Kalantara Guru. The evolution that occurred in Satyayuga, Tretayuga, and Dwaparayuga reaches from the ant egg called Andakam, which counts and knows the secret specialities of life, to the lizard egg. Darshana is the secret of the evolution of life that grows in that direction and lives in water and on land, indicating many unbroken unities. The owner of such a secret is the Kalantara Guru. That secret is one of the ways that came to complete the Yugadharma in the Guru-disciple relationship, which is entitled to God-realization. That is the secret knowledge of the secret called Manu, of the sage Manu, or the sage called Kalantara Guru. The Guru who has reached the light of the times of the lineage of Manu that has been made public in total!

To write and complete the dharma prescribed by fate for each period, five Gurus will come in each Yuga according to the Yugadharma. In such a secret combination, the visionaries who do not keep the dignity of the mind are the ones who have refined and written the lessons in scholarly logic in such a way that the measure of the times cannot be determined. In that way, people who do not know what the Namaskara lesson is have come up to Sandhyavandanam through the creation of Chaturvarnya. Gross or subtle, cause or effect

What we see today is righteous indignation unknowingly caught in the flaws of karma. Consequently, we do not know what constitutes a venerable action. The secret called Darshanamala explains this to us in a new way. The path of action dedicated to Brahman is the right path to understand the philosophical vision of each respective era. When the path of dharma along that way reaches the path of doctrine, it is the special vision that signifies the arrival of the cyclical repetition of the four ages.

Gurujayanthi Edition 1997

How Did Kali Nirnayam Become Impossible?

Long before Hinduism emerged, this earth underwent the transition of twenty-five chaturyugas. One might ask if chaturyugas did not exist before these twenty-five chaturyugas. They did. That is the chaturyuga system seen in Manu. It is ordained by destiny.

An unknown mistake has caused long-lasting disasters. That disaster grew and destroyed the repetitive capacity of what is known as Indian, Hindu, and Sanatana. These disasters did not operate only in the first three chaturyugas of this Manvantara (Vaivasvata Manvantara). To rectify that mistake, a mission took shape again in the fourth chaturyuga. That is, its operation started from the seventh chaturyuga. The Trinity system was completed in the eleventh chaturyuga of this Manvantara.

Manu's lineage had no faults. Attempts were made in various guises until today to rectify that unknown mistake. They did not succeed. Not only that, it also paved the way for many imaginative speculations. When imaginative speculation entered, the karmic capacity of time determination was lost. The Trinity system was carefully handled through the tenth chaturyuga of this Manvantara— through yoga, law, and the six foundational calculations. Yet, that lost karmic capacity could not be regained.

Of the nine mandalas mentioned, seven have forms. The remaining two exist in such a way that their forms cannot be centralized. It is a repetitive phenomenon caught in Rahu, Ketu, and Saturn. When the seven skies representing the seven mandalas were mentioned and their respective imaginary karma came into being, the Satya Yuga continued truly as truth. The natural prowess that shared and ruled the remaining three yugas traveled through changing times. Thus came the dharma propagation of Kali's destruction. That propaganda argument continues even today. Everyone knows that. The number of stories created so far using the refined language (Sanskrit) cannot be counted. A dangerous trap that occurred in many places, making karmic capacity incompatible with dharma, paved the way for the destruction of time itself. As a result, Kali became a dharma phenomenon declared to cause destruction.

We cannot move forward by denying what exists. The karmic capacity we have absorbed so far is the flaw of the twenty-five chaturyugas that have operated up to now. It became so entangled that no one could reach anywhere. Therefore, both the educated and the uneducated drowned alike, and time determination was lost. Crores of great souls who came to operate for some short period drowned in the uncertainty of this time. When Hinduism was completed in this way, Sanatana was lost. As a result of completing it without knowing where to start, among the writings praising the six-faced one, when asked the meaning of Omkara, due to ignorance, the foolishness of plucking off the face of knowledge occurred. Thus, not only was knowledge itself destroyed, but the refined cultures were also completed in a scholarly manner from Amarakosha to Sreeramodantham. The very beginning of Amarakosha is like this. So, it is about a natural state of transition recorded over ten chaturyugas.

This is what could be recorded for this Chaturyuga. This change in state also occurred at the beginning of this Kali. Because of that, the delusion called Kali-destruction caught on. Therefore, the descriptive words in the form of ornaments, which had been repeated and fulfilled until today, completed the deity. That duty of time passed away without saying anything in Kali. In a path that came across like this, Kali reached a place that is not dharma; Kali became destruction.

We need to know the dharma of Kali especially. But the change in state value, which lost all its karma capacity due to the adjective of Kali destruction for all time and danced in the arena of no-dharma, is what destroyed Kali dharma. Consequently, the Kaliyuga, which had been functioning well, also ceased to exist. For example, when the karma capacity from Kali to Treta Yuga worked without Satya Yuga, it drowned in the procession of Puranas and Epics that praised only the deity or only Parashakti. The star constellation is about the movements of the great souls who came to protect dharma in such times. Because they could not understand the meaning of this, our ancestors even tried to complete it by calling it 'star system'. They have written some things, saying that they asked God directly, in such a way that it is impossible to know whether it exists or not. A special story has also been added in the Puranas saying that I will not let your devotees go to see you.

Completing the many Deva-Asura traditions, the story of giving Ravana the intelligence of ten heads has also been attempted to be completed. Isn't this itself a very big failure? Another thing, when the word 'avatar' had to be used for the creators of the twenty-seven stars, twelve zodiac signs, and nine planetary positions, they had to move in a scholarly manner. Thus, the dharma called true vision stopped. Moreover, many crores of star systems still remain as remnants. If it is said that this is our world karma movement, who will understand it and how? Where will the dharma propagation work, which had been functioning according to the Yuga dharma, reach? In short, this is the dharma error in stating Kali destruction.

Because the time determination in the creative work of each great person in later times was not fixed, there were ups and downs in the Yuga junctions. This has greatly affected Indian culture. Because it was in India that the dharma of the Yugas was followed. This is actually the true religion called Sanatana. This is Indian culture; and the heritage of India. This fixed dharma should come through man. But humanity stands within the limits of the parts of Trimurti, Devendra, and Parashakti.

How did the statement of Kali destruction come about? The misfortune of having to record with twenty-seven of the star constellations that led to the Yugas that poured the rain of destruction since the time of Sri Krishna, and the misfortune of having to create the story Purana in an easy way by writing the story of Hari as a story and a play--such unseemly joined shortcuts are the reason why the Yuga dharma called Kali disappeared. Bringing these abbreviations through the Puranas, Shastras, and Vedas that should be in Kali, and since it should be useful as a vision realization, bringing suitable Vedanta Suktas for it, playing in the timeless arena, and proclaiming time in such a way that the movements of life cannot be recognized, is the change in state determined as Kali. Because of this, it is truly not available from the hands of the wise. All we have today is a procession of Puranas and Epics written and created from time to time. In the special story formed in this way, the Kali special became without time determination.

Birthday Edition 1997

Gurukulabrahmaniveditham

There was a time when the world had no realization of the Brahman's resolve or the glory of God. This does not mean that this knowledge is complete even now. Except for the opportunity to do only the things that have been told by Brahman from time to time, the world could not acquire that knowledge. The Brahman's resolve was fully realized only for seven Manvantaras and three Chaturyugas. Sathyathranan was the great man who came at the end of the third Chaturyuga. Although he knew that he was the heir to the Brahman's resolve, the secret of his own life was not fully revealed to him until the end of his life.

I am saying here what I have been told in my experience. I do not know if there is anything beyond this.

A Manvantara is the concept of the Saptarishis. A solar system is created by that concept. The solar system has nine planets that have form and are favorable to the theory of evolution. It has not been determined what the greatness of Rahu and Ketu is in it. These planets are divided into twelve zodiac signs and give twelve results. It is also not certain which results Rahu and Ketu should give. But everything has its own prescribed duties. There is an incompleteness in both of these.

The Saptarishis gave the concept for the creation of a solar system based on nine planets, twelve zodiac signs, and twenty-seven stars. Some people call it seven mandalas scientifically and based on some other experiences. These seven mandalas have form and nature. But Rahu and Ketu sometimes deviate from their positions while revolving like this. Therefore, we should not desire its complete results. Because no one can determine how the time difference will be when revolving in the karma of a Jupiter cycle. That is, their movement is not fixed. They travel in an unpredictable order.

There is a measure of Brahman's light for the movement of the solar system. Its effect may be felt in different places. But it will not leave this mandala. If there is a difference of one second in this movement, it will result in an error of up to a yama. It cannot be determined accurately. But in our calculation, there are seconds, minutes, nazhikas, vinazhikas, and even the most delicate thodi.

Whatever calculations there may be, the greatness that came in the concept of touches should not be wrong at all. That is the way of truth. That is human life.

Manvantara, Chaturyugas, and Yamas have a junction. Sandhyavandanam is performed as an act of resolving the Yamasandhi. There may be those who are awake for three yamas, and if added, those who are awake for five yamas. But we have been told the time of worship in all eight Yamasandhis.

There is a calculation of five gurus for an era. These gurus are the ones who bring the calculation of Sandhyavandanam and the recognition of times according to the Brahmanishchaya. But we cannot say how many great men come at the junction of the yugas. In four yugas, we are described the Brahmanishchaya. If there is a mistake in the Yugadharma at any time, that mistake cannot be taken back later. It requires a lot of effort. The lives and words of great men prove this.

In the knowledge of the Chaturvakya spoken by Sathyathranan, scholars and ordinary people fell into the trap so that they could not recognize the times. This is the truth that can be seen if we look at the last twenty-five Chaturyugas. Our subject is what we got to know about where the mistake occurred in this truth.

As evidence that this mistake occurred, there are lives that cross thousands of hurdles in front of us. The life of man, Pitru, Deva, Rishi, and Sanyasi! In these lives, seeing what the life of a Muni is, what the life of a Rishi is, and coming down like that, what the life of a Deva is, what the life of a Rishi under a Deva is, the path guided by the Rishi standing above the Deva is the truth that exists through the words of the Rishi. There was a time when nothing was known about this truth. A karmic skill that was obtained by stating where this truth exists above the Trimurti system was completed in Varkala. That occasion was a time of physical and mental torture. It is impossible to count or finish telling what kind of tortures. We are indebted to that Brahmanishchaya for making that Brahmanishchaya a person to take this back. The lives of great souls have become unable to decide what is right and wrong of the Manu lineage and how to resolve it.

Six Manvantaras have passed. The calculation is that each Manvantara has seventy-one Chaturyugas. A Chaturyuga has four yugas. The calculation is that there are five gurus for the resolution of each yuga. The smallest of the yugas is Kaliyuga. This itself is four lakh thirty-two thousand years. Double this is Dwapara. Dwapara and Kali together make Treta Yuga. Treta Yuga and Kali together make Krita Yuga. The period from the birth of Sri Rama to the birth of Sri Krishna comes after many lakhs of years, with five gurus for each yuga. Sri Krishna was born as a great man who fully knew the Kalidharma before the beginning of Kali. Manu has evaluated that Kali, the fourth quarter, belongs to the Shudra class. That is, Kali is the time when the full right of Brahmajnana is decreed to the Shudra, fully understanding all the subjects to be ruled and made to rule in the fourth quarter. As an example of this, Krishna was born in the Yadava clan. The original mistake occurred twenty-five Chaturyugas ago. When the sage named Sathyathranan came thinking to respect the truth, when he took and saw the decisive truth that had come as Chaturyugas and Manvantaras about what he was and what his predecessors were, the word Aham Brahmasmi was born from a great fall that had occurred, as indicated earlier. Later, with the Chaturvakyas...

When completed, it was not the perfection of the idea, but the perfection of the letters that was done. That is the mistake that has been continuing for twenty-five Chaturyugas. Gurukulabrahmaniveditham is a karma that corrects this mistake and brings about the perfection of the idea. This ceremony was previously conducted in a different manner.

The Shudra class are those without knowledge, so it was easy to deceive them. Each person in the Shudra class is a child of that mistake. Previously, there was a karma to start learning letters at the age of five. This karma was conducted equally for both boys and girls. This had an advisory nature. It will be done before the age of five. There is no such ceremony among the Brahmin community. They only start learning letters, but there is no ceremony. Kettukalyanam is the ceremony kept for this among the Shudra class. Similarly, ceremonies like Thirandukalyanam, then Kalyanam, and Pulikudi Kalyanam. When a girl is born and all these ceremonies are conducted, that house will surely become poor. Then, if four girls are born, there is nothing to say.

The ceremony shown among the Brahmins is the Guru giving advice and starting the letters. In the marriage ceremony, they show a karma ordained by the birth of Vyasa. By Sankalpakarma, Vyasa is named Krishnadwaipayana. Vyasa told us as if coming from Krishna's mouth. It is not certain in which Yugas Vyasa has come. It is widely agreed that Vyasa is the son of a fisherwoman. The mythological histories that poured and told the four Vedas and six Shastras are the truth ordained and written for Hindus. The ceremony shown by Brahmins is nine and eleven days. This ceremony is to sit the boys and girls and read the brief matters in the mythological epics and explain them physically and spiritually. The summary of the six Shastras and four Vedas will be read in six and four ten days. Marriage on the eleventh day. They show a ceremony in that marriage too. Now, even if it is not in everyone's house, it is in the houses of the chief Tantris. In others' houses, the father, mother, and the most needed ones sit, fill a large vessel with water, catch a fish, and make this girl and boy scoop it up while chanting Vedic mantras. That indicates the birth of Vyasa.

But the ignorance they made the Shudra class do is Kettukalyanam, Pulikudi, etc. That is to prevent the Shudra from getting Brahmajnana. Relatives and friends will be called to inform that a girl has come of age. An Ada will be made with Navadhanyas. Everyone together will put a mat on this Ada and make this grown-up girl sit on it, and everyone will stand around and shower flowers on this child's back. This Ada will be given to the Nair of the Illam. The other Adas will be cut and given to all relatives and friends. The name of this Ada is Poriyada. He who eats this Poriyada will not have Brahmavidya later.

Gurukulabrahmaniveditham is the karma we started to change this mistaken karma. The knowledge gained in the karma advised by the Guru is Brahmavidya. For this, this child should be kept as a Brahmacharini. In such a Brahmacharini, children worthy of Brahmajnana will be born after marriage. These children are called Salsanthanam. Such a ceremony was not in practice among us before. Therefore, God showed us this ceremony for us. This also means that the parents and the locals get the knowledge that the girl is old enough to acquire knowledge. If this advisory path is kept, one can at least reach the state of Saraswati, Parvati, Lakshmi. Knowing at least this much, we should raise girls and boys in a way that helps the good of the world. As Brahmachari and Brahmacharini, as a householder or Brahmacharya, Kaumara, Vanaprastha,

They can also go in the way of Sannyasa. This is the knowledge we have received. This tradition especially, everyone else should know. The position these children attain is also received by their parents. The purity of the Gurukula Brahma Nivedita tradition, when that soul takes birth again, is to make this tradition belong to the whole world; this is the purity of intention of this Karma. It is fulfilled by the sacrifice of millions of years of all the prophetic traditions, not just of Deva and Devi, as seen in the vision they receive. This is why it is said that at least the position of Saraswati, Parvati, and Lakshmi should be attained through vision. Even without doing any of these sacrifices, children with vision here are getting this state. This Karma is for that to come to our children through action. But not all children may get this blessedness. For the lineage of such children who do not have the right of the soul, this Karma is helpful to obtain this right through action. The Brahmacharis, Brahmacharinis, and householders here are proof of this.

What have the authors of the Puranas in the Vedic religious tradition done? The Puranas and epics are composed in such a way that they do not enable children to distinguish between good and evil and to become heirs to the Guru's Karma. Through the Guru's effort, while the Guru is present, we all get this blessedness. But what when the Guru is not there? Even while the Guru is alive, through the disciples or visionaries, all kinds of action skills of the Guru-disciple relationship are received by the disciples by the Guru's grace. This action skill is merged and sustained in nature. We must think whether the right to such a great ceremony is needed or not when the Guru is present. All this must be made a right in Karma, included in Dharma, and given to the world through the Guru lineage. In addition, the goal of this Karma called Gurukula Brahma Niveditam is to correct some of the flaws done according to custom by including them in the Vedic religion and to complete them in the Dharma of realization.

What we are accomplishing through this Karma is the completion of the Karma of Chaturvarnya to serve the perfect knowledge of Satya Yuga. For this, parents and guardians, regardless of male or female, make this Guru lineage the basis for the creation of the world caused by Brahma's determination.

Guruvani

============================================================

GURUVANIKAL · January 1998 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1324 to GV2:P1338
English of the whole month, in the translator's own paragraphs.

January 1998

Do not taint your faith. Never do it for the sake of love from another person. We must value the love for our life and the love for our lineage. Until now

If you haven't done it for that, you should at least start doing it from now on. When you do so, many sorrows will come. Let the good come. We shouldn't pay attention to that. If good comes, let it be good.

We have never looked up to a position of knowledge until today. Or if someone has spoken a word of knowledge, we have only jumped on it and destroyed it. Having caused destruction of lands and homes to the extent that neither man nor woman can grow on earth, we have changed into the position of a kind of ignorant people who are nothing and are conducting a test of strength. What is the benefit to us from this test of strength? When this test of strength reaches our children, what will it become? We don't have to go anywhere looking for fighting, murder, and robbery. It will be right in our homes. A tragic situation will arise where a good girl cannot be raised to become a good mother tomorrow. Instead of organizing and growing the love, affection, and respect from the mother and the knowledge of the father together, and spreading the light of that knowledge to the world, the father and mother are fighting each other.

You must decide what is needed for this. All the love and all the compassion to make such a decision and be brave are here. But you are not able to do it. The men and women growing up in your home are the ones who should impart a goodness of this world. You, the father and mother who are householders, will have to think, worry, grieve, and change according to this action. Otherwise, no matter how many sages or prophets come, nothing can be done here. Our history until today is proof of that. How many thousands of gurus and emperors came after Sree Krishna. What could be done here? Haven't we only been able to reach an action of fighting and winning? To change this, instead of leaning backward, we should see what happened in the past and move forward with its solution. Men and women, big and small, should be able to come forward in this way.

If each woman and each man does not have this care, you will not be able to escape. It is not to give the good and bad of the course of action done by the rulers like the imperial position which were our past miseries, but rather what should be the existence of goodness without evil coming into our hands, what should parents be, what should we be - parents are the ones who should make children capable of bringing them back to this thought. Similarly, one side is to remove the deity, the ancestors, and the sage from in front of you and bring your action forward there. This is the action of this ashram. But you do not understand it to its extent.

If an ancestor has many flaws, some of those things will be seen in you too. While this remains true, if you associate and act according to your tendencies, will you get any benefit or knowledge? Will you be able to get rid of those flaws? Will you be able to implement anything good in your home? Then what is the benefit? Father, mother, children, husband, and wife will all become a burden. This is a course of action where we ourselves are destroyed.

Regarding sages, not all of them can live as wise men. At the same time, if they grow according to their knowledge, we do not know how to utilize them. This is the truth of it. If we only sit to find fault with them, how will they correct their mistakes? We must correct our own mistakes. Sages must also correct their mistakes. This is its side. Otherwise, this side of the mistake will not end. If anyone says by Vedantic imagination that it will end like that, it is not a side that will happen.

Regarding India's dharma, a degraded dharma exists today. In this dharma, people have lost a way to survive. Therefore, even though many great men sent by God Himself came here, no one was able to fulfill God's will. How many of our scholars are qualified to ask how this happened? They have only been able to show scholarly pride by writing and teaching fragments of Vedanta. What exactly is the karma called Vivekachudamani by Shankaracharya? Scholars have elaborated on it and pushed it into a karma that has become oneself. That is, the karma called Vivekachudamani is considered to be confined to oneself. How can a karma that is confined to oneself be convinced to oneself? That means even scholars do not have this knowledge. Or if anyone has that knowledge, these scholars praise him as a great man and a great siddha. Moreover, this mere thousand-year history has been prolonged by calling it Adi Shankara. Now, if asked what is the reason for saying Adi Shankara, they close it by giving various examples. How will we achieve anything through the Adi Shankara in these examples?

Madhvacharya came to fill this gap. He also concluded that it can be patched up with Dvaita. What do you see Dvaita as? As two. Only by evaluating crores of systems can one understand what this Dvaita is. When portrayed following the footsteps of Advaita philosophy, it will end up in the fulfillment of Aham Brahmasmi, or the four Mahavakyas. Then, by formulating fragments of Vedanta, they will make it something that belongs to no one.

Who are these Siddhantins (theorists)? They are a set of people who came handling Siddhi (mystic powers). Along the way the Siddhanta originated, they evaluated the medical theories of Agastya and Bhogar, and made them tools to preserve the body. Thus, they discovered that one can live longer. But this is the practice of Yoga. We also have the knowledge written that one without Yoga is a corpse. And we never understood what Yoga is.

Setting aside the four Yoga rules of Hatha Yoga, Hamsa Yoga, Chandrika Yoga, and Raja Yoga, this has been included in Karma Yoga. Sri Krishna has entrusted us with Karma Yoga. What is Karma Yoga? What happened in the waking state (Jagrat), what happened in the dream state (Swapna), what happened in deep sleep (Sushupti), what happened in the fourth state (Turiya)? This must be known through experience. Then it becomes Turiya consciousness. All great men have said that all this should be practiced near a Guru. This is a truth in our experience here.

What becomes clear in this experience? In some Yuga dharmas, the Guru and oneself existed - one sees what all was done at that time. Both men and women see it equally. But even after seeing all this, you have an inability to stand firm. That shortcoming of ours is not our fault. The position of the knowledge passed down to us by our heritage is only that much. What do you see this great glory, imparted by God to be imbibed, as?

Today, there are men and women among you who fully see the Trimurti system. Coming through the Trimurti system, on which paths the Guru stands there - that is the difference in states. Men and women are seeing this. If this difference in states is not carefully observed and understood, you will not be able to grasp the word received from the Guru's mouth.

What is the transformation of state? That is, you are seeing it starting from a blade of grass. Starting from this, you yourself are seeing an interstellar nature that you pass through. There are seven stages in it. You are crossing all these seven stages. We must remember that the previously mentioned theorists cannot even explain two of these seven stages. Except for some people mentioning certain calculations, even great men do not say much about this. When the sixth step is crossed, it means the position of Devendra is crossed. Once the position of Devendra is crossed, the form is lost. Or one reaches the position of Parashakti. A woman must cross this position. There must be at least one mother in this lineage who has thus crossed the position of Parashakti. That is, the fourth state must be completed. The fourth state is completed when the six steps (bhumikas) are completed. Theorists and philosophers have written and completed this in many ways. But each of you must think about what the experience of our lineage is. You must also think about what kind of knowledgeable people you are to have this experience. If both men and women are to get this philosophical knowledge, what state must the Guru you get have attained?
