Guruvani February 1998

The status of Trimurti, Devendra, and Parashakti - many calculations have been brought forward for all these. Then all this has been combined and created. What we see here in the vision is not that calculation. What we see is purely a knowledge. Some of you who have visions see that some pass through all these different states. Some may not be seen. Whether seen or not, one need not consider it as more or less. Passing without seeing is also good in a sense. Because when the discriminatory knowledge of all this comes, some will have all their doubts cleared. For some, an ignorance will remain such that doubts will never be cleared. That can be said. There is an easy way for you. That is, it will be seen as many previous births. That is how male and female are seen. That is a perfect proof.

This knowledge called Gudha, which was told after Sri Krishna came, is coming into your experience. When a thousand people come here, at least two are coming that way. Those who come that way must complete the fourth state mentioned earlier. There are those who complete the Trimurti state itself. Many Mahatmas have crossed the Trimurti state. But Devendra is not ready to let them pass upwards from here. For that, another justification has been made up by telling a story. He is a person who does all this like this.

The five elements! What have we been made to understand about the five elements? This story is an answer to the question of what the dharma of the five elements is. What are the five elements? It comes to the five senses and the completion of the combination of the five senses is in the five koshas. How the five koshas are combined is written and brought through Vedanta. You will never be able to cross this. Because Vedanta will not come there. How has this been achieved? By bringing in a cultured language, giving interpretations, the commentator consciously giving interpretations even to the letters, books like Panchadashi, Vivekachudamani, and Siddhantakaumudi have been created. In Siddhanta Kaumudi, the five elements, the five senses, and the five koshas have been determined, life and soul have been determined, and thus eighteen philosophical initiations have been stated. But it is not the experience and the different states that have been determined. That is, without bringing a visionary attitude to these philosophical initiations, the scholars have traveled their own way. India has a course of action that was taken up and implemented by the kings. Those who implemented it like that were also called Rajarshis. These Rajarshis did this philosophical initiation according to the Yoga theory. Saying that one without yoga is a lump, by making them obey the Yoga theory, these philosophical initiations have been achieved in action.

The knowledge of Panchasiddhi, which is added to the five senses, becomes koshas and we come out of the Annamaya kosha. Annamaya kosha, Pranamaya kosha, Manomaya kosha, Vijnanamaya kosha - reaching this far, it has been scattered. When it reached the Vijnanamaya kosha, then came the misfortune of not being able to retrieve what the other koshas are like. But the method of clearly determining these other koshas is here (in Santhigiri). The completion of the Kosha theory is according to the growth of life. This is where the distinction between life and soul becomes relevant.

Until these four states are completed, the soul must cross six steps to complete these four states. All the Mahatmas who have come so far have said that the four states must be completed. No one knows what to do for that. Whatever has been accepted as an original concept of the ashram is definitely not Siddhantakaumudi.

The story of Angulimala has also appeared in the history of Sri Shankara. The same attitude is seen in the Kali devotion in the history of Sri Ramakrishna. Sri Ramakrishna is seeing this Angulimala. In the place of this Angulimala, Kali has completed it, in the sense that everything

Seen as a goddess. Bhadrakali, Bhadram Kalanavaka! The knowledge worshipped here is being completed through this devotion to the goddess when talking about Sri Ramakrishna. Thus, trying to complete it by telling the story of Angulimala. But how will this be right? It cannot be completed like that.

The Vaishnava tradition has been brought in another way. Kaliyamardana is shown to have been done by Sri Krishna. By drawing Ananthashayanam, everything is depicted as merging into Mahavishnu in that idol. But how far will this depiction go? Without a Guru, one cannot reach there.

Such a vision is obtained here (at Shantigiri) by both men and women. But not everyone will get this completely. But when ten thousand people come, there will be four people who see this completely. Because these four people will pass through each small state. When ten thousand people come like that, there will be four people in it - as men and women. The meaning of this cannot be explained and finished now.

How can I clearly explain to you how the vision that came through the five elements, the knowledge understood through the five senses, is situated in the five sheaths, and how it has been transferred to the soul? But it can be transferred to the soul. That is the Guru! The Guru who is the master of all arts. The one who transfers these sixty-four arts to the soul is the truth.

What have we turned these arts into? Ottanthullal, Thiruvathira, Chakyarkoothu, and many such performances, and the Koothambalams for them, are depicted as a part of the temples like an Oottupura. How will we overcome this? The masters of arts have danced, played, bitten, and destroyed it to such an extent that we can never move forward from this, even bringing it to the level of theory. Thousands of men and women have followed this. They have traveled along the path of this god and stopped there, playing, jumping, and dancing. Those who have a vision here are seeing this. They are shown how they have reached there without falling into a state. This is one state.

This is the second state given to the Guru. This is what was indicated earlier about the ignorance that is not fully understood. The reason for not understanding can be explained separately. Because one cannot understand and act, one cannot acquire the qualification. If you cannot understand this through this life of a man and a woman, you will not be able to pass even the fourth state. You must pass at least the state of what is being shown. You don't have to be a sanyasi to pass like that.

Sri Krishna is said to be the master of all arts. A great man who could master all arts with just a flute. Meera (Bhakta Meera) saw this in a vision and took refuge in it. But how is the story of Krishna brought into Gita Govindam? We could not know what the author of Gita Govindam imagined. Therefore, we could not grow scientifically. We did not know the science that the sound of a sound that has passed through all sixty-four arts can be heard through this flute. If you cannot know the eternity of those Gopis, you will not be able to take the experience of truth you get from here.

You must understand that it is Krishna himself who has brought us to this state.

It is that great soul who removed a Brahmarakshas in the position of Devendra, who stood obstructing its journey of completion, from that position. This Guru Parampara is a lineage that has been able to see what the Brahmarakshas and many other indwelling spirits have made of these great souls and the world. This is not a story that can always be told. Only by bringing you to the level of experience can each thing be explained. By coming into the Guru Parampara to complete the karma, God himself has seen this great thing, rejoiced, reached it, and you are getting an ordained karma to choose as your right.

Guruvani

March 1998

Today, Indian culture is absent even in matters of food. It is absent in dress and language. Neither we nor our parents know why this Western style is adopted. In Brahmin families now, no one calls out 'Appa'. It is 'Daddy'. Because of this, all the harm falls on the heads of the children. In that respect, the Brahmin and the Chandala here are alike.

What will we do with our children who have reached this point? In which direction will we correct them? When young children who grow up seeing this enter family life, no culture of the family will be useful to them. If even the Brahmin here has an imposed culture in food, dress, language, and everything else, where will the Shudra, who does not know the culture of reciting the Vedas or the principles, fall? The Shudra is one who has not even peeked into a single Shastra. It is said that Shastras should not be touched.

Then what will this Shudra do here, other than sing as he learned, build a Koothambalam, and play? What is the reason for having large temples in South India? The reason is the Koothambalam. Koothambalam means play; that is, Kathakali. There are no such large temples in North India.

In the Dwapara Yuga, the Varna Dharmas were not properly followed. In order to maintain Varna Dharma, the Shudra was given the position of a Kshatriya. This is where the mistake lies. This was bound to happen. This is what is meant by not knowing the Dharmas of the time.

Kamsa's birth was in a Shudra's house. From this position, is it possible to play as a Kshatriya? We have never seen this, until today. There is no need for a Shudra to play as a Kshatriya. In reality, it was that great power - the will of Brahma - that brought power to the Shudras. That is why the disembodied voice told him not to look up, but to look down. But Kamsa did not understand that. He could only take what the visible God and Goddess said. To the Yadava's knowledge, there is no knowledge about this disembodied voice. By giving knowledge among the Yadavas, he himself is explaining what the will of Brahma is - you must do exactly this, otherwise, someone will be placed in your house to kill you.

This knowledge was given even before Sri Krishna was born. Why was this knowledge given among the Shudras like this? But Kamsa handled it in the way the Trinity appeared and told him. That is, God has no form. He is not familiar with the fact that God is formless. He also does not know that there is a luminous entity called God everywhere. He killed seven of the eight children born to his own sister. The truth is that he did not get his hands on the eighth one. Because he did not get it, he sent many Putanas into the world. Still, he did not get Krishna. Thus Krishna escaped. And then? Neither this Shudra class, nor the Kshatriya class here, nor the Brahmin class had any information about Sri Krishna. Therefore, Krishna and the Pandavas were chased all over the country. Krishna ran from here and reached another shore. After creating Dwaraka from there, Krishna could not pass through that state. When a person is born, he must cross the zodiac and mandalas in which he was born. Krishna was born with that precaution.

What is the color of the sky? The sky has no blue. Then how do we see blue when we look? Sri Krishna was given a form in that color. The house where Krishna was born does not know the meaning of this color of Krishna. The clan, the Kshatriya, the Vaishya, or the Shudra do not know. This

If our knowledge about five thousand years of history is like this, how will we know the history before that?

This knowledge is being given to us in a special way. Whether it relates to a Brahmin or a Kshatriya, this knowledge is a special rule. This is a knowledge that has not existed until today. How will we understand this? For example: there are families who perform Guru Pooja and families who believe without performing Guru Pooja. After all of them came here, whatever was in those they worshipped - Shaktheyam, Shaivam, Vaishnavam, all three - they are seeing it. Even so, none of them are able to grasp the full extent of this idea. None of you should think that you are highly intelligent and capable or less so. It can be both. You are not doing all these activities for this. You should not try to acquire this knowledge, which gives you the opportunity to implement God's will through your life over a long period, through just any path. None of you came here as great devotees. But it's not that you have no devotion either. There is some kind of love. That love is devotion.

If we count them one by one - father, mother, wife, wealth, education - we have love for everything. But the fulfillment of all these loves combined is God or Guru. So, surrendering all our ignorance and knowledge there, achieve whatever can be achieved with our life. Otherwise, never think of behaving in a mere fourth-rate manner. That thought will enslave us. Absolutely, if this knowledge is needed for the land and the country through our children, let God give it to the world itself, let us do what we can - this is the understanding you need. You shouldn't desire anything else. Let God give everything that is to be given.

Many of you have been doing many things here for a long time. My understanding is that your effort has yielded very good results. However, some efforts have not yielded results. The reason for that is a lack of recognition among some people who worked here before. That shortcoming is human nature. That doesn't matter. From now on, we must work together without ever letting go of the love, respect, humility, and compassion to align with the truth from within. That compassion should not leave from within. This is what Narayana Guru says: "Without leaving your holy body, the thought too." So, giving one's entire thought to that holy body, that holy body should reside within my soul. God should not be kept at a distance. Should not be distant. That is absolutely the Upanishad. When we say Upanishad, the meaning is to whisper in the ear. Not to say it out loud outside. That is, not to entertain people by telling the meaning. It is giving it in one's ear. That 'Upa' (near), that conversation or that concept or that action, that dharma which is with us, we are hearing it in our ear. It is being told in the ear. Not being said from afar. If said from afar, we will not get it. It is absolutely told in our ear. Understand this as truth regarding us. Experience it as truth.

Some of you tell me, I heard such a word. It was like this. It was told to me like this. Your greatness is exactly that. Do not think that there is any other greatness. Because unless it is useful for our soul, we will not get it wherever we go. We should have that understanding.

Our people have some shortcomings. They have been unable to take up some things. Even those who stood with me, I know, have been unable to take them up. Does that mean if I blame them and reject them, they will improve? Now their thousands of

What is the use of telling you about the good or bad deeds of past lives? That is not what we need. Today, God has given you an opportunity to honor and make others honor that service-mindedness of yours. Do not lose it.

If possible, when you act and realize that glory through love, you will see the emergence of truth. Otherwise, if you keep love somewhere else and just show something outwardly, it will not come. Love is dissolving and merging into every action we do. It is flowing into it. Imagine pouring honey from one bottle to another. It does not break. In this way, without being two, love is continuously pouring from our minds. We have followed so many mantras, yantras, and tantras. Whatever we do or don't do, if there is no love and respect in the mind, everything will be lost. Even if we don't have love, there is something God does to us. You have experienced it. It's just that most people don't respect it that much. Whether you respect it or not, God's representative will show love to you. That is the truth. It is shown to you and to the world, and thus reaching that state called the compassion of life, we become overwhelmed and unable to stand due to reverence. This is the truth.

April 1998

There may be someone with a certain kind of personality serving with us. If there is a dark side to their character, we should recognize it and try to correct it. But if it cannot be corrected in any way, then do not try to correct them. Because they will never be corrected. The deepest part of their mind is, as some theorists or ascetics say - the darkness inside that theory.

There are many kinds of religions in the name of religion. None of this is our subject. The respect of love, the strictness of humility, the survival of dharma - this is our religion. In it, virtue increases and one does not see two instead of one. God is giving you an opportunity for this. You should not lose it. Your merit is the effort to bring it to the people in whatever way possible. Otherwise, none of you should desire to be smart, or to see others as small or big through it. Let whatever comes in us come. If all of you work together in this knowledge, within five years that goodness will return to you in such a way that you cannot even determine where and what you did. That will be useful to you forever. We should have a concept that we should be able to introduce our traditional knowledge in that useful karma.

This subject is a new knowledge as far as we are concerned. That is because we have another knowledge that we have worshipped and learned. That knowledge belongs to the Trinity system, or Shaktheyam, Shaivam, Vaishnavam. In this, each theorist thinks his own is great. If none of this, the varna dharmas of Shudra, Vaishya, Kshatriya and Brahmin can be taught. Other than teaching all this, we have never been taught worship according to the times.

What is time? Krita Yuga, Treta Yuga, Dwapara Yuga, Kali Yuga. This is heard as Kali. While all other yugas have their own dharma, Kali also has its own dharma. What have we been taught about the dharma of this Kali? Kali is bad. We were born, grew up and will die in this Kali. If so, can we live after Kali is over? Another thing, can we leave the dharma of Kali? Then which dharma should we take? We were never taught this. Isn't it because this was not taught that Kali became bad?

What is the dharma of Kali? Kali is Shudra Kal. What is our understanding of Shudra? The one in the last quarter, the one in the fourth quarter. Kali Yuga is the time when the one in the fourth quarter, who was denied knowledge, gets knowledge. That is, the dharma of Kali is to pave the way for the Shudra to attain Brahma Jnana. That doesn't mean others won't get Brahma Jnana. We - whoever is born - belong to the dharma of the Shudra varna. Even though the Shudra is said to have the right to Brahma Jnana, Kali is a time when all wise men, all theorists, and all evil-doers have the right to liberation. Still, what is the reason for specifically mentioning only the Shudra? In other yugas, the Shudra was not even given the right to perform karma. That knowledge somehow came about. One without purity of karma cannot practice dharma. To practice dharma and reach the attainment of virtue in that dharma, some reason

A Shudra has not been given any knowledge to reach the truth. This concept of Dharma is very flawed. Thus, we have become unable to recognize the Dharma of the times. We have a knowledge based on worshiping, loving, and believing according to what we were told. It is in this knowledge that Kali became bad and the Dharma of Kali disappeared.

Every human being is born, grows, and dies. This is the law of the earth itself. To know this as a principle and act upon it, and that too for the people to act together, no dress, language, or knowledge has been given. Getting it is also impossible in this systemic order. This shortcoming is not just for the Shudra and the Vaishya - the Kshatriya and the Brahmin could not take up their respective Dharma either.

What are the rights of Kshatriya Dharma? What has this Kshatriya, who has returned as a Yogabhrashta, done? We have been spun around in mischief without knowing how to ask any of this. Just as we have been spun around in mischief, even the virtuous are spinning. When we say virtuous, it means the one who is entitled to Brahmajnana. It is said that even he has been destroyed.

This is a difficult subject to understand. As far as we are concerned, even if we study for our entire lives, it is difficult to understand this. For example, what is the condition of Sree Rama? What do we know about Sree Rama, who passed away more than seventeen lakh years ago? We are celebrating what we heard someone say. The story has been written and completed in such a way that we cannot even think about what Sree Rama is or what Sree Rama's end was.

Why did Sree Rama go down to the river? A story has been written for that too. This is something that happened in this Chaturyuga itself. Sree Rama is a man of the Treta Yuga. What Sree Rama could not do, no one has been able to do to this day. We have not received any different knowledge for that. No one has even thought about why those who came after him could not do it.

This mistake did not happen recently. This fourth quarter of this Chaturyuga has only passed five thousand years. Moreover, how can we understand the magnitude of this mistake while staying within the smallest language in Kerala, the smallest land? Will these South Indians themselves understand? If we take South India as a whole, there is a Karma that has been written and completed up to the Dakshinamurthy Stotram. The Dakshinamurthy Stotram is only for South Indians. North Indians do not have it. How can we take a knowledge beyond these periods of time with the knowledge in these Karmas that have been written and kept like this? Along with this, many Upanishads, Vedas, Shastras - coming like this, all these have become intertwined into a knot that can never be untied, in such a way that none of them can be sustained. What should we do with this, in what way should we take it? This is our subject. This is not a subject only for the Brahmin, the Kshatriya, or the Chandala. It is said that Sree Rama's culture is being developed in South India. What is the point of bringing a culture from seventeen lakh years ago here? Why did Sree Rama go down to the river? Our scientific truths, special stories, Vedas, and Puranas and Epics are all a helpless acquaintance where we cannot ask and know any of this.

Let us all think together whether we should change from this or not. Even if we think, what are we going to get? Who can do this? Can we go beyond that without liberating Sree Rama? Thus, there is a need to liberate all the Gurus who have come to this day.

In recent times, many gurus have come and gone.

What is the real historical truth? We are seeing the time difference that comes as the earth, moon, and sun revolve. The karma that occurs in this time difference is always happening. How long has it been revolving like this? What will it be like when this Yugadharma - the nature of the passing of time - comes? We have never understood this. What is the point of us talking about sciences, puranas, and Vedic epics without understanding this? When we act directly according to Yugadharma, what will we do with our ancestral guru paths, prophetic paths, and theoretical paths? We cannot proceed without taking up these karmas. Can we just say that none of this exists and move on? For example, we all know about the twenty-seven stars. But we can see billions of stars in a single orbit. How did this happen? Which science can explain it? Other than making us believe by calling it a galaxy, no science is clear on how these stars came to be. What we need to understand is that all these are great souls. Because these great souls did not get the right to Jivanmukti (liberation), they themselves became yogis and stood there. How can one cross them and go there? It is impossible to cross over in any way.

Everything is playing within the horizon of the twenty-seven stars. But there are billions of stars situated inside this solar system. How can a soul cross all these stars? Be it a ghost, a god, or anything else, that is not an issue at all. The tragedy is the inability to cross this trap.

As far as we are concerned, this is clear. If those who have done Guru Pooja here are told that they should only do this, no one will understand this capacity for action. There is no point in you coming here without understanding this. Even standing here is of no use. You should not lose this experience anywhere. Then I will not be able to do anything. You have not received this experience by being sanyasis. This is something not seen anywhere else in the world. Anyone can show any amount of Siddhi (mystic powers). But you will not be able to see this capacity for action anywhere. This is not my greatness or your greatness. God has given you the destined karma.

GURUVANIKAL · July 1998 · Volume 2
Malayalam source paragraphs GV2:P1386 to GV2:P1400

July 1998

What is the general perception about an ashram? In a general perception that has passed and currently exists, ashrams are of four types - Yoga, Bhakti, Karma, and Jnana. These four are the very foundation of the earth's culture. There might have been many other related characteristics to this.

The course of action of householders should be viewed with great importance. Because they are the ones who should be helpful in the Karma Kanda (section of action). Brahmacharis and devotees are very few. They should only be that much. They are guides, after all. Therefore, it is enough if their number is small. But householders are not like that. Sannyasis and devotees must all arise from the householder. Therefore, it is the householders who need to improve.

To improve, a center is essential. It is said that religious principles are united. But customs and rituals are different. None should be denied. Only by respecting everything can the inner essence of each be understood. The essence of various religious principles is the same. I have learned this without a doubt through my life of experience.

Some deny the temple system. They can deny it. We also do that, don't we. Blind denial will do more harm than good. Denial should be with the discrimination of cause and effect. It should be by gathering and summarizing the scientific awareness and essences in religious principles. Sage Charvaka rejected many things taught by the Vedas using logic. It is not enough to say that observing and experimenting by knowing through self-experience are the methods suitable for scientific examination. The essences known through examination must be gathered, summarized, and established through action.

The Hindu religious code or culture is very vast. Not just Christians and Muslims in India, but almost all religious sects are those separated from Hinduism. They separated because of the excess of hardship they had to suffer from it. That is, Hinduism made a mistake somewhere. But the mistake made by Hinduism is God's will. Very few people know this. But even those who know do not act with determination.

We know about the Varnasrama Dharma, don't we. Since the time the world was created, changes have been happening to us according to the times. The people of the Satya Yuga were like gods who could see God directly and pray. When the Satya Yuga passed, the times also changed. How many Chaturyugas and Manvantaras have passed! One Manvantara is seventy-one Chaturyugas. Looking at this, how insignificant is a human's lifespan. Within this insignificant period of time, we can only acquire a very small amount of knowledge.

Even though a human's lifespan is very short, we should be able to know at least the harm brought by Varnasrama Dharma during that time. In Varnasrama Dharma, there is no place for a Shudra. But there is a Shudra in every home. There is no point in denying that. He works innocently for everyone. The Brahmin knew what reward God had kept for his innocence - the right to play, the authority to rule. That is why he was even given military leadership. Because of the arrogance of the kings, the Shudra could not escape from the bottom.

and struck, transferring all the power of sin onto his head. Even a Shudra becoming a king was according to the Brahmin's rules. Even the king, who was elevated to the status of a Brahmin by being passed under the belly of a golden cow and tied with a thread, was not permitted by the Vedas. He was only able to hear the Murajapam. Can the subjects of such a king hear the Vedas? In the Varna-Ashrama Dharma, a Shudra was caught and made a king. But he has no knowledge. This ignorant king is the visible god! Thus the people became ignorant and ruined. Buddha came to change this. Before that, at the beginning of Kali Yuga, Krishna came. He opposed everything that existed. He declared, "Sambhavami Yuge Yuge" (I will be born in every age).

How difficult is it to transform the embodiments of Kali in today's Kali Yuga into brilliant Yuga Karma Yogis? The situation is that there are only black Englishmen molded by the British in India today. These poor people completely imitated the British. Not only dress and language, but even administration is an imitation of them. Our tradition is one of having kings who only obeyed what the Viceroy said. We do not have our culture and our religion. Even one who has studied philosophy thinks our culture is just stories; and the West's is history. Thus we are slaves in every way.

In Kali Yuga, there is no place for a deity. In Kali Yuga, the place is for the Guru. How many great souls came and declared this truth! First, Moses came. When he said that the words he spoke were received from God, the world mocked him. Two thousand years later, Jesus came. When he was crucified, he showed his form to those who believed. Even though they were fishermen and had to endure severe punishments, they worked with determination. Those who worked multiplied twofold. The Jews were exhausted before their sacrifice. Christians still have that tradition today. They were able to establish the world's government. Among them, they came to India and did a favor. If Madan became Mathai, he could walk on the main road. We swallowed both the good and the bad of the West equally. We imitate them in everything. When they came here without peace and grew their hair long, every beedi roller and boatman here started growing their hair long.

Our country suffered such a fate because our mothers became ignorant. Our ancestors (Pitrus) have no purity. Children will be born in the family according to the zodiac sign in which the Pitru stands. Children with intelligence and logic will be born in a family where Pitru purity has been achieved. In that field, Brahmins have always been at the forefront. The entire Shudra lineage turned out to be drunkards. Even if he is knowledgeable, he will be perverse. The Brahmin, however, did another trick; three Sambandhams (alliances), one Veli (marriage). The one in the Brahmin's house is Veli. All others are Sambandham. To complete any ritual he performs, he needs the woman he married (Veli). Or if she is not there, he will show the Yoni Mudra and perform a Tantra. We are satisfied with his Mantra and Tantra, aren't we? What knowledge can be imparted to a lineage that has degraded to this level?

No Acharya has ever come who has performed Pitru purity in its complete ritualistic sense. Shankaracharya performed Vedic rituals. Even Buddha said that if you believe, everything will change. We must know that there are many Pitrus who have reached the position of Buddha. Pitrus (indwellers) are filled in all subtle realms. These indwellers are the ones who killed Jesus. Pitru dominance has been established in that position. Pitrus rule over the positions of all the Acharyas who have risen.

The light that Buddha saw, the ray of light mentioned in the Quran, the tongue of fire in the language of Christians - thus whatever religions describe that consciousness in whatever ways

Whatever has been described, all of that is here. The children here see that divine energy in all these ways. Ancestral purification, which no one has done with complete purity until now, is being performed here. The ritual of gathering all ancestors and merging them into the auspicious light is carried out here. If ancestral purification is done here, then you have no ancestral bondage, and no ancestral curse. Then what you need to focus on is how to nurture your lineage. The result of what you do today will bear fruit after twenty-five years.

We do not deny anything. Each is correct to its own extent. But, what is absolutely essential for us today? Good children must be born in our families. Our children must have the knowledge to independently achieve whatever we are running around for. There should be good Karma Yogis, Jnanis, and Sanyasis in your home. You, who are the foundation for such a noble creation, must have a clear understanding of the ashram.

August 1998

The prayer currently offered at Santhigiri brings liberation to all the Mahatmas who have ever wished to follow the path of truth in their worship. People like us may not benefit as much. What is this liberation? It is not like what is described in the Hindu Puranas or Vedic hymns. When did these Mahatmas incur their karmic flaws, and how long will it take to resolve them? Once resolved, they will return to the human world. They will be all-powerful. God is guiding us towards such a karma.

You know that when marriages are conducted here, certain sankalpa karmas (rituals based on intention) are performed by them. In their sankalpa, certain signs are also revealed to them. Only about twenty-five out of a hundred people may experience this. The rest may not. When you hear the word 'darshan' (vision), you have certain preconceived notions. Even someone who previously practiced black magic (Chathan Seva) might exhibit extraordinary powers if they get a vision. What benefit do people get from these extraordinary powers? Amazed by such powers, we follow the path of people with karmic flaws and turn towards adharma. We believe in the karma of ghosts who died in that manner or died in accidents, and we have a practice of worshiping them by lighting lamps. We do not know what the result of worshiping all this is. We also do not know what will befall our children. Even though we don't know any of this, a strong tendency towards sorcery is seen in foreign countries today. Yet, these are the people considered to be highly civilized. That is where this is seen. So, you must think about where this evil spirit resides and what it does.

The prayer performed at Santhigiri is a truth revealed by God to bring about a change from all this. You are the children who have the right to practice this. Children born since 1973 especially have that right. However, that right will not come to you immediately. Every twelve years, there will be a change in the government of this country. The change in all these governments will happen through these children. These children are going to be the ones who demonstrate what dharma is in this world and how to remove karmic flaws. By the time of your third generation, this will have happened. God is making us the guiding light of such a great doctrine.

This is a time when God is making each of us, each family, complete a great dharma.

What have we heard about Sanathana? We have been taught that it is the Hindu dharma. In what way have we been taught this? Just as the Hindu guiding principles speak of Shaktheyam, Shaivam, Vaishnavam, or Brahma, Vishnu, Shiva, in other religions too

This is seen in another imagination. Only that it is in their language. But, when it comes to the side of knowledge, this is not correct.

What was previously called Sanathana is the history of four faces. What are these four faces? It is a system that works as the faces of Kritha Yuga, Thretha Yuga, Dwapara Yuga, and Kali Yuga. It shows us how these four faces work. It passes it on to your children. It will not change until it reaches Sathya Yuga. After that, we have to know what will happen in the coming times. We must believe that this will not change if humans change it. If we can remove a major karmic flaw ordained by fate, at least to the extent of a grain of sand, according to the measure of Brahma's decision, that is enough. Thus it will spread to all parts of the world. After spreading, it will be experienced by all religious doctrines in our long history. Everyone will preserve it and contribute it to the world.

Looking at the whole world, there is an announcement that 2144 gurus came for this correction after the time of Krishna. But they could not correct it. When they correct a little, they will be driven away somehow. A brief description of it can be seen from the time of Moses. After the time of Sree Krishna, it starts from there. Those who have studied history know what that great man was made to do after it started. After that, until the time of Jesus, things like this kept coming. Even though all that came, it only shows the guidance for a hundred years, two hundred years, five hundred years, a thousand years, and two thousand years. No guidance beyond this can be said about them. You will think that with this the world will turn to ashes. It's not like that. Who will we be when we think that the guidance done by these great souls is only this much? In between this, some things are coming. It also has many kinds of negative aspects.

At one time, there was a rumor that the world was going to end. Then believers went to temples, churches, and other places of worship and did and made others do many things. Nothing happened that day. Later, many times it was announced here that such and such a thing was going to happen in such and such a place. What was said to happen was seen here in a vision. At all those times, there was an announcement to pray here. About ten or twenty years ago, there was an incident where people on the Kollam beach were relocated. The reason was that a storm would come. It was announced here and asked to pray. The people who came and went here were made to pray. A specific time period was also given for the prayer. That storm left here and reached some other place. But that storm did not go in vain. It went on and on and took away a whole island. On that day, a priest of a church on that island lamented like this: Where is the Lord I believed in? Father! What can they do? After that, it was said that a cyclone would come two or three more times. At all those times, the believers here were called and made to pray. God accepted that prayer too. That cyclone went to the Bay of Bengal.

Recently, you might have read in the newspapers the prediction that a large piece of rock is coming towards the earth. When looked here, it is not a piece of rock, but a comet with four or five eyes. There will be war even on the path it passes. This is also called a comet. In the old days, seeing this was a great fear. Its light will cover other places. It can be seen like a torchlight. After traveling a little, it will disappear. Then it will hit somewhere, and the place where it falls will be destroyed.

What is this comet? This is a great danger that has resulted from yoga systems like Hatha Yoga. They are independent. They are a kind of souls who do not even respect God. Through pranayama, certain other abilities developed by yoga, and by consuming certain types of medicines, they have conditioned their bodies in such a way that they will not perish even if they sit on a clod of earth. Such people can be seen in the Himalayan regions. A doctor named Somervell saw this during a Himalayan expedition and publicized it. He was in Thengapattanam (Neyyoor) for a few days. At that time, he came to treat Narayana Guru. But he said he would not treat him. Because a stronger medicine than what has already been given to that body should not be administered. Saying, "Let us pray for the Guru," he knelt down and prayed for five minutes. He told a story. While walking in the Himalayan regions, he saw an anthill in the shape of a person. He slowly approached, cleared the bushes, and looked, but there were no termites in the anthill. There was barely a single termite. But it was indeed a termite mound. The person did not know that this anthill had grown over him. After a while, he opened his eyes. When he opened his eyes, a person (Somervell) was standing there. The man inside the anthill asked: "Is Henry 4 still ruling the country?" Somervell replied, "No." From the time of Henry 4 to the time of Somervell, this man who sat inside the anthill calling upon God did not know whose reign it was. He had conditioned this body with Navapashanas and regulated it so that it could survive on a clod of earth, equally in rain, wind, and sun, without even a drop of water. If this is the true fact in our history, what have we embraced? What are we carrying around?

September 1998

This is the history of our thought on God; the truth of history is of this error. To resolve such an error that was brought upon humanity, God has sent two thousand one hundred and forty-four gurus and prophets over the past six thousand years. They could not accomplish it. If God has chosen us to correct that error, or if God wishes to fulfill that mission through poor, ignorant people like us, think about how fortunate each of you who are participants in that mission are, how blessed you are. Through this, you are becoming a path to save the world. You should think of yourselves in such an elevated manner. Instead, you shouldn't think that a coconut fell on your back, or that you encountered misfortune while walking on the northern side. Those are just some of the negative aspects that occur in a human's birth. It is the destruction that belongs to the zodiac sign of birth and the zodiac sign of the lineage that was investigated. What decision should you live by to escape from this destruction, how should you correct your children to create such a lineage of life - the action for this is what is advised from here. Sharing that action with you and the world, God is carrying out the action of implementing it through us.

The path in Santhigiri is the determination of Brahma. This path is what is called Sanathana. Four eras - Krita Yuga, Treta Yuga, Dvapara Yuga, Kali Yuga. Kali Yuga is the path by which man reaches wisdom through the accumulated actions up to Dvapara. That path has been wrong for the past twenty-five Chaturyugas. This error is being corrected now, here. Whether you have this understanding or not, whether you carry it with you or not, God has gifted you that knowledge. To complete this action, you pray upon seeing a form and name capable of a special action given by God. What are you seeing when you pray to this? How many karmic flaws the ancestors brought through worship, how much it has spread in this world - that is being seen from this little child to the mothers.

When you see this, you will have various kinds of pain. There will be no one to pamper you when you see this. Sometimes you might even get kicked and beaten. Even then, you must have the certainty and knowledge that 'I will do this'. We, who do not even think about why that father and mother had to sit in prison to give birth to Krishna, said we didn't kill him because we didn't get him in our hands. What did we turn Krishna himself into? This is the knowledge and familiarity we gained from the Hindu Dharma. This knowledge itself has been continuing for so long. If this is not changed, we will keep going towards an action that destroys the world. Due to the mistake in this system, billions of souls are standing corrupted in the atmosphere.

A short while ago, a stone fell from the atmosphere. It was not a stone. It was a meteorite. It exploded and fell into the Jupiter system. At first, scientists thought it was coming to Earth. It is waiting to fall into many other systems just like it fell into the Jupiter system. It is circling around for many miles to cause destruction. A huge scene like this is waiting above us. This can happen in all parts of this solar system. If this happens in all parts of the solar system, it will overturn all the theories of the Yugadharma. Jesus gave us evidence about this destroyer when he said, 'Satan, go away.' It did not go away. It continued until the crucifixion. This devil destroyed all the great souls who came to this day. Seeing this danger, some others have also called out and given evidence. The one who theorized this devil is karma dosha ruling us. It is seeing this karma dosha that the great ones called out.

For example, those who go to holy places die in accidents when they return. The Government of India tried in many ways to prevent this, but it did not work. Last year, at the Kumbh Mela in Allahabad, out of the lakhs of sanyasis who participated, some made claims and fought with each other. Some died. What is the claim of the sanyasis? Such and such a place is such and such a holy place. It belongs only to them. No one else should enter there. Another form of this is throwing stones at Satan. Many people die in that too. This is the specialty of devotion. Many such karma doshas that die and perish happen in the name of our theories. We should think about how to change these karma doshas. It is to change this that God makes us pray here. Therefore, when you pray, you should imagine and pray that whatever karma doshas have come in this world due to the order of worship should change and the souls standing as chitta due to the dosha of worship should get rebirth or be destroyed.

Our daily prayer is such a karma. God is making us do this world service. Brahma Muhurtha is the true dharma that we complete. You are praying here that whatever salvation should be given to us who are working by taking up such a corrupt tradition from that Brahma Nishchaya, whatever mistakes and good things are there in this karma that we do, you should take it all up and this karma should become holy and that holiness should reach fortune and all fortune should be given as a gift for these people.

Religious theories, epics, and various theories have tried in many ways to convince the things that came through the order of worship that went wrong for many ages. All these attempts are their intellectual exercises. If you try to fill God with literal knowledge, will it be useful for the conduct of Brahma Nishchaya? You should think about this.

October 1998

The purpose of birth should be to fulfill the divine will of the Almighty, who is the master of all the human beings born and dying across ages, not just the spiritually meritorious of ancient times. Whether material or spiritual, the good aspect is to realize the right and wrong that have come and gone in the world so far, and to know the reason for that right and wrong. We all know that if there is a right, there will also be a wrong along with it. Whatever human nature has been passed down, from the highly intelligent to the utter fool and those in between, all are born as heirs to a system of karma.

It is written that our doctrine is one that does not hurt even a blade of grass, a worm, or an ant. Moreover, some gurus of certain lineages walk around with their mouths covered to control the air going in and out while speaking. In principle, all this is correct. But how can the active karma of human life be fulfilled through these methods? Or else, how can men and women, young and old, realize the field of belief in the guru lineage? Today, we stand together in a meaningless karma of pretending to be a scholar or an ignoramus by talking too much about the gravity or guidance in the karma of speaking with an intellect that does not lose its dignity and an intellect that does not distinguish between honor and dishonor.

The aspect of Hindu or Sanatana dharma is a philosophy that shows compassion to life or living beings. The religious doctrines contain a philosophy that has been controlled for billions of years. Does it mean that it is complete if a scholar adds a sloka by bringing it into the form of vowels and consonants through literal interpretation and extending it to an infinity where one cannot distinguish between what is and what is not? Similarly, we possess an ignorance where doctrines have been formed through learning, teaching, knowing, and not knowing for a long time. Although we can go like that if we remember the meaning of any religious doctrine, do you think that all the meaning brought to us from our ancestors who acted by imagining a devil or each name and form that has come and gone so far has been completed with these fragments of Vedanta? Or else, many historians came and wrote over time, great scholars, not just Siddhantakaumudi, Laghusiddhantakaumudi, but if we say we should only take the good and discard the bad in the karma that has been copied for millions of years with right and wrong mixed together, with what will we discard it? With what will we take it? This habit of thinking has become necessary and essential for all of us.

Today's subject before us is the many historical truths that have been lost, along with the ultimate truth of many theories that are considered to be the meaning of this world. We look at that historical truth through the visionary who says everything is karma and the visionary who says there is no karma at all. From which side are they speaking? Both have spoken truthfully in two ways. In what way did this karma come into being, and in what way did it cease to exist? We all know that if there is karma, there will be a cause and effect in it. Many people - many great men - have tried their whole lives to find the extent of the glory of the time we were born, where these two are intertwined, without establishing that it exists or does not exist. Should we understand that all investigations are complete if these truth-seekers have strived for us over many periods and read or written up to the encyclopedia to establish that everything in the world exists?

We know a truth: I, mine, my father, mother, children, wife, husband, and thus the matter of measuring the enjoyment for the good of all that is mine is everything we said earlier. If everyone applauds and passes the best word among those measured, and goes about babbling the goodness of that word, should we believe that the knowledge of dharma, karma, jnana, and vijnana, and the familiar karma and dharma have been transformed and their pros and cons have been returned?

However, experience so far has proven that it is not like this at all. The goal of all the truth that begins by saying I am speaking the truth, I have seen the truth, is that each person's own truth or own life belongs to a large, broad mind, a goal of a long time. What have we been able to learn as good and bad that we have studied so far? That is true for all thinking people. Thus, the yoga, karma, jnana, and bhakti that form the truth spoken knowingly and unknowingly about anything are all true as long as there are humans in many ways. If we speak pure and true words about that truth, how can we reach it by giving karma and dharma for ages, whether truth and untruth are yet to come or not?

Can the experiential truth that originated and was experienced from such thought be completed by vocalizing it with words like this? It speaks of good and bad

What have we been able to do so far other than establishing it on the side? This stands before us as a big issue. From humans to gods who rule and make others rule the world, which has emerged as good and bad irrespective of religious differences. What have we achieved with a life that bargains with the dharma and karma of the special rule seen by respecting and making others respect the karma path that has come through the concept of God-Goddess or the prophetic system and guru system of the respective religion, writing and making others write stories, playing and making others play, using words that can reach up to the path of salvation if necessary, and putting it into practice?

Our goal is to complete the qualifications of the respective times of the fourteen Manus known as the Manu lineage. What it is like is that each Kaliyuga has been brought about as the end nature of each Chaturyuga by evaluating the karma and dharma spoken about Manu and karma in the karma path called Swayambhu. The reason for bringing Kali to such an end is to carefully know what Kali Dharma is after reaching the light of time as humans, grass, flowers, plants, and medicinal plants. If we think about how we have passed through the Manu lineage, the trinity system, the Vedic religion that emerged from it, and the Varnashrama dharma, have we been respecting and worshiping the truth imparted by the Vedanta religion by carefully studying the Vedas and epics? This cannot be counted and said. Therefore, Buddha, reaching the attainment of the world of Nirvana with the three refuges, thought for many times and showed the three refuges without any other way. Kaliyuga is ordained for us to reach at some point in a truth that has been sought after for many times. The truth that this body is for the benefit of others is that karma, dharma, and truth work together by taking and seeing the right and wrong of the other three times. Our theoretical religion is to take and see such a time-bound nature and work it out.

The information is that seventy-one Chaturyugas make up one Manvantara in the Manvantara calculation. The twenty-eighth Chaturyuga of the seventh Manu (Vaivasvata Manu) out of such fourteen Manvantaras is currently going on. The trinity system and the Sanatana religion system are to see and act from where it went wrong. The goal of Kali is to take and see all that has been acquired and complete it. Instead, our Vedanta religion has only one God, only one Brahman, only one prophet, only one Guru. This is where we have made a mistake. To correct this, that Brahman determination itself has stood as our support and shade and worked for many times. Evaluating the respective times, our inability to stand dissolved in the karma of seeing and completing it is the reason for our destruction so far.

Anyone with some knowledge knows that any state is completed by the Kali period. Guru Pooja at Santhigiri is a karma of its own. The six Manvantaras and the first three Chaturyugas of the seventh Manvantara shown by God or Brahman are true. Manvantara is an imagined dharma that has been lying as Manvantaras up to the measure of life, maintaining the path of dharma by working while standing in this true karma. When followers of such a Manu come, they are called visionaries. In the same way, a visionary who continuously completes a Manvantara in guru paths has many followers in guru-disciple relationships. In that, each guru or each prophet completes the right and wrong in the Kaliyuga itself, counts each state in the special situation of God, makes it pure, and attains the truth.

Thus, there is a visionary who has completed many Chatur Yugas. There is a visionary who has divided a Yuga among five gurus. Among them, the fifth is a person who works here and there and evolves from one to another. It is this fifth person who transfers that Yuga to the next Yuga. Thus, the guru who completes a Chatur Yuga is also a visionary, and a Manvantara is composed of many such gurus. Reaching fourteen such Manvantaras is written as a Kalpa, which is a Brahma Kalpitam. If it is calculated that a guru who has discovered this much has completed one day of Brahma, how many days and nights combined would make up the lifespan of this Brahma.

In reality, a human being does not even need to think and understand such things. Because, with the measure of his life, what can he think about this? Or else, what can be thought out about this in one lifetime? But it is not like that, there should be an action in life where a human lives and learns what he has done until the end of his life. We do not know the secret that we all live to know and live the blessedness achieved through the sacrifice of working until the end of life by visionaries, gurus, and thousands of gurus in many ways, who have seen and spoken in the gross and subtle, in cause and effect, that a theoretical action of good and bad exists here as billions of humans lived and performed actions. Therefore, even if we cannot describe and know all this, if we do not know the truth of life that attains dharma through an action sacrificed for us by someone who knew, they will become nothing one by one.

The number one has value only when we start counting as one. Otherwise, in the action called numbers, one will become nothing. Similarly, the meaning of each of our lives is the counting of many lives. Even after saying this in this way, in many places, it has come to be said as just a single word.

November 1998

When someone begins to write the life story of a person who has lived to be a hundred years old, it becomes a composition mixed with right and wrong. Then one might ask, why write this? Through this, to understand a truth, it is not just the billions of souls like you and me who have lived, but the billions of human resources that represent the evolution of life. In that context, does God exist or not? Or if God does not exist, what is the point of us holding onto such a concept of God? When truth, dharma, and justice—which have been brought to the level of a world encyclopedia wearing the veil of knowledge—are presented as a picture shaped like a vast, unrecognizable forest or a huge mountain, can one understand the path of action that discovered the plants and medicinal herbs, from the banyan tree growing in that forest to the hardwood trees in all parts, and how and where to use them? We are proceeding in a manner where we do not know how to recognize it and formulate a guideline to understand its qualities. For example, if we look at people born and raised in the homes of physicians, they also know some medicine. They might know to pluck the root of a plant or something. We know nothing about the long period of hardship involved in discovering this. Or we could not respect and understand anything regarding that truth of life. One who administers a single herbal remedy or a raw medicine becomes the master of medicine. That person has no understanding of the pros and cons resulting from this. The disease might get cured. However, it is not to say that we should walk backward to learn and recognize every fact that has existed up to today. But regarding a theory of karma that has grown as mentioned, when we study its concepts, Narayana Guru has written a prayer fragment for children to read: 'Before time passes forward, building a fort with memories,' and 'Counting one by one, when the counted things end, the inner self stands still like the seeing eye' (like the eye of your mind). When we praise and learn the billions of theories of the guiding lights whose thoughts have evolved in that manner, how can we recognize the mistakes that might occur?

How will we recognize the truth that has occurred between the truth of a beginning and an end that has been stated? This is the truth. How can we explain the essence of the word 'truth', which is each of us who has grown up before us, not knowing where the end of the truth that starts with the assertion 'I have seen the truth' lies, or where the destruction of that truth lies? The word that completes it is truth.

There is only one. Thus, everything is one. When we try to understand the truth or untruth that has spread across the world in crores of knowledge regarding what kind of knowledge is the realization shown by taking a photo of the forest mentioned earlier—the summary that the essence of everything is one—in order to know the secret of the universe called truth or untruth, various studies we have today have emerged. With the advancement of those studies, if we talk about the facts, stories, and theories of science that discovered the atom and the subatomic particle, does it mean that we have understood the entire position of knowledge that has spread and risen to this day? Even there, the truth of 'you' and 'I' remains.

When saying this, we all know—our knowledge about the truth is limited only to the extent of our individual knowledge and what we have experienced and counted. But aren't each of us standing at the measure of the horizon, discovered according to the precision of the eye that measures the sky, the forest, the backwaters, and the sea that make up the world where the many living beings that have come so far have grown? Similarly, for us who state the measure of truth, there remains a truth of not knowing to whom, what, and how to explain it. That too is a truth.

Let us view an evolutionary theory that discovers such truths and untruths like this. Oh! His dream, or the dream I saw, or the waking state I experienced, or the deep sleep of 'I', the gross, the subtle, the cause, and the effect—it is about such a path of discovery that is spoken of from the atom to the absolute. Thus, each of us is a great repository of thousands of absolute wholes. Each of us has a nature of knowing the gross, the subtle, and the absolute through the thought of God, by distinguishing the gross, the subtle, the cause, and the effect that sees the absolute of each person in that way, or sees the truth of life. "He knows nothing, poor thing"—we only know a specific state of truth mixed with truth and untruth that is completed and stopped by saying such words. We do not reach the karma of the visionaries who say "if one counts one by one and the meaning ends." Thus, we cannot complete anything in the position of a visionary. We cannot even complete ourselves. The completion of such a thing is our subject. When reaching that subject, each of our subjects wanders away from the imagined karma like a sky-flower found by counting one by one. Previously it was thought that an atom could not be split. Now it has been discovered that even an atom can be split—atom, subatomic particle, or the ultimate atom.

Because we have reached such a level of learning, without the realization of the vision of karma and the path of karma that measures our lifetime and reaches up to the calculation of Manvantaras, we will fall into the group of saying "I know, I have heard, I have known," like completing the measure of demonic nature by talking about unrespectable outcasts and idiocy. Or else, each of us will stand at the point of concluding that "if all that is measured as 'I, I' ceases, within it is not many but becomes one." Thus, feeling that this ego called 'I' has completed the ego of many things, we continue as the essence of the ego. Having learned such an ego, how will you and I recognize the path of karma that has been completed as something else? Again, we say that it has become the completion of the truth of a scholar who recognizes the sound form using the dictionary of sounds formed by letters, the truth of an ignorant person, and each knowledge in between. This person who speaks of the completion of 'Aham Brahmasmi' (I am Brahman), and thus each person having become 'Aham Brahmam', completely the scholar, the ignorant, the forest dwellers, and the karma, dharma, merit, and sin in between,

Does it mean that the meaning of the Upanishadic light and secret of the forest dwellers has been completely understood? Does it mean that the truth, dharma, etc., which measure all this with intellect, dharma, karma, and merit, have all come under our control? We know one thing, a knowledge as big as us. Billions of our knowledge. But when we know or try to know that our evolutionary knowledge of life through the ages is completely inadequate, we can only see the sum of good and evil that aligns with the truth, dharma, and adharma that may come our way, within our measure. In this truth, there are those who are beginning to learn, those who have learned and reached a stage, those who have been promoted to the next class, and those who express the love and respect of each class. This should not be just enjoying telling the stories of the English, or ancestors, or doctorate holders, but when we come to know if we have achieved anything, it becomes a slight counting of what the part of knowledge is - or knowing the secret and the evolutionary truth of life of each one that stands completely counted one by one. Or knowing how the knowledge divided into the five elements, five senses, and five sheaths, and the mixed life are connected to the Supreme Soul. This is the theory of life. Everyone should not study and become a scholar, and end up seeking the condition of enjoying saying 'I' standing in the karma where everyone's scholarship dawns. For that too, there is a logical secret told by Narayana Guru, the essence of 'I' also continues. When we learn and reach this like this, like the differences in states or like the class system, everyone is great when standing in the sum of their class or knowledge or luck. But this is not the greatness of our knowledge. This is where the truth told by our ancestors dawns. Continuing like this is the secret of life. A long-sighted person or a state is said about a life that has been completed by long births and deaths with such a secret. The evolutionary secret of each life is the combination of thousands of such states. Thus, each theorist is a combination of thousands of evolutionary secrets. Thus, a great visionary who has gone and seen many truths is the one who should teach us the truth in an exemplary manner. The one who tells the meaning of many such truths is the visionary of the Yuga Dharma. This opportunity we got to be born is a Kali Yuga that has passed so much of such Yuga Dharma. The completion of such Kali Yuga is the complete secret that leads each of us and brings us there. Thus, the visionary of the Yuga Dharma is the one who separates and shows the truth and untruth through the path of vision that shows the ultimate secret of the secret of life. That is each Acharya. The Yuga Dharma is the combination of many such Acharyas.

Thus, as they kept writing, again realizing the unbroken and long dharma of the Yugantara Dharma, the knowledge of waking, dreaming, and deep sleep, and the light of the stream of light that always knows the truth became each completion. That is the summary of the Upanishads. The meaning of such shorthand is the life shown by the forest dwellers. It is not possible for us to distinguish and understand this. But we have an easy way. It was to say that that so much had to be said. We can also say it in a single word.

That is what is known as waking, dreaming, deep sleep, and Turiya. Without dismissing it as a dream, without dismissing it as the madness of waking, it is a veil of knowledge that recognizes the karma called waking, deep sleep, and Turiya. This is the theoretical secret of the ego that says Aham Brahmasmi. Without being destroyed by stating the theory in that way, without completing by stating the meaning of the theory, one step is said about the seven thousand four hundred and forty-four visions completed in the stream of light of the knowledge of the previously mentioned waking and deep sleep.

Regarding such a philosophy of vision, there is no need to complete it by saying it exists or does not exist. In the place of knowledge, knowing this much, through seven thousand four hundred and forty-four visions, standing in the supreme position called the presence of God, the presence of the Lord, which elevates each one of us, the truth of truths is the presence of God. Following that, the presence of the Lord. Thus, experiencing the Brahma presence of the other and the Supreme, what is spoken is the meaning of life.

The truth of the Guru is to explain and complete each aspect of knowledge or to know and complete it. Such a truth is the greatness of a life where realization and familiarity come together. The path of dharma of such a great stream of light is completion. The ignorance or knowledge that goes to see the end of the Vedas is to become exhausted by thinking according to one's own intellect, speaking of such completion as great or small. The attainment of Manvantara or the measure of Chaturyuga is that which passes through six Manvantaras and twenty-eight Chaturyugas of this kind of knowledge, where right and wrong are mixed, which is truth and untruth, taking back the changes, and where knowledge and the completion on the part of knowledge have passed many times.

This is the Guru's word that we must know and grasp. When that word becomes truth, the Guru also becomes God; the life dharma with the greatness of the Supreme. This is the word is truth, truth is Guru, Guru is God. It is to see and know this that the ancestors have spoken of the tradition of vision. The secret of knowledge called Grihasthashrama, Brahmacharya, Vanaprastha, and Sannyasa, which are the main parts of this, can only be completed by all of us if it is completed through the Grihasthashrami. Such completion is the continuous right of life.

This is what we have theorized as the Guru. The difference between the Guru who is created by the meaning of the word using the meaning of vowels called vowels and consonants by people called scholars or knowledgeable people, and the experienced Guru is this. The truth of Gurupooja in Santhigiri is to see, know, and complete this.

December 1998

Gurukalabrahmaniveditham

There was a time when the world had no realization about the Brahma determination or the glory of God. It does not mean that the knowledge is complete even now. Except for getting the opportunity to do only the things that were told from Brahma in those times, the world could not take that knowledge. The complete realization about the Brahma determination was seven

Only a Manvantara and three Chaturyugas. The great Sanat Satyatmanan came at the end of the third Chaturyuga. Although he knew that he was the heir to the Brahma resolution, the secret of his own life was not fully revealed to him until his last days.

I am stating here what has been revealed in my experience. I do not know if there is anything beyond this.

A Manvantara is the conception of the Saptarshis. By that conception, a solar system creation is formed. The solar system has nine planets with form and subject to the theory of evolution. The greatness of Rahu and Ketu in it has not been determined. These planets spread into twelve zodiac signs and give twelve results. It is not certain what results Rahu and Ketu should give in it. But there are destined things said for everything. An incompleteness is seen in both of these.

The Saptarshis gave a conception for a solar system creation in the calculation of nine planets, twelve zodiac signs, and twenty-seven stars. Scientifically and by some other experiences, there are those who call it seven spheres. These seven spheres have form and nature. But Rahu and Ketu sometimes travel out of place while circling like this. Therefore, we should not desire its full result. Because no one can determine how the time difference will be when traveling around in the karma of a Jupiter year. That is, their movement is not fixed. They travel in an unpredictable order.

There is a measure of Brahma light for the movement of the solar system. It may shift here and there and its effect may be felt. But it will not leave this sphere. If there is a difference of one minute in this movement, it will cause an error of up to a yama. It cannot be accurately determined. But in our calculation, there are minutes, seconds, nazhikas, vinazhikas, and even the most delicate trudi.

Whatever calculations there are, there should be no error in the greatness that came in the conception of the Saptarshis. That is the path of truth. That is human life.

There is a junction for Manvantaras, Chaturyugas, and Yamas. Sandhyavandanam is done as a karma to remove the Yamasandhi. There may be those who are awake for three yamas and at most five yamas. But we have been told the time of worship in all eight yamasandhis.

There is a calculation of five gurus for an era. These gurus are the ones who bring the calculation of Sandhyavandanam according to the Brahma resolution and the recognition of times. But we cannot say how many great people come in the Yugasandhi. In the four yugas, the Brahma resolution is described to us. If an error occurs in the Yugadharma at any time, that error cannot be recovered later. It takes a lot of effort for that. The lives and words of great people prove it.

Satyatmanan said the Chaturyakya. In knowledge, scholars and commoners fell into a pit in such a way that times could not be recognized. If you look at the past twenty-five Chaturyugas, this truth can be seen. Our subject is what was revealed about where the error occurred in this truth.

As proof that this mistake has occurred, there are lives that have crossed thousands of hurdles before us. The life of a human, a pitru, a deva, a rishi, and a sannyasi! Having seen what the life of a muni is in these lives, what the life of a rishi is, and coming down, what the life of a deva is, and what the life of a rishi under a deva is, the path guided by the rishi standing above the deva is the truth that exists through the words of the rishis. There was a time when nothing was known about this truth. A skill that was acquired by being told where this truth exists above the Trimurti system was completed in Varkala. That occasion was a time of physical and mental torture. It is impossible to count or finish telling what kind of torture it was. To take this back, we are indebted to that Brahma resolve that made that Brahma resolve a person. The life of mahatmas is one that has come without being able to decide what is right and wrong in the Manu lineage and how to fulfill it.

Six Manvantaras have passed. The calculation is seventy-one Chaturyugas for each Manvantara. Four Yugas make one Chaturyuga. The calculation is five Gurus for the resolution of each Yuga. The smallest of the Yugas is Kali Yuga. This itself is four lakh thirty-two thousand years. Dwapara is double this. Dwapara and Kali together make Treta Yuga. Treta Yuga and Kali together make Krita Yuga. The period from the birth of Sree Rama to the birth of Sree Krishna has come after many lakhs of years, at the rate of five Gurus for each Yuga. Sree Krishna was born as a great man who fully knew the Kali Dharma before the beginning of Kali. Manu has evaluated that Kali, the fourth quarter, belongs to the Shudra class. That is, Kali is the time when the full right of Brahmajnana is decreed to the Shudra, fully understanding all the subjects that are to be ruled and made to rule in the fourth quarter. It is as an example of this that Krishna was born in the Yadava clan. The original mistake occurred twenty-five Chaturyugas ago. It was mentioned earlier that the word Aham Brahmasmi originated from a great fall that occurred when a rishi named Satyathranan thought about honoring the truth and saw what he was and what his predecessors were, and took up the decisive truth that has come as Chaturyugas and Manvantaras. Later, when it was completed with four sentences, it was not conceptual completeness, but literal completeness that was brought about. That is the mistake that has been continuing for twenty-five Chaturyugas. Gurukula Brahmaniveditham is an action that corrects this mistake and brings about conceptual completeness. This ceremony used to be held in a different way before.

The Shudra class are ignorant, which is why it was easy to fool them. Every one in the Shudra class are children of that mistake. There was a ceremony in the past to start the alphabet at the age of five. This ceremony was performed equally for boys and girls. This had an advisory nature. This will be done before the age of five. There is no such ceremony among the Brahmin community. Among them, there is no ceremony other than starting the alphabet. The ceremony kept for this among the Shudra class is Kettukalyanam. Similarly, Thirandukalyanam, then Kalyanam, Pulikudi Kalyanam, etc. When a girl is born and so many ceremonies are performed, that house will definitely become impoverished. So if four girls are born, there is nothing to say.

A ceremony shown among the Brahmins is the Guru giving advice and starting the alphabet. What they show in the marriage ceremony is an action ordained by the birth of Vyasa. It was through a Sankalpa Karma that Vyasa got the name Krishna Dwaipayana. Vyasa told us as if it came from Krishna's mouth. Vyasa in which all Yugas

It is not certain whether it has come. It is widely accepted that Vyasa is the son of a fisherwoman. The mythological histories that divided and explained the four Vedas and the six Shastras are the prescribed, written truths for Hindus.
